### **Introduction**

**Call of the Forsaken** is EverQuest's 20th expansion. It was announced on August 5, 2013, and officially launched on October 8, 2013.

- For the original announcement, see [this news story](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/story.html?story=32830).
- For the expansion release patch message, see [this news story](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/story.html?story=33184).

Additional expansion content is expected to be released throughout 2014.

- The first of these updates was announced as **Hate Rising** in December 2013's [Letter From the Producer](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/story.html?story=33574), with the live release happening on [January 22, 2014](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/story.html?story=33706).

- The second update was announced in March 2014's [Letter From the Producer](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/story.html?story=34006), with the live release of **Tower of Rot** happening on [May 21, 2014](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/story.html?story=34402).

- The third update consisted of new spells and AAs, and was released on [June 18, 2014](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/story.html?story=34558).

- The fourth update was officially announced in a [post by the Producer](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/story.html?story=34567) as bringing about the final zone: **Argin-Hiz**, which went live on [July 16, 2014](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/story.html?story=34728).

* * *

### **Content & Features**

#### **Initial Release**

The expansion's officially announced features (released October 8, 2013):

- Mercenary AA â€“ Increase the skill of your mercenary with new alternate advancements.
- Mercenary Gear â€“ Raise the effectiveness of your mercenary with by earning new items they can use.
- Heroic Adventures â€“ Delve into our mission-style content that allows for unique experiences each time you play through them, including a chance at new loot, rares, and currency you can collect and spend at special merchants!
- Shared Bank Slots â€“ Share more with an increase in shared bank slots!
- New Crafting Recipes â€“ Take your craft to new heights with new recipes for all!
- More Spells, AAs and Items â€“ Further your skills and power.
- New Raids!

#### **Hate Rising**

The first update to the Call of the Forsaken expansion was **Hate Rising** (released January 22, 2014):

- New content (same Tier) expands upon the current COTF storyline
- Tier 1 Breastplates, Vambraces, and Boots are in
- 3 new group missions (Ethernere, Neriak Fourth Gate, Dead Hills)
- 4 new raids (Ethernere, Bixie Warfront, Neriak Fourth Gate, Dead Hills)
- 15 new heroic adventures (6x Ethernere, 6x Neriak Fourth Gate, 3x Bixie Warfront)
- 15 new nameds spread throughout the heroic adventures
- 5 new collection sets
- Lower level vendorbought gear (using the same Marks of Valor currency):

  - Level 85 (variety of Underfoot gear and weapons)
  - Level 90 (variety of House of Thule gear and weapons)
  - Level 95 (variety of Veil of Alaris gear and weapons)

- All heroic adventures (new and old) now scale down in difficulty to Level 85
- Note: No new zones, AAs, or spells in Hate Rising

  - New sections of the Bixie Warfront and Neriak Fourth Gate are now open (accessed in heroic adventures)

#### **Tower of Rot**

The second update to the expansion was **Tower of Rot** (released Wednesday, May 21, 2014):

- 1 new zone (the first Tier 2 zone in COTF)
- 10 named mobs
- 6 quests
- 1 heroic adventure
- 2 missions
- 1 raid
- 4 collection sets
- Note: No new AAs or spells with Tower of Rot.

The opening of the Tower of Rot (this is the structure [seen here](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/scenery2/deadhills-cooper3-tower.jpg) in the Dead Hills) was preceded by [pre-launch events](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=7524) emanating out of Qeynos and the Dead Hills.

#### **Argin-Hiz**

The fourth update to the expansion was **Argin-Hiz** (released Wednesday, July 16, 2014):

- 1 new zone (the second of two Tier 2 zones in COTF)
- 10 named mobs
- 6 quests
- 1 heroic adventure
- 3 missions (one of these missions isn't expected until July 23)
- 2 raids (one of these raids isn't expected until July 23)
- 4 collection sets
- Note: No new AAs or spells with Argin-Hiz

* * *

### **What's Going On**

#### **Initial Release**

The once peaceful plains of West Karana have been disrupted by a series of cataclysmic events, all leading up to the abrupt arrival of a powerful dragon from another thread, known as Lady Lendiniara. The force of her entry has torn an invisible seam that binds together countless realities, exposing what ancient texts refer to as Ethernere.

Without knowing how or why the dragon oracle arrived in their world, many Norrathians view Lady Lendiniara as a threat, while others interpret her arrival to mean the end of times is near. Factions begin to form, some prophesizing the demise of the world, and others uniting to confront the dragon invader.

Whether this winged harbinger is friend or foe, one thing is certain â€“ she did not travel through Ethernere alone. There is only one within this world who possesses the knowledge to send Lady Lendiniara back to her home, but can you really trust the ramblings of a madman?

In your quest to send Lady Lendiniara back to her world, you will travel to many previously undiscovered areas within Norrath, searching for artifacts of great power, while also encountering familiar faces many thought to be lost in time.

#### **Hate Rising**

With Lady Lendiniaraâ€™s presence threatening the existence of Norrath, an unlikely alliance has been forged with The Forsaken to send the dangerous dragon home. At Zebuxorukâ€™s request, the bravest will fight their way through recently discovered lands to recover magical artifacts that contain the power to return Lady Lendiniara to her world.

Adventurers will face adversaries at every turn, from those who refuse to release the magical artifacts, to others who are determined to see the dragon dead at all costs. Before all comes to a close, a familiar face will emerge with a new directive, leaving the fate of Norrath with one decision: Will you put your trust in the sly speech of a Teir'Dal or the endless ramblings of a madman?

#### **Tower of Rot**

The lead-up to the Tower of Rot's opening began with [Captain Russell Cooper](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=44047)'s heroic adventure chain.

After having [cleared a path](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=7274) to the [excavation site](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=7275) in the [Dead Hills](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/zone.html?zstrat=942), you got a [look](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/scenery2/deadhills-cooper3-tower.jpg) at the [Descending Tower](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=7276)...

In time, you would encounter a rather angry Undead Lord by the name of [Kyle Bayle](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=45701), the apparent long-dead leader of the original ill-fated Qeynos expedition into the Dead Hills many years prior. While you managed to defeat Kyle Bayle's Captain and Sergeants, the Undead Lord managed to [retreat back into his tower](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=7413).

Meanwhile, the guards of Qeynos have been trying to remedy the disaster site that is [West Karana](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/zones.html?zstrat=939). This situation has gone unresolved, however, and creatures of the Ethernere (having initially appeared due to the time ripples caused by [Lady Lendiniara](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=44340)'s appearance) have managed to breach the [Qeynos Hills](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/zone.html?zstrat=17) and the [city of Qeynos](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/zone.html?zstrat=4), itself.

[Captain Deidre Harath](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=46981) arrived to deal with the problem in Qeynos, and [Gahlith Wrannstad](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=46980) \- one of the Qeynos caster guildmasters - was tasked with finding a way to rid Qeynos of the Ethernere creatures for good. The citizens of Qeynos aren't the only ones having a problem with the Ethernere invasion, however. These creatures have also begun to invade the site of the Tower of Rot, itself, and are being fought off by the tower's undead forces ( [Erron Ailspoc](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=47029) was sent to recover some samples from these beings).

What links the Tower of Rot with those time ripples is not yet known for sure, but rumors suggest something inside the tower will lead to an answer.

#### **Argin-Hiz**

Warm winds begin to blow in Ethernere-Tainted West Karana and the South Desert of Ro. Winds like these must portend the arrival of something or someone. Stay alert for when the time comes!

A hot ash wind is blowing from the portals that have appeared in Ethernere-Tainted West Karana and South Ro. Creatures of ash have ventured out of the portals and are roaming menacingly throughout South Ro and North Ro. Guards from Freeport have come here to bring order to this region and are beseeching powerful adventurers to travel here to assist with quelling the blaze.

Lanys T\`vyl is waiting for you in the Ethernere-Tainted West Karana. She would like for you to further investigate this situation. The last artifact needed to send Lady Lendiniara back to her home may be located beyond the ash portals that have appeared in these areas, and the only way to know for sure is to gather other powerful allies to your side to investigate for yourself.

#### **Expansion Lore**

For more lore surrounding the Call of the Forsaken expansion, check out [this wiki article](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/wiki/eq:Call_of_the_Forsaken_Lore).

* * *

### **Getting There**

- How to get to "Call of the Forsaken" zones:

  - In the regular West Karana next to the Qeynos Hills zone line is a bonfire that, when clicked, zones you into the Ethernere.
  - Ethernere Tainted West Karana has portals to the other three Tier 1 zones.

    - In order to use these portals, you must attune yourself to them. See [**this quest entry**](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=7244) for details.

  - Ethernere Tainted West Karana has a portal The Void (H).

    - No attuning necessary to zone into The Void.

  - Druids and Wizards have port spells to the Ethernere Tainted West Karana.
  - The Guild Hall portal can be set to the Ethernere Tainted West Karana.

* * *

### **Progression**

- For a list of tasks and adventures in "Call of the Forsaken", see our main progression article: [Progression Through the Call of the Forsaken Expansion](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/wiki/eq:Progression_Through_Call_of_the_Forsaken)

- In the group game, there are no locked zones in this expansion. All zones are open.

- None of the kill tasks have any pre-requisites for requesting (although you'll need a Dark Elf illusion to request those in Neriak Fourth Gate).

- None of the heroic missions have any pre-requisites for requesting.

  - Note: The heroic missions, themselves, are progressive, meaning you have to do one heroic mission to request the next in its storyline. There are six such storylines as of the initial release (17 heroic missions in six progressive storylines).

    - "Hate Rising" added 15 new heroic adventures.
    - "Tower of Rot" added one new heroic adventure.
    - "Argin-Hiz" added one new heroic adventure.

- Each zone has a central story arc (a zone's partisan tasks and missions). Partisan tasks for a particular zone have to be completed in a certain order.

  - The partisan task arc in Ethernere Tainted West Karana can be started at any time.
  - The partisan tasks in the Dead Hills and Bixie Warfront both require you to have completed the partisan tasks in Ethernere Tainted West Karana.
  - The partisan tasks in Neriak Fourth Gate require you to have first completed all partisans in the other three zones.
  - The partisan tasks in Tower of Rot and Argin-Hiz don't have any pre-requisites.

* * *

### **Heroic Adventures**

Heroic adventures are customized shared tasks (1-6 players) that take place in instanced zones and scale in level, difficulty, and loot quality (based on the highest level player in the group) down to Level 85 (note: with the initial release, they scaled down only to Level 95, but a [Letter From the Producer](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/story.html?story=33574) in December 2013 indicated a change). They are designed for groups of 2-3 players and their mercenaries, and shouldn't take an average group any longer than 45-60 minutes to complete.

What do we mean by "customized"? Well, instead of a standard shared task with an unchanging set of steps to run through, heroic adventures have a randomized component to them whereby there are multiple options (or 'possible directives') for each step in the task. In other words, you can complete one of these heroic adventures, and then the next time you run through the same adventure, you could have a different set of task steps and encounter different NPCs.

Loot-dropping nameds (such as the Dreadmole of the Bixie Warfront or the Monstrous Minnow of the Dead Hills) can also appear in these adventures, and completion results in group currency that can be used to buy gear (both player and mercenary) and spells from vendors in the Ethernere.

It should be noted that there is plenty of content outside of heroic adventures as they are but one facet of Call of the Forsaken (there are also regular partisan and mercenary tasks, as well as regular group missions), and every zone has a static (non-instanced) version.

- A list of heroic adventures in "Call of the Forsaken" can be found in our main progression article: [Progression Through the Call of the Forsaken Expansion](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/wiki/eq:Progression_Through_Call_of_the_Forsaken)

### **Armor**

- Armor is produced in a similar manner as previous expansions, except there is no template to buy, and the adorning agent is ALL/ALL.

  - For more details, see our main armor article: [Call of the Forsaken Armor Guide](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/wiki/eq:Call_of_the_Forsaken_Armor_Guide)

- Various vendors (all found in [this camp](http://zam.zamimg.com/images/e/5/e566a8267a77acc35797cb718792e0be.jpg)) are located in the Ethernere Tainted West Karana, offering armor in exchange for Marks of Valor (the currency received as you complete heroic vendors):

  - Level 100: [Requisitioner Rhies](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=44033)
  - Level 95: [Merchant Sertan](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=45652)
  - Level 90: [Merchant Nyvan](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=45651)
  - Level 85: [Merchant Akele](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=45650)

* * *

### **Mercenary Enhancements**

- You can boost your mercenaries' strength and overall usefulness in the form of gear (four slots: primary, secondary, head, and chest) and passive AAs.

  - For a look at the mercenary AA window, [click here](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/images/cotf-merc-tab.jpg).

- Mercenaries will gain AA experience with you (you'll have to have them spawned in order for them to gain experience), and gear can be purchased from [Mercenary Morloro](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=44022) ( [**map**](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/spawns/ethernere-mercenary_morloro.jpg)) in the northwestern Qeynosian camp of the Ethernere Tainted West Karana. This gear is sold for Marks of Valor, which is rewarded from doing heroic adventures.

- Mercenary gear can also drop from named creatures.

* * *

### **AAs**

- There are no locked AAs. All AAs are immediately available.

* * *

### **Spells**

- Rank I Spells
- These can be purchased without any pre-requisites. They are found on the vendors in the northwestern part of Ethernere Tainted West Karana:

  - Bards: [Allan Tavies](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=44023) ( [**map**](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/spawns/ethernere-allan_tavies.jpg))
  - Clerics and Paladins: [Danelia Baker](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=44024) ( [**map**](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/spawns/ethernere-danelia_baker.jpg))
  - Druids and Rangers: [Roth Berydon](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=44025) ( [**map**](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/spawns/ethernere-roth_berydon.jpg))
  - Warriors and Monks: [Captain Jupitus](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=44026) ( [**map**](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/spawns/ethernere-captain_jupitus.jpg))
  - Casters: [Saera Millican](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=44027) ( [**map**](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/spawns/ethernere-saera_millican.jpg))

- Rogue, Shadow Knight, and Necromancer vendors are found in the far northeastern part of the zone, just south of the portal area:

  - Rogues and Shadow Knights: [Julaea Zimero](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=44028) ( [**map**](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/spawns/ethernere-julaea_zimero.jpg))
  - Necromancers: [Sevanta Frie](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=44029) ( [**map**](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/spawns/ethernere-sevanta_frie.jpg))

- Beastlord, Berserker, and Shaman vendors are found in the north-central part of the zone:

  - Beastlords: [Jarkan Arksan](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=44030) ( [**map**](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/spawns/ethernere-jarkan_arksan.jpg))
  - Berserkers: [Sund Toksig](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=44031) ( [**map**](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/spawns/ethernere-sund_toksig.jpg))
  - Shaman: [Spiritcaller Temvin](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=44032) ( [**map**](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/spawns/ethernere-spiritcaller_temvin.jpg))

- Rank II Spells

  - Some Rank II spells (tradeable) are found in Heroic adventures (in chests or as random drops).
  - All Rank II spells (no trade) can be purchased via group currency (Marks of Valor).

    - See [Requisitioner Rhies](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=44033) ( [**map**](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/spawns/ethernere-requisitioner_rhies.jpg)) in the northwestern vendor camp of Ethernere Tainted West Karana.

- Rank III Spells

  - These spells are obtained via [raid drops](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/item.html?item=113134), to be turned into [Requisitioner Sheon](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=44021) ( [**map**](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/spawns/ethernere-requisitioner_sheon.jpg)) in the northwestern vendor camp of Ethernere Tainted West Karana.
  - See [this writeup](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=7294) for more details.

- Portal Spells

  - Wizard portal spells are bought from [Jimmar Kadd](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=44034) ( [**map**](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/spawns/ethernere-jimmar_kadd.jpg)) in the east-central part of Ethernere Tainted West Karana.
  - Druid portal spells are bought from [Well Wailet](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=44035) ( [**map**](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/spawns/ethernere-well_wailet.jpg)) in the north-central part of Ethernere Tainted West Karana.

    - As part of the Call of the Forsaken expansion, Wizards and Druids can also purchase a Guidestone from this vendor (used to set a tertiary bind location).

## Overview

There are no locked zones in this expansion. All zones are open.

Progression in Tier 1 is fairly linear. You must complete zone partisan tasks in the order of Ethernere -- Bixie Warfront / Dead Hills -- Neriak Fourth Gate.

- Tier 1:

  - [Ethernere Tainted West Karana](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/zone.html?zstrat=939)
  - [Bixie Warfront](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/zone.html?zstrat=941)
  - [Dead Hills](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/zone.html?zstrat=942)
  - [Neriak Fourth Gate](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/zone.html?zstrat=943)

Tier 2 consists of two zones (released May 2014 and July 2014, respectively). There are no pre-requisites for starting partisan tasks in either Tier 2 zone.

- Tier 2:

  - [Tower of Rot](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/zone.html?zstrat=999)
  - [Argin-Hiz](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/zone.html?zstrat=1004)

For both tiers, mercenary tasks and heroic adventure task arcs have no zone pre-requisites (you can request these at any time).

* * *

## List of Tasks

Listed here are all tasks and group missions.

- **Bolded** tasks represent that zone's central task arc.

  - These tasks make up a linear series to be completed in the order they're listed below.

- Unbolded tasks are stand-alone, generally with no pre-requisites other than a minimum level of 85.

### Tier 1

- Portal Activation

  - **[Overcoming the Cataclysm](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=7249) \- solo task from [Guard Jacobson](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=44036) ( [map](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/spawns/ethernere-guard_jacobson.jpg); in the northwestern Qeynosian camp)**

#### Argin-Hiz

- Partisan Tasks

  - [A Dimming Light](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=7615) \- solo task from [a pained laborer](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=47271) ( [**map**](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/spawns/arginhiz-a_pained_laborer.jpg) \-\- outside the mines)

# Call of the Forsaken Portal Attuning

## Quest Text / Walkthrough

Modified: Tue Dec 5 05:21:04 2023 |
---

**Call of the Forsaken Info & Guides: Overview \| Progression & Task List \| Raiding \| Visible Armor**

---

_In the regular West Karana, next to the Qeynos Hills zone line, is a bonfire that, when clicked, zones you into the "Ethernere Tainted West Karana" (which is the 'hub zone' for the Call of the Forsaken expansion)._
_In far northeastern corner of the Ethernere Tainted West Karana are several portals that can be used to port to other Call of the Forsaken zones. In order to use some of these portals, you'll have to attune yourself to them._
_Note: There are no tasks involved with this. All you need to do is zone into the new zones from their older-world connections:_
Tier 1
Stone Hive to the Bixie Warfront
Dulak's Harbor to the Dead Hills
Neriak Third Gate to Neriak Fourth Gate
Tier 2
Dead Hills to the Tower of Rot
South Ro to Argin-Hiz
_(Details and maps below.)_

---

The Void (H)
_There is no attuning necessary to zone into The Void (H). Upon clicking the portal:_
You step into the portal and are instantly overcome with the sensation that you are falling hundreds of feet a second. Your vision blurs as space and time rush quickly around you. All in an instant you find yourself at the end of time, but something is different...
LOADING, PLEASE WAIT...
You have entered The Void.

---

Bixie Warfront
_When you click the Bixie Warfront portal, you see:_
Through the clouded portal you see projections of bixies marching in formation. Yet when you try and step through the portal nothing happens. You are not yet attuned to the Doomscale's gate to the Bixie Warfront.
_Head to the Stone Hive (in the Serpent's Spine) and access the Bixie Warfront through there, and then use the portal in the Bixie Warfront to zone into the Ethernere Tainted West Karana:_
You now feel attuned to the Doomscale gateway.
_You are now attuned to the Bixie Warfront._

---

Dead Hills
_When you click the Dead Hills portal, you see:_
Through the clouded portal you see projections of a bleak mountainside. Yet when you try and step through the portal nothing happens. You are not yet attuned to the Doomscale's gate to The Dead Hills.
_Head to Dulak's Harbor (connects through the Stonebrunt Mountains of Odus) and access the Dead Hills through there:_
You sneak below deck and wait for the Stormwave to set sail for the Dead Hills.
_Then use the portal in the Dead Hills to zone into the Ethernere Tainted West Karana:_
You now feel attuned to the Doomscale gateway.
_You are now attuned to the Dead Hills._

---

Neriak Fourth Gate
_When you click the Neriak portal, you see:_
Through the clouded portal you see projections of a dark cave. Yet when you try and step through the portal nothing happens. You are not yet attuned to the Doomscale's gate to Neriak - Fourth Gate.
_Head to the city of Neriak and into Neriak Third Gate, and access the Fourth Gate through there. Then use the portal in Neriak Fourth Gate ( **click for map image**) to zone into the Ethernere Tainted West Karana:_
You now feel attuned to the Doomscale gateway.
_You are now attuned to Neriak Fourth Gate._

---

Tower of Rot
_When you click the Tower of Rot portal, you see:_
Through the clouded portal you see the lonely flickering of torchlight in a darkened hall. Yet when you try and step through the portal nothing happens. You are not yet attuned to the Doomscale's gate to the Tower of Rot.
_Head to the newly opened area of the Dead Hills (the southwestern part of the zone) and click on the doorway into the Tower of Rot. Then head to its Ethernere portal ( **click for map image**):_
You now feel attuned to the Doomscale gateway.
_You are now attuned to the Tower of Rot._

---

Argin-Hiz
_When you click the Argin-Hiz portal, you see:_
Through the clouded portal you see projections of a great tower of flame. Yet when you try and step through the portal nothing happens. You are not yet attuned to the Doomscale's gate to Argin-Hiz.
_Head to the Southern Desert of Ro to the area just east of the Wizard spires, where you'll see a giant burning tree. Clicking on this will zone you into Argin-Hiz. Just east of the zone-in inside Argin-Hiz is the portal to the Ethernere Tainted West Karana._
You now feel attuned to the Doomscale gateway.
_You are now attuned to Argin-Hiz._

---

For "dulak.txt" map file:

For "stonehive.txt" map file:
P 393.1629, -594.5096, 37.8295, 0, 0, 0, 1, To\_the\_Bixie\_Warfront

**Call of the Forsaken Info & Guides: Overview \| Progression & Task List \| Raiding \| Visible Armor**

---

_This solo task is the first in a series of three. It begins with Guard Jacobson ( **click for map image**) in the Ethernere Tainted West Karana. He is located at the Qeynos Guard camp in the northwestern part of the zone._
_Pre-Requisites for Requesting:_
\- Level 95
\- No tasks
Guard Stewarn says 'Why are you bothering Jacobson? He has things to do!'
Guard Varsmith says 'Be quick with your business! Jacobson has important matters to attend to!'
You say, 'Hail, Guard Jacobson'
Guard Jacobson says '\_\_\_\_\_? I have not heard the name, but there are so many in these plains whose names I will never know and whose faces I will never see. Perhaps in time I will meet their bones and wonder what they looked like when they still walked in flesh. Even my own guards who patrolled this land lie buried beneath their towers that toppled when the [cataclysm] came. Too many souls have been lost in West Karana to even know or be forgotten.'
You say, 'What cataclysm?'
Guard Jacobson says 'We heard the rumblings that night from our garrison in Qeynos. A crash louder than any thunderclap tore the clouds from the sky. It was not long before the first survivors arrived, dirty and bleeding, carrying only their clothes and nightmares. Guard Ason led a tattered group of merchants, and the [tale] he told surpassed my fears.'
You say, 'What tale?'
Guard Jacobson says 'The hills glowed in the twilight, but were lit not by the sun. An inner light, a patch of green fire, silently swept across the grasses. Guard Ason climbed atop his tower and gazed upon the lake of light at the moment it [happened].'
You say, 'What happened?'
Guard Jacobson says 'Like a stone cast into the pool of stars, or a comet returning to the sky, the dragon breached the surface of fire. Aflame it arced and fell back to the plains. Ason dropped to his knees and covered his ears as the retort sounded across the land. The ruptured ground swelled and blistered, like skin scalded by forge steel. Lava veins spidered from the wound, weeping fiery blood. The plains creased and buckled. The tower lurched, and Ason [saw] the ground coming toward him.'
You say, 'Saw what?'
Guard Jacobson says 'Bleeding from the head and disoriented, Ason watched the destruction unfold with disbelief. Hills and plains rose up and crashed together like waves in a storm. Trees snapped and drowned in the rocky tumult. Houses and huts were crushed or burned in the onslaught of lava and the steaming waters of the Serpent River. Ason called out to Guard Justyn but heard no [reply].'
You say, 'No reply?'
Guard Jacobson says 'Ason found Justyn's helmet crushed beneath the fallen tower. He carried it with him, blood falling like tears from the bent metal. As he walked, he was joined by others who had survived. The remnants of West Karana arrived at our garrison, and I [knew] what we must do.'
You say, 'Knew what?'
Guard Jacobson says 'The garrison emptied and encamped here. We are the last hope of the living and dead. Who knows how many we have lost to the upheavals, the lava, or the creatures that have appeared? Investigator Sedgelock has pressed toward our forward camp, trying to administer aid and discover the nature of the disaster. If you have any sympathy for the people of West Karana, I would be in your debt for any [help] you can provide, as we are stretched thin.'
You say, 'I will help'
Guard Jacobson says 'Thank you, \_\_\_\_\_. Meet Investigator Sedgelock near the settlement due east from here.'
You have been assigned the task 'Overcoming the Cataclysm'.
In the wake of the cataclysm in the Ethernere Tainted West Karana, Guard Jacobson has tasked you with aiding the dispossessed villagers and discovering the nature of the catastrophe.

---

Speak with Investigator Sedgelock 0/1 (Ethernere Tainted West Karana)
_Task Window Says:_ Find and speak with Investigator Sedgelock, who has traveled east from the main guard camp and is attending to the burning settlement.
_Investigator Sedgelock ( **click for map image**) is found at location -1700, -1800, -215 (north-central area)._
You say, 'Hail, Investigator Sedgelock'
Investigator Sedgelock says 'Jacobson sent you? I wish I could say that your presence mattered.' Investigator Sedgelock points to the flaming houses. 'You're late. We're all too late. Lieutenant Rolth, valiant as he is, fights a losing battle. Strange creatures swarm beneath the ground and emerge to complete the destruction the dragon began. The villagers are dead or dying. What do you [hope] to accomplish?'
You say, 'I hope for a lot...'
Investigator Sedgelock says 'I only hope to learn what has happened and what might happen. Do you think Qeynos is safe? The Karanas are lost! Let them burn. We'll sift the ashes and mark them upon the Qeynos doorposts. Maybe then we'll be [safe].'
You say, 'Safe?'
Investigator Sedgelock says 'No, no, I am no fool. I know safety comes not from magic or superstition, or from sentiment for what is dead and gone. It comes from knowledge, and that is why I'm here. Aid Lieutenant Rolth if you must. Destroy those glowing dogs and their tamers, but tell me when you have finished fighting for a lost cause and are ready accept the futility of this land's salvation.'

---

Kill 8 skirth razers 0/8 (Ethernere Tainted West Karana)
Kill 3 gehein skirthtamers 0/3 (Ethernere Tainted West Karana)
_Task Window Says:_ If you want to help the relief effort, assist Lieutenant Rolth in destroying the razers and skirth tamers that are attacking the settlement.
_Skirth razers and tamers are found in and around the burning houses in the north-central part of the zone (near the Investigator NPC). The razers have relatively small amounts of hitpoints and hit for a max ~2,500. The tamers are regular mobs (hit for a max ~12,750 with a regular amount of hitpoints)._

---

Speak with Investigator Sedgelock 0/1 (Ethernere Tainted West Karana)
_Task Window Says:_ Tell Investigator Sedgelock you have dealt with the invaders.
You say, 'Hail, Investigator Sedgelock'
Investigator Sedgelock says 'Still fighting for the cause of hope, \_\_\_\_\_? You almost make this old man wish he were young enough to believe in the impossible and hardy enough to strive for it. But now you must wake from your dream to the nightmare of the real. Did you see the dust scintillating from the bodies of the tamers? Surely there must be more waiting in the tunnels below. Descend into the depths and bring me back any clumps of dust you can find. Perhaps it will tell me more about the evil that assails us. And if you find any other evidence of how these creatures have so swiftly arrived in the Karanas, don't hesitate to tell me!'

---

Collect samples of Ether Ash 0/5 (Ethernere Tainted West Karana)
_Task Window Says:_ Find some ash, left by the strange invaders, lying around in the tunnels beneath the settlement.
Search the tunnels below Investigator Sedgelock to find out how creatures are arriving in West Karana 0/1 (Ethernere Tainted West Karana)
_Task Window Says:_ You should search the tunnels for any portals the invaders might have used to enter Norrath.
_Enter the tunnel area and find ash ground spawns ( **click for map image**). Known in-game /locations (note there are others not listed here):_
-1450, -2160, -292 _(ash)_
-1560, -2330, -300 _(ash)_
-1675, -2300, -296 _(ash)_
-1320, -1945, -263 _(ash)_
-1445, -2310, -282 _(ash)_
-1280, -2215, -280 _(portal update)_
Investigate the portal you found in the tunnels 0/1 (Ethernere Tainted West Karana)
_Task Window Says:_ Touch the portal and discover its nature.
_Click on the portal to spawn the Spirit of Ollysa Bladefinder, and then hail it._
Speak with the Spirit of Ollysa Bladefinder 0/1 (Ethernere Tainted West Karana)
_Task Window Says:_ Hail the Spirit of Ollysa Bladefinder.
You say, 'Hail, Spirit of Ollysa Bladefinder'
Spirit of Ollysa Bladefinder looks around and blinks, not recognizing the faces before her. 'Who...are you? What is this place? Are you looking for...a short sword? My shop is all a mess...the wall caved in...it's hard to breathe, isn't it?' The Spirit of Ollysa Bladefinder begins to fade and disappear back through the portal.

---

Give 5 Ether Ash to Investigator Sedgelock 0/1 (Ethernere Tainted West Karana)
_Task Window Says:_ When you have collected enough ash from the tunnels, deliver it to Investigator Sedgelock.
Speak with Investigator Sedgelock 0/1 (Ethernere Tainted West Karana)
_Task Window Says:_ Speak to Investigator Sedgelock about all you have found.
You say, 'Hail, Investigator Sedgelock'
Investigator Sedgelock weighs the ash you hand to him. He dumps it into a flask and pours a blue liquid over it. The mixture pops and fizzles. 'These creatures come from nowhere on Norrath known to me. Your description of the portal you found only confirms this for me. Hurry east to our forward camp and tell them what I have found. Guard Jalson will direct you.'

---

Speak with Guard Jalson 0/1 (Ethernere Tainted West Karana)
_Task Window Says:_ You must carry word about your discoveries to the forward guard camp. Guard Jalson will point the way forward.
_Guard Jalson ( **click for map image**) is found at -1900, -3550, -453._
You say, 'Hail, Guard Jalson'
Guard Jalson says 'The forward camp? Good luck making it through these lava fields. Soon after we arrived, the ground opened up and began spewing chunks of lava into the air. Several of our tents and men were swallowed in the fire. We were in the middle of escorting survivors to base camp, but now they're hunkered down, caught between a fiery end and that blasted cult of dragon worshippers! If you could destroy the lava rents and some of the infernal creatures that tend to them, perhaps no more lives will be lost today! Tell those cowering refugees when it's safe to move!'

---

Kill 6 lava protectors 0/6 (Ethernere Tainted West Karana)
Destroy some lava rents so that refugees can run to safety 0/3 (Ethernere Tainted West Karana)
_Task Window Says:_ The path to the guard camp is beset by rents of lava, which is also preventing refugees from fleeing east. Destroy the lava rents and their protectors in order to make the path safe.
_Kill the mephits and large mounds of lava nearby. Note that the lava rents spawn "lava rocks" around them that have a damage aura (~7,000 dmg)._
Lava rocks seem to be a random spawn. When you attack them, the protectors attack you -- they also aggro.
The rocks die fast so if you can survive the protectors, burn down the rocks first.

---

Speak with a cowering refugee 0/1 (Ethernere Tainted West Karana)
_Task Window Says:_ Now that the path is safe, tell the cowering refugees that they can return to safety.
_"a cowering refugee" ( **click for map image**) is in a small structure nearby, at about -1340, -3780, -451 by the rock bridge over the lava river._
You say, 'Hail, a cowering refugee'
a cowering refugee shudders, 'Is it safe? Really? Do I just run for it?' The cowering refugee bolts out of the tent.
It runs off but it respawns in a few minutes. This is a group update.

---

Hail Guard Jalson 0/1 (Ethernere Tainted West Karana)
_Task Window Says:_ Tell Guard Jalson that you have made the way safe.
You say, 'Hail, Guard Jalson'
Guard Jalson says 'Thanks, \_\_\_\_\_. You've done the people of West Karana a great service! I know Guard Mathys, commander of the forward camp, will want to hear what you have to say.'

---

Speak with Guard Mathys 0/1 (Ethernere Tainted West Karana)
_Task Window Says:_ Enter the forward guard camp and tell Guard Mathys what has transpired.
_Hail Guard Mathys ( **click for map image**) at about -1490, -4200, -471 on the same side of the lava river as the cowering refugee - a little ways back surrounded by other guards._
You say, 'Hail, Guard Mathys'
Guard Mathys says 'The lava flows, but a cold wind blows from the maw that gapes above us, waiting to swallow Norrath. What you have told me must be true. You are brave or foolhardy to come here. Though I value your efforts, we shall see how valuable you'll be when facing down a dragon!'
It appears the mysterious invaders that are besieging West Karana have come from a place outside of Norrath, a place inhabited by the spirits of the dead. You told this to Guard Mathys, but he seems more concerned about the dragon that has appeared.
_Rewards:_
300 platinum
Experience

**Call of the Forsaken Info & Guides: Overview \| Progression & Task List \| Raiding \| Visible Armor**

---

_This task is the second in a series of three. It begins with Commander Galenth ( **click for map image**) in the Ethernere Tainted West Karana. He is located in the northeastern part of the zone._
_Pre-Requisites for Requesting:_
\- Level 95
\- Completed "Overcoming the Cataclysm"
You say, 'Hail, Commander Galenth'
Commander Galenth says 'Are you a slayer of dragons? Don't lie to me! Your fancy dress tells the truth better than your mouth! We have problems here, problems bigger than burnt homes and stranded settlers: problems with bigger teeth. No, I heard of your work, how you coddle the civs. You'd be more fit as a [worshiper] of dragons than a slayer of them!'
You say, 'A worshiper?'
Commander Galenth says 'You disagree? I mark you unfairly? Twenty years in the guard, and you learn how to judge a man. You learn that most are weak, that they'll sell themselves to anything that gives them a warm bed, warm soup, and a mind free of worries and thoughts. You learn that fancy folks in fancy dress are [followers], not fighters!'
You say, 'Followers?'
Commander Galenth says 'And that's why I'll use you. You're gonna follow me, and you're gonna convince those cultists you follow the dragon. You can follow [instructions], too, can't you?'
You say, 'Instructions?'
Commander Galenth says 'Your dress isn't fancy enough, and those dragon lovers aren't gonna embrace you and seat you at the right claw of their scaly god the first time you bend a knee! Thievery is in order. Either you [help] and show you're worth something, or I'll find another silk-pantalooned dandy to do the deed!'
You say, 'I suppose I'll help...'
Commander Galenth says 'Good. You're gonna steal one of their fancy cloaks from their supply crates or barrels, and then you'll listen in on the resurrectionist ceremonies. I need to know what these dragon lovers are planning!'
You have been assigned the task 'Not of This World'.
Commander Galenth has asked you to learn more about the Doomscale cultists and their connection to the dragon.

---

Find a ritualist robe in one of the cultists' barrels or crates 0/1 (Ethernere Tainted West Karana)
_Task Window Says:_ You need to find a robe to disguise yourself as a cultist. You can probably find one in barrels or crates owned by the cultists. But be careful they aren't around to see you stealing their goods!
_Loot a "Cloak of the Doomscale Ritualist" from one of the barrels or crates (does not always drop) in the northeastern part of the zone ( **click for map image** \-\- just south of the portal area and Lady Lendiniara). Note that the Doomscale NPCs in the area may attack you when you do this._

---

Get close to the resurrectionists and listen to them while wearing the robe you stole 0/1 (Ethernere Tainted West Karana)
_Task Window Says:_ Wearing the robe, get near the resurrectionists and listen to their chants.
_This update is around /location -1115, -4150, -471. Stand in the middle of the resurrectionist circle and click the robe for the update._
Wearing the cultist robe, you pass as one of them and hear them chanting, 'Powers beyond Norrath, shrouded creatures beyond the veil of death, hear us! Release unto us the souls of the once-living. Return to this world the ones who wait in the gray that they might guide us into realms beyond, realms revealed by the Dragon of Doom!' You see the souls of dead appearing and disappearing from the vents connected to otherworldly places.
an unstable spirit appears out of the mist.
an unstable spirit decoheres and returns to the mist.
an unstable spirit dissipates.

---

Speak with Commander Galenth 0/1 (Ethernere Tainted West Karana)
_Task Window Says:_ Tell Commander Galenth what you have discovered.
You say, 'Hail, Commander Galenth'
Commander Galenth says 'My fancy chum, back once more! What a fine follower you make! A place beyond the grave? So the dragon comes from a land of the dead...monstrous! The sooner we strike, the sooner we save these lands! But the troops aren't going anywhere until we secure more knowledge about the dragon and its lovers. Sorkins went southwest to the farms to follow the cult, but he hasn't reported back. Find that dog and make sure he hasn't deserted, and collect what knowledge you can!'

---

Speak with Guard Sorkins 0/1 (Ethernere Tainted West Karana)
_Task Window Says:_ The cultists also have plans for the southern regions of West Karana. Find Guard Sorkins and get his report on what's happening.
_Guard Sorkins and Henina Miller are found in the far south-central boundary of the zone ( **click for map image**)._
You say, 'Hail, Guard Sorkins'
Guard Sorkins says 'I know, I know: Galenth waits on my report! I followed a group of cultists and overheard them talking about sacrificing souls in order to gain more power in the other world. But I can't just leave now! Henina will tear my arm out of the socket! Apparently her boys have disappeared, and one was taken by the group of cultists. See if you can calm her down!'

---

Speak with Henina Miller 0/1 (Ethernere Tainted West Karana)
_Task Window Says:_ Cultists have kidnapped Henina's son. Ask her what happened.
You say, 'Hail, Henina Miller'
Henina Miller says 'Thank goodness you're here! This Sorkins fellow is worthless! I've told him a dozen times to go get [Furball] back, but he just makes excuses about needing to brief his commander! He's nothing better than a coward! I told him about my plan to use the scarecrows to make a bomb, but not even that would give him courage!'
You say, 'Furball?'
Henina Miller says 'Yes, my son! Well, he's my adopted son, but I love him just as much as Cleet Jr. or Tiny! A group of cloaked villains passed through the flames and grabbed Furball while we were searching for some of our lost belongings. If only the rest of the [family] were here, we'd get the job done ourselves!'
You say, 'Where is your family?'
Henina Miller says 'Cleet and Tiny set off to Qeynos to get help a few days ago, and I haven't seen them since. I hope they're okay! Cleet Jr. and Furball stayed here to protect the farm, but Cleet Jr. just couldn't sit still. He's wandered off to find the find the Qeynos guard and join them. That boy just thinks about fighting all day and never listens! A fat lot of good the Qeynos guard has been doing anyway! We really need your help, \_\_\_\_\_! If you can bring me some pumpkin pieces from those flaming jacks, I know a little secret [recipe] that will make this easier.'
You say, 'What about that recipe?'
Henina Miller says 'Well, I did say it's a secret, but I know that if you bake some pieces of flaming wisp lightstones into a pie, those cultists will be in for a treat! Go to it, please! Bring me those pumpkin pieces, and save Furball!'

---

Collect Flaming Pumpkin Pieces from the flaming jacks 0/7 (Ethernere Tainted West Karana)
_Task Window Says:_ Henina Miller wants you to rescue her son, Furball, and she has a plan. She has asked you to collect Flaming Pumpkin Pieces from the flaming jacks so that she can bake them into an explosive pie.
_In the south-central part of the zone, find and kill scarecrow mobs until you've looted seven items. These are average mobs, hitting for a max ~13,000 (mezzable; stunnable)._
Deliver 7 Flaming Pumpkin Piece to Henina Miller 0/7 (Ethernere Tainted West Karana)
_Task Window Says:_ Give the Flaming Pumpkin Pieces to Henina Miller so she can make the pie.
Henina Miller says 'That's everything I need! You're so kind and helpful! You know, it almost feels like I've met you before, but my memory isn't as good as it used to be. Anyway, I cooked this up nice and fresh in those coals over there. It'll give those cultists a real kick!'
_You receive a "Jack Bomb"._

---

Throw the Jack Bomb at Ritualist Danin and his followers 0/1 (Ethernere Tainted West Karana)
_Task Window Says:_ Find Furball Miller and throw the Jack Bomb on Ritualist Danin and his followers.
_Get close to Ritualist Danin ( **click for map image**), target him, right-click the Jack Bomb, then left-click anywhere within the Ritualist's general area:_
a Doomscale ritualist outstretches his hand towards the Dragon.
Ritualist Danin falls to the ground, scorched by the exploding pumpkin. As his life fades, his voice rasps, 'Now my brethren and I shall be the sacrifice! We travel to the gray, the space unseen by gods and mortals. We make way for all who follow, for all of Norrath that shall be swallowed! The dragon will claim her brood and secure them in the gray for all eternity.'

---

Speak with Furball Miller 0/1 (Ethernere Tainted West Karana)
_Task Window Says:_ Tell Furball Miller that he is safe.
_Furball Miller ( **click for map image**) is found at the bottom of the lava fall in the central part of the zone._
You say, 'Hail, Furball Miller'
Furball Miller says 'BARK! Thank you, \_\_\_\_\_! I thought I was going to be skinned, cooked in lava, and eaten! They kept talking about bleeding this world and offering its life to the dragon. Our blood would secure their place in the world between worlds. It's crazy, bark! Is mother alright? She must be worried! I can get back from here, \_\_\_\_\_. I'm worried about Cleet Jr., though! He went southwest, chasing after cultists and following some guard name Wagsley, trying to find adventure. He's not cut out for that, bark! Go find him!'

---

Speak with Guard Wagsley 0/1 (Ethernere Tainted West Karana)
_Task Window Says:_ Cleet Miller, Jr. wants to join the Qeynos Guard and has run after a guard named Wagsley. Find Wagsley and discover where Cleet, Jr. has gone.
_Guard Wagsley ( **click for map image**) is found in the southwestern part of the zone._
You say, 'Hail, Guard Wagsley'
Guard Wagsley says 'Cleet Miller, Jr.? Yeah, I've heard of him. He's that stupid boy right over there! Got himself run up the tree by those glowing ghastlies! I would just hoof it back to camp, but I kind of feel bad leaving a kid behind who's been crying for his mommy for over an hour. But look at the size of those creatures! I'm not going anywhere near 'em! What, are you going to do something?'
You have discovered that the Doomscale cultists are trying to lower the barrier between the world of Norrath and the threshold of the afterlife. In the process, you saved Furball Miller from being sacrificed. However, Cleet Miller, Jr. is still in quite a predicament.
_Rewards:_
225 platinum
Experience

_This group mission begins with Commander Galenth ( **click for map image**) in the Ethernere Tainted West Karana._
_Pre-Requisites for Requesting:_
\- Level 95
\- Completed "Overcoming the Cataclysm"
\- Completed "Not of this World"
\- Completed "Answering the Call"
You say, 'Hail, Commander Galenth'
_Without pre-requisites, you see:_
Commander Galenth says 'What?! I'm busy right now! Talk to Guard Jacobson if you're so eager to help!'
_With pre-requisites, you see:_
Commander Galenth says 'So, have you figured out what you are yet, \_\_\_\_\_? Are you a [worshiper] of the dragon, or are you a [killer] of the dragon lovers?'
You say, 'I'm a killer... Yes...'
Commander Galenth says 'That's what I need, \_\_\_\_\_: the ones who worship battle! Since we're in a pinch, though, you'll do. We don't have much time. Can you muster a [small] force or a [large] one?'
You say, 'I have a small force'
Commander Galenth says 'Making yourself useful...good! [Ready] your force!'
You have been assigned the task 'The Doomscale Horde'.

---

**Summary**
\- Say "help" to start the event.
\- Kill off five timed waves of adds while keeping Commander Galenth alive (he can be healed).
\- Kill Deathmaster Sarenth.

---

Speak to Commander Galenth 0/1 (Ethernere Tainted West Karana)
You say, 'Hail, Commander Galenth'
Commander Galenth says '\_\_\_\_\_! It's about time you got here! You're slower than Wagsley, and that's damn slow! Jacobson's still fooling around, trying to call reinforcements from Qeynos. We're on our own now! Are you prepared for death? You should be; it's been a slaughterhouse here. The damn cultists have been [attacking]!
You say, 'Attacking?'
Commander Galenth says 'Don't stand there thinking about the lovers you'll never have, or the mountains you'll never climb, or the ale you'll never drink! Grab your weapons and [help]! With my strength, my brains, and your shields of meat, we just might pull through this!'
You say, 'We'll help!'
Commander Galenth shouts 'Stand fast! Here they come again!'
_This begins the event._
_NOTE: At no time should you get near the sentinels on the opposite side of the event area. If you do so, you get death touched._

---

Survive the attacking waves of the Doomscale Horde 0/1 (Ethernere Tainted West Karana)
_Here, you will have five waves of mobs to contend with. You can't let the Commander die, or the event resets to the beginning._
_This emote indicates another wave has just spawned and is on its way in:_
Commander Galenth says 'Fight for your homes! Fight for Qeynos! Fight for whatever the hell will make you tear the cultists apart! None shall live!'
_This is the emote you see if you kill a wave before the next one spawns:_
Commander Galenth says 'You almost make me proud! You fight with intensity I've not seen since...well...when I used to spar as a new recruit! Stay alert! We're not out of this yet!'
_These two emotes appear to be random:_
Commander Galenth shouts 'Impressive! You actually managed to kill a few of them! But don't get full of yourself. Those damn cultists won't give up so easily!'
Commander Galenth flings the blood off his blade. 'The madness never ends! Here they come again!'
_After the fifth wave, you get a named "Deathmaster Sarenth"._

---

Kill Deathmaster Sarenth 0/1 (Ethernere Tainted West Karana)
_Deathmaster Sarenth casts "Massive Strike" and "Blazing Rain", and buffs itself with "Reflective Skin"._
Massive Strike: Directional 35', (0)
1: Knockback (12) and Toss Up (8)
2: Decrease Hitpoints by 37590
Blazing Rain: AE 50', Fire (-940)
1: Decrease Hitpoints by 31200 per tick _(4 ticks)_
Reflective Skin: Self 0', Unresistable (0)
1: Increase Chance to Reflect Spell by 50%

---

Open the chest of the Doomscale Horde 0/1 (Ethernere Tainted West Karana)
The chest is called "Doomscale Cult Coffers."

Experience

_This is a solo task that begins with Commander Zavrus ( **map image**). She is a drakkin found in the Queen's Wet River bixie camp (from the Ethernere portal stone, head east up the ramp and just follow the ramp until you come to the queen's camp)._
_Pre-Requisites for requesting:_
\- Level 85
\- Completed Ethernere Tainted West Karana tasks:
\-\-\- "Overcoming the Cataclysm"
\-\-\- "Not of This World"
\-\-\- "Answering the Call"
You say, 'Hail, Commander Zavrus'
Commander Zavrus says 'Greetings. I don't suppose you are for hire, are you? I'm conducting an [investigation] of the recent militarization of the Stone Hive bixies. The Wet River bixies are trying to find the source of the music and sounds causing it, and I have a few people already helping, but it is not enough. Frislo here has done what he can, but while I am sympathetic with his plight, there are more lives at stake than just his brother Jacyll's.'
You say, 'I'll help with this investigation'
_Without pre-requisites, you see:_
Commander Zavrus says 'I don't think you can help me at this time.'
_With pre-requisites, you see:_
Commander Zavrus says 'I'm grateful for the help. With everyone else preparing for war, I've been low on resources to do anything for actually preventing war. I've been working with the Wet River bixies to learn more about this sound that drives the Stone Hive. It is likely some sort of magical source, but beyond that I don't know anything.'
You have been assigned the task 'The Bixies of War'.
A strange sound is echoing through the caverns, and only the Wet River bixies' wax prevents others from succumbing to the strange effects. The Stone Hive is now on the move.
Assist Commander Zavrus from Crescent Reach with the investigation into militarization of the Stone Hive bixies.

---

_Map points for "bixiewarfront.txt" map files:_
P 440.3686, 43.4288, -80.3821, 240, 0, 0, 2, Stone\_Hive\_Order
P 434.5842, 300.0411, -112.2936, 240, 0, 0, 2, Stone\_Hive\_Order
P 1187.6378, 222.1013, -174.6282, 240, 0, 0, 2, Stone\_Hive\_Order
P 817.3863, 222.4731, -183.3348, 240, 0, 0, 2, Stone\_Hive\_Order

---

Speak to Scout Reneza 0/1 (Bixie Warfront)
_Task Window Says:_ Find Scout Reneza of the Wet River bixies. Zavrus believes that she may be able to help.
_Scout Reneza ( **map image**) is found in the same camp._
You say, 'Hail, Scout Reneza'
Scout Reneza says 'Commander Zavrus has asked you to look into this? Very well. I will assist you, only because our interests coincide. The Stone Hive is no longer our ally. This music of war, rage, and fury does not affect us since we have protected ourselves with the [wax]. The wax will keep you sane as well. We'll need to find information from the Stone Hive members themselves to get any further in finding the source, however.'
You say, 'Wax?'
Scout Reneza says 'That vile sound. If we do not stop it soon, who knows what might happen next? If you'd like to help, contact the drakkin called Zavrus. She is a member of the Crescent Reach military.'

---

Return to Commander Zavrus 0/1 (Bixie Warfront)
_Task Window Says:_ Inform Commander Zavrus of what you've learned.
You say, 'Hail, Commander Zavrus'
Commander Zavrus says 'Reneza was helpful, I see. She did seem the reasonable sort. I think we should start by taking out some Stone Hive bixies and seeing if we can find any useful leads. I'll see what I can find out on my end.'

---

Locate clues by taking out Stone Hive bixies 0/1 (Bixie Warfront)
_Task Window Says:_ Find clues from Stone Hive bixies. Troopers, diggers, lancers, and soldiers may have information.
_Kill bixies in the static (non-instanced) Bixie Warfront until you get an update. These include:_
a bustling digger
a frantic harvester
a resolute trooper
a vigilant soldier
an edgy lancer
_These spawn in the large clearing in the tunnel behind Naturalist Yrelia ( **map image** of Yrelia)._
a resolute trooper has been slain by \_\_\_\_\_!
a resolute trooper had some interesting information that completes part of the puzzle. Your victim has left behind a few clues: you found information that Warden Lorenz was the person who handled the prisoner Jacyll and some near-mad scribblings about a war march. It looks like you'll need to do more investigating, but first, you should let Zavrus know what you found out.

---

Discuss the findings with Commander Zavrus 0/1 (Bixie Warfront)
_Task Window Says:_ Report your findings to Commander Zavrus.
You say, 'Hail, Commander Zavrus'
Commander Zavrus says 'This is a good start. I haven't been able to get much done here, but I'll keep looking. In the meantime, I'll need you to follow up on this.'

---

Interrogate bixies 0/1 (Bixie Warfront)
This is reported to update from "a spider hunter" that was standing next to Charlbroiled Chanon.
Find information by killing bixies 0/1 (Bixie Warfront)
Collect Stone Hive Orders and give them to Commander Zavrus 0/5 (Bixie Warfront)
Defeat Warden Lorenz to learn about Jacyll's fate 0/1 (Bixie Warfront)
_Task Window Says:_ Interrogate non-hostile bixies, defeat hostile bixies, collect Stone Hive orders, and find Warden Lorenz. Hostile bixies that may have information could include guards, protectors, investigators, or honey benders. Non-hostile bixies that may be willing to talk could include vendors, tenants, friendly hunters, or frightened bixies.
_The above four steps can only be completed inside instances involving heroic adventures. Speak with Naturalist Yrelia, Snazlieu the Shadow, or Ambassador Crizlyna to initiate these tasks (they are shared tasks for 1-6 players -- a list of them can be found in **this progression writeup**). Note: All four steps are known to be updateable inside the "Espionage Begins At Home" instance._
_For the "interrogate" step, just hail non-KOS bixies until you get an update:_
You say, 'Hail, a quiet tenant'
a quiet tenant says 'The song fills us all with strength and zeal. The warriors in particular have found great energy. There is a non-bixie playing it. Looks like one of the people of Crescent Reach.'
_For the "find information" step, kill KOS guards, protectors, investigators, and honey benders until you get an update (these mob types are most common in heroic adventures involving the residences - see Snazlieu the Sneak to trigger these tasks):_
a supply guardian has been slain by \_\_\_\_\_!
a supply guardian had notes about the "power of the drum" on one of the corpses. There are references to the "power it brings."
_For the "collect" step, "Stone Hive Orders" are found as ground spawns inside bixie residences (trigger heroic adventures beginning with Snazlieu the Shadow to access their houses; this may also be possible in Ambassador Crizlyna's tasks, but this is not confirmed)._
_Warden Lorenz can only be reached inside Heroic Adventures involving the residential district (those triggered by speaking with Snazlieu the Shadow). It's possible to invis to his location, kill him, and leave the zone without doing anything else. Note that he casts a 50' range AE for 40,000 damage (average amount of hitpoints, average DPS)._
_For a map to the Warden's location, **click here** (he's west of the residences)._
Warden Lorenz has been slain by \_\_\_\_\_!
Warden Lorenz's corpse buzzes pitifully and hits the ground.
Warden Lorenz had a list of prisoners in a small journal. It appears Jacyll is a prisoner of some importance, but the exact reasons are not revealed. He is the Queen Bixie's personal prisoner.
_Upon handing in the Stone Hive Orders, you see:_
Commander Zavrus says 'Now these are fascinating. Thank you.'

---

Discuss the revelations with Scout Reneza 0/1 (Bixie Warfront)
_Task Window Says:_ Report the results of your foray to Scout Reneza.
You say, 'Hail, Scout Reneza'
Scout Reneza says 'So the sound of the drum, that horrible music. That which we are protecting against, is made from a Stone Hive prisoner named Jacyll? I appreciate you sharing this information with us. You should tell the soldier from Crescent Reach your findings.'

---

Give your final report to Commander Zavrus 0/1 (Bixie Warfront)
_Task Window Says:_ Give your final report to Commander Zavrus.
You say, 'Hail, Commander Zavrus'
Commander Zavrus nods. 'It looks like Jacyll and this drum might be what's causing the bixie's behavior. This is incredibly serious. However, if we can stop him from playing this artifact and rescue him, we might be able to avoid a war. You've been an incredible help. Thank you.'
You've learned much. The Stone Hive bixies are being controlled by a magical drum played by Jacyll, a prisoner of the Stone Hive bixies. It will be necessary to free him to recover the drum and halt the march of war.
_Rewards:_
Experience (3-4 AAs at Level 100)
262 platinum, 5 gold
_(This task is repeatable with a 6-hour lockout - lockout from the time the task was requested.)_

---

**Precursor & Lore**
_While not a part of this task or any progression, the Drakkin named Jeslellathina of Crescent Reach (found beneath the ladder that leads up to the new zone) has a few things to say about the Bixie Warfront:_
You say, 'Hail, Jeslellathina'
[Jeslellathina] plays with a bit of flame wrapping around her knuckles as she says, 'Have you ever seen a [building burn], \_\_\_\_\_?'
You say, 'Jeslellathina...'
Jeslellathina laughs, holds her flaming hand up to her mouth and blows a kiss of flame at you. She says playfully, 'Don't mock the name, honey. It's specifically chosen for its purpose, just like you've been.'
You say, 'A building burn?'
Jeslellathina looks up at you, confused by your answer. She asks again, 'Well, have you?'
You say, 'No...'
Jeslellathina snaps her hand shut with surprising force and snuffs the flame out. 'What a pity. You're really missing out. [Honeycomb] burns quite well, too, though.'
You say, 'Honeycomb?'
Jeslellathina says 'Oh, have you not heard already? I'd assumed everyone in Crescent Reach had by now; the gossip here is quite out of control. Well, we're here on a [rescue] mission.'
You say, 'Rescue?'
Jeslellathina says 'Jacyll, one of the finest bards I've ever heard, and a personal friend of mine, was kidnapped by the Stone Hive bixies. Thus, the [ladder]!'
You say, 'What's this ladder?'
Jeslellathina looks a little irritated that you require more explanation. She begins flicking flames off of her fingers as she recites, 'Attention, all brave adventures that set foot inside Crescent Reach's borders! The esteemed and well-respected bard, Jacyll, has been kidnapped by the Stone Hive Bixies and taken to their [underground camp].'
You say, 'What underground camp?'
Jeslellathina says 'Yes, yes. I was getting to that.' Jeslellathina takes a deep breath and continues drearily, 'Their newly excavated underground tunnel network can be accessed via a subterranean pathway and one super long ladder. The authorities of Crescent Reach can guarantee safe passage into the underground fortress, but assumes no [liability] for...'
You say, 'Liability?'
Jeslellathina's head snaps up like she fell asleep, smiles, and says, 'Oh yes, it'd be a shame to miss that part. As I was saying, Crescent Reach assumes no liability for any serious injuries, BEST FRIENDS, decapitations, life-threatening predicaments, BEAUTIFUL FLOWERS, diseases, bug bites, or HEAPING PILES OF TREASURE that may be acquired within [the cavern].'
You say, 'The cavern?'
Jeslellathina shoots spark into the sky and says, 'We've taken to calling it the Bixie Warfront, but there's really no official term for the place. It's just an extension of the existing Stone Hive the bixies constructed long ago on the eastern edge of Blightfire Moors. This one's much [more dangerous], though.'
You say, 'More dangerous?'
Jeslellathina holds a finger up to her lips and puts her ear to the ground, motioning for you to do the same. She asks, 'Do you hear that subtle thumping? It's been going non-stop ever since we discovered the cavern. The entire complex is a massive military wonderland: factories, battlefields, fortresses, training camps--they've got [everything] down there.'
You say, 'Everything?'
Jeslellathina says 'Everything. Including our friend, Jacyll. If you think you're up for the challenge, head down the ladder and speak to his brother, Frislo. He'll make sure you're [put to work] finding the stolen drakkin.'
You say, 'Put to work?'
Jeslellathina says 'Oh, yes. It's definitely work. Oh, wait. Are you accepting?' Jeslellathina doesn't wait for your response, immediately reciting, 'The people of Crescent Reach, and especially the family and friends of Jacyll thank you for your contribution. You may return to Crescent Reach for refreshments and possible dancing after Jacyll is rescued.'

This group mission begins with Frislo [npc=44041] ( **click for map image**) in the Bixie Warfront. He is a drakkin found in the Queen's Wet River bixie camp (from the Ethernere portal stone, head east up the ramp and just follow the ramp until you come to the queen's camp).
Pre-Requisites for Requesting:
\- Level 95
\- Completed The Bixies of War [quest=7260] (Bixie Warfront partisan task)
You say, 'Hail, Frislo'
Frislo glares at you, 'So you're the one who failed to rescue my brother? The one who thought it would be a good idea to use explosives at the center of a hive full of crazed bixies?' The exasperated drakkin rolls his eyes, 'Do you have any idea what you've done? The hive is now swarming with more bixies than before! You need to [help] undo what you've done and free my brother from captivity.'
You say, 'How can I help?'
Frislo takes a deep breath in an attempt to calm down, 'I suppose I can't completely discount your efforts, since at the very least, you were able to locate where Jacyll's being held. It'll take a lot more force to break him free, though. Whatever material the bixie's are using to imprison him won't shatter under your first alone. I hope you brought a solid [group] of allies, or perhaps a [raid]?'
You say, 'I have a group'
Frislo says 'Let me know when your group is ready and we can [begin] this rescue.'
You have been assigned the task 'Saving Jacyll'.
Frislo needs your help rescuing his brother from the center of the bixie hive.
You say, 'Let's **begin**'
Frislo nods. 'I'll run ahead and make sure the path is clear. Meet me at the center of the hive where Jacyll is being kept.'
LOADING, PLEASE WAIT...
You have entered Bixie Warfront.

---

**Task Steps**
Meet Frislo at the center of the hive where Jacyll is being held 0/1 (Bixie Warfront)

Speak with Frislo 0/1 (Bixie Warfront)
_Task Window Says:_ Frislo appears anxious to speak with you.
Destroy the translucent wall surrounding Jacyll 0/1 (Bixie Warfront)
_Task Window Says:_ A thick wall of unknown material is keeping Jacyll trapped. Use magic and might to break the wall.
Defeat Pelzia the Crazed 0/1 (Bixie Warfront)
_Task Window Says:_ The hive is attacking! Defeat Pelzia the Crazed before she kills you and your allies.

_Task Window Says:_ The hive is attacking! Defeat Warchanter Neezzee before she kills you and your allies.

_Task Window Says:_ The hive is attacking! Defeat General Vaxa and her Lieutenants before she kills you and your allies.

_Task Window Says:_ Frislo looks as though he has something to say.

_Task Window Says:_ Your efforts shouldn't go unrewarded. Break the Honeycomb chest and claim whatever is inside.

**The Event: Triggering & Pelzia the Crazed**
_Head up to the top of the hive, and hail Frislo:_
You say, 'Hail, Frislo'
Frislo points at the translucent cage surrounding his brother, 'Here! We need to use all our strength to destroy this wall!'
_DPS "a glass wall" to 75%. At 75%, it locks health and "Pelzia the Crazed" spawns on the bottom floor (not auto-aggro):_
Your aggressive actions have caused the hive to stir.
Frislo looks around, unsure of what to do next. 'Do you hear that? That buzz? The bixies are swarming. They're going to attack!' He eyes his brother before peering back at your group, 'You need to stop them before they reach Jacyll!'
_Pelzia the Crazed spawns in the entry room of the hive. She sees invisibility and has a fair-sized aggro range. She hits for a max ~16,750 and casts two AEs:_
Stinging Blade: Area PC 30', Unresistable (-1000)
1: Stun (2.00 sec/200)
2: Decrease Hitpoints by 53200
Wing Bash: PB AE 500', Unresistable (-1000)
1: Knockback (8) and Toss Up (2)
2: Decrease Hitpoints by 10640
_Kill Pelzia the Crazed to update the task._
Pelzia the Crazed has been slain by \_\_\_\_\_!
Frislo shouts 'Quick! Come help break down this wall while the hive is quiet. We won't have long.'

**The Event: General Vaxa and the Lieutenants**
_DPS "a glass wall" to 25% to spawn "General Vaxa" and three lieutenants._
Your aggressive actions have caused the hive to stir.
Frislo groans as he hears the familiar buzzing, 'Are you kidding me? More bixies?!' The frustrated drakkin looks toward his brother, 'Don't worry, Jacyll. We'll get you out of here eventually.' Frislo nods in your direction, 'You know what to do. Slaughter those bixies!'
_These four mobs are aggro-linked, so you'll have to handle them all at once. All four mobs cast the random single-target spell "Venomous Sting". This acts as a reverse-knockback, pulling the target towards the NPC casting it:_
Venomous Sting: Single Target, Unresistable (0)

2: Add effect: Venomous Sting
General Vaxa hits for a max ~14,000. The lieutenants each hit for a max ~11,400 and are rootable/snareable and mezzable.

Frislo shouts 'Quick! The wall is about to break. Just a bit more brute force and my brother should be free!'

**Completing the Task**
_Destroy the glass wall..._
Frislo hugs his brother while giving gentle reassurance, 'It's okay, Jacyll. You're safe now, you're safe.' The relieved drakkin loosens his embrace and reaches for the drum that his brother is still clutching, 'You'll never have to play for these bixies again, I promise.' Frislo takes the drum and turns toward you.
_Speak with Frislo..._
You say, 'Hail, Frislo'
Frislo says 'Thank you for helping me rescue my brother. I'm sure it'll take a while for Jacyll to return to his normal self, though who knows? Maybe one day he'll be comfortable playing music again. For now however, I want this drum out of our lives. Please take it far away from here when you [leave], and ensure that it doesn't fall into the wrong hands.'
_...And open the chest to complete the task._
You rescued Jacyll from the main hive in Bixie Warfront.
You say, 'Let's leave'
Frislo hands you the drum in a gesture of trust and respect.
_You receive "Selo's Drum"._

**Call of the Forsaken Info & Guides: Overview \| Progression & Task List \| Raiding \| Visible Armor**
**About Heroic Adventures**
\- Heroic adventures in Call of the Forsaken scale in level: As low as **Level 75** and up to current maximum level.
\- Scale is based on the highest level player **present in the zone** and affects difficulty, loot quality, and mob levels.
\- There is some variation/randomization in the task steps you'll be assigned as you progress through the task.
\- Time of completion will vary by your group's makeup (anywhere from 30-90+ minutes).
\- Collection items appear as ground spawns midway through tasks (you'll see an emote indicating their appearance).
\- Named mobs (or their PHs) spawn midway through tasks (usually, but always, at the same time collection items appear).
\- These tasks have individual **3-hour lockout timers** (timer gained upon completion).
**Experience in Heroic Adventures**
\- Mobs in these instances grant some experience, but the bulk of experience is **earned upon task completion**.
\- As of a patch in November 2014, less difficult tasks were tuned to reward less experience and more difficult ones were tuned to reward more.
\- For an added experience bonus, check out **Guard Hamarn's "Daily Tasks"** [quest=7988].

---

_This is a heroic adventure (1-6 players) that begins with Snazlieu the Shadow ( **map image**) in the Bixie Warfront. She is located a little ways north from the portal to the Ethernere Tainted West Karana._
_Pre-Requisites for Requesting:_
\- Level 75
\- Completed "Espionage Begins At Home" (heroic adventure)

---

**TASK SUMMARY**
\- Say "embark on an adventure" to request the task and "ready" to zone in.
**Step #1:**
\- Kill plant bundlers 0/5
\-\-\- Kill update
\- Collect soggy plant parts 0/6
\-\-\- DPS plant parts and loot them
**Step #2:**
\- Plant tracking bugs on tired runners 0/5
\-\-\- Right-click "Tracking Bugs" on rushed runners (doing so will spawn two hostile bixies)
\- Kill delivery florists 0/5
\-\-\- Kill update
**Step #3:**
\- Escort a late delivery bixie 0/1
\-\-\- Speak with Crolling Crixtanus, and kill the waves of mobs that spawn (not auto-aggro)
\- Collect aggressive antennae 0/15
\-\-\- Kill raging bandits, reclaimers, and bitter thieves until you've looted 15 of these
\- Kill rioters in the residential area 0/10
\-\-\- Kill hostile bixies in the residential district
**Step #4:**
\- Kill Cristonix and Luncher 0/2
\-\-\- Kill the named mobs Cristonix and Luncher (splitable)
\- Survive the guards' ambush 0/6
\-\-\- Kill 3 groups of mobs
**\- At this point in the task, collectibles spawn in the zone (ground spawns) and a named (or its PH) has a chance to spawn.**
**Step #5:**
\- Kill factory workers 0/10
\-\-\- Kill any 10 hostile bixies in the factory
\- Destroy catapults 0/4
\-\-\- Collect 20 "Volatile Components" (dropped from mobs in factory), stand on top of catapults, and right-click the items.
\- Support striking workers 0/4
\-\-\- Speak with Kelly (adds spawn), Crutchy (defend from scabs), Delancey (kill him), and Mush ("wants help with slogan")
**Step #6:**
\- Kill Colonel Honeybags 0/1
\-\-\- Kill the named
\- Escape the factory riot and kill the approaching riot cops 0/5
\-\-\- Kill 5x "a riot cop"
\- As soon as the objective for Step #6 is done, the task completes.

---

**NAMED MOBS**
_"Dreadmole" (or its PH) has a chance to spawn after the fourth step is completed. Known loot from this named:_
Bixie Summoner's Stone
Digested Dread Ring
Helm of Latent Ether
Mole Whisker Ring

---

**TASK IN DETAIL**
========================================================================
TRIGGERING THE TASK
========================================================================
You say, 'Let's embark on an adventure'
Snazlieu the Shadow says 'Very well. Let me know when you're [ready] to depart.'
You have been assigned the task 'Always Follow the Money'.
The Stone Hive bixies have begun shuttling all of the supplies to a large cavern in the northern edge of the residential area. Wet River Hive scouts report that the bixies are building weapons of war at an increase rate. Follow runners into the cavern and see if you can locate, and sabotage, the bixies' production line.
You say, 'Ready!'
Snazlieu the Shadow says 'Good luck... No, don't tell me your name. It's easier this way.'
LOADING, PLEASE WAIT...
You have entered Bixie Warfront.
========================================================================
STEP #1
========================================================================
Kill plant bundlers 0/5 (Bixie Warfront)
_Task Window Says:_ You found a group of bixies that are bundling plant parts floating down the river so they can be carried in larger quantities. Put an end to their nefarious bundling of vegetation!
_If You Get This Step: Kill update._
Collect soggy plant parts 0/6 (Bixie Warfront)
_Task Window Says:_ The bixies appear to be using this honey river to float supplies down from the surface hive. Perhaps this is what's corrupting them. Smash some of the floating plants and grab some samples to test back at camp.
_If You Get This Step: Attack the soggy plant parts as they flow down the stream of honey (cannot be stunned or snared, so have to DPS them while they'e mobile). Loot form them "Soggy Plant Parts". If you don't have enough DPS to destroy them on their first go-around, they do appear to respawn at the beginning at the same health you left them._
========================================================================
STEP #2
========================================================================
Plant tracking bugs on tired runners 0/5 (Bixie Warfront)
_Task Window Says:_ It looks like some of the bixies are carrying supplies from the river to another location. Place a tracking bug on some of them to find out where they're taking the supplies.
_If You Get This Step: You receive "Tracking Bugs" in your inventory. You'll need to find "a rushed runner" and right-click the "Tracking Bugs" on them (a good place to catch them is at the end of the honey river). Doing this spawns 2x "an alerted guard" which attack (hit for a max ~12,000; mezzable; stunnable)._
Two shiny tracking bugs leap from your hands and race towards the rushed runner, latching onto its clothing.
