**Call of the Forsaken Info & Guides: Overview \| Progression & Task List \| Raiding \| Visible Armor**
**About Heroic Adventures**
\- Heroic adventures in Call of the Forsaken scale in level: As low as **Level 75** and up to current maximum level.
\- Scale is based on the highest level player **present in the zone** and affects difficulty, loot quality, and mob levels.
\- There is some variation/randomization in the task steps you'll be assigned as you progress through the task.
\- Time of completion will vary by your group's makeup (anywhere from 30-90+ minutes).
\- Collection items appear as ground spawns midway through tasks (you'll see an emote indicating their appearance).
\- Named mobs (or their PHs) spawn midway through tasks (usually, but always, at the same time collection items appear).
\- These tasks have individual **3-hour lockout timers** (timer gained upon completion).
**Experience in Heroic Adventures**
\- Mobs in these instances grant some experience, but the bulk of experience is **earned upon task completion**.
\- As of a patch in November 2014, less difficult tasks were tuned to reward less experience and more difficult ones were tuned to reward more.
\- For an added experience bonus, check out **Guard Hamarn's "Daily Tasks"** [quest=7988].

---

This is a heroic adventure (1-6 players) that begins with Naturalist Yrelia in the Bixie Warfront. She is located at about -790, -2120, 50 (up the ramp just east of the portal to the Ethernere Tainted West Karana).
Pre-Requisites for Requesting:
\- Level 75
\- No tasks

---

**TASK SUMMARY**
\- Say "embark on an adventure" to get the task and "ready" to zone in.
**Step #1:**
\- Kill encroaching spiders near the hunting camp 0/4
\-\-\- Kill update
**Step #2:**
\-
Speak with Gralice at the hunters' camp 0/1
\-\-\- "final advance" updates the task
\-
Speak with Charlbroiled Chanon at the hunters' camp 0/1
\-\-\- dialogue update
**Step #3:**
\-
Draw out and kill lurking spiders 0/8
\-\-\- In the tunnel to the east and onto the spider nest bridges, step over the small rock piles to spawn lurker spiders (kill update)
\-
Squish egg sacs 0/12
\-\-\- Destroy egg sacs (spawns spiderling adds)
**Step #4:**
\-
Free spider hunters 0/6
\-\-\- Clear mobs from wiggling cocoons, then break them open
\-
Collect colorful venom 0/10
\-\-\- This item drops from "a poisonous spider" (found in groups of 3-6; the large ones carry 1-2 bottles venom on them)
\-
Burn undying spiders and resilient egg sacs 0/10
Task Window Says: Some of the spiders in the nest have developed amazing resistance to traditional weapons and spells. The bixie hunters have developed a special tool for killing these undying spiders and their resilient egg piles. Weaken them, and then use the Bixie Fire Blowers to finish the job.
\-\-\- DPS undying spiders and resilient egg sacs (note: these spawn spiderling adds) to 10% and click the torch you received
**\- At this point in the task, collectibles spawn in the zone (ground spawns) and a named (or its PH) has a chance to spawn.**

**Step #6:**
\-
Save Flace Woon from creaky treants 0/10
\-\-\- Speak with Flace to trigger the mini-event; if you look at a treant face-to-face, it stops attacking and remains immobile
\-
Kill Mosscreeper 0/1
\-\-\- Clear the smaller treants to spawn Mosscreeper, then kill Mosscreeper
\-
Hunt wild beasts with Charlbroiled Chanon 0/15
\-\-\- DPS mobs to 10% at which point Chanon kills them
\- As soon as the objective for Step #6 is done, the task completes.

**TASK IN DETAIL**
========================================================================
TRIGGERING THE TASK
========================================================================
You say, 'Hail, Naturalist Yrelia'
Naturalist Yrelia's antennae twitch as she stares up through the rocky tunnel. A breeze from above carries the faint scent of [mildew and blood].
You say, 'Mildew and blood?'
Naturalist Yrelia says 'There is war both above and below, \_\_\_\_\_. The [wild beasts] grow restless under the [oppressive influence] of the bixies.'
You say, 'Wild beasts?'
Naturalist Yrelia says 'Beetles, snakes, moles, and others giving into their most violent tendencies. But the biggest threat are the [spiders].'
You say, 'Oppressive influence?'
Naturalist Yrelia says 'The Stone Hive Bixies may not have been friendly to outsiders invading their land...' Naturalist Yrelia gives you a knowing look before continuing, "But they used to care for nature. Nurture it. Now they [aggravate and abuse] it.'
You say, 'What about the spiders?'
Naturalist Yrelia says 'Wise, strong, and numerous is how you want to describe your allies, \_\_\_\_\_, not your enemies. The spiders have a [large nest] near here, and I'm less certain with every passing minute that we can abide its presence.'
You say, 'How do they aggravate and abuse their nature?'
Naturalist Yrelia says 'You've seen how they're acting recently. This isn't the Stone Hive bixies we've worked alongside for generations. These are monsters. Monsters with no aim but self-[destruction] and [foolish pride]'.
You say, 'What large nest?'
Naturalist Yrelia says 'I've never seen spiders breed so quickly. I've only explored the first few caves I came across inside of the [massive underground complex], but in just those few rooms, I saw [hundreds of eggs] and hatchers tending to them.'
You say, 'What about this self-destruction?'
Naturalist Yrelia looks at you with sadness and desperation in her eyes, "Surely you've heard what their final plan is, right? To conquer Crescent Reach and slay the mighty dragons. Bixie hives are not typically known for their arrogance or stupidity. Will you [help] us stop them before they get themselves killed?
You say, 'And foolish pride?'
Naturalist Yrelia says 'Our allies in the Stone Hive have a long history of flourishing alongside nature, encouraging its growth for a shared protection. Now, they plunder it for trophies and [bragging rights].'
You say, 'Tell me abou this massive underground complex'
Naturalist Yrelia says 'It's better if you see it for yourself. Are you rested up and ready to [embark on an adventure]?'
You say, 'And the hundreds of eggs?'
Naturalist Yrelia says 'Trust me, we do not want them to hatch, if they haven't already. How are you feeling? Are you ready to [embark on an adventure]?'
You say, 'Bragging rights?'
Naturalist Yrelia says 'Hunting the massive spiders in the nearby nest has become a local sport. Returning with the corpse of a giant spider over your shoulder has become the highest sign of military prowess. Many return victorious every day, but even more are dying on the hunt and being left behind by their fellow hunters. There is no honor in a wasted death, and I fear the spiders' retaliation will be too much for the nearby bixie homesteads to bear. True strength comes from working alongside nature, not against it, \_\_\_\_\_. Will you [help]?'
You say, 'I will help'
Naturalist Yrelia says 'The time for talking is done. Action is needed now. Will you [embark on an adventure] on behalf of us and our allies?'
You say, 'Let's embark on an adventure...'
Naturalist Yrelia says 'Excellent. There's an urgent matter that suits you perfectly. Let me know when you're [ready] to confront it.'
You have been assigned the task 'The Great Hunt'.
A group of paticularly bloodthirsty bixies have broken off from the hive to hunt deadly spiders that have nested nearby. Find these hunters and ensure that their crusade is successful, so that they don't accidentally trigger a backlash from the spider nest that'd surely wipe out the hive.
You say, 'Ready!'
Naturalist Yrelia says 'May cool winds carry you and soft ground catch you. Fight well, \_\_\_\_\_.'
LOADING, PLEASE WAIT...

========================================================================
STEP #1
========================================================================
Kill encroaching spiders near the hunting camp 0/4 (Bixie Warfront)
Task Window Says: You discovered a camp of savage bixies that have broken off from the hive and seem to be hunting spiders on the outskirts of the area. Prove your worth by slaying some spiders in front of them.
If You Get This Step: These mobs are located at the zone-in. They can have a lot of hitpoints and some of them AE rampage (hit for a max ~11,000).
========================================================================
STEP #2
========================================================================
Speak with Gralice at the hunters' camp 0/1 (Bixie Warfront)
Task Window Says: The spider hunters' camp appears to be ruled by Gralice, a particularly harsh-looking bixie hunter. Speak with her and find out what you can about their activities.
If You Get This Step: Follow through Gralice's dialogue, updating the step with "final advance".
You say, 'Hail, Gralice'
Gralice twitches towards you and grunts. She laughs cruelly and says, 'Pathetic! I could see the [fear] in your eyes.'
You say, 'Fear?'
Gralice says 'There's no point in denying it, \_\_\_\_\_ You fear me, and you should. I am terrifying. I alone have [hunted] in these caves for weeks, and lived to [reign] over this camp.'
You say, 'Hunted?'
Gralice says 'We bixies have not always been respected for our military prowess. The world saw us as gardeners, nature-lovers, and honey collectors. Down here, in the new hive, we [prove them wrong].'
You say, 'You reign?'
Gralice roars at the top of her voice and nearby spider [hunters] cautiously shift away from her cautiously as she shouts, 'I am the queen of this camp. Do any dare defy me or my claim?'
You say, 'Prove them wrong?'
Gralice says 'Have you not seen the might of the bixie military yet, fool? Then I pity you, and welcome the \_\_\_\_\_ subjugation. The military is strong, but we, the spider [hunters], we are stronger.'
You say, 'What hunters?'
Gralice says 'There is no greater predator in this region than the vicious and powerful spiders. We hunters, the strongest bixies in the entire hive, have fought our way into this area, pouring out our blood for every inch of territory gained as we [push the spiders back].'
You say, 'Push the spiders back?'
Gralice raises her weapon into the air ahd shouts, 'We have pushed them all the way to their nest! The spiders lurk within, waiting for our [final advance]. And we will not disappoint!' The rest of the hunters cheer.
You say, 'Your final advance?'
Gralice gives you a wicked smile as she grabs your arm and says very sternly, 'You will be our forward scouts in this mission. Run into the nest, and fight your way to the top. We'll watch from here to see where they wait in ambush for us.'
=====================
Speak with Charlbroiled Chanon at the hunters' camp 0/1 (Bixie Warfront)
Task Window Says: At the center of the spider hunters' camp is a jovial barbecue chef, Charlbroiled Chanon. The other hungers seem to respect her greatly. Speak with her and find out what you can about their activities.
If You Get This Step: This is a dialogue update; follow through the dialogues with Charlbroiled Chanon.
========================================================================
STEP #3
========================================================================
Draw out and kill lurking spiders 0/8 (Bixie Warfront)
Task Window Says: The hunters at the camp didn't seem to take you seriously, telling you to go to the nearby spider nest and simply wait for spiders to find you. You have the sneaking suspicion that they're right about the spiders finding you. Delve into the spider nest cautiously, and wait for lurking spiders to find you in the first tunnel.
If You Get This Step: As the task says, head into the east tunnel. In there, you'll see small piles of brown rocks on the ground. These signal the spawn locations of your kill targets. Run over a pile of rocks to spawn "a lurker", which attacks. Kill eight of these for the update. These mobs hit for a max ~13,000, AE rampage, and are stunnable/mezzable.
=====================
Squish egg sacs 0/12 (Bixie Warfront)
Task Window Says: The hunters claimed to have killed most of the mature spiders in the caves in their most recent raids. Some reported that they suspect a new batch of spiders will hatch soon. They prefer to wait until the spiders are dangerous enough to kill some of their hunting party before starting a new raid. Smash the spiderlings in the egg sacs before they grow.
If You Get This Step: Destroy the egg sacs (note: spawns spiderling adds).
Location of "Egg Room": -142, -1968, -35.51
========================================================================
STEP #4
========================================================================
Free spider hunters 0/6 (Bixie Warfront)
Task Window Says: There are more mature spiders in this nest than hunters led you to believe. The nastiest of the bunch, the web spinners, seem to be capturing bixie hunters and preserving them for eating later. Find the web spinners, kill them, and free any captured bixie hunters that are still alive. Some of the cocoons have already been feasted on and you'll find nothing but corpses inside. Instead, look for wiggling cocoons which are much more likely to contain someone that can still be saved.
If You Get This Step: Head out of the spider tunnel and into the central nest area. On the bridges, around the caves, and inside the caves, you'll find cocoons called "a wiggling cocoon". To make these killable, you have to kill the "a web spinner" near it. With the web spinner dead, you can free the hunter trapped in the cocoon (these tend to have other spiders near them as well, but they aren't technically necessary to kill).
=====================
Collect colorful venom 0/10 (Bixie Warfront)
Task Window Says: There are more mature spiders in this nest than the hunters led you to believe. The poisonous ones seem to be particularly nasty. Take out the packs of poisonous spiders and collect samples of their poison to develop an antidote with later.
If You Get This Step: This item drops from "a poisonous spider" (found in groups of 3-6; the large ones carry 1-2 bottles venom on them).
=====================
Burn Undying Spiders and Resistant Egg Sacks (0/10)
Task Window Says: ?
If You Get This Step: With the "Bixie Fire Blower" you received in your inventory, DPS undying spiders and resilient egg sacs past 20% health, and then click the item to finish them off. Note that the egg sacs, while being DPS'd, spawn weak spiderling adds.
========================================================================
STEP #5
========================================================================
Find an exit to the surface 0/1 (Bixie Warfront)
Task Window Says: You can see natural light coming from the top of this spider nest. Fight your way to the top and find a path that leads out to the surface.
If You Get This Step: Note that a named has a chance to spawn inside the zone at this point in the task. Make your way up to the top of the spider ramps and down the tunnel that leads to the surface.
========================================================================
STEP #6
========================================================================
Save Flace Woon from creaky treants 0/10 (Bixie Warfront)
Task Window Says: Flace Woon was incredibly relieved to find you in the forest clearing. He told you he's being attacked by angry treants. Help him kill the treants. Treants will freeze and stop attacking if you get close to them and look directly at them.
If You Get This Step: Speak with Flace Woon to trigger this mini event. Once triggered, the creaky treants surrounding the area move in and begin attacking Flace Woon. Don't worry about keeping her alive as she locks health at 10%. The treants each hit for a max ~12,500; are stunnable/mezzable; have a lot of hitpoints; and are social with each other (so if you aggro one, you aggro any nearby as well). They will initially focus their attention on Flace Woon, but you can draw their aggro from her. You can stop a treant from moving and from attacking by looking at it directly, face-to-face (you'll see an emote indicating it's become "frozen in place". As long as you keep looking at it, it will not attack back.
You say, 'Hail, Flace Woon'
Flace Woon doesn't look at you while she speaks to you, 'Thank the gods you came, \_\_\_\_\_. The treants here have been picking off our scouts one by one. I tried to help them, but they've cornered me here. We need to [kill them] once and for all.
You say, 'How do we Kill them?'
Flace Woon says 'The treants won't move while you're looking at them! Try to keep vision on as many as possible while you fight them. Are you [ready]?'
You say, 'Ready!'
Flace Woon says 'Here they come!'
a creaky treant has been slain by \_\_\_\_\_!
Flace Woon looks sad as she picks up a fallen leaf and tucks it into her pocket.
This completes the task.
=====================
Kill Mosscreeper 0/1 (Bixie Warfront)
Task Window Says: You encountered Gralice, a stern spider hunger, as you were climbing out of the nest. She is eager for bloodshed, no matter who it is. Treant have taken over the courtyard in front of you and Gralice is itching for a fight. Try to keep her alive as she hunts the smaller treants. If you kill enough, Mosscreeper will no doubt arrive to defend his minions.
If You Get This Step: Clear the nine smaller treants to spawn Mosscreeper, which spawns with three additional treants. Mosscreeper, itself, has ~8 million hitpoints; hits for a max ~13,000; and casts "Hold the Tree Line" and "Creeping Moss":
Hold the Tree Line: Self 0', Unresistable (0)
1: Block Melee or Spells (50), Max: 0
2: Root
Creeping Moss: Single Target, Disease (0)
1: Illusion: New Treant
2: Root
3: Decrease Hitpoints by 4000 per tick (3 ticks)
4: Increase AC by 1500
**Keep Gralice alive or the task ends.**
=====================
Hunt wild beasts with Charlbroiled Chanon 0/15 (Bixie Warfront)
Task Window Says: You encountered a bixie spider hunter by the name of Charbroiled Chanon as you were climbing out of the nest. She seems obsessed with cooking everything she kills, and is eager to eat something beside spider meat. Help her hunt wild animals in the small forest clearing above the spiders nest. She'll be able to salvage top -quality inredients from anything you kill, but if you bring injured animals close to her, she'll eagerly help you finish them off.
If You Get This Step: DPS mobs to 10% at which point Charlbroiled Chanon kills them.
Charbroiled Chanon spawns on the group immediately after completing the previous step, and will follow one group member around (like a pet).
If you hail her:
Umbraeques says, 'Hail, Charbroiled Chanon'
Charbroiled Chanon says, 'Oh, I just love hunting! You bring the beasties close to me when they're weak, and I'll kill them to ensure the tastiest parts are preserved.
Any mob that's gets to 10% health near her will be killed instantly.
When she kills a mob you see this:
Charlbroiled Chanon says, 'I'll take it from here, team!'

**Call of the Forsaken Info & Guides: Overview \| Progression & Task List \| Raiding \| Visible Armor**
**About Heroic Adventures**
\- Heroic adventures in Call of the Forsaken scale in level: As low as **Level 75** and up to current maximum level.
\- Scale is based on the highest level player **present in the zone** and affects difficulty, loot quality, and mob levels.
\- There is some variation/randomization in the task steps you'll be assigned as you progress through the task.
\- Time of completion will vary by your group's makeup (anywhere from 30-90+ minutes).
\- Collection items appear as ground spawns midway through tasks (you'll see an emote indicating their appearance).
\- Named mobs (or their PHs) spawn midway through tasks (usually, but always, at the same time collection items appear).
\- These tasks have individual **3-hour lockout timers** (timer gained upon completion).
**Experience in Heroic Adventures**
\- Mobs in these instances grant some experience, but the bulk of experience is **earned upon task completion**.
\- As of a patch in November 2014, less difficult tasks were tuned to reward less experience and more difficult ones were tuned to reward more.
\- For an added experience bonus, check out **Guard Hamarn's "Daily Tasks"** [quest=7988].

---

_This is a heroic adventure (1-6 players) that begins with Naturalist Yrelia ( **map image**) in the Bixie Warfront. She is located at about -790, -2120, 50 (up the ramp just east of the portal to the Ethernere Tainted West Karana)._
_Pre-Requisites for Requesting:_
\- Level 75
\- Completed "The Great Hunt" (heroic adventure)

---

**TASK SUMMARY**
\- Say "embark on an adventure" to get the task and "ready" to zone in.
**Step #1:**
\- Speak with a fleeing spider hunter 0/1
\-\-\- "run" updates the task
**Step #2:**
\- Rescue frightened bixies 0/5
\-\-\- Clear the spiders and killer bixies around the "a frightened bixie" mobs
\- Help stalwart defenders kill aggressors 0/5
\-\-\- Kill the hostile mobs around the frightened bixies, and hail the stalwart defenders for updates
\- Kill enraged bixies 0/6
\-\-\- Kill update ("an enraged \_\_\_\_\_" bixies).
**Step #3:**
\- Destroy tunnel walls 0/6
\-\-\- Kill the makeshift barricades that lead to the spider nest (some of these may spawn ambush adds)
\- Activate spider traps 0/7
\-\-\- Click on the traps (they look like little trampolines); note you can get 1-4 adds upon each click
\- Kill triumphant spiders 0/8
\-\-\- Simple kill updates
**Step #4:**
\- Save, then knock out spider wrestlers 0/4
\-\-\- Clear the spiders around each "a spider wrestler", then click the gas on the wrestler
\- Give antivenin to dying hunters 0/4
\-\-\- Kill venomous swarmers (aggro-linked 10 at a time; weak mobs), loot their venom, click it to turn it into antivenin, which you turn in to dying hunter NPCs
\- Kill digging moles 0/6
\-\-\- Simple kill update
**\- At this point in the task, collectibles spawn in the zone (ground spawns) and a named (or its PH) has a chance to spawn.**
**Step #5:**
\- Scare spiderlings into their dens 0/30
\-\-\- Kill 10x "a youngling guardian" (each aggro-linked with three spiderlings) to update the task
\- Gather stolen supplies 0/10
\-\-\- Kill sticky spiders and agile thieves on the ramps and in the rooms, and loot supplies from their corpses
\- Kill infested bixies 0/10
\-\-\- These spawn "an infester" at 50% (normal trash mob) or 4x "a flesh eater" (lower difficulty)
\- Take back stolen supplies 0/10
\-\-\- In an odd turn of events, you stumbled across a large pile of goods missing from the Bixie neighborhood. The sticky spiders seem to be stealing them, both intentionally and without realizing it. Kill the nearby spiders and secure any supplies they have taken.
**Step #6:**
\- Kill Curtis Fig, the bixie avenger, and his mole 0/2
\-\-\- Kill the two nameds at the top of the spider nest (aggro-linked)
\- Kill the enraged adventurers 0/4
\-\-\- Kill the four NPCs at the top of the nest (splittable)
\- As soon as the objective for Step #6 is done, the task completes.

**Call of the Forsaken Info & Guides: Overview \| Progression & Task List \| Raiding \| Visible Armor**
**About Heroic Adventures**
\- Heroic adventures in Call of the Forsaken scale in level: As low as **Level 75** and up to current maximum level.
\- Scale is based on the highest level player **present in the zone** and affects difficulty, loot quality, and mob levels.
\- There is some variation/randomization in the task steps you'll be assigned as you progress through the task.
\- Time of completion will vary by your group's makeup (anywhere from 30-90+ minutes).
\- Collection items appear as ground spawns midway through tasks (you'll see an emote indicating their appearance).
\- Named mobs (or their PHs) spawn midway through tasks (usually, but always, at the same time collection items appear).
\- These tasks have individual **3-hour lockout timers** (timer gained upon completion).
**Experience in Heroic Adventures**
\- Mobs in these instances grant some experience, but the bulk of experience is **earned upon task completion**.
\- As of a patch in November 2014, less difficult tasks were tuned to reward less experience and more difficult ones were tuned to reward more.
\- For an added experience bonus, check out **Guard Hamarn's "Daily Tasks"** [quest=7988].

---

**Patched Heroic**
_On October 17, 2013, the following changes were made to this heroic adventure_
\- They're Everywhere! - The dialogue in the combat report for this Heroic Adventure has been adjusted to more clearly reflect the status of the bixie guards.
\- They're Everywhere! - Enemy NPCs converted to fight for the players' side have had their logic improved, and should no longer betray the players.
\- They're Everywhere! - Bixiefoot no longer has a chance to spawn during the event that has invaders come from all 4 routes.
\- They're Everywhere! - Hatching Matriarchs now spawn adds based on their current health, not time passed.
\- They're Everywhere! - Friendly NPCs in this Heroic Adventure do less damage, have more hit points, and will no longer prevent the player getting credit for kills.
\- They're Everywhere! - Enemy NPCs in this Heroic Adventure should no longer spawn at inappropriate times.
\- They're Everywhere! - The Queen and her Advisor can no longer be knocked unconscious, and should never disappear during the mission.

---

_This is a heroic adventure (1-6 players) that begins with Naturalist Yrelia ( **map image**) in the Bixie Warfront. She is located at about -790, -2120, 50 (up the ramp just east of the portal to the Ethernere Tainted West Karana)._
_Pre-Requisites for Requesting:_
\- Level 75
\- Completed "The Great Hunt" (heroic adventure)
\- Completed "They've Gone Too Far This Time" (heroic adventure)

---

**TASK SUMMARY**
\- Say "embark on an adventure" to get the task and "ready" to zone in.
\- NOTE: As you kill hostile mobs in this task, loot the "Healing Honey Flask" items from their corpses. You will need them in the final battle.
**Step #1:**
\- Alert Queen Shulia Fiume of the impending attack 0/1
\-\-\- Run through the queen's dialogues
**Step #2:**
\- Trap aggressive bixie scouts 0/3
\-\-\- Kill update (northwest tunnel)
\- Weaken enraged treants 0/3
\-\-\- These appear near Naturalist Yrelia; DPS them until they become non-aggro
\- Convert reckless assassins 0/3
\-\-\- They spawn at the lower tunnel; DPS to 20% and then hail for update (not aggro-linked; can be split)
**Step #3:**
-Kill hive defenders 0/6
\-\-\- Mobs spawn nearby Ambassador Crizlyna in 3 waves of 2. Soldiers and defenders count toward kill total.
\-\-\- a hive soldier
\-\-\- a hive defender
\-\-\- As each wave spawns you receive the following yellow emote: "reinforce the main hive path"
\- Kill the cliknar drum hunters 0/8
\-\-\- These spawn at the ramp into the water; snareable/rootable
\- Kill bixie rioters 0/6
\-\-\- Three can spawn near Snazlieu the Sneak, and three up by the treant tunnel.
\- Kill enraged beasts 0/15
\-\-\- Kill update (mobs come from the east tunnel).
**Step #4:**
\- Kill hive defenders 0/4
\-\-\- Two groups of 2 Hive defenders spawns in "main path" tunnel where Ambassador Crizlyna hangs out.
\- Kill the cliknar drum hunters 0/3
\-\-\- These spawn at the ramp into the water; snareable/rootable
\- Kill bixie rioters 0/6
\-\-\- Three can spawn near Snazlieu the Sneak, and three up by the treant tunnel.
\- Kill enraged beasts 0/15
\-\-\- Kill update (mobs come from the east tunnel).
**Step #5:**
\- Kill royal hive guardians 0/?
\-\-\- ?
\- Kill mindless cultists 0/8
\-\-\- These spawn around the ramp up top towards the meadow
\- Kill hardened bixie veterans 0/8
\-\-\- These spawn in waves of two at a time in the water (hit fairly hard)
\- Kill hatchling matriarches 0/2
\-\-\- These spawn near Snazlieu the Shadow and keep spawning hatchling adds
**\- At this point in the task, collectibles spawn in the zone (ground spawns) and a named (or its PH) has a chance to spawn.**
**Step #6:**
\- Use Healing Honey Flasks to revive injured Wet River Hive bixies 0/10
\-\-\- Flasks are dropped by all hostile bixies in the task; hand these to friendly bixies on the ground
**Step #7:**
\- Kill your attackers 0/18
\-\-\- 1 group at each of the three tunnels spawns at the same time. More then 18 total. Maybe some mommy spiders pets?
\- Kill the four invasion leaders 0/4
\-\-\- Ambush by four named mobs with varying abilities; kill them
\- Kill the spider matriarches 0/2
\-\-\- Kill the matriarches (spawn 3 adds every 15-20 seconds)
\- Defend Queen Shulriya Fiume against assassins 0/9
\-\-\- Kill update
**Step #8:**
\- Speak with the Queen to make sure she's OK 0/1
\-\-\- Hail the queen to complete the task
\- As soon as the objective for Step #8 is done, the task completes.

**TASK IN DETAIL**
========================================================================
TRIGGERING THE TASK
========================================================================
You say, 'Let's embark on an adventure...'
Naturalist Yrelia says 'Excellent. There's an urgent matter that suits you perfectly. Let me know when you're [ready] to confront it.'
You have been assigned the task 'They're Everywhere!'.
The wild animals, bixies, and cliknar in the surrounding area have discovered the Wet River Hive's camp in the water caverns. Wet River Hive scouts are reporting aggressive movement towards the camp from all directions. Defend Queen Shulriya Fiume and the camp at all costs.
You say, 'Ready!'
Naturalist Yrelia says 'May cool winds carry you and soft ground catch you. Fight well, \_\_\_\_\_.'
LOADING, PLEASE WAIT...
You have entered Bixie Warfront.
========================================================================
STEP #1
========================================================================
Alert Queen Shulia Fiume of the impending attack 0/1 (Bixie Warfront)
_Task Window Says:_ Report the impending invasion to the queen at the top of the water cavern immediately, so she can help raise the defenses.
_If You Get This Step: Make your way around from the zone-in and speak with the queen._
You say, 'Hail, Queen Shulriya Fiume'
Queen Shulriya Fiume says 'Greetings, \_\_\_\_\_. I'm glad you've returned to our camp. Rigzz here was just telling me about a new plan... Wait, [what's wrong]?''
You say, 'Well, this is what's wrong...'
Queen Shulriya Fiume nods slowly as you speak. She waits for awhile and finally says, 'Then I'm even more glad that you're here. We will need your strength to [defend the camp].
You say, 'Defend the camp?'
Queen Shulriya Fiume says 'We chose this cavern as our forward base because of its direct access to much of the Stone Hive's facilities. That accessibility will hurt us. Rigzz, give us a rundown of the situation.'
Strategist Rigzz opens her book and begins speaking rapidly, 'Yes, m'lady. There are three [primary routes] into and out of the camp. Well, five, if you count the [underwater tunnels].'
You say, 'What are the primary routes?'
Strategist Rigzz says 'The path up to the forest clearing is in the northeast corner of the cavern, on the highest path.'
Strategist Rigzz says 'The path to the residential area is in the northwest corner of the cavern, but is on the lowest path.'
Strategist Rigzz says 'The path to the main hive is directly to the left of the residential route, on the low path.'
You say, 'What underwater tunnels?'
Strategist Rigzz says 'There are two underwater tunnels--one in the north, and one in the south. Both are glaring holes in the [security] of this cave.'
You say, 'Security?'
Queen Shulriya Fiume says 'We can discuss improvements to our defenses in the future, Rigzz. Right now, we need to work with [what we have].'
You say, 'What we have?'
Queen Shulriya Fiume says 'We have two guards at every above-water route. I'll send two more to the ramp up from the water to keep an eye on those entrances. The majority of the guard will [stay] on this highest platform.'
You say, 'We'll all stay'
Queen Shulriya Fiume says 'Our enemies can dig and slink, \_\_\_\_\_. We cannot risk an incursion squad launching into an unguarded civilian area.'
Strategist Rigzz says 'You and the forward guards will need to keep the pathways safe. The reserves will catch anything you let through and hold them off as long as they can. Are you [ready]?'
You say, 'Ready!'
Queen Shulriya Fiume says 'Then go with the blessing of the Wet River Hive, \_\_\_\_\_. Be strong this day.'

**Call of the Forsaken Info & Guides: Overview \| Progression & Task List \| Raiding \| Visible Armor**
**About Heroic Adventures**
\- Heroic adventures in Call of the Forsaken scale in level: As low as **Level 75** and up to current maximum level.
\- Scale is based on the highest level player **present in the zone** and affects difficulty, loot quality, and mob levels.
\- There is some variation/randomization in the task steps you'll be assigned as you progress through the task.
\- Time of completion will vary by your group's makeup (anywhere from 30-90+ minutes).
\- Collection items appear as ground spawns midway through tasks (you'll see an emote indicating their appearance).
\- Named mobs (or their PHs) spawn midway through tasks (usually, but always, at the same time collection items appear).
\- These tasks have individual **3-hour lockout timers** (timer gained upon completion).
**Experience in Heroic Adventures**
\- Mobs in these instances grant some experience, but the bulk of experience is **earned upon task completion**.
\- As of a patch in November 2014, less difficult tasks were tuned to reward less experience and more difficult ones were tuned to reward more.
\- For an added experience bonus, check out **Guard Hamarn's "Daily Tasks"** [quest=7988].

_This is a heroic adventure (1-6 players) that begins with Ambassador Crizlyna ( **map image**) in the Bixie Warfront. She is located a short distance north from the portal to the Ethernere Tainted West Karana._
_Pre-Requisites for Requesting:_
\- Level 75
\- No tasks

---

**TASK SUMMARY**
\- Say "embark on an adventure" to request the task and "ready" to zone in.
**Step #1:**
\- Get instructions from Infiltrator Nallai 0/1
\-\-\- "today" updates the task
**Step #2:**
\- Collect alluring flowers 0/10
\-\-\- loot 10 flowers from bixies outside the hive
\- Acquire royal guard-stamped passes 0/8
\-\-\- Kill or bribe (100pp) honey-addicted guards until you've collected enough passes
\- Find and drink a Bixie Illusion potion 0/1
\-\-\- "a drunk chemist" drops "Murky Potion"
\- Kill sneaky honey stealers 0/5
\-\-\- kill 5 of these mobs (note: they phase in and out of sight around the honey moat)
**Step #3:**
\- Gain access to the main hive 0/1
\-\-\- speak with the keyholder at the front door of the main hive
**Step #4:**
\- Kill fired bixie employees 0/13
\-\-\- kill 13 "a fired \_\_\_\_\_" mobs inside the hive
\- Debate philosophers 0/12
> **Task Window Text:** The main hive seems to be filled with philosophers debating the meaning and value of war. There seems to be a lot of different opinions. Debate some and see if you can uncover some details about what's causing this hive-wide obsession with war.
\-\-\- Speak with and, if necessary, kill philosophers inside the hive.
\- Collect honeycomb chips 0/10
\-\-\- These are known to drop from mobs "a cackling controller" and "a mind controlled slurper".
**\- At this point in the task, a named ("a Bixie Guardian" or its PH) has a chance to spawn.**
**Step #5:**
\- Debate the master philosopher 0/1
\-\-\- speak with and kill Jonnagnon the Dreamer
\- Kill the juiced warlord 0/1
\-\-\- kill Kritty the Crusher
**\- At this point in the task, collectibles spawn in the zone (ground spawns).**
**Step #6:**
\- Collect creepily violent books 0/20
\-\-\- click book ground spawns to spawn mobs; ground spawns and mob kills should update
\- Collect weird chemicals 0/6
\-\-\- kill "a volatile chemist"; "a training surgeon"; and "a confused patient" until you've looted enough
\- Kill test subject moles 0/3
\-\-\- kill the three test subject moles
\- Kill mutating bixies 0/8
\-\-\- kill update (note that at 30%, they regen to 100% and strip themselves of debuffs)
**Step #7:**
\- Obtain the first honeycomb 0/1
\-\-\- kill and loot Hexthresh
\- Stop the butterfly slaughter 0/1
\-\-\- complete mini event: prevent butterfly from being killed
\- Kill Kralynisia, the war alchemist 0/1
\-\-\- Kill the named, Kralynisia
\- As soon as the objective for Step #7 is done, the task completes.

**TASK IN DETAIL**
========================================================================
TRIGGERING THE TASK
========================================================================
You say, 'Hail, Ambassador Crizlyna'
Ambassador Crizlyna smiles politely and nods to you while lowering herself until her front feet graze the ground, 'Well met, \_\_\_\_\_. I trust you're here on [business]?'
You say, 'Business?'
Ambassador Crizlyna says 'Well, if you're here on vacation, you'll leave disappointed, I'm afraid.' Ambassador Crizlyna smirks, then adjusts her face to a somber grimace and continues, 'Have you heard about the [fate] befallen our poor [Stone Hive allies]?'
You say, 'What fate?'
Ambassador Crizlyna says 'Oh dear, yes. Our esteemed [bixie allies] in the Stone Hive have been corrupted by an evil force. They've turned from [wise protectors of nature] to savage beasts that [lash out] at anything that gets near! This has happened to most [clearly against their will].'
You say, 'What about your allies?'
Ambassador Crizlyna smiles weakly, as if she's had to explain this countless times, 'You may have noticed our skin is a different color than the Stone Hive bixies. We're from the [West River Hive], located south of here along [Turnback River].'
You say, 'They were wise protectors of nature?'
Ambassador Crizlyna says 'Well, yes, of course.' She gives you a stern look and a strained smile, 'You wouldn't criticize our long-standing and respected allies while enjoying the hospitality of our well-protected [camp], would you?''
You say, 'How did they lash out?'
Ambassador Crizlyna says 'As the [Wet River Hive]'s primary ambassador to all of our esteemed allies, I've spent much of my life in the housing of other hives and tribes, ensuring our relations remain strong. Naturally, Her Highness brought me with her on this expedition to discover what our [Stone Hive allies] were up to. I've visited Queen Pelzia on many occasions, but this time, I found I was [not welcome].'
You say, 'What is the Wet River Hive?'
Ambassador Crizlyna says 'There are hundreds of bixie hives, some larger than others. Ours is the other major hive in the region, so we naturally sought an [alliance] with the Stone Hive bixies. Bixies need to work together to ensure mutual success in the face of an increasingly dangerous world filled with monsters, demons, and [gnomes].'
You say, 'Gnomes...?'
Ambassador Crizlyna says 'What?'
You say, 'What is the Turnback River'?
Ambassador Crizlyna says 'The river that runs throughout all of Antonica, of course. It's where all of this water around us comes from.' Ambassador Crislyna looks at you like she's concerned you might be a bit stupid, 'You didn't think it was raining in here, did you?''
You say, 'What about your camp?'
Ambassador Crizlyna smiles naturally and resumes a more comfortable posture, 'Her Highness was most wise to personally investigate the [odd behavior] of our Stone Hive allies. Our [military escorts] have kept this camp safe for weeks while our scouts explore the area for [clues].'
You say, 'Why are you not welcome by the Queen?'
Ambassador Crizlyna says 'Their massive [underground hive] is completely surrounded by guards and workers, furiously stirring the honey, preparing for hunts, or getting into brawls in the street. Very unlike bixies to behave in such a brutish manner.' She pauses and looks at you, as if expecting a protest, but continues quickly, 'I was able to get halfway up the front steps before the [crazed guards] closed in.'
You say, 'What is this alliance?'
Ambassador Crizlyna says 'Mostly a non-aggression pact arrangement, with the standard trade arrangements, diplomat exchanges, and safe haven passage, et cetera. I'm sure the details would bore you. What you need to know is that the Stone Hive bixies are our trusted allies, and we will come to their aid in this, their [hour of need], so that...' She pauses to look at you directly, 'opportunistic outsiders won't try to take advantage of their vulnerable situation.''
You say, 'Tell me about the military escorts'
Ambassador Crizlyna looks lustfully at a nearby guard, then catches herself and looks back you with a polite smile, 'Oh yes, I'm sure you've seen them around here. Strong, muscly types.'
You say, 'What about these clues?'
Ambassador Crizlyna says 'Well, you'll have to ask Her Highness, Queen Shulriya Fiume, if you want specifics regarding the more brutish activities. I keep to the [royal chambers] of our allies.'
You say, 'What underground hive?'
Ambassador Crizlyna says 'I'm sure you've seen the Stone Hive located above ground, just east of Blightfire Moors. If you haven't, I highly recommend you [visit] some time. It's quite beautiful. But they've built [another] down here.'
You say, 'What crazed guards?'
Ambassador Crizlyna says 'Their eyes were red, their muscles swollen. Completely consumed by the enraged effect of that [cursed drum]! They practically ripped my wings off as I flew through one of the [waterfalls] back into this cavern.'
You say, 'Visit? Me?'
Ambassador Crizlyna says 'Well, yes, our Stone Hive allies don't much like outsiders such as yourself. They're less tolerant of your violent nature and tendency to [ransack] every place you visit.'
You say, 'We don't ransack...'
Ambassador Crizlyna gives you a patronizing smile and says, 'Let's just say there's a reason we haven't invited you back to our own hive quite yet. But your skills do have their uses, and we'd love for you to visit our allies' [underground hive], where your more forceful tactics are very much needed.'
You say, 'Another hive?'
Ambassador Crizlyna says 'A few months ago, our primary ambassador to the Stone Hive disappeared. When I arrived to investigate the matter, we discovered the Stone Hive bixies were [digging] underground at an alarming rate!'
You say, 'Tell me about the waterfalls'
Ambassador Crizlyna looks proud and brushes some moisture off of her wings, 'While I respect and support all of our allies, being a [Wet River Hive] bixie certainly has its benefits. We embrace the water and the strength it brings us.'
You say, 'Digging?'
Ambassador Crizlyna says 'Well, they mostly used their trained moles for the digging, but the effect was the same. Within a few weeks, they'd dug a massive [cavern system] and filled it with the tools and trainings of [war].'
You say, 'What about their cavern system?'
Ambassador Crizlyna says 'It's marvelous what they've managed to build so quickly. Countless tunnels, dozens of caverns. Factories, dojos, training grounds, homes, and so much more. If they weren't using it all for a doomed [war], I'd applaud them for it.'
You say, 'A doomed war?'
Ambassador Crizlyna sighs and shakes her head, 'I don't understand it. They've become obsessed with conquering Crescent Reach. This corrupting aggression has gotten the best of them, and they feel forced to prove their prowess by slaying dragons. They'll get themselves killed. We must [stop] that.
You say, 'Stop it?'
Ambassador Crizlyna says 'There is still much work to be done at the main hive if we are to ensure our and our allies' safety, \_\_\_\_\_. Let me know when you're [ready] to depart.'
You say, 'What's this cursed drum?'
Ambassador Crizlyna's eyes get big for a moment before she composes her face into a fixed smile, 'Drum? I'm sorry, you must've misheard me. Please, I believe we've talked enough now. There is much to be done at the Stone Hive's underground hive. Are you willing to [embark on an adventure] to aid us and our allies? I hope so, after enjoying the safety and hospitality of our camp.'
You say, 'I'll embark on an adventure here...'
Ambassador Crizlyna says 'Excellent. I believe you'll find there is much to be done in the hive. Let me know when you're [ready] to depart.'
You have been assigned the task 'Assault the Main Hive'.
The Wet River Hive bixies have discovered the source of the Stone Hive's militaristic fury: a magical drum capable of influencing emotions. It's being constantly played at the center of the underground hive to keep bixies obsessed with war. Ambassador Crizlyna has recruited you to help Infiltrator Nallai weaken the hive's defenses for their primary strike force, which will try to sabotage or steal the drum.
You say, 'Ready!'
Ambassador Crizlyna says 'Best of luck to you, \_\_\_\_\_. And do try to avoid hurting as many of our allies as you can. We may need them later.'
LOADING, PLEASE WAIT...
You have entered Bixie Warfront.
========================================================================
STEP #1
========================================================================
Get instructions from Infiltrator Nallai 0/1 (Bixie Warfront)
_Task Window Says:_ ?
_If You Get This Step: Follow Infiltrator Nallai's dialogues ("today")._
You say, 'Hail, Infiltrator Nallai'
Infiltrator Nallai says 'Thanks for coming, \_\_\_\_\_. We're in the final stages of [our mission] and you're the only ones I [trust] to help me see it through.'
You say, 'Your mission?'
Infiltrator Nallai says 'Our [spies] have identified a weak point in the [main hive]'s defenses. We need you to find a way in and [neutralize any threats] inside.'
You say, 'Why trust me?'
Infiltrator Nallai says 'We've had our rough spots during these missions, but when honey hit the pool, you always had my back. I appreciate that.'
You say, 'What have your spies found?'
Infiltrator Nallai says 'Thanks for your help in creating openings for our spies to enter the Stone Hive community, by the way. The integrated spies are providing the bulk of our intel, and only a few have [converted].'
You say, 'What about the main hive?'
Infiltrator Nallai says 'After everything we've seen down here, I guess it's not surprising that the Stone Hive bixies have built another massive hive underground. It's just outside this building, and it's [packed with bixies].'
You say, 'Neutralize any threats?'
Infiltrator Nallai says 'There's some weird types going in there. The hive seems to serve multiple purposes as a [science lab], [university], and a [government building].'
You say, 'Tell me about those converted'
Infiltrator Nallai looks toward the doorway to make sure no one has snuck inside and whispers, 'You hear that powerful [drum], right?'
You say, 'What about science lab?'
Infiltrator Nallai says 'I saw a lot of scientists running around inside and out [today]. Prepare yourself for some weird experiments. I doubt they're studying the effects of sleep in there.'
You say, 'What about the university?'
Infiltrator Nallai says 'Bixies have typically studied the earth and nature, but the students I saw walking in [today] are obsessed with war. One of our spies debated espionage theories with their teacher for over an hour!'
You say, 'What about the government building?'
Infiltrator Nallai says 'I don't want to get into the details of bixie physiology right now, but, yes, bixies still need a form of government. The senators I've seen walking around the hive aren't prone to diplomacy, if you know what I mean. Luckily, I haven't seen any out there [today].'
You say, 'And the drum?'
Infiltrator Nallai says 'We believe that the drum is corrupting the Stone Hive bixies' emotions. It's driving them to insane levels of bloodlust, and bringing out their most savage instincts. We need to put an end to it. But before we can do that, we need to clear out the hive. That's what you're going to do [today].'
You say, 'Let's do it today'
Infiltrator Nallai says 'Your mission is simple, \_\_\_\_\_. Get inside that hive, assess the threats awaiting us, and eliminate anything that'll prevent our strike team from reaching the drum chamber after you.'
========================================================================
STEP #2
========================================================================
Collect alluring flowers 0/10 (Bixie Warfront)
_Task Window Says:_ The bixies around the main hive seem to be obsessed with the local flora. If you can acquire some, the flowers may prove a valuable bargaining chip with the guards blocking your way into the main hive.
_If You Get This Step: Simply kill mobs outside the hive until you've gotten all 10 updates._
=====================
Acquire royal guard-stamped passes 0/10 (Bixie Warfront)
_Task Window Says:_ ?
_If You Get This Step: Kill or bribe (100 platinum) honey-addicted guards until you've collected enough passes._
=====================
Find and drink a Bixie Illusion potion 0/1 (Bixie Warfront)
_Task Window Says:_ ?
_If You Get This Step: "a drunk chemist" (spawns feigned death among the mobs outside the palace) drops "Murky Potion"._
=====================
Kill sneaky honey stealers 0/5 (Bixie Warfront)
_Task Window Says:_ ?
_If You Get This Step: These mobs phase in and out of visibility around the honey moat. Kill five of them to update._
========================================================================
STEP #3
========================================================================
Gain access to the main hive 0/1 (Bixie Warfront)
_Task Window Says:_ Two grumpy guards near the main hive entrance might be interested in your recent activities. Go speak with them.
_If You Get This Step: Speak with the guards to open the door to the hive._
_Without having completed the previous step, you'll see this:_
You say, 'Hail, a grumpy keyholder'
a grumpy keyholder looks at you with menacing eyes and grunts, 'You shouldn't be this close to the [hive], \_\_\_\_\_. You lookin' for a [fight]?'
You say, 'Let me in the hive!'
a grumpy keyholder says 'You want in the hive, \_\_\_\_\_? I'm still going to need to see some official papers or something like that to open the door for you.'
You say, 'I want a fight'
a grumpy keyholder spits in your face. 'You're not worth it, scum. Get out of here.'
_Once on this step, you see:_
You say, 'Hail, a grumpy keyholder'
a grumpy keyholder snatches the bundle of flowers from your hands, shoves them in its face, and inhales deeply. It looks at you and says contently, 'How did you know these were my favorite? They normally only have these [inside].'
You say, 'I'd like inside now...'
a grumpy keyholder looks at the other hive gate guard and nods.
a grumpy keyholder says 'You want into the hive? We can make an exception for you. Besides, we weren't even paid this week.'
a grumpy keyholder inserts a small golden key into the door and the solidified honey chunk shoots up into the hive wall.
a grumpy keyholder says 'I doubt the crazies inside will be happy to see you, \_\_\_\_\_. Give that pompous Jonnagnon a punch for me, eh?'
========================================================================
STEP #4
========================================================================
Kill fired bixie employees 0/13 (Bixie Warfront)
_Task Window Says:_ The main hive seems to be having some inner trouble. The scientists and supporting staff that are housed are getting kicked out. They're refusing to leave and the situation looks likely to escalate soon. Take out the potential rioters before they strike.
_If You Get This Step: Kill 13 "fired" bixies inside the main hive._
=====================
Debate bixie philosophers 0/12 (Bixie Warfront)
_Task Window Says:_ ?
_If You Get This Step: Speak with and, if necessary, kill philsophers._
=====================
Collect honeycomb chips 0/10 (Bixie Warfront)
_Task Window Says:_ The bixies have captured drakkins from Crescent REach and have found a way to mind-control them! They're currently being forced to eat honey constantly inside the main hive. Kill the slaves or controllers and collect the honeycomb chips inserted into their necks.
_If You Get This Step: These are known to drop from mobs "a cackling controller" and "a mind controlled slurper"._
========================================================================
STEP #5
========================================================================
Debate the master philosopher 0/1 (Bixie Warfront)
_Task Window Says:_ The main hive bixies highly value the ability to debate the philosophies of war. The master philosopher has emerged from the higher levels of the hive. Debate him to prove your worth.
_If You Get This Step: Jonnagnon the Dreamer spawns at the top of the steps._
You say, 'Hail, Jonnagnon the Dreamer'
Jonnagnon the Dreamer says 'Well met, \_\_\_\_\_. I'm eager to discuss your opinions on war. Which school of thought do you belong to: [pacifism], [eschatological], [political], [realism], [cataclysmic], [just war], or [peace through strength]?'
You say, 'Just war...'
Jonnagnon the Dreamer says 'Very well, we can apply morals to war, if you insist. What aspect of war do you object to? The [killings], the [conscription], or the [costs]?'
You say, 'The killings...'
Jonnagnon the Dreamer says 'That's my favorite part of war! Allow me to show you why!'
_Jonnagnon the Dreamer attacks; hits for a max ~15,000, AE rampages, and has a lot of hitpoints. Kill him to update this step._
Jonnagnon the Dreamer has been slain by \_\_\_\_\_!
A few points of shining light catch your eye.
=====================
Kill the juiced warlord 0/1 (Bixie Warfront)
_Task Window Says:_ ?
_If You Get This Step: Kill Kritty the Crusher._
========================================================================
STEP #6
========================================================================
Collect creepily violent books 0/20 (Bixie Warfront)
_Task Window Says:_ ?
_If You Get This Step: Click book ground spawns to spawn mobs ("a raving writer"; "a book slapper" - AE rampager). Ground spawns and mob kills should combine to update this step._
=====================
Collect weird chemicals 0/6 (Bixie Warfront)
_Task Window Says:_ ?
_If You Get This Step: Kill "a volatile chemist"; "a training surgeon"; and "a confused patient" until you've looted enough "Weird Chemicals"._
=====================
Kill test subject moles 0/3 (Bixie Warfront)
_Task Window Says:_ Bixie scientists were conducting brutal experiments on restrained giant moles, but they broke out of their cages and have exacted revenge on their testers. Put these poor creatures out of their misery!
_If You Get This Step: Head up the main steps and into the side rooms where you'll find the moles. These mobs will buff themselves with either "Blinking" (a short-term invulnerable buff), "Bubbled" (a rune)._
=====================
Kill mutating bixies 0/8 (Bixie Warfront)
_Task Window Says:_ Bixie scientists in the higher levels of the main hive have been looking for ways to increase their strength. Things didn't seem to go well, and the lab appears to be overrun by bixies in different processes of mutation. Clear out the labs.
_If You Get This Step: DPS "a mutating bixie" to 30% at which points it turns into "a mutated bixie", strips its debuffs, and regenerates to 100%. Kill the mutated bixie. (Repeat x8)_
========================================================================
STEP #7
========================================================================
Obtain the first honeycomb 0/1 (Bixie Warfront)
_Task Window Says:_ At the peak of the main hive, an elder bixie sits inside of an archway, supposedly protecting the first honeycomb ever created on Norrath. If the bodies lying around him are any indication, however, he's become overzealous in his protection of it and has become violent.
_If You Get This Step: Make your way up the hive and engage Hexthresh (hits for a max ~11,500; AE rampages; is stunnable)._
Hexthresh says 'Unpure eyes may not gaze upon the First Honeycomb and live. Begone, heathens!'
Hexthresh has been slain by \_\_\_\_\_!
Hexthresh's corpse's final act is swallowing the honeycomb whole.
_Loot the First Honeycomb to complete the task._
=====================
Stop the butterfly slaughter 0/1 (Bixie Warfront)
_Task Window Says:_ A pack of bixies near the top of the hive has been completely overcome by bloodlust and have started slaughtering all of the butterflies. Once the last butterfly dies, they'll no doubt turn on other bixies. Kill them before they kill the last butterfly.
_If You Get This Step: You will have to defend "a quivering butterfly" from 5x "a butterfly carver". If the butterfly dies, you have to restart the step (takes about 3 minutes for the butterfly to die with little or no intervention). Carvers DPS the butterfly as long as they are in melee range of it (regardless of who's actually tanking them). Until a carver reaches 50%, it cannot be pulled away from the butterfly. After a carver passes 50% health and if you have enough aggro, it will follow you. If you have enough DPS, you can just burn through this event. If you lack in DPS, you will probably have to try pulling them away from the butterfly. Kill all five carvers to complete the task._
=====================
Kill Kralynisia, the war alchemist 0/1 (Bixie Warfront)
_Task Window Says:_ ?
_If You Get This Step: Kill Kralynisia (has ~11 million hitpoints; casts "Crazy Juices" and "Scalding Oil"). Kill her to complete the task._

Scalding Oil: Single Target, Unresistable (0)
1: Decrease Spell Haste by 20%
2: Inhibit Melee Attacks by (20%)
3: Decrease Hitpoints by 1000 per tick

_This group mission begins with Templar Gemma ( **click for map**) in the Dead Hills. She is a gnome found in a tent in the dock camp, just west of the West Karana portal stone._
_Pre-Requisites for requesting:_
\- Level 95
\- Completed "For the Sake of the Forsaken" (Dead Hills partisan)
You say, 'Hail, Templar Gemma'
Templar Gemma peers up at you with weary eyes and sighs, 'It's good to finally see someone who's not injured or otherwise maimed. I could use a break.' The exhausted healer wipes blood from her hands while nodding towards the unconscious gnome to her right, 'Sometimes I wonder if it's even worth trying to save them. I mean, what kind of life could he possibly lead after what the [xulous] have done to him?'
You say, 'What did the Xulous do?'
Templar Gemma says 'I take it you haven't spent much time in the northwest? It's probably best. The xulous are the horrific offspring of Bertoxxulous and butchers, at best. They're determined to bring their behemoths back to life at any cost. Although they're often at war with the ogres to the east, they've taken to kidnapping smaller races and using them for experiments. Maybe they think because we're small we're easy targets? Truth is, we're a resourceful bunch, which is why so many of us have escaped, but not without [injury].'
You say, 'Injury?'
Templar Gemma says 'Even if you manage to escape physically unscathed like myself, the nightmares will surely haunt you for a lifetime.' The gnomish healer pauses with noticeable regret, 'Sometimes I feel guilty for escaping. It doesn't feel right being free when so many [others] are still being tortured. I guess that's why I remain here, hoping that healing the injured will somehow nullify my guilt. It hasn't worked yet.''
You say, 'What others?'
Templar Gemma pauses while she tries to recall the details of her captivity, 'I wasn't in any shape to count my fellow prisoners. If I were to judge by the number of screams I heard, I'd say nearly a dozen were in need of [rescue].'
You say, 'We'll rescue them'
Templar Gemma blinks with disbelief, 'Would you really put your life on the line for a bunch of strangers? The xulous are not to be underestimated. They fight hard and they fight dirty. You won't be able to break their camp alone. Perhaps if you had a [raid] force or maybe a well-trained [group], you'd be able to reach the encampment where they keep the prisoners.
You say, 'I have a group'
Templar Gemma looks up at you in awe, 'Wow. You're really going to do it, aren't you? The least I can do is [lead] you safely to the outskirts of their camp when you're ready. Once you're there though, you're on your own. I'll remain close by to heal the injured, but I'll never step foot in the xulous camp again.'
You have been assigned the task 'Xulous Prime'.
Templar Gemma has led you to the Xulous camp so you can free the gnomes from captivity.
You say, 'Lead me there'
Templar Gemma urges you to follow.
LOADING, PLEASE WAIT...
You have entered The Dead Hills.

---

Locate where the gnomes are being held captive 0/1 (Dead Hills)
_This is a location update just ahead of the zone-in. Note that the task locks once this step updates._

---

Free the gnomes that are still alive from their captors 0/4 (Dead Hills)
_Kill the xulous mobs around the injured gnomes in order to free them. Once all four are freed, Xulous Prime spawns. He spawns in the northeastern part of the camp, so you'll want to be clear of that area upon the last mob's death unless you're ready to engage at that moment._
Xulous Prime appears within the camp to defend his territory.

---

Defeat Xulous Prime 0/1 (Dead Hills)
_Task Window Says:_ Kill the xulous leader before he kills you!
_Xulous Prime hits for a max ~16,250 and has approximately 20 million hitpoints. He has an AE DD+knockback ("Toxic Blast") and a single-target random DOT ("Festering Pustules"). If this DOT isn't cured, an add ("A Rotting Corpse") spawns._
Toxic Blast: PB AE 60', Disease (-1000)
1: Decrease Hitpoints by 46845
2: Knockback (8) and Toss Up (2)
Festering Pustules: Single Target, Unresistable (-1000)
1: Decrease HP when cast by 14400
2: Decrease Hitpoints by 23940 per tick
3: Trigger Effect: Living Dead on Fade
4: Increase Disease Counter by 90
_Rotting Corpses cast "Decaying Flesh" as well as the same "Festering Pustules" as above:_
Decaying Flesh: Single Target, Unresistable (-1000)
1: Decrease Hitpoints by 14850 per tick
2: Decrease Mana by 4050 per tick
3: Decrease Endurance by 2400 per tick
_At 80% and 40% health, a disease cloud aura spawns and follows around a random target (the aura moves very slowly). If caught in the aura, you are inflicted with "Breath of Xulous Prime":_
Breath of Xulous Prime: Single Target, Unresistable (-1000)
1: Decrease Spell Haste by 30%
2: Decrease Hitpoints by 26040 per tick _(1 tick)_
3: Decrease Movement by 50%
_Note: These auras do NOT respawn upon repeat attempts (if you wipe out and let the event reset)._
Xulous Prime has been slain by \_\_\_\_\_!
A cloud of dust and filth fills the air as Xulous Prime's lifeless body crashes to the ground.

**Call of the Forsaken Info & Guides: Overview \| Progression & Task List \| Raiding \| Visible Armor**
**About Heroic Adventures**
\- Heroic adventures in Call of the Forsaken scale in level: As low as **Level 75** and up to current maximum level.
\- Scale is based on the highest level player **present in the zone** and affects difficulty, loot quality, and mob levels.
\- There is some variation/randomization in the task steps you'll be assigned as you progress through the task.
\- Time of completion will vary by your group's makeup (anywhere from 30-90+ minutes).
\- Collection items appear as ground spawns midway through tasks (you'll see an emote indicating their appearance).
\- Named mobs (or their PHs) spawn midway through tasks (usually, but always, at the same time collection items appear).
\- These tasks have individual **3-hour lockout timers** (timer gained upon completion).
**Experience in Heroic Adventures**
\- Mobs in these instances grant some experience, but the bulk of experience is **earned upon task completion**.
\- As of a patch in November 2014, less difficult tasks were tuned to reward less experience and more difficult ones were tuned to reward more.
\- For an added experience bonus, check out **Guard Hamarn's "Daily Tasks"** [quest=7988].

_This is a heroic adventure (1-6 players) that begins with Captain Russell Cooper ( **click for map**) in the Dead Hills. He is located at about -845, -345, 65 (in the clearing north of the zone-in/dock area)._
_Pre-Requisites for Requesting:_
\- Level 85
\- No tasks

---

**TASK SUMMARY**
\- Say "work" to request the task.
\- Head northwest to Scout Gatius Lienweathar and click the ladder to zone into the instance.
**Step #1:**
\- Clear the roadway of rats 0/10
\-\-\- Kill 10 rats
\- Destroy the rat's nests along the road 0/4
\-\-\- Kill 4 nests (each spawns two adds)
\- Kill the undead that are halting the scouting party's progress 0/10
\-\-\- Kill any 10 undead
**Step #2:**
\- Collect substance samples from the local oozes 0/5
\-\-\- Loot drop from oozes in the zone
\- Obtain Necromantic Samples for later study 0/5
\-\-\- Loot drop from undead in the zone
\- Collect samples of Floral Animating Force 0/5
\-\-\- Loot drop from ivymaws and mantraps in the zone
**Step #3:**
\- Inspect the remains of ancient Qeynosian weaponry
\-\-\- /inspect update
\- Inspect the remains of ancient Qeynosian fishing boats
\-\-\- /inspect update
\- Inspect the remains of ancient Qeynosian pottery
\-\-\- /inspect update
**Step #4:**
\- Clear a path along the route to Scout Zatkin 0/10
\-\-\- Kill updates at the end of the path
\- Search through the containers near the end of the route 0/5
\-\-\- ?
\- A spectre blocks your path! Defeat Commander William Helsel 0/1
\-\-\- Kill the named (no surprises, but a lot of hitpoints)
**Step #5:**
\- Speak to Scout Leader Ryan Zatkin 0/1
\-\-\- Hail the Scout Leader
\- As soon as the objective for Step #5 is done, the task completes.

---

**MAP**
!

---

**NAMED MOBS**
_Three named mobs ("Lieutenant Robert Ward"; "Oozoroze"; and "Vorovelze"; or their respective PHs "a skeletal myrmidon"; "an acrid muck"; and "a ghastly ivymaw") have chances to spawn after you complete the third step in the task. They drop the same stat items (can also drop Tier 1 armor templates and Rank II spells):_
Blood Drinker's Coating
Forgotten Juggernaut's Pauldrons
Lost Mercenary's Pauldrons

---

**TASK IN DETAIL**
========================================================================
TRIGGERING THE TASK
========================================================================
You say, 'Hail, Captain Russell Cooper'
Captain Russell Cooper says 'Another bright-eyed recruit, eh? Well ya might as well get to [work]. I'd give ya the grand tour but there's too much to be done in too little time. Maybe if ya survive yer first assignment, we'll have more to discuss.'
You say, 'What work?'
Captain Russell Cooper says 'Just head that way 'til ya meet up with Scout Lienweathar. He'll be standing by the makeshift ladder.'
You have been assigned the task 'Clearing a Path'.
If the expedition of archaeologists and scouts it to have any luck reaching the Qeynosian ruins, they are going to need your help clearing a path through the unforgiving terrain. Meet up with Scout Adam Nevins and work with him to clear a path through the hostile flora and fauna. One of our scout leaders forged a path several days ago, but we have not heard back from him in quite some time. Hopefully, he has not fallen victim to the treacherous hills...
_Make your way northwest to Scout Gatius Lienweathar, and then click the ladder next to him to zone into the instance._
You say, 'Hail, Scout Gatius Lienweathar'
Scout Gatius Lienweathar says 'Ah, \_\_\_\_\_, it is good to see you. Just over this ladder is your destination. I'm sure Captain Russell has filled you in on all of the details.'
LOADING, PLEASE WAIT...
You have entered The Dead Hills.
Scout Adam Nevins says 'Ah good, \_\_\_\_\_. You're just in time. Scout Leader Zatkin was last reported to have traveled down this path before losing contact. Given the number of beasts that infest the area, it's no wonder he hasn't made it back yet. If you need to return to camp for any provisions then just head back down the road you took to get here.' The half elf's composure wavers for a moment as he sighs, 'I hope Ryan is all right.'
========================================================================
STEP #1
========================================================================
Clear the roadway of rats 0/10 (Dead Hills)
_Task Window Says:_ Our weaker scouts are finding it impossible to progress any further while being accosted by giant rats. It would be immensely helpful to our cause if you could clear the road of these pox-bearing beasts.
_If You Get This Step: Kill any 10 rats along the path._
=====================
Destroy the rat's nests along the road 0/4 (Dead Hills)
_Task Window Says:_ We need you to clear away any rat's nests you come across as you make your way down the ancient roads toward the ruins.
_If You Get This Step: Several "a scavenger nest" lie in your path. Destroying them (damage/spells) spawns 2x "a vicious pup" that are relatively simple kills. Killing these adds isn't required for the task, but they have a large aggro range._
=====================
Kill the undead that are halting the scouting party's progress 0/10 (Dead Hills)
_Task Window Says:_ The scouting party's progress into the hills has hit its first stumbling block. It seems the spirits that still inhabit the ruins aim to keep intruders out of their home. The spectres have proven too strong for our party to take on alone, so we need your help in clearing a path so we can move forward.
_If You Get This Step: Kill any 10 undead mobs. Note that the fallen disciples seem to put out the most DPS (flurries). Mobs here hit for a max ~13,000 (blue cons) to ~15,500 (yellow cons)._
========================================================================
STEP #2
========================================================================
Collect substance samples from the local oozes 0/5 (Dead Hills)
_Task Window Says:_ We have heard reports that aberrations of caustic acid have come to life and are devouring any foreign creature that crosses their path. If we are to understand how to combat these sentient slimes we will need substance samples so we can formulate weaponry and antidotes. Dispose of any oozes you come across and collect samples for testing.
_If You Get This Step: Kill "a \_\_\_\_\_ muck" mobs until you've collected 5x "Ooze Sample". These are uncommon drops._
=====================
Obtain Necromantic Samples for later study 0/5 (Dead Hills)
_Task Window Says:_ A number of the undead inhabitants found in the hills radiate with increased power. It would be helpful to our cause if you could collect samples of whatever it is that is empowering these undead as you are clearing the path toward the tower.
_If You Get This Step: Kill undead mobs until you've collected 5x "Necromantic Sample"._
=====================
Collect samples of Floral Animating Force 0/5 (Dead Hills)
_Task Window Says:_ ?
_If You Get This Step: Kill ivymaws and mantraps until you've collected 5x "Floral Animating Force"._
========================================================================
STEP #3
========================================================================
Inspect the remains of ancient Qeynosian weaponry 0/5 (Dead Hills)
_Task Window Says:_ ?
_If You Get This Step: You'll find decaying weaponry around the area. Get close to them, /target them, and do "/inspect" (can be done with all forms of invis without breaking it)._
=====================
Inspect the remains of ancient Qeynosian fishing boats 0/5 (Dead Hills)
_Task Window Says:_ As you make your way through the hills, inspect the remains of any fishing boats the ancient Qeynosian expedition party may have build while trying to survive here.
_If You Get This Step: You'll find rickety vessels around the coastline of the water in the zone. Get close to them, /target them, and do "/inspect" (can be done with all forms of invis without breaking it; note, however, that ooze mobs see invis)._
=====================
Inspect the remains of ancient Qeynosian pottery 0/5 (Dead Hills)
_Task Window Says:_ As you make your way through the hills, inspect the remains of pottery the ancient Qeynosian expedition party may have built while trying to survive here.
_If You Get This Step: You'll find supply urns around the area. Get close to them, /target them, and do "/inspect" (can be done with all forms of invis without breaking it)._
========================================================================
STEP #4
========================================================================
Clear a path along the route to Scout Zatkin 0/10 (Dead Hills)
_Task Window Says:_ We fear our Scout Leader may have fallen prey to the innumerable dangers in these hills. If he is still alive, then the beasts of the hills must have him trapped. Dispose of any creatures found near the end of this leg of the trail so that he can return back to camp.
_If You Get This Step: Clear mobs from the path northwest of the Scout Leader's location. Only mobs in this relative area count towards this step._
=====================
Search through the containers near the end of the route 0/5 (Dead Hills)
_Task Window Says:_ As you make your way down the road in search of our Scout Leader keep an eye out for any supplies that may have been abandoned. Collect them as you progress, they will be invaluable to the scouting party that follows in your steps
_If You Get This Step: Open the Small Supply Cache [item=113976] via /open_
=====================
A spectre blocks your path! Defeat Commander William Helsel 0/1 (Dead Hills)
_Task Window Says:_ It seems that our Scout Leader is unable to return to us because of a fearsome spectre that haunts the road. Clear the final leg of this path by putting the ancient Qeynosian spirit to rest.
_If You Get This Step: Kill the named mob "Commander William Helsel". This seems to be a standard undead mob with a bloated amount of hitpoints (~5.5 million). No AEs or adds._
========================================================================
STEP #5
========================================================================
Speak to Scout Leader Ryan Zatkin 0/1 (Dead Hills)
_Task Window Says:_ Meet up with Scout Leader Ryan Zatkin and work out what must be done next.
_If You Get This Step: Hail the Scout Leader to complete the task._
You say, 'Hail, Scout Leader Ryan Zatkin'
Scout Leader Ryan Zatkin says 'Fine work clearing the road there...' He pauses and looks panicked as he realizes that he does not know your name, '\_\_\_\_\_.' He smiles, reassured that he tip-toed gracefully around that faux pas. He continues, 'So the scouts thought I was dead? I'm surprised they had so little faith in me that they elected me leader and yet don't have the confidence that I can survive my job.' He laughs, thinking himself to be the most hilarious speaker around. 'No matter then. Thank you for your efforts. This stone should take you back to camp. I'll be along shortly to brief the officers on what I've found.'
We are relieved to hear that Ryan is alive and well. The information he gathered will be invaluable in our efforts to push forward and discover the mystery of these ruins. The entire crew is grateful for your valiant effort today.
