**TASK IN DETAIL**
========================================================================
TRIGGERING THE TASK
========================================================================
You say, 'Hail, Skulk the Madaxe'
Skulk the Madaxe beams a wide yellow-toothed smile, 'So the tribe thinks yer a friend, good! Here's what we do. You go back and [stop] their little skeleton problem and they give you the artifact we need. Simple!'
You say, 'Let's stop them'
Skulk the Madaxe says 'Just say [when] you're ready to head back out there.'
You have been assigned the task 'Death Peace'.
Skulk is sure that the artifact he is after rests somewhere in the custody of the Stonecap ogre clan. He has sent you back to their village on the promise of an exchange and is waiting for your return down the road. Your party will put an end to their ancestors' suffering by putting their undead to rest and they will offer you custody of their most important relic.
The tribe's most prized artifact rests on the body of an ancient fallen hero buried in their graveyard. They have since sealed the graveyard off to protect the buried remains from coming into contact with the curse that is bringing their fallen back to life. Oracle Udwoona holds the key to the graveyard and was last seen traveling to the sealed caves of divination to uncover the source of this spreading curse.
You say, 'When...'
LOADING, PLEASE WAIT...
You have entered The Dead Hills.
_Two Ogre Elders greet you inside:_
Elder Grambik says 'Oracle Udwoona holds the key to the burial ground. Tell her that your party has the blessings of Elder Grambik to visit our sacred site to put an end to this nightmare. You should be able to find her within the caves of divination to the south. If you wish to [leave], simply give the word.'
Elder Brembar says 'We would accompany you to meet the Oracle ourselves, but too many hostile beasts have taken residence along our once peaceful roads. I suppose that is why you outsiders have taken to calling this place the 'Dead Hills.''
========================================================================
STEP #1
========================================================================
Clear the road leading to the sealed caves 0/15 (Dead Hills)
_Task Window Says:_ The creatures that inhabit the Dead Hills are becoming more hostile and taking residence in the roadway. Clear the road of dangers on your way to the southern caves where Oracle Udwoona was last seen.
_If You Get This Step: Straight kill updates (mix of puslings, sporali, insects, and undead leading south)._
========================================================================
STEP #2
========================================================================
Speak with Gatekeeper Yeeatru at the Sealed Caves 0/1 (Dead Hills)
_Task Window Says:_ Now that the roadway is clear, Gatekeeper Yeeatru should allow you into the caves of divination.
_If You Get This Step: This is a hail update._
You say, 'Hail, Gatekeeper Yeeatru'
Gatekeeper Yeeatru says 'So you say Grambik sent you?' She pauses, 'Very well. The Oracle went into the caves a few hours ago. Though it's not uncommon for her to be gone for days at a time, I am a bit worried for her safety given the situation we're in.' She turns toward the massive iron door and waves her left hand, drawing arcane patterns in the air.'
========================================================================
STEP #3
========================================================================
Make contact with Oracle Udwoona 0/1 (Dead Hills)
_Task Window Says:_ Make your way through the caves of divination and find Oracle Udwoona.
_If You Get This Step: This is a location update in the south part of the caves._
========================================================================
STEP #4
========================================================================
Defend Oracle Udwoona from the spectres 0/4 (Dead Hills)
_Task Window Says:_ Oracle Udwoona is too versed in the ideals of pacifism to put up a fight against the hostile spirits. Do away with them to save her from certain death.
_If You Get This Step: Kill the four mobs attacking the Oracle. These will generally be chain casting an AE melee slow and have high aggro on Udwoona (who actually can't die, rather locking at 1% health)._
Oracle Udwoona bows her head, 'The spirits call for me to join them.'
_Upon killing the four mobs:_
Oracle Udwoona breathes heavily, 'I thank you for saving my life, but mourn for the spirits that have lost their way. Tell me, travelers: why are you here?'
You say, 'I need the key'
Oracle Udwoona unclasps an ancient looking relic from around her neck and hands it to you carefully. 'Simply stand in front of the iron gate with this necklace held tightly in your hand. If the road is free of danger and your heart is pure of intent, the way shall open for your party.'
Oracle Udwoona says 'Our burial ground lies north on the western road. Beware that the gate will remain sealed until the road is cleared of its hostile inhabitants.'
=====================
Defeat the Xulous that have killed Oracle Udwoona 0/4 (Dead Hills)
_Task Window Says:_ It seems you are too late to save the oracle. Dispose of the xulous that have infiltrated the ogres' sacred cave.
_If You Get This Step: Kill the "a shadowy ambusher" mobs and "a shadow of pestilence". On Udwoona's body (target the body and /inspect it), you will find a key._
========================================================================
STEP #5
========================================================================
Clear the road leading to the graveyard 0/10 (Dead Hills)
_Task Window Says:_ The road leading to the ogres' burial is also infested with hostile creatures. Clear a path north.
_If You Get This Step: Several "a lingering spectre" and sporali mobs block your path to the graveyard. Kill encounter a few of them for the update._
========================================================================
STEP #6
========================================================================
Open the gate to the Stonecap graveyard 0/1 (Dead Hills)
_Task Window Says:_ The oracle's key should be able to lift the seal placed on the burial ground.
_If You Get This Step: This is a location update._
The ground trembles as the colossal iron door slides open under the light of the Oracle's Amulet.
========================================================================
STEP #7
========================================================================
Kill the Xulous that have invaded the graveyard 0/6 (Dead Hills)
_Task Window Says:_ The xulous have infiltrated the graveyard and are raising the spirits. Put a stop to them to stem the flow of restless undead.
_If You Get This Step: Kill the xulous mobs in the graveyard area. Note these are Level 104 mobs and see through all forms of invis._
=====================
Put the ogre spirits to rest 0/6 (Dead Hills)
_Task Window Says:_ The flow of restless undead appears to be stemming from this sacred graveyard. Free the souls of the ethereal ogres by dispersing their ethereal forms into nothingness.
_If You Get This Step: Kill shades for the update (note that skeletons don't count towards the step, but they are social with the shades)._
========================================================================
STEP #8
========================================================================
Obtain the sacred relic of the Stonecap Clan 0/1 (Dead Hills)
_Task Window Says:_ The Stonecap Clan's most sacred relic was buried long ago with their most revered hero. Exhume his remains to claim your reward. Hopefully, the curse has not claimed him as well.
_If You Get This Step: This is a loot update. Kill Shade of Grukkark and loot the artifact from his corpse. The Shade has about 8.5 million hitpoints; hits for a max ~16,500; AE rampages; and isn't reported to cast any spells. Below 40% health, however, he starts calling for the aid of nearby mobs (a very large assist range; you may want to clear the whole graveyard before engaging him if adds are going to be a problem for your group):_
The Shade of Grukkark calls out for reinforcements.
_Kill the shade and loot Grukkark's Zek Insignia from his corpse._
========================================================================
STEP #9
========================================================================
Deliver the Stonecap Relic to Skulk the Madaxe 0/1 (Dead Hills)
_Task Window Says:_ It is said that Grukkark's insignia was given to him by Rallos Zek the Warlord before the Stonecap Clan's succession from the Rallosian army. No material thing holds as much historic significance to these people and they have entrusted you with its delicate care. Meet back up with Skulk the Madaxe at the southern end of the western road to see if it is what he is looking for.
_If You Get This Step: Find Skulk at the southern end of the path..._
Skulk the Madaxe says 'Let's see what ya found!'
_Turn in the insignia to complete the task._

**Call of the Forsaken Info & Guides: Overview \| Progression & Task List \| Raiding \| Visible Armor**

---

_This task is the first in a series of three. It's a shared task (1-6 players) that begins with Zebuxoruk in The Void (H). Note: Only the "Zebuxoruk" in The Void (H) will initiate this task (the Seeds of Destruction versions in Void A-G won't work)._
_To enter The Void (H), go to the portal area in the very far northeastern corner of the Ethernere Tainted West Karana and use the westernmost portal._
_Pre-Requisites for Requesting:_
\- Completed "Answering the Call" (third Ethernere Tainted West Karana task)
\- Completed "The Bixies of War" (Bixie Warfront partisan task)
\- Completed "For the Sake of the Forsaken" (Dead Hills partisan task)
You say, 'Hail, Zebuxoruk'
Zebuxoruk says 'Your journey is not yet complete. The Fourth Gate is home to items that hold immeasurable power - the first, a staff that connects the living and the dead; the second, an ancient scroll whose words allow one to commune with their god. To obtain the first, you must obtain the second. You cannot do this alone. There is [one] within Third Gate who may seem an unlikely ally, but you must trust in him, and he must trust in you.'
You say, 'One?'
Zebuxoruk says 'A devout member of The Dead, a loner who has spent a lifetime searching for the scroll. He will need your help, and you will need his. A partnership must be formed if you wish to navigate Neriak - Fourth Gate undetected. I can [send] you to him now, if you wish.'
You have been assigned the task 'The Missing Scroll'.
You need to recover two powerful artifacts from Neriak Fourth Gate. Zebuxoruk claims you will need the help of an unlikely ally.

---

Find your unlikely ally and ask him about the missing scroll 0/1 (Neriak Third Gate)
_Task Window Says:_ Zebuxoruk claims an ally awaits you in Neriak Third Gate.
_Say "send" to Zebuxoruk, and he will port you to Neriak Third Gate. There, you find Noxhil V`Sek. Ask him about a "missing scroll", and then follow his dialogues. You are assigned an expedition called "Northern Felwithe" (minimum players: 1; maximum players: 6). Tell him "send" to port into the instanced version of Felwithe._
_NOTE: If you have low faction with "The Dead", Noxhil V`Sek won't respond to you, but you can still say "agree" to update the task and trigger the expedition (the dialogues will just be a little off)._
You say, 'Hail, Noxhil V`Sek'
Noxhil V`Sek says 'Yes, \_\_\_\_\_? What is it? I don't have time for idle chatter.'
You say, 'Tell me about the missing scroll'
Noxhil V`Sek gives you his full attention, 'What do you know of the missing scroll? It was scribed long ago by the great necromancer G'han. With it, we can commune directly with Innoruuk. Unfortunately, one portion of the scroll was taken from us by a rogue who was hired by the Koada'Dal. In Felwithe, it doth lie. The Indigo Brotherhood now attempts to [reclaim] it.'
You say, 'Reclaim it?'
Noxhil V`Sek says 'A power struggle between the royals is playing out within Fourth Gate. All assets are being divided by any means, including subterfuge and thievery. If the legend of the scroll is true, it is an item that would considerably increase the power of whoever possessed it, swaying the city in favor of either the King or the Queen. Fourth Gate would fall into utter chaos. There are those that may not approve of my methods, but I serve Neriak, and no one else. I'd be willing to assist you with your entry into Fourth Gate if you [agree] to search for the missing half of the scroll.'
You say, 'I agree'
Noxhil V`Sek says 'I do not know the exact location of the scroll, though it is rumored to be kept under guard within the Paladin's guild. I can [send] you there quickly, but I cannot enter Felwithe myself.'
You say, 'Send me...'
Noxhil V`Sek says 'Be careful. I imagine the guards are on edge as of late.'
LOADING, PLEASE WAIT...
You have entered Northern Felwithe.

---

Enter Felwithe and make your way to the Paladin's guild 0/1 (Felwithe)
Defeat the guards blocking your way! 0/4 (Felwithe)
_Task Window Says:_ Noxhil insists the missing half of the scroll is being held in Felwithe, and he wants you to retrieve it. Many guards stand between you and the Paladin guild!
_From the zone-in, head due east. The paladin guild hall is just inside the entrance gate, and stairs to the basement can be found on either side of the entry room. Kill at least four guards along the way. Guards hit for a max ~12,000, are mezzable and stunnable, but not rootable._

---

Defeat General Jyleel 0/1 (Felwithe)
Defeat Seria Woodwind 0/1 (Felwithe)
_Task Window Says:_ General Jyleel and Seria Woodwind do not want you to pass!
General Jyleel calls out to Seria, 'Alert the guards! We have intruders. Do not let them pass!'
Seria Woodwind says 'Time to die, \_\_\_\_\_!'
_These two NPCs are in the basement, and are believed to be aggro-linked. Each hits for a max ~15,000. Both are stunnable, but not rootable/snareable. Only General Jyleel is known to be mezzable (Seria, unknown)._

---

Search for the scroll in the secret area behind the Paladin's guild 0/1 (Felwithe)
_Task Window Says:_ The scroll should be somewhere within the halls behind the Paladin guild.
_Head through the false wall in the back of Seria and Jyleel's room and into the tunnels. This step updates at around /location 50, -160, 32 (this is a chest on the north balcony overlooking the city entrance). Upon update, two guards spawn and attack. A third guard (static zone spawn) may also add, but isn't a necessary kill._
Guard Hutain says 'Time to die, \_\_\_\_\_!'
Guard Jassong says 'Time to die, \_\_\_\_\_!'

---

Defeat the guards who ambushed you 0/2 (Felwithe)
_Task Window Says:_ Do not let the guards stop you from obtaining the scroll!
_Unlike the previous two mobs you dealt with, these two guards are NOT aggro-linked. They are standard mobs for the zone (hit for a max ~12,000; stunnable; mezzable; immune to root/snare)._

---

Speak with Lieutenant Rosaed 0/1 (Felwithe)
_Task Window Says:_ A Lieutenant of the Emerald Warriors wishes to call a truce.
_Lieutenant Rosaed spawns upon the guards' deaths._
Lieutenant Rosaed shouts 'Enough of the bloodshed! It is clear what you're looking for, but the scroll is no longer here.'
You say, 'Hail, Lieutenant Rosaed'
Lieutenant Rosaed says 'The scroll was in our possession, it is true, but I swear on my honor as an Emerald Warrior that it is no longer within the walls of this city. There is no need to spill more blood. A better thief than you breached our defenses and made off with the scroll. One of my guards caught a glimpse of the bandit, but could only describe her as a cloaked Teir`Dal. My advice to you is to [return] to whoever sent you, and demand to know why they set you up.'

---

Return to Noxhil V`Sek in Neriak Third Gate 0/1 (Neriak Third Gate)
_Task Window Says:_ Return to Noxhil and assess the situation. You must be near Noxhil to receive your reward.
You say, 'Hail, Noxhil V`Sek'
Noxhil V`Sek appears genuinely shocked by your report, 'The scroll has been stolen once again? I promise you, \_\_\_\_\_, I had nothing to do with this. A cloaked female, you say? Someone from the Dreadguard or Dark Arm, I'm sure; a blatant grab for power, orchestrated by the royals. If either party is successful, then I fear Fourth Gate will fall into civil war. That must be avoided at all costs, even if it means sending you into the city to put a stop to it. I warn you though, the city is in turmoil. I would not wander Fourth Gate without a [disguise].
You attempted to recover the scroll of G`Han from Felwithe, only to find it has been stolen once again.
You say, 'What disguise?'
Noxhil V`Sek says 'I will help using what little I know of illusions. While within Fourth Gate, you will be cloaked in magic that will cause others to see you as a dark elf. This should allow you to safely pass through most parts of the city. Be warned, however, the royals still bicker. Those loyal to the King or Queen will attack on sight any who don't share their loyalty. I will head to Fourth Gate now. Find me once you've arrived, as we have much to discuss.'
_Rewards:_
150 platinum
_From here on, when you enter Neriak Fourth Gate you will be illusioned as a Dark Elf, making you non-KOS to the city's residents._

**Call of the Forsaken Info & Guides: Overview \| Progression & Task List \| Raiding \| Visible Armor**

---

_This task is the second in a series of three. It is a solo task that begins with Noxhil V`Sek ( **click for map image**) in Neriak Fourth Gate._
_Pre-Requisites for Requesting:_
\- Completed "The Missing Scroll" (Neriak #1)
\- Must be a Dark Elf (whether naturally, or illusioned)
You say, 'Hail, Noxhil V`Sek'
Noxhil V`Sek says 'I see you made it into Fourth Gate. I will warn you once more, \_\_\_\_\_: It's not a safe city to wander. We're on the brink of civil war, and it's getting worse by the day. With the whereabouts of the scroll unknown, I'm more troubled than ever. Something so powerful cannot land in the hands of royals, who will use it only to increase their hold over the city. We should begin the search immediately, assuming you're still [interested] in helping.'
You say, 'I am interested'
Noxhil V`Sek seems pleased with your willingness to help. 'I'll start the search by investigating the barracks and the library. You should see what information you can find within the Halls of the Dead and the palace. We'll regroup here and discuss our findings.'
You have been assigned the task 'Fourth Gate Investigation'.
Noxhil V`Sek has asked you to help gather information that may lead to the missing scroll.

---

Investigate Darklight Palace 0/1 (Neriak Fourth Gate)
_Task Window Says:_ Explore Darklight Palace, searching for someone who might be willing to speak candidly about recent events.
_This is the complex in the south-central part of the zone - updates near the royal bed chambers at around -1685, -1200, 115 ( **click for map image**)._
_If you speak with NPCs in and around the palace, you'll see these kinds of responses:_
a Teir`Dal sentry says, in Dark Elvish, 'Out of the way. Tarant will have my hide if I miss my rounds.'
a Teir`Dal sentry says, in Dark Elvish, 'All praise Innoruuk, our lord and creator.'
Xavier T`Ser sharpens his rusty blade with slow and steady movements. 'Move along, \_\_\_\_\_. I'm not looking for work.'
Guard Vo`Dani says, in Dark Elvish, 'Perhaps you'll join me for a jug of red wine later? You're buying.'
Guard Vi`Rix says, in Dark Elvish, 'Should you witness anything suspicious, let me know immediately.'
Guard Vis`Moor smirks. 'We're going up to Nektulos later to hunt leatherfeet. You should join us. There's nothing so satisfying as skewering a halfling.'
Guard T`Sanne says, in Dark Elvish, 'A blade can never be too sharp. A mind can never be too focused. A hatred can never be too severe.'
Guard G`Efia says, in Dark Elvish, 'It is an honor to be so near our king, Naythox Thex. May Innoruuk protect him.'
Rysva T`Azzl glares. 'I have nothing to say to the likes of you.'
Issia D`Unnar says 'You need to prove your dedication to our cause before I can discuss such matters with you.'
Guard Zexus says, in Dark Elvish, 'Hate be with you, \_\_\_\_\_.'
Guard S`Kor stretches, flexing muscles in an obvious attempt to impress you.
_None of them will be of any help, until you get to the royal bedrooms in the far south part of the zone. As you enter, you see:_
Manservant Ryzal laughs. 'It's a good thing the queen has been away for quite some time. I don't think she'd appreciate these thin walls.'
a manservant scolds his friend. 'With walls so thin, you should be more careful with your words, Ryzal.'

---

Find someone willing to speak about recent events 0/1 (Neriak Fourth Gate)
_Task Window Says:_ You can hear the servants gossiping in the corner. Maybe they'd be willing to include you in the conversation.
_Speak with Manservant Ryzal ( **click for map image**). Upon running through his dialogue, two guards spawn and attack._
You say, 'Hail, Manservant Ryzal'
Manservant Ryzal says 'Can I help you, \_\_\_\_\_? It's a busy night. The king has a [visitor].'
You say, 'What visitor?'
Manservant Ryzal glances over his shoulder and whispers, 'A [lady] friend, I think.'
You say, 'A lady...'
Manservant Ryzal continues with a smile, eager to share his opinion on the matter. 'I'm not certain who she is, but it's not her first time visiting the palace. She first showed up a few weeks ago, welcomed with drawn blades and uncertainty. Quickly, it seems, the king has warmed to this strange woman. A new ally, I presume? Their relationship couldn't be anything other than business related, for certain. Even so, I'm sure the [queen] wouldn't be too pleased.'
You say, 'Where is the queen?'
Manservant Ryzal says 'She hasn't set foot within the palace in weeks, which can't be good for her marriage -- though it has been good for my ears; they needed a break from the constant yelling. I hate to think how their squabbles affect the [prince], though.'
You say, 'The prince?'
Manservant Ryzal continues without taking a breath. 'He's such an impressionable young man -- far too eager to prove himself, if you ask me. He's a capable and callous warrior, no doubt, but he lacks the experience of his father and the patience of his mother -- though I really shouldn't be saying such things. I'd lose my tongue if anyone important happened to over --' The Teir`Dal is cut off by the sound of guards storming the room.
_2x "a Darklight guard" spawn and attack._
a Darklight guard says 'I'll win the king's favor for this, for sure!'
Defeat the guards who ambushed you 0/2 (Neriak Fourth Gate)
_Task Window Says:_ Nearby guards must have heard your conversation. Kill them before they have a chance to report what transpired.
_You have 2x "a Darklight guard" to contend with. They each hit for a max ~10,000 and are stunnable and mezzable._
a Darklight guard says 'But I was so close to retirement...'
a Darklight guard has been slain by \_\_\_\_\_!

---

Investigate the Halls of the Dead 0/1 (Neriak Fourth Gate)
_Task Window Says:_ Explore the Halls of the Dead, searching for someone who might be willing to speak candidly about recent events.
_This is the complex in the northwestern part of the zone. On the highest floor are the Queen's chambers ( **click for map image**). This updates around 775, 470, 32. Upon reaching this location update, you'll be knocked out and ported to the barracks._
Elite Guard D`Bious calls out, 'Detain this trespasser! Outsiders will not be permitted within striking distance of the queen.'

---

Eavesdrop on the guards holding you captive 0/1 (Neriak Fourth Gate)
_Task Window Says:_ As you regain your bearings, you overhear the guards speaking about the queen.
a barracks guard sighs. 'Looks like it'll be another long night for us.'
a barracks guard replies, 'I think we'll have a lot of long nights until we find out who tried to assassinate the queen.'
a barracks guard nods in agreement. 'At least it'll be a quiet night. How much trouble can a passed out prisoner be?'

---

Defeat the guards who hold you captive 0/2 (Neriak Fourth Gate)
_Task Window Says:_ Kill the guards so you can escape!
_2x "a barracks guard": They hit for a max ~10,000; are splittable (not aggro-linked); mezzable; and stunnable. They aren't rootable._

---

Report what you've learned to Noxhil V`Sek 0/1 (Neriak Fourth Gate)
_Task Window Says:_ Noxhil V`Sek will want to hear what you've learned.
You say, 'Hail, Noxhil V`Sek'
Noxhil V`Sek listens carefully as you recount the information you've just learned. 'These are interesting developments, indeed. I'd like to find out more about who's behind the assassination attempt and who's been visiting the king. The two could be unrelated, but I have a hunch that with a bit of digging, we'll find out how these pieces fit together. See if you can sneak your way into the king's bedchamber and find anything that might help us discover who the king's new friend is and what she is doing here.'

---

Sneak into King Naythox's bedchamber 0/1 (Neriak Fourth Gate)
_Task Window Says:_ Return to Darklight Palace and see if you can find evidence of a visitor within the king's bedchamber.
_Head back into the south-central part of the zone and to around -1690, -1120, 115 ( **click for map image**)._

---

Slaughter the servants before they alert the guards 0/2 (Neriak Fourth Gate)
_Task Window Says:_ Dead dark elves can't talk.
_Kill 2x "a servant" in the room. They each hit for a max ~12,500 and are stunnable and mezzable._
a servant says, in Dark Elvish, 'Could you be so kind as to die for me?'
a servant says 'Oh, dear. This will never do.'
a servant has been slain by \_\_\_\_\_!

---

Search the room for any evidence of a recent visitor 0/1 (Neriak Fourth Gate)
_Task Window Says:_ There must be something in the king's bedchamber that will tell you more about his recent visitor.
_On the dresser in the west bedroom is a note ( **click for map image**), which you need to click on. This spawns two mobs that attack._
On the nightstand is a handwritten note which reads: Naythox, my dear, you allow your lingering affections to get in the way of what is best for this city. Your wife already possesses the scroll, and soon she will have the staff as well if you don't do something to stop her. You must act now, before it's too late. I trust you'll have made a decision before we meet again.
a Teir`Dal patroller says 'I'll win the king's favor for this, for sure!'
a Teir`Dal patroller says 'I'll win the king's favor for this, for sure!'

---

Kill the patrollers that discovered you 0/2 (Neriak Fourth Gate)
_Task Window Says:_ Nearby guards have been alerted to your presence. Kill them before they have a chance to call for help!
_2x "a Teir`Dal patroller" spawn and attack. They each hit for a max ~10,000 and are stunnable and mezzable (immune to root/snare). Kill them to update the task._

---

Tell Noxhil V`Sek what you read in the note 0/1 (Neriak Fourth Gate)
_Task Window Says:_ Return once again to Noxhil V`Sek and report what you've discovered.
You say, 'Hail, Noxhil V`Sek'
Noxhil V`Sek doesn't seem surprised as you tell him about the note you found within the king's bedchamber. 'So it appears we have a third party at play here, one who preys on the king's desire in order to manipulate his political actions. That is a threat we cannot ignore, but the more pressing matter is locating the scroll. At least we know it is in the queen's possession. She would keep an item of such importance locked away in the Great Library, which is under heavy guard. Neither you nor I would be able to breach its defense alone.'
After investigating Neriak - Fourth Gate, you uncovered the location of the missing scroll.
_Rewards:_
Experience (4-5 AAs at Level 100)
262 platinum, 5 gold

**Call of the Forsaken Info & Guides: Overview \| Progression & Task List \| Raiding \| Visible Armor**

---

_This task is the third in a series of three. It is a solo task that begins with Noxhil V`Sek ( **click for map image**) in Neriak Fourth Gate._
(Steps to recruit a member of a particular group are completed in the following subsequent steps. Keep this in mind, so you don't think you are missing something)
_Pre-Requisites for Requesting:_
\- Completed "The Missing Scroll"
\- Completed "Fourth Gate Investigation"
\- Must be illusioned as a Dark Elf
You say, 'Hail, Noxhil V`Sek'
Noxhil V`Sek says 'All of our information suggests that the scroll is being held within the Great Library, an area controlled by the queen. Its doors remain locked and under heavy guard. Even if you managed to somehow sneak inside, you wouldn't stand a chance against the assortment of clerics that swarm the library. You'll need a [group] made up of Neriak's most skilled thieves and assassins if you're still [interested] in recovering the scroll.'
You say, 'A group?'
Noxhil V`Sek says 'You'll need to be very selective about whom you choose and how you solicit their help. If word gets out that you're trying to break into the Great Library, the city guard will put a price on your head that even your closest allies won't be able to refuse. If you're [ready] to start recruiting, I can offer suggestions about where to find hired help that will be both brutal and discreet.'
You say, 'I am ready'
You have been assigned the task 'Cautious Recruitment'.
Noxhil V`Sek believes that you will not be able to enter the Great Library without assistance. He suggested a few places where you might find a willing recruit.
Noxhil V`Sek tells you, 'There's a group of mercenaries known as the Crimson Fist, made up of former members of the Indigo Brotherhood. If anyone were capable of carrying out a heist like this, it would be someone from their ranks. They're known to loiter outside the palace, looking for odd jobs.'
Noxhil V`Sek tells you, 'The Royal Guard is always recruiting. The barracks are filled with those eager to serve the king and those who chose a military life over one spent in a dungeon. It is within the latter group that you may find someone desperate enough to aid you in your heist.'
Noxhil V`Sek tells you, 'Some within the Spurned might be willing to risk their life for a chance to glimpse the knowledge within the Great Library. Its many tomes are often off limits for those loyal to the king.'

---

Recruit a member of the Royal Guard 0/1 (Neriak Fourth Gate)
Head to the barracks and find a suitable recruit 0/1 (Neriak Fourth Gate)
_Task Window Says:_ A new recruit within the barracks might be the easiest to convince.
_In the barracks, speak with "a new recruit" ( **click for map image**)._
You say, 'Hail, a new recruit'
a new recruit gives you a warning wet with sarcasm. 'I wouldn't wander these barracks for too long. The Royal Guard may decide you're here to join their ranks!' The Teir`Dal laughs at his own joke. 'It seems they can enlist anyone against their will. Get accused of [stealing] and wind up in the barracks!'
You say, 'Stealing?'
a new recruit emphasizes his self-proclaimed innocence. 'I was accused of stealing chess pieces from the Drawing Room. Ridiculous, right? Everyone knows that area is inaccessible. You'd have to be a brilliant thief to sneak your way in there.' A mischievous smile flashes across the dark elf's face for a brief moment. 'Apparently it doesn't matter whether I'm guilty or not. I'm stuck serving the king now!' The recruit salutes the air, mocking his new position. 'I'd rather do [anything] other than this.'
You say, 'Anything?'
a new recruit says 'Anything. Why? Did you have something in mind?'
Prince Tarant Thex charges into the room, his blade drawn. With one swift movement, he cuts down the new recruit before addressing you. 'No one but I commands the Royal Guard. I know what you are up to, \_\_\_\_\_, and it is a matter about which we must speak.'
a new recruit died.
Speak to Prince Tarant Thex 0/1 (Neriak Fourth Gate)
_Speak with Prince Tarant Thex (found in the barracks hall just south of the zone's central area) and follow through his dialogues._
You say, 'Hail, Prince Tarant Thex'
Prince Tarant Thex says 'You're going to steal from my mother.' The commander of the Dreadguard continues before you can protest. 'And I am going to [help] you. I'm familiar with the library's layout. You will need me if you have any hope of navigating its grand halls.''
You say, 'Why would you help me?'
Prince Tarant Thex says 'I don't need to explain my motivations to you. I am agreeing to help, and you will help me in return. There is a Teir`Dal within the Halls of the Dead that I believe is in grave danger. It is likely that his guards have been compromised and can no longer protect him. I need you to sneak into his area while he is away and dispose of his guards, allowing my guards to provide competent protection.'
Defeat the guards within the Keeper's room while he is away 0/4 (Neriak Fourth Gate)
_Task Window Says:_ Keeper Scura occupies two rooms two rooms within the second story of the Halls of the Dead. Defeat his guards while he is not in the room.
_The Keeper ( **click for map image**) is found on the second floor of the House of the Dead (structure in the northwestern part of the zone). The guards in those rooms are leashed to the rooms, and Keeper Scura T`Lur paths between the rooms (every 10 minutes). If you are fighting guards within sight of the Keeper, the Keeper attacks (you'll want to avoid this)._
Return to Prince Tarant Thex 0/1 (Neriak Fourth Gate)
You say, 'Hail, Prince Tarant Thex'
Prince Tarant Thex says, in Dark Elvish, 'My guards have taken their position within the Halls of the Dead. I feel better already, knowing that Keeper Scura T`Kur will have the protection of the Dreadguard, even if he doesn't know it. When the time is right, I will honor my word and aid you in your mission to enter the Great Library.'

---

Recruit a member of the Spurned 0/1 (Neriak Fourth Gate)
Head to Darklight Palace and find a suitable recruit 0/1 (Neriak Fourth Gate)
_Task Window Says:_ The Spurned are made of intelligent casters who often spend their time reading and researching.
_Speak to Kavi V`Lask ( **click for map image**) in the dining room of the palace._
You say, 'Hail, Kavi V`Lask'
Kavi V`Lask continues to scan the bookshelf while speaking. 'Unless you're prepared to tell me the location of Miragul's [missing works], save your words. I cannot waste any more time'.
You say, 'Missing works?'
Kavi V`Lask sighs. 'His old journals, filled with information that will help the Spurned animate the creations they have been constructing. I've searched every bookcase in this palace and come to the conclusion that the missing notes must be in the hands of the Dead.'
Recover one of Miragulâ€™s missing journals 0/1 (Neriak Fourth Gate)
_Task Window Says:_ Kavi V`Lask might be willing to help if you recover one of Miragul's missing journals from necromancers within or around the Halls of the Dead.
_Kill necromancer mobs (NPCs wearing black robes(also has worked on Yellow robed casters in this area)) in the House of the Dead until you get this update. You will see no text other than the regular "task update" message. You may need to be on the mob's hate list in order to have a chance at an update._
Return to Kavi V`Lask 0/1 (Neriak Fourth Gate)
You say, 'Hail, Kavi V`Lask'
Kavi V`Lask blinks in surprise. 'You found one of the missing journals? This discovery is both wonderful and worrisome. I was beginning to think the notes were a myth, though it is now a fact that the Dead have been hoarding his research all along. I can only imagine what they keep locked away in the [Great Library].'
You say, 'Great Library?'
Kavi V`Lask says 'The Great Library is an area controlled completely by the queen. What tomes can be found within the library is a mystery to all but those who dwell there. With the existence of Miragul's notes verified, I'd risk my life to walk those halls in search for other hidden knowledge.'

---

Recruit a member of the Crimson Fist 0/1 (Neriak Fourth Gate)
Head to the Crimson Fist camp to find a suitable recruit 0/1 (Neriak Fourth Gate)
_Task Window Says:_ The Crimson Fist are former members of the Indigo Brotherhood who linger across the bridge outside of Darklight Palace. Ask one of them to join your group.
_Speak with Xavier T`Ser ( **click for map image**), who is found at a camp across the bridge from the palace in the southern part of the zone). Tell him you want him to "join your group", then follow his dialogues to get the update._
You say, 'Hail, Xavier T`Ser'
Xavier T`Ser cleans his rusty blade while speaking. 'I don't waste time with pleasantries, \_\_\_\_\_. Name your job, and I'll name my price.'
You say, 'Join my group'
Xavier T`Ser says 'So this is a job that requires a group? If I have to rely on others to not get me killed, then my [price] will be doubled.'
You say, 'What price is that?'
Xavier T`Ser says 'The Crimson Fist needs new weapons. Many of my men are still using blades from their days in the Brotherhood. Unlike our old allies, we're not in the pocket of the king. If you want my help, you'll need to bring me weapons that are worthy of the Crimson Fist.'
Collect weapons from guards around Neriak Fourth Gate 0/4 (Neriak Fourth Gate)
_Task Window Says:_ Xavier wants new weapons for his men before he'll agree to help you. Guards around Fourth Gate have decent blades.
_Kill any four guards in the zone._
Return to Xavier T`Ser 0/1 (Neriak Fourth Gate)
You say, 'Hail, Xavier T`Ser'
Xavier T`Ser looks over the weapons to ensure they meet his standards. 'These seem like suitable payment for future services. My guys will be happy to have something new to swing. Just say the word when you're ready to form your group, and I'll make sure I'm available.'

---

Return to Noxhil V`Sek now that the recruitment process is complete 0/1 (Neriak Fourth Gate)
You say, 'Hail, Noxhil V`Sek'
Noxhil V`Sek seems surprised. 'So you managed to find people willing to join your group? I hope you chose well. The hardest part is still ahead. Breaking into the Great Library is no easy feat. We'll have to wait until the timing is right before we begin, and we should lay low until then. The last thing we need is to arouse any suspicion.'
You recruited a capable group of thieves and thugs to help you steal the Scroll of G`Han from within the Great Library.
_Rewards:_
Experience (4-5 AAs at Level 100)
262 platinum, 5 gold

**Call of the Forsaken Info & Guides: Overview \| Progression & Task List \| Raiding \| Visible Armor**

---

_This is a group mission that begins with Prince Tarant Thex ( **click for map**) in Neriak Fourth Gate._
_Pre-Requisites for Requesting:_
\- Level 85
\- Completed "The Missing Scroll"
\- Completed "Fourth Gate Investigation"
\- Completed "Cautious Recruitment"
\- Must be illusioned as a Dark Elf
You say, 'Hail, Prince Tarant Thex'
Prince Tarant Thex says 'You? I had not expected you to dare to return here. I should slay you for your impertinence, but I might have need of you. Are you here with just a small [group], or have you actually brought a [force] with you?'
You say, 'I have a group...'
Prince Tarant Thex says 'Normally I would never involve outsiders in family matters, but these are unusual times. The king and queen have been at war for decades. Their battles have always been quiet and indirect. But recently both have seen [divinations] from some other realm, and that has changed things.'
You say, 'Divinations?'
Prince Tarant Thex says 'I haven't seen them, and obviously they don't confide in me. What I know is that they have had visions of powerful creatures -- golems, actually -- from some other realm. They have been building these creatures and plan to use them to [defeat each other]. Perfect, really: They don't have to get directly into battle with each other and therefore don't risk looking weak if they lose.'
You say, 'Defeat each other?'
Prince Tarant Thex says 'Fools. They want each other dead but won't take any real risks. Their hatred for each other is matchless, but their unwillingness to fail is just as great. I can't interfere; I can't risk the wrath of both of them. But they hate you immensely already, as any dark elf will. You could go and [defeat these golems]. As long as they both lose, then things won't change. If one of them gains control of the dark elves the world would certainly change in a way you would not appreciate.'
You say, 'We'll defeat these golems'
Prince Tarant Thex says 'Good. Go then. I can send you to where they confront each other even now, as soon as you are [prepared].'
You have been assigned the task 'Houses of Thex'.
The prince has asked you to help defuse the upcoming conflict between the king and queen. To do this, he believes you will need to destroy both the golems they have created.
You say, 'I am prepared...'
Prince Tarant Thex says 'Go then. I shall send a servant to return you if you need to flee or if by some miracle you manage to complete your task.'
LOADING, PLEASE WAIT...

---

**Task Steps**
Speak with King or Queen Thex 0/1 (Neriak Fourth Gate)
Kill both of the golems 0/2 (Neriak Fourth Gate)
Open the chest for your reward 0/1 Neriak Fourth Gate)

---

**Triggering the Event**
_All mobs are non-aggro until the event is triggered. Speak with either the Queen or the King to trigger the event._
_With the Queen, you see:_
You say, 'Hail, Queen Cristanos Thex'
Queen Cristanos Thex shouts 'What are you doing here, scum? Go away. [What happens] here has nothing to do with you.'
You say, 'What happens?'
Queen Cristanos Thex shouts 'This is of NO MATTER TO YOU! Begone! If you [interfere] in any way, I will destroy you and use your corpse as a scullery maid!'
You say, 'Me? Interfere?'
Queen Cristanos Thex shouts 'That can be the only reason you are here. Fine, the king will have to wait. I will destroy you first!'
King Naythox Thex shouts 'In this one thing, wife, I will join in your hatred. But when they are slain we will finish what we started!'
_With the King, you see:_
You say, 'Hail, King Naythox Thex'
King Naythox Thex shouts 'Well, it's you. I see you've come to witness the queen's [fall]. Please hold back your laughter at her suffering. I want her to hear mine.'
You say, 'Her fall?'
King Naythox Thex shouts 'Of course she will fail. That is her destiny. You just [sit aside] and watch. Do NOT interfere. I know you will wish to assist me, but I can't allow that. Her defeat must be at my hands.'
You say, 'We will not sit aside!'
King Naythox Thex shouts 'I said, stay out of this. If you [insist] on interfering, I won't hesitate to kill you first.'
You say, 'We insist!'
King Naythox Thex shouts 'Well then, my dear wife will have to wait.'
Queen Cristanos Thex shouts 'No, husband. I will help you destroy them. This is NOT their affair!'
_Once they're done shouting at each other, the two golems aggro (zonewide auto-aggro on anyone in the zone)._

---

**The Golems**
_To complete the event, you have to kill both "Bone Crusher" and "Nightmare Devourer". There are no balancing or proximity requirements, and they have fairly long leash ranges (will warp back to spawn point if leashed)._
_Due to their mechanics, you'll probably want to keep them separated from each other._
_Each golem hits for a max ~12,500 and has some tricks._

---

**Bone Crusher**
_Bone Crusher has a few emotes:_
Bone Crusher shouts 'Sooo cooooold!'
\-\-\- _5-second warning for the AE "Cold Jolt":_
Cold Jolt: PB AE 110', Chromatic (-850)
1: Decrease HP when cast by 53846
2: Decrease Movement by 50%
3: Inhibit Melee Attacks by (35%)
Get the one called \_\_\_\_\_, my creation!
\-\-\- _10-second warning for the targeted AE "Sonic Jolt":_
Sonic Jolt: Targeted AE 75', Chromatic (-850)
1: Decrease Hitpoints by 23076 per tick
2: Increase Effective Casting Level by 85
_As long as Bone Crusher is a live, auras called "an energy pulse" will appear from the cave behind King Naythox Thex. These auras will target a specific player, who should kite the aura around until it dissipates. The auras move at a very slow speed, so should be easy for any class to kite. Anyone caught in one of these auras will have "Jolt" chain-casted upon them._
An energy pulse moves toward \_\_\_\_\_.
\-\-\- _This is the emote that indicates the aura's target._
Jolt: Single Target, Chromatic (-900)
1: Decrease Hitpoints by 20000
2: Stun (0.05 sec)
**There may be another emote missing here (what is it?)**, one which indicates the spawn of "a power-loaded crystal" which AEs "Explosive Response":
Explosive Response: PB AE 45', Unresistable (-900)
1: Decrease Hitpoints by 80000
2: Knockback (5) and Toss Up (5)

---

**Nightmare Devourer**
_Nightmare Devourer has an aura around it, "Cartilage Decay":_
Cartilage Decay: Single Target, Chromatic (-850)
1: Decrease Hitpoints by 10000 per tick
2: Decrease Movement by 25%
_It also buffs itself "Death's Echo" throughout the event:_
Death's Echo: Self 0', Unresistable (0)
1: Increase Chance to Reflect Spell by 20%
_It has one emote-based spell attack._
The one called \_\_\_\_\_! Take that one's essence, my pet!
\-\-\- _10-second warning for the targeted AE "Grave Chill":_
Grave Chill: Targeted AE 75', Chromatic (-900)
1: Decrease Mana by 2000 per tick
2: Inhibit Melee Attacks by (70%)
3: Decrease Attack Speed by 170%
_Nightmare Devourer spawns health-based adds called "a voracious ghoul" ( **at what health points, specifically?**). Note that these adds despawn upon the event's completion._

---

**Completion**
_Kill the two golems to complete the event._
King Naythox Thex shouts 'Impossible! I guess you knew you could not defeat me, Cristanos. If you must rely on outsiders to ensure your survival, then surely you and your followers are doomed. Come, it is time we retired.'
Queen Cristanos Thex shouts 'What? You fool! My hatred for you grows even stronger! Come, we have plans to make.'
_Rewards:_
Experience
225 platinum

_This is a kill task that begins with Mariela V`Lask ( **click for map**) in Neriak Fourth Gate. She's located at 260, -275, 2 (outside the tunnel that leads to the House of the Dead in the city's northwest). You must be in Dark Elf form in order to speak her, otherwise she will brush you off._
You say, 'Hail, Mariela V`Lask'
Mariela V`Lask looks up from her notes with an eerie smile. 'Hello there, \_\_\_\_\_. Don't let my 'little' creation frighten you. She's harmless, I promise. In fact, she doesn't even move yet. I won't attempt to animate her until I have all the [pieces] I need to bring her to life.'
You say, 'What pieces?'
Mariela V`Lask says 'Bones, mostly. Some flesh to patch up the joints would be nice as well, but I'm not being too picky. There will be time to perfect my creation in the future.'
You have been assigned the task 'Deadly Constructs'.
Mariela V`Lask needs help collecting pieces to build her construct. Golem and skeleton bones would be best.

_This is a kill task that begins with Guard V`Hul ( **click for map**) in Neriak Fourth Gate. He's found at -690, -1895, 80 (outside the Palace in the southeastern part of the zone). You must be in Dark Elf form in order to speak him, otherwise he will brush you off._
You say, 'Hail, Guard V`Hul'
Guard V`Hul continues to look straight ahead while speaking. 'You'd better be bothering me to offer your [assistance] to the Royal Guard. Otherwise, remove yourself from my presence.'
You say, 'How can I be of assistance?'
Guard V`Hul says 'The rate at which the queen's forces are growing is alarming. The fact that she's mustering a private army sends a bad message to the king. Does she not care that her foolish actions could push us to the brink of a civil war? Clearly she thinks only of herself and not of this city. Her army needs to be [cut down], but it cannot come at the order of the king.'
You say, 'Yes, they need to be cut down...'
Guard V`Hul nods silently in agreement. 'Then you understand what needs to be done. Return to me discreetly when the job is done, and I will see to it that you are rewarded.'
You have been assigned the task 'Uneven Armies'.
Guard V`Hul believes that the imbalance between the king's and queen's armies could cause civil war. Slaughter some of the Dead to bring the armies back in balance.
The Halls of the Dead are located in the northwest corner of Fourth Gate.

---

_This is a kill task that begins with Arch Lich Eliza ( **click for map**) in Neriak Fourth Gate. She's located at 775, 580, -32 (first floor of the House of the Dead). You must be in Dark Elf form in order to speak her, otherwise she will brush you off._
You say, 'Hail, Arch Lich Eliza'
Arch Lich Eliza speaks without looking away from the bookshelf. 'Tell me, \_\_\_\_\_, are you loyal to the queen or to the king? I try to distance myself from the dramatics of royalty, but her majesty's devotion to the necromantic arts has drawn me to these halls. Her collection of summoning tomes is unrivaled! Of course, it's far from complete. The king refuses to let some of the books leave the palace -- a bit obsessive, if you ask me -- but I digress. If you're inclined to [help] further the research being done here, I have just the task in mind.'
You say, 'I will help'
Arch Lich Eliza lowers her voice. 'Find your way to Darklight Palace and recover books from the guards, even if you have to bludgeon them. Your efforts won't go unrewarded.'
You have been assigned the task 'Bludgeoning for Books'.
Arch Lich Eliza would like to add a few books to the almost-complete collection of summoning bones.
Kill guards within Darklight Palace who carry the missing tomes. The entrance to Darklight Palace is located in the southeast corner of Fourth Gate.

---

Slay guards within Darklight Palace to recover tomes 0/8 (Neriak Fourth Gate)
Return to Arch Lich Eliza 0/1 (Neriak Fourth Gate)
_Rewards:_
Experience
60 platinum

_This is a kill task that begins with Dov C`Luzz ( **click for map**) in Neriak Fourth Gate at about 100, -590, 1 (along the side of the water in the central part of the zone). You must be in Dark Elf form in order to speak him, otherwise he will brush you off._
You say, 'Hail, Dov C`Luzz'
Dov C`Luzz casts his line into the water before looking up. He scans you head to toe in silent contemplation before shaking his head. 'No, you won't do. Not even the bottom dwellers would want to nibble your flesh, though perhaps you could be of some [help]. You see, I've run out of bait, and I've yet to secure a meal for the evening. A bit of ghoul flesh should be enough to lure something tasty out of this pond.'
You say, 'I will help'
Dov C`Luzz nods as the deal is made. 'I'll be sure to pay you for your service, but don't imagine for a moment I'd share my meal with you.'
You have been assigned the task 'Ghoul Bait'.
Dov C`Luzz is hungry and out of bait. He can't catch his dinner without something to lure the fish.
Kill ghouls for their flesh. Apparently it makes excellent bait!

---

Kill ghouls for their flesh 0/8 (Neriak Fourth Gate)
_Ghouls are found outside the House of the Dead in the northwestern part of the zone (through the tunnel northwest of Dov C`Luzz's location)._
Return to Dov C`Luzz 0/1 (Neriak Fourth Gate)
_Rewards:_
Experience
60 platinum

_This is a kill task that begins with Keliv N`Ryt ( **click for map**) in Neriak Fourth Gate. He is located at 250, -2085, 72 (the area in the east-central part of the zone). You must be in Dark Elf form in order to speak him, otherwise he will brush you off._
You say, 'Hail, Keliv N`Ryt'
Keliv N`Ryt looks you over carefully, making note of all your visual characteristics. 'You resemble her a little, though her lips were a bit fuller. I suppose that's what drew me to her at the Maiden's Fancy. Something about [her] said, 'Invite me to your room, Keliv. I'll make all your dreams come true!' Of course, what I got was a nightmare! I went to bed with a beauty and woke up robbed of everything I own!'
You say, 'Her?'
Keliv N`Ryt scratches his head in contemplation. 'Nadine? No, no ... that wasn't it. Natalia? Nalia, maybe? It was something with an 'N.' I know that much, but I won't let something as simple as a name stop me from getting my revenge. I'll just [hire] someone to take out everyone in this city whose name starts with an 'N' until I find my belongings!'
You say, 'You can hire me!'
Keliv N`Ryt lets out a surprised laugh. 'You're willing to do this dirty work for a little coin? You're sick, twisted, and far more appealing than I first thought, \_\_\_\_\_.'
You have been assigned the task 'Never Trust a Teir`Dal'.
Keliv N`Ryt's dreams were crushed when a woman he met at the Maiden's Fancy robbed him blind while he slept. He seeks revenge, but he cannot remember her name...
Keliv thinks the thief's name starts with an 'N,' and he wants you to kill any dark elf female whose name starts with that letter.

---

Kill dark elf females whose names start with N 0/4 (Neriak Fourth Gate)
_You'll have to do some searching for this one. As the task says, any female dark elf whose name begins with an 'N' counts towards this step._
Return to Keliv N`Ryt 0/1 (Neriak Fourth Gate)
_Rewards:_
Experience
45 platinum

_See Requisitioner Sheon [npc=44021] ( **click for map image**) in the Ethernere Tainted West Karana._
You say, 'Hail, Requisitioner Sheon'
Requisitioner Sheon says 'Hello, \_\_\_\_\_. If you have been as interested in the [disturbances] as we have, and are working to [prevent] further problems, I may be able to [assist] you.'
You say, 'How do we prevent more problems?'
Requisitioner Sheon says 'We know fighting the intruders can help solve the initial problem -- perhaps let these fields feed Qeynos once again -- but there must be deeper [causes] at the root, and I will gladly offer my [assistance].'
You say, 'What disturbances?'
Requisitioner Sheon says 'Look around you. This was once a gentle plain, inhabited by farmers, animals, and the occasional bandit. Now, there's a war going on, and we're not entirely sure how to [stop] it.'
You say, 'What are the causes?'
Requisitioner Sheon says 'I know not what they are, but I am certain these few intruders you see here or elsewhere are not the masters of their own actions. If you can discover these truths you may take a step towards saving us all. I pray you can.'
You say, 'How do we stop it?'
Requisitioner Sheon says 'We know fighting the intruders can help solve the initial problem -- perhaps let these fields feed Qeynos once again -- but there must be deeper
You say, 'How about that assistance?'
Requisitioner Sheon says 'If you have worked towards defeating the major players in this upheaval, I can offer you [items] or [knowledge] that can help you in your fight.'
You say, 'What items?'
Requisitioner Sheon says 'Impress me with your deeds, and I will offer you items of power.'
You say, 'What about that knowledge?'
Requisitioner Sheon says 'If you have managed to capture a bit of the essence of Ethernere, I can give you knowledge of a new ability.'
Bring her a Captured Essence of Ethernere [item=113134] (from any Call of the Forsaken raid), and in exchange, receive a Rank III spell, tome or song for your class.

**Call of the Forsaken Info & Guides: Overview \| Progression & Task List \| Raiding \| Visible Armor**
**About Heroic Adventures**
\- Heroic adventures in Call of the Forsaken scale in level: As low as **Level 75** and up to current maximum level.
\- Scale is based on the highest level player **present in the zone** and affects difficulty, loot quality, and mob levels.
\- There is some variation/randomization in the task steps you'll be assigned as you progress through the task.
\- Time of completion will vary by your group's makeup (anywhere from 30-90+ minutes).
\- Collection items appear as ground spawns midway through tasks (you'll see an emote indicating their appearance).
\- Named mobs (or their PHs) spawn midway through tasks (usually, but always, at the same time collection items appear).
\- These tasks have individual **3-hour lockout timers** (timer gained upon completion).
**Experience in Heroic Adventures**
\- Mobs in these instances grant some experience, but the bulk of experience is **earned upon task completion**.
\- As of a patch in November 2014, less difficult tasks were tuned to reward less experience and more difficult ones were tuned to reward more.
\- For an added experience bonus, check out **Guard Hamarn's "Daily Tasks"** [quest=7988].

---

_This is a heroic adventure (1-6 players) that begins with Lark D`Tradis ( **click for map image**) in Neriak Fourth Gate. She is located at 600, -1800, 67 (outside the tunnel due north of the bridge in the far northeastern part of the zone)._
_Pre-Requisites for Requesting:_
\- Level 75
\- No tasks

---

**TASK SUMMARY**
\- Say "help" to request the task and "ready to go" to zone in.
**Step #1:**
\- Clear a path to the first room 0/6
\-\-\- Kill update (warriors and knights).
\- Clear a path to the first room 0/6
\-\-\- Kill update (evokers and conjurers).
**Step #2:**
\- Recover Shirik's Missive from one of his soldiers 0/1
\-\-\- Kill commanders until you get an update.
\- Question Shirik's soldiers about his whereabouts 0/1
\-\-\- Hail commanders and engage in dialogue or kill until you get the update.
\- Recover Shirik's Missive from his fallen soldiers 0/1
\-\-\- /inspect corpses and /open until you get update (armed corpses spawn 1-2 ambush mobs).
**\- Task LOCKS at this point.**
**Step #3:**
\- Clear a path to the next room 0/8
\-\-\- Kill update (undead constructs).
\- Clear a path to the next room 0/8
\-\-\- Kill update (bats and beetles).
\- Clear a path to the next room 0/8
\-\-\- Kill update (brainwashed Teir`Dal soldiers).
**\- At this point in the task, collectibles spawn in the zone (ground spawns) and a named (or its PH) has a chance to spawn.**
**Step #4:**
\- Defeat Shirik's constructed guardian 0/1
\-\-\- DPS gargoyle down to 15% to update step.
\- Get through Shirik's trap 0/1
\-\-\- Four gargoyle statues; /inspect them to see if armed; use pickpocket or disarm skills to obtain keys, or kill and loot.
**Step #5:**
\- Clear a path to the Doomscale portal 0/6
\-\-\- Kill mobs that ambush you on the way to the portal (mobs don't see invis).
\- Clear a path to the Doomscale portal 0/6
\-\-\- Kill brainwashed Teir`Dal citizens.
\- Clear a path to the Doomscale portal 0/6
\-\-\- Kill doppelgangers of your group.
**Step #6:**
\- Make camp in the safety of the portal room 0/1
\-\-\- Location update.
**Step #7:**
\- Defend Lark from the hostile spirits 0/11
\-\-\- Kill four waves of mobs (2, then 2, then 3, then 4).
**Step #8:**
\- Speak with Lark 0/1
\-\-\- Hail update.
\- As soon as the objective for Step #8 is done, the task completes.

**TASK IN DETAIL**
========================================================================
TRIGGERING THE TASK
========================================================================
You say, 'Hail, Lark D`Tradis'
Lark D`Tradis says, in Dark Elvish, 'Oh yes, you will do perfectly.' She pauses, 'Oh, the necessary pleasantries... Hello, \_\_\_\_\_, I have a proposition for you. You see my foolhardy father stormed off after an argument with the king's loyalists a few days ago and I have not seen or heard from him since. Thus, I could use your [help] in tracking him down. You will be paid fairly, of course.'
You say, 'I will help'
Lark D`Tradis says, in Dark Elvish, 'Excellent, are you [ready to go] now?'
You have been assigned the task 'The Toll Road'.
Lark D`Tradis has enlisted your party's aid in finding her father. She worries that his senility has claimed the last of his senses and that he will get himself and her family killed in his aimless war against both houses of Thex.
You say, 'Ready to go!'
Lark D`Tradis says, in Dark Elvish, 'I shall meet you on the road. I believe it was this way...'
LOADING, PLEASE WAIT...

========================================================================
STEP #1
========================================================================
Clear a path to the first room 0/6 (Neriak Fourth Gate)
_Task Window Says:_ ?
_If You Get This Step: Kill update (kill six Teir`Dal warriors and knights)._
=====================
Clear a path to the first room 0/6 (Neriak Fourth Gate)
_Task Window Says:_ A small brigade of arcanists stand guard aiming to stop any outsiders from passing through the tunnels. They exhibit and eerie soullessness in their quiet vigilance.
_If You Get This Step: Kill update (kill six evokers and conjurers). Note that these mobs tend to be very resistant to spells and discs._
========================================================================
STEP #2
========================================================================
Recover Shirik's Missive from one of his soldiers 0/1 (Neriak Fourth Gate)
_Task Window Says:_ ?
_If You Get This Step: In the first clearing are five named commanders. They hit for a max ~17,000, and are immune to mez and stun. Kill these until you loot "Shirik's Missive"._
=====================
Question Shirik's soldiers about his whereabouts 0/1 (Neriak Fourth Gate)
_Task Window Says:_ ?
_If You Get This Step: Using the language Elder Teir`Dal, hail the commanders, see how they respond to the hail, and then respond to them however you'd like (some responses will generate aggro; some will ignore you; one will update the step)._
'What do you need?' _(compassionate / "appeal to compassion")_
'What'da want?' _(stupid / "act stupid")_
'Yes?' _(bluffing / "bluff")_
'Hail, \_\_\_\_\_' _(cunning / "outsmart")_
'What!?' _(warlike / "warrior's approach")_
=====================
Recover Shirik's Missive from his fallen soldiers 0/1 (Neriak Fourth Gate)
_Task Window Says:_ ?
_If You Get This Step: You'll find five commander corpses. /inspect them (will tell if you if armed) and /open until you get update. When you /open an armed corpse, you get ambushed by 1-2 mobs._
_Reportedly, you can avoid the ambush by using the "Archaeologist's Automated Antiquity Analyzer" on the corpses._

_Task Window Says:_ ?

=====================
Clear a path to the next room 0/8 (Neriak Fourth Gate)

_If You Get This Step: Standard kill update (bats and beetles). Note that the cave bats in this area see invisibility._
=====================
Clear a path to the next room 0/8 (Neriak Fourth Gate)
_Task Window Says:_ ?
_If You Get This Step: Standard kill update (brainwashed Teir`Dal soldiers)._
========================================================================
STEP #4
========================================================================
Defeat Shirik's constructed guardian 0/1 (Neriak Fourth Gate)
_Task Window Says:_ A stone behemoth stands guard in front of a wall of flame. No doubt left by Shirik himself as a last ditch effort should his guards have failed to halt any intruders.
_If You Get This Step: DPS "Shirik`s Guardian" down to 15% health (hits for a max ~21,000; has a lot of hitpoints). As you fight it, it alternates between an "enraged" state (AE rampages and some flurries; immune to all ranged DPS) and weakened (vulnerable to all DPS)._
The grinding of stone can be heard throughout the cavern as the great creature comes to life!
Shirik's Guardian glows red with rage!
Shirik's Guardian staggers, momentarily weakened.
_At 15%:_
Shirik's Guardian stumbles backwards through the wall of flame and escapes down the hall.
=====================
Get through Shirik's trap 0/1 (Neriak Fourth Gate)
_Task Window Says:_ Several stone statues stand idly by in front of a wall of flame. You think you spot a shimmering light in one of their hands, but upon closer inspection you are unsure if your mind was simply playing tricks on you.
_If You Get This Step: Four gargoyle statues are in the room. /inspect them to see if they're armed. You can use pickpocket or disarm skills to obtain keys (this will require MANY attempts with many failures), or you can kill them and loot (DPS to 70% to aggro, then kill). Right-click the key to update the step._
_You can also target them and /open them. Doing this gives the emote:_
You grasp onto the gargoyle's claws and begin to pull with all your might. The sound of grinding stone grows louder as its grip loosens slightly.
_...And the ward takes 10% damage. At 60%, the ward turns into a Level 104 "a stone ward" and attacks with the emote:_
The cavern shakes as the stone gargoyle lets loose a furious roar!
_Kill these until you loot a "Frozen Key". Target "a stone ward" and click/cast the key at it. This completes the step._

========================================================================
Clear a path to the Doomscale portal 0/6 (Neriak Fourth Gate)

=====================

_Task Window Says:_ ?

=====================
Clear a path to the Doomscale portal 0/6 (Neriak Fourth Gate)
_Task Window Says:_ In the distance you hear the soft echo of laughter. Ahead on the trail you spot a frightening sight.
_If You Get This Step: Kill worthy adversaries (hopefully, they're worthy) along the path. These mobs hit for a max ~15,750 and occasionally flurry and rampage._
The hall ahead of you is suspiciously empty, save for a few points of glowing light.
Tread with care as you progress and perhaps you'll get the jump on whatever silent assassins may be lying in wait.
Umbraeques trips over a stone, alerting nearby assassins!
Umbraeques carefully nudges a trapped stone allowing you to get the jump nearby assassins!
(I was invis on the second one, not sure if that made a difference)
========================================================================
STEP #6
========================================================================
Make camp in the safety of the portal room 0/1 (Neriak Fourth Gate)
_Task Window Says:_ Though the road has been treacherous you have made it this far. Soon you will reach the library that Lark believes her father has barricaded himself in. Ensure Lark has made it safely to the campfire by the Doomscale portal and await further instructions.
_If You Get This Step: This is a location update in the Ethernere portal room. NOTE: This will trigger the spawn of two hostile mobs. As you approach Lark, you see:_
A cold gust of wind whistles through the cavern. In the chill you hear a whispered voice say, 'Turn back!'
a lingering bonewalker sets its sights upon \_\_\_\_\_.
a lingering bonewalker sets its sights upon \_\_\_\_\_.
Lark D`Tradis ducks down by the fire seeking protection from the phantoms. She cries, 'Father! Stop this at once!' You assume this would be a bad time to [leave].
========================================================================
STEP #7
========================================================================
Defend Lark from the hostile spirits 0/1 (Neriak Fourth Gate)
_Task Window Says:_ It would appear this camp is hardly safe at all! Protect yourself from the bloodthirsty undead.
_If You Get This Step: Here, you'll need to kill four waves of mobs, the first of which spawns as you approach Lark D`Tadris in the portal room. Sometimes the mob names will be different for each wave._
\- Two mobs: 2x "a lingering bonewalker"
\- Two mobs: 2x "a phantom"
\- Three mobs: 1x "a lingering bonewalker" + 1x "a phantom" + 1x "a projection"
\- Four mobs: 2x "a lingering bonewalker" + 1x "a phantom" + 1x "a projection"
_Note: When the mobs initially spawn, they aggro any players within their aggro range regardless of invis or IVU; however, the mobs themselves are NOT auto-aggro (if you are standing far enough away from their spawn points - which are around the room - they won't actually aggro you). The mobs all hit for a max ~10,000 and are varyingly susceptible to crowd control._
_As the last of them are killed:_
Lark D`Tradis shouts, in Dark Elvish, 'I think that's the last of them!'
========================================================================
STEP #8
========================================================================
Speak with Lark 0/1 (Neriak Fourth Gate)
_Task Window Says:_ Speak with Lark about what must be done next.
_If You Get This Step: Hail to complete the task._
You say, 'Hail, Lark D`Tradis'
Lark D`Tradis breathes an exhausted sigh of relief as she hastily sits by the campfire, 'Thank you, \_\_\_\_\_. I need some time to collect myself before we journey further. You may take your [leave] now and return to me when you are ready to continue.'
You've done well to clear the road to the library, but there is still a ways to go to find Lark's father.

**Call of the Forsaken Info & Guides: Overview \| Progression & Task List \| Raiding \| Visible Armor**
**About Heroic Adventures**
\- Heroic adventures in Call of the Forsaken scale in level: As low as **Level 75** and up to current maximum level.
\- Scale is based on the highest level player **present in the zone** and affects difficulty, loot quality, and mob levels.
\- There is some variation/randomization in the task steps you'll be assigned as you progress through the task.
\- Time of completion will vary by your group's makeup (anywhere from 30-90+ minutes).
\- Collection items appear as ground spawns midway through tasks (you'll see an emote indicating their appearance).
\- Named mobs (or their PHs) spawn midway through tasks (usually, but always, at the same time collection items appear).
\- These tasks have individual **3-hour lockout timers** (timer gained upon completion).
**Experience in Heroic Adventures**
\- Mobs in these instances grant some experience, but the bulk of experience is **earned upon task completion**.
\- As of a patch in November 2014, less difficult tasks were tuned to reward less experience and more difficult ones were tuned to reward more.
\- For an added experience bonus, check out **Guard Hamarn's "Daily Tasks"** [quest=7988].

---

_This is a heroic adventure (1-6 players) that begins with Lark D`Tradis ( **click for map image**) in Neriak Fourth Gate. She is located at 600, -1800, 67 (outside the tunnel due north of the bridge in the far northeastern part of the zone)._
_Pre-Requisites for Requesting:_
\- Level 75
\- Completed "The Toll Road" (heroic adventure)

---

**TASK SUMMARY**
\- Say "continue" to request the task and "ready to go" to zone in.
**Step #1:**
\- Clear a path to sealed library 0/7
\-\-\- Kill update (brainwashed Teir`Dal knights and wizards).
\- Clear a path to sealed library 0/7
\-\-\- Kill update (undead mobs).
**Step #2:**
\- Obtain the library key from an ousted librarian 0/1
\-\-\- Kill ousted librarians until you loot a key (each librarian has two aggro-linked guards).
\- Destroy the crystals that shield the library doors 0/4
\-\-\- Kill the four crystals (each has two aggro-linked guards).
**\- The task LOCKS at this point.**
**Step #3:**
\- Attempt to enter the sealed library 0/1
\-\-\- Click on the door (if a key was looted in previous step, the player with the key needs to click).
**\- At this point in the task, collectibles spawn in the zone (ground spawns) and a named (or its PH) has a chance to spawn.**
**Step #4:**
\- Defeat Shirik's ethereal guard 0/12
\-\-\- Kill 12 undead mobs; these spawn one at a time and attack (up to three mobs can be up at a time).
**Step #5:**
\- Defeat Shirik's constructed guardian 0/1
\-\-\- DPS the gargoyle to 8% health.
\- Defeat Diran D`Tradis 0/1
\-\-\- Aggro's after previous step completed; spawns an add at 76%, 31%, and 6% (locks health until adds are killed).
**Step #6:**
\- Speak with Lark 0/1
\-\-\- Hail update
\- As soon as the objective for Step #6 is done, the task completes.

!

---

**NAMED MOBS**
_"Rilen D`Tradis" (or its PH, "a Teir`Dal captain") spawns after the third step is completed. Known loot from this named:_
Aegis of Prince's Court
Boots of Latent Ether
Guardian Ornament
Tome: Last Stand Discipline Rk. II
