**TASK IN DETAIL**
========================================================================
TRIGGERING THE TASK
========================================================================
You say, 'Hail, Lark D`Tradis'
Lark D`Tradis says, in Dark Elvish, 'I find it strange that those red eyed madmen all spoke Elder Teir`Dal, such a dialect is rare to hear even in the royal houses. Stranger still is the resistance we're facing in finding my father, even in his most reclusive hours I was always able to speak to him..' She sighs, looking more worried by the moment, 'If you are ready to [continue] on toward the library, just let me know.''
You say, 'Let's continue'
Lark D`Tradis says, in Dark Elvish, 'Excellent, are you [ready to go] now?'
You have been assigned the task 'The Sealed Gate'.
Using the information you gathered on your previous outing, Lark is sure her father has barricaded himself in the central library. She's still unsure what foolish endeavor he has undertaken, but needs your help to reach him before he does anything to endanger himself.
You say, 'Ready to go!'
Lark D`Tradis says, in Dark Elvish, 'I shall meet you on the road. I believe it was this way...'
LOADING, PLEASE WAIT...
You have entered Neriak - Fourth Gate.
========================================================================
STEP #1
========================================================================
Clear a path to sealed library 0/7 (Neriak Fourth Gate)
**Task Window Says:** Using the information you've gathered on your previous outing, Lark is sure her father has barricaded himself in the central library. She's still unsure what foolish endeavor he has undertaken, but needs your help to reach him before he does anything to endanger himself.
Another group of soldiers stands guard on the road leading to the sealed library. There is nothing unsettling in their lifeless demeanor.
_If You Get This Step: Kill update (brainwashed Teir`Dal knights and wizards)._
=====================
Clear a path to sealed library 0/7 (Neriak Fourth Gate)
_Task Window Says:_ A small group of undead constructs has gathered to stand watch on the road leading to the library. Return their bones to the dust of the earth to gain safe entrance to the sealed chambers.
_If You Get This Step: Standard kill update -- any undead mobs around the zone-in area count towards the step._
========================================================================
STEP #2
========================================================================
Obtain the library key from an ousted librarian 0/1 (Neriak Fourth Gate)
_Task Window Says:_ ?
_If You Get This Step: Kill ousted librarians until you loot a key (each librarian has two aggro-linked guards)._
=====================
Destroy the crystals that shield the library doors 0/4 (Neriak Fourth Gate)
_Task Window Says:_ A powerful magical force emanates from the library doors. In the distance you spot a pillar of light feeding energy to the sealed chambers. Dispersing this power at its source will surely expedite your entry.
_If You Get This Step: Kill the four resonating crystals in the area. Each has two aggro-linked "a shield \_\_\_\_\_" mobs that hit for a max ~14,000 (stunnable; mezzable; charmable, but very resistant). Note that the focuser is an enchanter and will have buffed both with haste, and will cast mez spells on players. The crystals themselves have a fair amount of hitpoints, but don't attack back._
The door feels hot to the touch and immobile as if held in place by some great force.
_After the crystals are destroyed, you see:_
A brief flash of light fills the cavern as the crystal shatters into tiny fragments.
========================================================================
STEP #3
========================================================================
Attempt to enter the sealed library 0/1 (Neriak Fourth Gate)
_Task Window Says:_ Make your way back to the library door and attempt to open it.
_If You Get This Step: Click on the door (if a key was looted in previous step, the player with the key needs to click). NOTE: Doing this leads to the trigger of three aggro mobs._
========================================================================
STEP #4
========================================================================
Defeat Shirik's ethereal guard 0/12 (Neriak Fourth Gate)
_Task Window Says:_ A hoard of necromantic beasts has come to halt your ingress. It seems Shirik truly does not want anyone interfering with his plans!
_If You Get This Step: After attempting to open the library door, three undead mobs spawn and attack (hit for a max ~12,000; single-target rampage; flurry; stunnable; mezzable). You'll have to kill 12 mobs here. Each time you kill a mob, another one rushes in and attacks, up to a total of 12._
The anguished cries of the dead fill the air as necromantic guardians run through the library doors.
_Note that if you drop aggro, the mobs will stay up and idle._
_WARNING: Right after this step completes, a short script unfolds and then a named becomes aggro in front of the library doors._
========================================================================
STEP #5
========================================================================
Defeat Shirik's constructed guardian 0/1 (Neriak Fourth Gate)
_Task Window Says:_ ?
_If You Get This Step: After the previous step is completed, the gargoyle above the library aggro's and attacks. DPS it to 8% health to update the step._
_It has four stances:_
\- Normal (lasts for first 25 seconds of the fight and does not repeat).
_After the initial 25 seconds passes, the following three stances cycle:_
\- Enraged (lasts for 15 seconds)
_\-\-\- immune to ranged DPS; frequently AE rampages; hasted attacks._
\- Shielded (lasts for 10 seconds)
_\-\-\- rooted in place; susceptible to ranged attacks; for every nuke or DOT cast on it AND for every 2% damage to it, casts directional AEs for 61k dmg (left/right/rear)._
\- Weakened (lasts for 15 seconds)
_\-\-\- becomes unrooted; hasted attack speed._
=====================
Defeat Diran D`Tradis 0/1 (Neriak Fourth Gate)
_Task Window Says:_ Diran D`Tradis, Lark's elder half-brother, has been surveying your attempts to enter his father's fortress and has taken it upon himself to stop you. If saving Shirik means killing his power-crazed heirs, then so be it. Defeat him to gain entrance to the library.
_If You Get This Step: Diran D`Tradis aggro's after a short script completes, following the previous step._
The sound of mockingly slow applause echoes out over the library.
A calm voice speaks in Elder Teir`Dal, 'So much bloodshed for a such misguided cause. Tell me, sister, why put these fools up to this?'
Lark D`Tradis looks up toward the top of the library, 'Brother? W... why has father done all of this?'
Diran D`Tradis glares down at his younger sibling with contempt, 'Always the fool, Songbird. Our dealings are none of your interest or concern. Must I slaughter your misguided allies to get you to leave us, or will cowardice simply overtake you again?'
Lark D`Tradis clenches her fist, choking back tears, 'Enough! You know better than anyone father will be killed for this revolt!'
Diran D`Tradis leaps down from the library's catwalk, landing with surprising grace for a fully armored knight.
Diran D`Tradis grins and calls out to you, 'Stop us, if you can!'
Diran D`Tradis says, in Elder Teir'Dal, 'Make peace with your gods.'
_Diran D`Tradis hits for a max ~21,000; single-target rampages; and flurries. He has a lot of hitpoints and spawns a single add at 76%, 31%, and 6% (locks health until adds are killed). The adds hit for a max ~12,000 and are stunnable. As an add spawns, you see this emote:_
Diran D`Tradis calls fourth a loyal guardian to bolster his defenses. _(sic)_)
Diran`s guardian has been slain by \_\_\_\_\_!
A disembodied voice says, 'Turn back.'
Diran D`Tradis has been slain by \_\_\_\_\_!
Diran D`Tradis's corpse shouts, 'Sister, know well that you have damned our family!'
Lark D`Tradis says, in Dark Elvish, 'I am so sorry, brother...'
========================================================================
STEP #6
========================================================================
Speak with Lark 0/1 (Neriak Fourth Gate)
_Task Window Says:_ Speak with Lark to determine your next move.
_If You Get This Step: Hail Lark to complete the task._
You say, 'Hail, Lark D`Tradis'
Lark D`Tradis does her best to contain her sobbing, 'Brother...' She takes a moment to regain a measure of her composure, 'Though we were never particularly famous, our family is known for two things throughout Neriak, green eyes and mastery of elemental magic. Strange as it may sound, we've never studied necromancy. I fear now that it is not my father we will find within the library but his captors.'
Whatever lies within the sealed library will put an end to Lark's worries, one way or another...

**Call of the Forsaken Info & Guides: Overview \| Progression & Task List \| Raiding \| Visible Armor**
**About Heroic Adventures**
\- Heroic adventures in Call of the Forsaken scale in level: As low as **Level 75** and up to current maximum level.
\- Scale is based on the highest level player **present in the zone** and affects difficulty, loot quality, and mob levels.
\- There is some variation/randomization in the task steps you'll be assigned as you progress through the task.
\- Time of completion will vary by your group's makeup (anywhere from 30-90+ minutes).
\- Collection items appear as ground spawns midway through tasks (you'll see an emote indicating their appearance).
\- Named mobs (or their PHs) spawn midway through tasks (usually, but always, at the same time collection items appear).
\- These tasks have individual **3-hour lockout timers** (timer gained upon completion).
**Experience in Heroic Adventures**
\- Mobs in these instances grant some experience, but the bulk of experience is **earned upon task completion**.
\- As of a patch in November 2014, less difficult tasks were tuned to reward less experience and more difficult ones were tuned to reward more.
\- For an added experience bonus, check out **Guard Hamarn's "Daily Tasks"** [quest=7988].

---

_This is a heroic adventure (1-6 players) that begins with Lark D`Tradis ( **click for map image**) in Neriak Fourth Gate. She is located at 600, -1800, 67 (outside the tunnel due north of the bridge in the far northeastern part of the zone)._
_Pre-Requisites for Requesting:_
\- Level 75
\- Completed "The Toll Road" (heroic adventure)
\- Completed "The Sealed Gate" (heroic adventure)

---

**TASK SUMMARY**
\- Say "continue" to request the task and "ready to go" to zone in.
**Step #1:**
\- Investigate the first floor of the library 0/1
\-\-\- Location update.
**Step #2:**
\- Defeat the Arcanist's guardians 0/12
\-\-\- Kill the dark elves and ghosts around the room.
**\- The task LOCKS at this point.**
**Step #3:**
\- Defeat Shirik's Corrupted Guardian 0/1
\-\-\- Kill Shirik's Corrupted Guardian.
**\- At this point in the task, collectibles spawn in the zone (ground spawns) and a named (or its PH) has a chance to spawn.**
**Step #4:**
\- Clear a path to the upper floor 0/8
\-\-\- Kill update.
**Step #5:**
\- Look for Shirik D`Tradis 0/1
\-\-\- Location update and script trigger.
**Step #6:**
\- Defeat Rylit, Tarant`s Arcanist 0/1
\-\-\- Five ghosts; DPS 10% of each one's health at which point it will reveal itself as either 'fake' or 'real' (repeat x5 until 'real one' is dead).
**Step #7:**
\- Speak with Lark 0/1
\-\-\- Hail update.
\- As soon as the objective for Step #7 is done, the task completes.

---

**NAMED MOBS**
_"Bloody Tome" (or its PH, "a hatestorm dervish") has a chance to spawn after the third step is completed. Known loot from this named:_
Guardian Ornament
Longbow of the Royal Court

---

**TASK IN DETAIL**
========================================================================
TRIGGERING THE TASK
========================================================================
You say, 'Hail, Lark D`Tradis'
Lark D`Tradis says, in Dark Elvish, 'I'm no longer convinced these traps and obstacles are my father's doing, all of this is unlike him. I fear someone or something is controlling him and my brothers. I just hope we can get to him in time. If you are ready to [continue] I'm sure the end to this journey lies in that library.'
You say, 'Let's continue...'
Lark D`Tradis says, in Dark Elvish, 'Excellent, are you [ready to go] now?'
You have been assigned the task 'Late Checkout'.
Lark no longer fears that her father aims to bring about his own destruction. She is convinced someone or something is manipulating him and her family. Whatever it may be, the answer rests within the sealed library.
You say, 'Ready to go!'
Lark D`Tradis says, in Dark Elvish, 'I shall meet you on the road. I believe it was this way...'
LOADING, PLEASE WAIT...
You have entered Neriak - Fourth Gate.

---

3. Investigate the first floor of the library 0/1 (Neriak Fourth Gate)





> _Task Window Says:_ Though the flames at the entrance of the library have dissipated, the first floor is uncomfortably warm. However, the end of this story stands in the center of the room.



_If You Get This Step: This is a location update._



a Teir`Dal arcanist says, in Elder Teir'Dal, 'So the songbird returns to the nest...'




Lark D`Tradis shouts, in Dark Elvish, 'What have you done to my father!?'




a Teir`Dal arcanist says, in Elder Teir'Dal, 'The old man has proved invaluable to my master. So easily drawing away the attention of the divided houses while we work.'




Lark D`Tradis shouts, in Dark Elvish, 'Answer me!'




a Teir`Dal arcanist says, in Elder Teir'Dal, 'We are not quite done with him, but your mercenaries are proving to be quite a distraction.'




The wizard raises his hands in the air and the hall begins to shake.




A great shockwave throws you backwards against the wall.




_Everyone is warped back to the zone-in and feigned death._

4. Defeat the Arcanist's guardians 0/12 (Neriak Fourth Gate)





> _Task Window Says:_ The wizard who has been manipulating Shirik's gargoyle has shielded himself from intrusion. Defeat his guardians to destroy the shield preventing you from entering the center of the room.



_If You Get This Step: Kill the dark elves and ghosts in the room (nothing particularly special about them; the dark elves are a bit harder than the ghosts)._



a Teir`Dal arcanist shouts, in Elder Teir'Dal, 'Fight all you want, you'll never save him!'




As the final guardian falls the shield protecting the center of the room dissipates.




a Teir`Dal arcanist shouts, in Elder Teir'Dal, 'Very well, Songbird. Let us see which of your father's creations shall survive this confrontation!'

5. Defeat Shirik's Corrupted Guardian 0/1 (Neriak Fourth Gate)





> _Task Window Says:_ The powerful gargoyle has nowhere to run. Defeat it once and for all!



_If You Get This Step: Kill Shirik`s Corrupted Guardian._



_As with the previous heroic adventure, this corrupted guardian has the same four stances:_



\- Normal (lasts for first 25 seconds of the fight and does not repeat).




_After the initial 25 seconds passes, the following three stances cycle:_



\- Enraged (lasts for 15 seconds)


_\-\-\- immune to ranged DPS; frequently AE rampages; hasted attacks._



\- Shielded (lasts for 10 seconds)


_\-\-\- rooted in place; susceptible to ranged attacks; for every nuke or DOT cast on it (including weapon procs) AND for every 2% damage to it, casts directional AEs for 61k dmg (left/right/rear)._



\- Weakened (lasts for 15 seconds)


_\-\-\- becomes unrooted; hasted attack speed._



_NOTE: Each time Lark D`Tradis is hit by one of these retaliation AEs, the Corrupted Guardian gains 10% mitigation to spell and melee attacks (up to a maximum 50%)._



_It hits for a max ~22,000; single-target rampages; and flurries. Note that this mob does not reset, so if you wipe out, you'll have to re-engage it from 100% in its current stance and with its current mitigations. You'll see emotes as the fight goes on:_



**Shirik`s Guardian (Shielding) retaliates to \_\_\_\_\_'s spell.**

\-\-\- _(response to a nuke, DOT, or proc in shielded form)_



**Shirik's Guardian retaliates to your continued assault.**

\-\-\- _(response to 2% damage in shielded form)_



**Lark D`Tradis winces as the gargoyle's breath surrounds her.**

\-\-\- _(Guardian gains 10% damage mitigation)_



**Shirik's Guardian gains a measure of Lark's power.**

\-\-\- _(Guardian gains 10% damage mitigation)_



**Shirik's Guardian glows red with rage!**

\-\-\- _(Guardian enters enraged form)_



**Shirik's Guardian surrounds itself with a powerful shield.**

\-\-\- _(Guardian enters shielded form)_



**Shirik's Guardian staggers, momentarily weakened.**

\-\-\- _(Guardian enters weakened form)_

6. Clear a path to the upper floor 0/8 (Neriak Fourth Gate)





> _Task Window Says:_ ?



_If You Get This Step: Standard kill updates._

7. Look for Shirik D`Tradis 0/1 (Neriak Fourth Gate)





> _Task Window Says:_ ?



_If You Get This Step: This is a location update and triggers a script._

8. Defeat Rylit, Tarant`s Arcanist 0/1 (Neriak Fourth Gate)





> _Task Window Says:_ ?



_If You Get This Step: Five ghosts; DPS 10% of each one's health at which point it will reveal itself as either 'fake' or 'real' (repeat x5 until 'real one' is dead)._



_Note: One of the AE effects here has an Alcohol Tolerance check (unknown level required to prevent drunkedness)._



The Rylit, Tarant`s Arcanist casts "Unease" (slows spell casting), "Misery" (30k DOT and drunk if Alcohol Tolerance is below 330), "Neglect" (silence on melee skills), "Turmoil" (prevents detrimental casting), and "Agitation" (melee slow and snare). These are spell IDs 42016-42020.




These all last 3 ticks, and they are all targeted AEs (20'), so your group can mitigate problems if you spread out from each other. The drunk effect of "Misery" lasts MUCH longer than 3 ticks, so the further below the 330 checkpoint you are, the more ...miserable... a fight you'll be in for.




His projections (ghosts) can also cast these effects, but do so much less frequently.




_Five ghosts of the arcanist: DPS them one at a time to 90%. As each reaches 90%, it reveals itself as a fake target or a real target. If it's a fake target, that single ghost despawns. If it's a real target, then all the ghosts despawn and the arcanist spawns in its place. DPS the arcanist to 75% to respawn the five ghosts._



_Repeat this process again to 75% (arcanist to 50%), then to 50% (arcanist to 25%), then to 25% (arcanist to 0%), then to its death._



_All ghosts cast random targeted AEs (you'll have one AE going off every 10 seconds). The arcanist also casts random targeted AEs (two casts at a time every 30 seconds AND one cast for every 4% health lost)._

9. Speak with Lark 0/1 (Neriak Fourth Gate)





> _Task Window Says:_ ?



_If You Get This Step: Hail Lark to complete the task._

**Call of the Forsaken Info & Guides: Overview \| Progression & Task List \| Raiding \| Visible Armor**
**About Heroic Adventures**
\- Heroic adventures in Call of the Forsaken scale in level: As low as **Level 75** and up to current maximum level.
\- Scale is based on the highest level player **present in the zone** and affects difficulty, loot quality, and mob levels.
\- There is some variation/randomization in the task steps you'll be assigned as you progress through the task.
\- Time of completion will vary by your group's makeup (anywhere from 30-90+ minutes).
\- Collection items appear as ground spawns midway through tasks (you'll see an emote indicating their appearance).
\- Named mobs (or their PHs) spawn midway through tasks (usually, but always, at the same time collection items appear).
\- These tasks have individual **3-hour lockout timers** (timer gained upon completion).
**Experience in Heroic Adventures**
\- Mobs in these instances grant some experience, but the bulk of experience is **earned upon task completion**.
\- As of a patch in November 2014, less difficult tasks were tuned to reward less experience and more difficult ones were tuned to reward more.
\- For an added experience bonus, check out **Guard Hamarn's "Daily Tasks"** [quest=7988].

---

_This is a heroic adventure (1-6 players) that begins with Rozz T`Ver ( **click for map image**) in Neriak Fourth Gate._
_Pre-Requisites for Requesting:_
\- Level 75
\- No tasks

---

**TASK SUMMARY**
\- Say "recover" to request the task and "begin" to zone in.
**Step #1:**
\- Speak to Rozz T'Ver just outside the palace entrance 0/1
\-\-\- Hail update.
**Step #2:**
\- Gather reports from the queen's spies within the palace 0/3
\-\-\- Kill guards around "a nervous guest" NPCs, then hail the guests.
\- Mingle with chatty nobles and gossiping guests 0/2
\-\-\- Get near gossiping or chatty NPCs while under the effects of "Fine Attire" (to aquire: Kill guards; loot "Ornate Bone Key", used to open "an ornate chest" that are found around the palace).
\- Loot journals from guests and nobles within the palace 0/4
\-\-\- Kill palace guests and visiting nobles until you've looted 4x "Bound Journal", then right-click one of the journals.
**Step #3:**
\- Bribe a member of the Dreadguard 0/1
\-\-\- Kill palace guests and visiting nobles; loot "Heavy Sack of Gold", used to bribe Dragoons (will either update task, or attack).
\- Beat information out of a Dragoon 0/1
\-\-\- Kill Dragoons until you find one that locks at 50% health (will update step).
\- Get a Dragoon drunk to reveal information 0/1
\-\-\- Kill inebriated nobles and tipsy guests; loot "Ruby Port", used to get a guard drunk (will either take it an update task, or attack). Note: tipsy guests appear to be a better source of the ruby port.
**\- At this point in the task, collectibles spawn in the zone (ground spawns) and a named (or its PH) has a chance to spawn.**
**Step #4:**
\- Spy on the king and his advisor 0/1
\-\-\- Location update on the balcony above the king's throne room.
**Step #5:**
\- Rescue the queen's remaining allies 0/4
\-\-\- In a room north of the king's room, you'll find several captive allies; clear it of guards.
\- Kill all the traitors 0/8
\-\-\- Kill 8x "a traitorous Teir`Dal".
\- As soon as the objective for Step #5 is done, the task completes.

Guards stand at attention as Guard Captain N`Mar enters the palace.

**TASK IN DETAIL**
========================================================================
TRIGGERING THE TASK
========================================================================
You say, 'Hail, Rozz T`Ver'
Rozz T`Ver says 'In the aftermath of the construct battle, the king and queen are more at odds than ever. Her majesty has been banished from the palace, and all those loyal to her killed or imprisoned. It's left the queen quite bitter and paranoid. I'm afraid her lust for [revenge] will interfere with the greater goal of winning this war. Without ears and eyes in the palace, the queen cannot gain ground in this ongoing battle. She needs an ally on the inside; one to [recover] information about what the king has planned.'
You say, 'I'll recover some information...'
Rozz T`Ver appears relieved, 'I knew I could count on you, \_\_\_\_\_.' The dark elf scans her surroundings before continuing, 'Meet me outside the palace entrance when you're [ready]. We have much to discuss.'
You have been assigned the task 'The King's Court'.
Explore Darklight Palace and discover information about the king's plans.
You say, 'Ready!'
Rozz T`Ver says 'Make haste, \_\_\_\_\_.'
LOADING, PLEASE WAIT...
You have entered Neriak - Fourth Gate.
========================================================================
STEP #1
========================================================================
Speak to Rozz T'Ver just outside the palace entrance 0/1 (Neriak Fourth Gate)
_Task Window Says:_ Rozz T`Ver is waiting just outside Darklight Palace to the left.
_If You Get This Step: Hail Rozz (just east of the zone-in point)._
You say, 'Hail, Rozz T`Ver'
Rozz T`Ver appears relieved to see you, 'I'll make this brief, since we don't have much time. What allies the Queen has left remain in hiding, fearful for their lives. They must be reached at all costs. Their accounts of palace activities will be crucial in helping the queen get one step ahead of her deplorable husband. You need to ferret out her allies and retrieve their reports.
========================================================================
STEP #2
========================================================================
Gather reports from the queen's spies within the palace 0/3 (Neriak Fourth Gate)
_Task Window Says:_ You need to collect reports from spies within the palace. They may not be so eager to speak with guards around.
_If You Get This Step: Kill guards around NPCs called "a nervous guest", and then hail the guests._
a nervous guest leans in and whispers, 'It's not safe to talk with guards nearby.'
a nervous guest lets out a sigh of relief, 'I was worried the guards might discover my true identity. Many of those loyal to her majesty have been executed or imprisoned. If the rumors are true, some have even bargained for their lives by betraying the queen.'
a nervous guest speaks with urgency, 'My report will be brief, I'm afraid. The guards may be dead, but others will soon take their place. The palace is becoming a dangerous place for those of us aligned with the queen. The king has been meeting with many of his most trusted advisors, often in secret, protected by the Dreadguard.'
a nervous guest glances around nervously before speaking, 'You arrived just in time. Something big is going to happen tonight. The Dreadguard is assembling in the throne room as we speak, likely to provide protection when the king and his advisors meet in secret. It's crucial for the queen to have eyes and ears on this meeting. Finding out its location should be your top priority.'
=====================
Mingle with chatty nobles and gossiping guests 0/2 (Neriak Fourth Gate)
_Task Window Says:_ ?
_If You Get This Step: Kill guards until you loot an "Ornate Bone Key". With this key, open up "an ornate chest" (found around the palace) until you get a hold of "Fine Attire". Then under the effect of Fine Attire, get near gossiping or chatty NPCs until you get an update._

Bribe a member of the Dreadguard 0/1 (Neriak Fourth Gate)
_Task Window Says:_ ?
_If You Get This Step: Kill palace guests and visiting nobles until you a loot "Heavy Sack of Gold". Take this item and hand it to Dragoons. Upon turn-in, they'll either accept it and update the task, or attack you._
=====================
Beat information out of a Dragoon 0/1 (Neriak Fourth Gate)
_Task Window Says:_ ?
_If You Get This Step: Kill Dragoons until you find one that locks at 50% health (will update step)._
=====================
Get a Dragoon drunk to reveal information 0/1 (Neriak Fourth Gate)
_Task Window Says:_ Palace life is a party to some! It's possible that some inebriated guests might have some strong spirits. It'll probably take at least a few drinks to get a guard talking.
_If You Get This Step: Kill inebriated nobles and tipsy guests until you've looted a "Ruby Port". Hand in this item to Dragoons until you find one that accepts it (others will give it back to you and attack). It will take four Ruby Port hand-ins to the same guard before it begins talking._
_Dragoons are non-aggro to begin with. Upon hand-in, if rejected, you'll see something like this (right before it attacks - this particular Dragoon then remains aggro/KOS until it's dead):_
Dragoon N`Mare says 'What is this? Dreadguards don't drink on the job. I'll kill you for your ignorance!'
Dragoon N`Mare says 'This is going to end quickly for you.'
_When you find the Dragoon that accepts them (there will be only one in the zone, and which one is random), you'll see these responses as you hand them in one by one:_
Dragoon D`Jerna glances around to make sure no one is looking, 'One sip wouldn't hurt, right? It's been a long day.'
Dragoon D`Jerna eagerly accepts another glass, 'This really hits the spot. It's so smooth and sweet. I've been working too many shifts, trying to keep up with the king and all his meetings.'
Dragoon D`Jerna slurs a bit while speaking, 'Another glass, you say? Why, I don't mind if I do. It would be a shame to let such a good port go to waste. It'll help pass the time until the latest meeting takes place.'
Dragoon D`Jerna struggles to stand upright, 'I'm not sure my legs work anymore, ha! But It's not like I have to go anywhere, anway! The meeting's taking place right in this very room.' The guard empties the glass in one gulp, 'Thanks for the drink, friend. If you want to witness a boring exchange between the king and his advisor, I suggest you do so from the gallery above.'
_The fourth hand-in updates the step._

========================================================================
Spy on the king and his advisor 0/1 (Neriak Fourth Gate)
_Task Window Says:_ The king will be meeting with his advisor in the throne room. The gallery above should provide a safe spot to eavesdrop.
_If You Get This Step: This is a location update on the balcony above the king's throne room. WARNING: If you get too close, the King will aggro you and summon you to your death._
King Naythox Thex scans the room before speaking, 'Let's make this quick, Rozik. What do you have to report?'
Advisor Rozik D`Vin says 'The prisoners are secured in the parlor, your majesty. They're refusing to talk, for now, but I have ways of making people speak...'
King Naythox Thex grits his teeth, 'They'll spill the queen's secrets or die. It's that simple. Use whatever tactics you can to recover information, no matter how gruesome. We must find out if that conniving wench is now allied with my wife. If the two of them have formed a partnership, our city will fall.'
Advisor Rozik D`Vin nods, 'Understood, your majesty. I'll continue the interrogation at once.'

**Call of the Forsaken Info & Guides: Overview \| Progression & Task List \| Raiding \| Visible Armor**
**About Heroic Adventures**
\- Heroic adventures in Call of the Forsaken scale in level: As low as **Level 75** and up to current maximum level.
\- Scale is based on the highest level player **present in the zone** and affects difficulty, loot quality, and mob levels.
\- There is some variation/randomization in the task steps you'll be assigned as you progress through the task.
\- Time of completion will vary by your group's makeup (anywhere from 30-90+ minutes).
\- Collection items appear as ground spawns midway through tasks (you'll see an emote indicating their appearance).
\- Named mobs (or their PHs) spawn midway through tasks (usually, but always, at the same time collection items appear).
\- These tasks have individual **3-hour lockout timers** (timer gained upon completion).
**Experience in Heroic Adventures**
\- Mobs in these instances grant some experience, but the bulk of experience is **earned upon task completion**.
\- As of a patch in November 2014, less difficult tasks were tuned to reward less experience and more difficult ones were tuned to reward more.
\- For an added experience bonus, check out **Guard Hamarn's "Daily Tasks"** [quest=7988].

---

_This is a heroic adventure (1-6 players) that begins with Rozz T`Ver ( **click for map image**) in Neriak Fourth Gate._
_Pre-Requisites for Requesting:_
\- Level 75
\- No tasks

---

**TASK SUMMARY**
\- Say "revenge" to request the task and "begin" to zone in.
**Step #1:**
\- Tear down portraits of the king 0/5
\-\-\- Clear guards away from the paintings, then click on the paintings.
\- Destroy statues of the king 0/3
\-\-\- Clear guards from the statues, then DPS the statues for the update.
\- Tear down warrior banners within the palace 0/5
\-\-\- These are the blue banners around the palace; clear guards from them, then click for the update.
**Step #2:**
\- Kill all palace guests, courtiers, and nobles 0/12
\-\-\- Kill update: "a palace guest"; "a palace courtier; "a visiting noble".
\- Poison dinner guests by tainting their food 0/6
\-\-\- Kill diseased rats until you've looted 6x "Rancid Rat Meat", then use the meat in the dining area.
**\- At this point in the task, collectibles spawn in the zone (ground spawns) and a named (or its PH) has a chance to spawn.**
**Step #3:**
\- Slaughter guards near the king's bedchambers 0/6
\-\-\- Kill Dragoons in or around the king's bedroom chambers.
**Step #4:**
\- Pour vials of acid into the king's bathing pool 0/4
\-\-\- Kill "a royal apothecary" until you've looted 4x "Vial of Effusive Acid", then use the acid in the bedroom's pool.
\- Place poisonous spider egg sacs within the king's bedchambers 0/6
\-\-\- Kill "a darklight widow" until you've looted 6x "Spider Egg Sac", then use the sacs in the bed chambers.
\- Set the king's bedchamber on fire 0/1
\-\-\- Kill "a fire elemental" until you've looted 1x "Elemental Ember", then use it in the bed chambers.
\- As soon as the objective for Step #4 is done, the task completes.

---

**MAP**
!

---

**NAMED MOBS**
_Dragoon Torrex (or its PH, "Guard V`Rett") spawns after the second step is completed. Known loot from this named:_
Bracer of Latent Ether
Braided Leather Cord
Buckler of the Turncoat's Anguish
Etched Gold Girdle

**TASK IN DETAIL**
========================================================================
TRIGGERING THE TASK
========================================================================
You say, 'Hail, Rozz T`Ver'
Rozz T`Ver says 'In the aftermath of the construct battle, the king and queen are more at odds than ever. Her majesty has been banished from the palace, and all those loyal to her killed or imprisoned. It's left the queen quite bitter and paranoid. I'm afraid her lust for [revenge] will interfere with the greater goal of winning this war. Without ears and eyes in the palace, the queen cannot gain ground in this ongoing battle. She needs an ally on the inside; one to [recover] information about what the king has planned.'
You say, 'Revenge?'
Rozz T`Ver says 'If you could enter the palace and cause complete chaos, your destruction might satiate the queen's hunger for revenge, allowing her to focus on more pressing issues, like her husband's devious plans. If you're willing to wreak havoc within the palace, then you should [begin] at once.'
You have been assigned the task 'Hate Hath No Fury'.
Enter Darklight Palace and cause chaos and destruction, in the name of the queen.
You say, 'Let's begin!'
Rozz T`Ver says 'Make haste, \_\_\_\_\_.'
========================================================================
STEP #1
========================================================================
Tear down portraits of the king 0/5 (Neriak Fourth Gate)
_Task Window Says:_ Tear down paintings of the king hung on palace walls, but beware of guards!
_If You Get This Step: Clear guards away from the paintings, then click on the paintings. Note that guards are mezzable, but immune to stuns, roots, and snares._
=====================
Destroy statues of the king 0/3 (Neriak Fourth Gate)
_Task Window Says:_ Attack statues of the king around the palace to destroy them, but beware of guards!
_If You Get This Step: Clear guards from the statues, then DPS the statues for the update. Guards are mezzable, but immune to stuns, roots, and snares. If you attack a statue while guards around it are still alive, you see:_
Your actions alert the nearby guards!
_Note: The "statues" have odd aggro, and if they drop off aggro, they immediately regenerate to 100% health. It seems you can usually avoid this from happening if you stand in very close proximity range of it. (For mechanics purposes, the NPC you are attacking is actually a nameless invisible man in front of the statue.)_
=====================
Tear down warrior banners within the palace 0/5 (Neriak Fourth Gate)
_Task Window Says:_ Tear down blue warrior banners hung from the palace walls, but beware of guards!
_If You Get This Step: These are the blue banners around the palace; clear guards from them, then click for the update. Note that guards are mezzable, but immune to stuns, roots, and snares. If you click a banner while guards are still nearby, they aggro you._
========================================================================
STEP #2
========================================================================
Kill all palace guests, courtiers, and nobles 0/12 (Neriak Fourth Gate)
_Task Window Says:_ ?
_If You Get This Step: Kill update: "a palace guest"; "a palace courtier; "a visiting noble"._
=====================
Poison dinner guests by tainting their food 0/6 (Neriak Fourth Gate)
_Task Window Says:_ Place rancid rat meat on the table within the dining area and watch as dinner guests dine to their deaths! Rancid meat can be found on diseased rats that roam outside in front of the palace.
_If You Get This Step: Kill diseased rats until you've looted 6x "Rancid Rat Meat", then use the meat in the dining area._
a dinner guest calls out to the other hungry guests, 'It's time to dine, friends! Look at this lovely meal the palace servant has brought us.'
A dinner guest takes a small bite of the rancid meat and begins to convulse before dropping lifelessly to the floor.
a dinner guest died.
========================================================================
STEP #3
========================================================================
Slaughter guards near the king's bedchambers 0/6 (Neriak Fourth Gate)
_Task Window Says:_ Kill all the king's guards that linger near his bedchambers.
_If You Get This Step: Kill Dragoons in or around the king's bedroom chambers. These mobs hit for a max ~13,000, and are stunnable and mezzable, but not rootable/snareable. Note that a named (Dragoon Torex) has a chance to spawn as a roamer in the middle of all the Dragoon spawns here. This mob is considerably more difficult than trash mobs and does not count towards this step._
========================================================================
STEP #4
========================================================================
Pour vials of acid into the king's bathing pool 0/4 (Neriak Fourth Gate)
_Task Window Says:_ An acid bath is just what the king needs! Only royal apothecaries handle such corrosive substances.
_If You Get This Step: Kill "a royal apothecary" until you've looted 4x "Vial of Effusive Acid", then use the acid in the bedroom's pool to complete the task. These mobs hit for a max ~13,000._
_(Task completes here; there is no completion text.)_
=====================
Place poisonous spider egg sacs within the king's bedchambers 0/6 (Neriak Fourth Gate)
_Task Window Says:_ ?
_If You Get This Step: Kill "a darklight widow" that roam outside in front of the palace. until you've looted 6x "Spider Egg Sac", then use the sacs in the bed chambers to complete the task._
_(Task completes here; there is no completion text.)_
=====================
Set the king's bedchamber on fire 0/1 (Neriak Fourth Gate)
_Task Window Says:_ ?
_If You Get This Step: Kill "a fire elemental" until you've looted 1x "Elemental Ember", then use it in the bed chambers to complete the task._
_(Task completes here; there is no completion text.)_

_This is a heroic adventure (1-6 players) that begins with Rozz T`Ver ( **click for map image**) in Neriak Fourth Gate._
_Pre-Requisites for Requesting:_
\- Level 75
\- Completed "The King's Court" (heroic adventure)

---

**TASK SUMMARY**
\- Say "intervene" to request the task and "ready" to zone in.
**Step #1:**
\- Speak to Rozz T`Ver just outside the palace barracks 0/1
\-\-\- Hail update (response depends on what step follows).
**Step #2:**
\- Slaughter all the sleeping guards 0/12
\-\-\- Kill update.
\- Obtain attack plans Dreadguard captains 0/4
\-\-\- Loot 4x "Dreadguard Attack Plans" from the captains (each is in the side rooms and aggro-linked with nearby guards).
\- Disarm the Dreadguard 0/8
\-\-\- Loot 8x "Sharpened Two-Handed Sword" (50% drop rate from the Dragoons).
**Step #3:**
\- Report back to Rozz T`Ver just outside the palace barracks 0/1
\-\-\- Hail update.
**Step #4:**
\- Find and use the key to the queen's bedchamber 0/1
\-\-\- Kill guards until you loot "Queen's Bedchamber Key", then use the key to unlock the doors.
\- Pick the lock on the queen's bedchamber door 0/1
\-\-\- Kill guards until you loot "Lock Picking Kit", then use the kit to unlock the doors.
\- Break down the queen's bedchamber door 0/1
\-\-\- Click the doors to the queen's chambers to spawn adds; kill four waves (2, then 2, then 2, then 3).
**\- At this point in the task, collectibles spawn in the zone (ground spawns) and a named (or its PH) has a chance to spawn.**
**Step #5:**
\- Find the queen's escape plan hidden within her bedchamber 0/1
\-\-\- Click on the painting at -1715, -1185, 115 to receive "Small Brass Key" or "Folded Note".
\- Find the queen's escape plan hidden within her bedchamber 0/1
\-\-\- Click on the bookshelf to receive "Small Brass Key" or "Folded Note".
\- Find the queen's escape plan hidden within her bedchamber 0/1
\-\-\- Open the ornate chest and loot "Small Brass Key" or "Folded Note".
**Step #6:**
\- Find and unlock the secret escape route after the parlor has been cleared of guards 0/1
\-\-\- Kill the 12 guards, then click on the bookshelf at /loc -1210, -700, 45.
\- Create a portal to allow passage into the palace 0/1
\-\-\- Kill dark elves and stonetalons until you've looted 1x "Dark Elf Blood" and 3x "Enchanted Stone"; right-click the stones in the bedroom; then right-click the blood; then click the portal to port back to Rozz T`Ver.
\- As soon as the objective for Step #6 is done, the task completes.

**Call of the Forsaken Info & Guides: Overview \| Progression & Task List \| Raiding \| Visible Armor**
**About Heroic Adventures**
\- Heroic adventures in Call of the Forsaken scale in level: As low as **Level 75** and up to current maximum level.
\- Scale is based on the highest level player **present in the zone** and affects difficulty, loot quality, and mob levels.
\- There is some variation/randomization in the task steps you'll be assigned as you progress through the task.
\- Time of completion will vary by your group's makeup (anywhere from 30-90+ minutes).
\- Collection items appear as ground spawns midway through tasks (you'll see an emote indicating their appearance).
\- Named mobs (or their PHs) spawn midway through tasks (usually, but always, at the same time collection items appear).
\- These tasks have individual **3-hour lockout timers** (timer gained upon completion).
**Experience in Heroic Adventures**
\- Mobs in these instances grant some experience, but the bulk of experience is **earned upon task completion**.
\- As of a patch in November 2014, less difficult tasks were tuned to reward less experience and more difficult ones were tuned to reward more.
\- For an added experience bonus, check out **Guard Hamarn's "Daily Tasks"** [quest=7988].

---

_This is a heroic adventure (1-6 players) that begins with Marla Gaslow ( **click for map image**) in the Ethernere Tainted West Karana. She is located in the guard camp where the Guild Hall portal sends you (/loc -1000, 345, -315)._
_Pre-Requisites for Requesting:_
\- Level 75
\- No tasks

---

**TASK SUMMARY**
\- Say "help" to request the task and "ready" to zone in.
**Step #1:**
\- Speak to Fredrick Gaslow just outside the burning village 0/1
\-\-\- Hail update.
**Step #2:**
\- Use water to put out the fire that is blocking access to the farm 0/8
\-\-\- Kill water mephits and loot "Water Sphere", then right-click it on the farm fires.
\- Use dirt to put out the fire that is blocking access to the farm 0/8
\-\-\- Kill mudsouls and mudspirits and loot "Clump of Dirt"; right-click item on farm fires.
**Step #3:**
\- Rescue people from within the burning structures 0/5
\-\-\- Kill the fire mephits around the NPCs in the area.
\- Rescue animals around the farmland 0/5
\-\-\- Kill the fire mephits around the horses in the area.
\- Salvage the crops by extinguishing burning scarecrows 0/14
\-\-\- Kill update (scarecrow mobs).
**Step #4:**
\- Return to Fredrick Gaslow 0/1
\-\-\- Hail update.
**\- At this point in the task, collectibles spawn in the zone (ground spawns) and a named (or its PH) has a chance to spawn.**
**Step #5:**
\- Recover food from bandits 0/6
\-\-\- Loot "Loaf of Bread" from bandits (50% drop rate).
\- Recover supplies from bandits 0/6
\-\-\- Loot "Crate of Supplies" from bandits (50% drop rate).
\- Recover clean water from bandits 0/6
\-\-\- Loot "Fresh Water" from bandits (50% drop rate).
**Step #6:**
\- Return to Fredrick Gaslow 0/1
\-\-\- Hail update.
**Step #7:**
\- Kill burning treants to prevent the fire from spreading 0/10
\-\-\- Kill update.
\- Kill fire beetles to prevent the fire from spreading 0/10
\-\-\- Kill update.
\- Kill scorched stumps to prevent the fire from spreading 0/10
\-\-\- Kill update.
**Step #8:**
\- Speak with Fredrick Gaslow once again 0/1
\-\-\- Hail update.
\- As soon as the objective for Step #8 is done, the task completes.

**TASK IN DETAIL**
**========================================================================**
**TRIGGERING THE TASK**
**========================================================================**
You say, 'Hail, Marla Gaslow'
Marla Gaslow remains silent and still for a moment, before blinking away the confusion. 'Please excuse my rudeness. I'm so used to being ignored that I forgot my manners.' The quaint farmer bows before you in introduction, 'My name's Marla. I come from a small farming and fishing village to the south, well, what's left of it. We've been hit real hard by recent [events].'
You say, 'What about those events?'
Marla Gaslow says 'Our land has suffered since that creature decided to make our plains her home. The nearby mountainside rises and falls on a whim, pouring lava into the streams and farmlands that once helped feed our families. It's a disaster! A disaster we can't survive much longer without some [assistance].'
You say, 'Assistance?'
Marla Gaslow says 'I've been to every tent and spoken with every guard. Most of them ignored me completely, while others acknowledged my presence long enough to assure me that there were more pressing matters than assisting a small village that no one's ever heard of.' The farmer sighs. 'We're desperate, \_\_\_\_\_. Someone needs to [help].'
You say, 'I'll help!'
Marla Gaslow takes your hand gently in hers with a warm smile, 'Thank you, \_\_\_\_\_. Thank you from the bottom of my heart. I'll show you the way to the village when you are [ready] to leave.'
You have been assigned the task 'A Helping Hand'.
Marla Gaslow comes from a small fishing and farming village to the south that is in peril due to the recent cataclysmic events. She has pleaded with you to find her husband and help save the village.
You say, 'Ready!'
Marla Gaslow says 'This way, \_\_\_\_\_.'

You say, 'Hail, Fredrick Gaslow'
Fredrick Gaslow sighs with relief at your arrival, 'You must have been sent by my wife. Thank goodness she found help. I was trying to evacuate people from the farm, but the fires kept spreading. Now there's a giant wall of flames between me and the village. You have to help put it out! We have people trapped inside their burning homes.

Fredrick Gaslow cries for the loss of his village, 'It's painful to watch our homes go up in flames, but at least with your help not all was lost. I'll need to tend to those who are injured, but it's going to be tough without supplies. Our burning village made an easy target for bandits. While we were trying to get everyone to safety, those honorless thugs ransacked our homes and ran off to their camps in the east. How are we supposed to fight back? We're farmers and fishers.' Fredrick looks up at you with hope in his eyes, 'You, though, you could show those thugs a thing or two.'

**TASK IN DETAIL**
========================================================================
TRIGGERING THE TASK
========================================================================
You say, 'Hail, Marla Gaslow'
Marla Gaslow says 'I heard that the village nearly burned to the ground. Is it true?' The farmer fights back tears as she speaks, 'At least not all was lost. Lives and crops were spared; I guess I should be thankful for that, but where will our families go? Surely things will get worse before they get better. We need [shelter] if we plan to survive.'
You say, 'Shelter?'
Marla Gaslow says 'There's a camp run by the McMannus family, just west of our village. They've managed to salvage much of their shelters and parts of their streams still run with clean, cool water. The survivors from my village need to seek refuge in their camp, but the McMannus family is weary of outsiders. We're simple farmers who lack the skills of negotiation. You, on the other hand, you've been places and seen things! I can tell by that sparkle of adventure in your eye. Please, \_\_\_\_\_, [help] us secure refuge in the McMannus' camp? I know this is something you can accomplish.'
You say, 'We will help!'
Marla Gaslow takes your hand gently in hers with a warm smile, 'Thank you, \_\_\_\_\_. Thank you from the bottom of my heart. I'll show you the way to the village when you are [ready] to leave.'
You have been assigned the task 'Seeking Refuge'.
Marla Gaslow has asked that you help the displaced people of her village find refuge in the McMannus camp to the south.
You say, 'Ready!'
Marla Gaslow says 'This way, \_\_\_\_\_.'

========================================================================
Seek out Einhorst McMannus and secure refuge for the villagers 0/1 (Ethernere Tainted West Karana)
_Task Window Says:_ Einhorst is the leader of the McMannus clan. You'll need to speak with him about seeking refuge for the displaced villagers.
_If You Get This Step: Einhorst McMannus is inside the western hut in the McMannus camp._
You say, 'Hail, Einhorst McMannus'
Einhorst McMannus says 'You reek of smoke and despair, traveler. I don't suppose you ventured here from our neighboring village, the one that just burned to the ground? If you've come seeking [refuge], I'm afraid you won't find it here.''
You say, 'They want refuge'
Einhorst McMannus says 'Take a look around. This camp is made up of clan McMannus. We're strong, hardworking, and never accept defeat. This is why we are still here, and others have perished. The weak will not prosper within our walls.' The toughened barbarian looks you over with new interest, 'But you're not one of the weak ones, are you \_\_\_\_\_? I think there is more to you than your appearance lets on. If you can prove yourself useful, then I will see about letting you and your villagers into our camp, but you'll have to impress my family as well. Each of them will have tasks for you. Earn their respect, and you will earn mine.'
========================================================================

You say, 'Hail, Hefholm McMannus'
Hefholm McMannus says 'Greetings, \_\_\_\_\_. Einhorst said I'd be paid a visit by someone looking for refuge in our camp, our home that was built by the McMannus family. It may not look like much now, but we will rebuild. If you want a place within our walls, you will need to aid our efforts. Our shelters are constructed with wood and thick mud, that hardens like rock when dry. Show that you have the strength and skill to gather supplies, and you will earn my respect.'
Hefholm McMannus says 'This camp was built by my family. Our blood, sweat, and strength have kept us alive. We may have lost a great deal during recent events, but we will rebuild and be stronger than ever.'

You say, 'Hail, Sharhessa McMannus'
Sharhessa McMannus wipes her hands off before offering one up in greeting, 'Nice to meet you, \_\_\_\_\_. Einhorst mentioned you're trying to find refuge for some villagers. I hate turning folks away, but we already have too many mouths to feed. You wouldn't believe how much some of the McMannus men can eat! I suppose if you could prove to me that you can hunt and gather for yourself, I'd be willing to give my blessing.

=====================

_If You Get This Step: Loot 8x "Bear Meat" from bears. These are fairly weak mobs, hitting for a max ~4,500 with fewer hitpoints than typical mobs here._
=====================
Collect lion meat to help feed the refugees 0/8 (Ethernere Tainted West Karana)
_Task Window Says:_ ?
_If You Get This Step: Loot 8x "Lion Meat" from lions._
========================================================================
STEP #6
========================================================================
Speak to Sahen McMannus 0/1 (Ethernere Tainted West Karana)
_Task Window Says:_ (nothing)
_If You Get This Step: Speak with Sahen McMannus to update the step. She's found in the southwestern corner of the main McMannus island._
You say, 'Hail, Sahen McMannus'
Sahen McMannus says 'Hail, \_\_\_\_\_. Let's make this quick. I need to focus my attention on the fields and mountains that surround our camp. We face many threats out here, but the Harrowing Horde are the worst. They suck the life out of the soil, leaving empty wasteland in their wake. I won't let you and your villagers into this camp unless I know for certain that you're strong enough to protect them, and yourself. Kill Harrowing Horde creatures, and you will have my blessing to stay within the camp.'
========================================================================
STEP #7
========================================================================
Kill Doomscale cultists that threaten the McMannus' camp 0/14 (Ethernere Tainted West Karana)
_Task Window Says:_ ?
_If You Get This Step: Standard kill update._
=====================
Kill Harrowing Horde creatures threaten the McMannus' camp 0/14 (Ethernere Tainted West Karana)
_Task Window Says:_ The Harrowing Horde tend to gather in small groups north of the McMannus camp.
_If You Get This Step: Standard kill update (skirths, tiruns, orafiks, geheins)._
=====================
Kill undead that threaten the McMannus' camp 0/14 (Ethernere Tainted West Karana)
_Task Window Says:_ ?
_If You Get This Step: Standard kill update (skeletons, ghouls, flesh eaters)._
========================================================================
STEP #8
========================================================================
Return to Einhorst McMannus 0/1 (Ethernere Tainted West Karana)
_Task Window Says:_ Return to Einhorst McMannus to see if you will be allowed refuge in his camp.
_If You Get This Step: Speak with Einhorst McMannus to complete the task._
You say, 'Hail, Einhorst McMannus'
Einhorst McMannus says 'I've spoken with my family members, and it appears you won't be a burden on our camp. However, the McMannus family will not be responsible for your guests. You will be held accountable for their actions, and I trust that you will ensure they are fed and otherwise cared for. All that being said, welcome to our family.'

**Call of the Forsaken Info & Guides: Overview \| Progression & Task List \| Raiding \| Visible Armor**
**About Heroic Adventures**
\- Heroic adventures in Call of the Forsaken scale in level: As low as **Level 75** and up to current maximum level.
\- Scale is based on the highest level player **present in the zone** and affects difficulty, loot quality, and mob levels.
\- There is some variation/randomization in the task steps you'll be assigned as you progress through the task.
\- Time of completion will vary by your group's makeup (anywhere from 30-90+ minutes).
\- Collection items appear as ground spawns midway through tasks (you'll see an emote indicating their appearance).
\- Named mobs (or their PHs) spawn midway through tasks (usually, but always, at the same time collection items appear).
\- These tasks have individual **3-hour lockout timers** (timer gained upon completion).
**Experience in Heroic Adventures**
\- Mobs in these instances grant some experience, but the bulk of experience is **earned upon task completion**.
\- As of a patch in November 2014, less difficult tasks were tuned to reward less experience and more difficult ones were tuned to reward more.
\- For an added experience bonus, check out **Guard Hamarn's "Daily Tasks"** [quest=7988].

---

_This is a heroic adventure (1-6 players) that begins with Marla Gaslow ( **click for map image**) in the Ethernere Tainted West Karana. She is located in the guard camp where the Guild Hall portal sends you (/loc -1000, 345, -315)._
_Pre-Requisites for Requesting:_
\- Level 75
\- Completed "A Helping Hand" (heroic adventure)
\- Completed "Seeking Refuge" (heroic adventure)

---

**TASK SUMMARY**
\- Say "help" to request the task and "ready" to zone in.
**Step #1:**
\- Speak to Einhorst McMannus 0/1
\-\-\- Hail update.
**Step #2:**
\- Kill the Harrowing Horde that block the path between the village and the camp 0/14
\-\-\- Kill update (creatures of the Ethernere).
\- Kill the undead that block the path between the village and the camp 0/14
\-\-\- Kill update (undead).
\- Kill the bandits that block the path between the village and the camp 0/14
\-\-\- Kill update (bandits).
**Step #3:**
\- Speak with Fredrick Gaslow 0/1
\-\-\- Hail update.
**Step #4:**
\- Kill the fire mephits that surround Ron Miller 0/12
\-\-\- Kill update.
\- Recover the villager's missing possessions from traitorous guards 0/4
\-\-\- Kill traitorous guards and loot different items until you've gotten four updates.
\- Heal the injured villagers using vibrant leaves from the nearby treants 0/4
\-\-\- Kill treants and loot 4x "Vibrant Leaf"; right-click the leaf on injured villagers.
**Step #5:**
\- Speak with Fredrick Gaslow 0/1
\-\-\- Hail update.
**\- The task LOCKS at this point.**
**\- At this point in the task, collectibles spawn in the zone (ground spawns) and a named (or its PH) has a chance to spawn.**
**Step #6:**
\- Ensure the path is clear so the villagers can reach the McMannus camp safely 0/1
\-\-\- Kill four waves of mobs (three mobs per wave that spawn as ambushes).
**Step #7:**
\- Inform Einhorst McMannus of the refugee's arrival 0/1
\-\-\- Hail update.
\- As soon as the objective for Step #7 is done, the task completes.

**About Heroic Adventures**
\- Heroic adventures in Call of the Forsaken scale in level: As low as **Level 75** and up to current maximum level.
\- Scale is based on the highest level player **present in the zone** and affects difficulty, loot quality, and mob levels.
\- There is some variation/randomization in the task steps you'll be assigned as you progress through the task.
\- Time of completion will vary by your group's makeup (anywhere from 30-90+ minutes).
\- Collection items appear as ground spawns midway through tasks (you'll see an emote indicating their appearance).

**TASK IN DETAIL**
**TRIGGERING THE TASK**
You say, 'Hail, Brother Estle'
Brother Estle shakes his head as he surveys the devastation. 'When I first came to minister to the people of West Karana, I never thought this would be their fate. I imagined I would carry the message of the Prime Healer to all the people and bring peace to their souls. I imagined these lands would prosper and that my brothers and I would establish a new temple that would be the spiritual foundation of the Karanas. But when the light of [reality] shines, imagination often withers like shadows in the noonday sun .'
You say, 'Reality?'
Brother Estle says 'Who can say what shadow plays of imagination will foretell the future? The first act in my mind's theatre never showed to me the betrayal by my brother, Trintle. His spirit having been fouled by the cult of Bertoxxulous in the sewers below Qeynos, he attacked me one night on the road. Were it not for the help of a passing adventurer much like you, my body would have been as broken as these lands are now. Instead, Trintle went to his grave, and Chintle and I thought long about whether to abandon our mission and return to the temple. We decided the souls of the people were more important than our anguish at the loss of our brother. We thought our [vision] was still possible.'
You say, 'What vision?'
Brother Estle says 'Now I can see how naive it was. The play has become a tragedy.' Brother Estle sighs. 'The old visions are unwelcome. I can think of nothing lofty. What is left to me now is worry and duty. As a healer, I must attend to the injured. If I lose their bodies, I will also lose their souls. If only [Brother Chintle] we're here to aid me in such an impossible task.'
You say, 'Brother Chintle?'
Brother Estle says 'He was always even more naive than I. He could never accept that Brother Trintle's evil was not some sort of possession or mind control, just as he could not accept that such an enormous calamity could fall upon West Karana apart from the wrath of some evil spirit or god. Shortly after the tragedy occurred, Chintle began speaking of visions -- visions of Brother Trintle and the truth behind what happened here. He ran off in the night, and I have not seen him since. I fear for his safety and sanity, but now my [task] has doubled in difficulty without him. I cannot search for him when there is so much suffering with which I must contend.'
You say, 'What task?'
Brother Estle says 'There are injured who need healing, dead that lie unburied, and hungry who need food. The tasks are more than enough for one man, and the Qeynos guard are already stretched thin. You have seen the plight of the people. Will you [help], adventurer? If we can tend to the needs of the people, perhaps then I can search for Chintle and heal his troubled mind.'
You say, 'I will help!'
You have been assigned the task 'Life After Death'.
Brother Estle needs your help: The village has been destroyed and people are dead or gravely injured. He has asked you to help him restore order to the village, since his fellow mercenary, Brother Chintle, has gone missing.
Brother Estle says 'Thank you, \_\_\_\_\_. [Go] and prepare. Speak to me when you are ready.'
**STEP #1**
Speak with Brother Estle 0/1 (Ethernere Tainted West Karana)
_Task Window Says:_ Speak with Brother Estle to find out how you can help the villagers.
_If You Get This Step: Hail update. His response depends on the next step._
_If you get rubble piles next:_
Brother Estle says 'When the earthquakes shook the village, homes collapsed. Many people were trapped in the rubble. We tried to free them, but widow spiders, displaced by the quakes, have infested the destroyed homes. I am sure it will not be long before those left alive will be consumed by the spiders. Help the trapped villagers if you can. Clear the rubble and kill the widows.'
_If you get injured villagers next:_
Response?

---

**STEP #2**
Destroy the rubble piles and widows that have trapped the villagers in their homes 0/37 (Ethernere Tainted West Karana)
_Task Window Says:_ When the earthquakes came, many villagers were trapped in their homes when they collapsed. Now widows have taken residence in the rubble and threaten the lives of the villagers. Destroy the rubble piles and kill the widows to free the trapped villagers.
_If You Get This Step: These are the "a rubble pile" and "a rubble widow" destructible objects around the village. The widows are fairly simple mobs, and the rubble piles don't have too many hitpoints. Some of the piles will spawn trivial mobs called "a widowling"._
=====================
Create a healing poultice and use it to cure the injured villagers 0/8 (Ethernere Tainted West Karana)
_Task Window Says: Brother Estle needs your help to concoct a poultice that can heal the injured villagers. He nees you to : Collect 3 Bear Bile samples from bilious bears. Collect 3 Sapling Resin samples from offended saplings. Collect 3 Saplings Charcoal samples from burnt saplings. Give the samples to Brother Estle, and he will make the poultice. You can then heal the injured villagers using the poultice._
_If You Get This Step: Kill bilious bears, offended saplings, and burnt saplings until you've looted 3x "Bear Bile", 3x "Sapling Resin, and 3x "Sapling Charcoal" (respectively). Hand in these items to Brother Estle to receive "Brother Estle's Poultice", then right-click this item on "an injured villager" mobs._

---

**STEP #3**
Speak with Brother Estle 0/1 (Ethernere Tainted West Karana)
_Task Window Says:_ Speak with Brother Estle to find out what else he needs you to do.
_If You Get This Step: Hail update (receive "Brother Estle's Prayer Book")._
=====================
Speak with Brother Estle 0/1 (Ethernere Tainted West Karana)
_Task Window Says:_ Speak with Brother Estle to find out what else he needs you to do.
_If You Get This Step: Hail update (receive "Grave Shovel" and "Corpse Rope")._
=====================
Speak with Brother Estle 0/1 (Ethernere Tainted West Karana)
_Task Window Says:_ Speak with Brother Estle to find out what else he needs you to do.
_If You Get This Step: Hail update (no item given)._
Brother Estle says 'The miasma of death is overpowering. So many have died that it will take ages to bury them all. It pains me to think that we cannot pay the proper respect to the dead, but the living will surely join them if we do not do what is necessary. We need the hottest fires. Burning spirits have been flitting around the village, no doubt loosed from the depths of the earth by the quakes. Kill them, take their essences, and use them to kindle the piles of corpses.'

---

**STEP #4**
Read Estle's prayer over the dead villagers 0/11 (Ethernere Tainted West Karana)
_Task Window Says: Brother Estle wants to give safe travel to the souls of the recently departed villagers. Use his prayer book over the dead villagers to speak his words to the lingering souls._
_If You Get This Step: Target "a dead villager" and right-click the Prayer Book to spawn "a departing spirit"; kill the spirit._
=====================
Dig graves, drag the decomposing villagers to the graves, and bury them 0/11 (Ethernere Tainted West Karana)
**Task Window**: Decomposing corpses are starting to stink up the village. Brother Estle wants you to give them a proper burial. To do so, he has given you a Grave Shovel and a Corpse Rope. Use the Grave Shovel in the open area to the west of the village, past the last houses, to dig graves. Use the Corpse Rope on the decomposing villagers to drag them to the graves. If a decomposing villager touches a grave, they will be buries automatically.
_If You Get This Step: Around /location 200, 0, -97, right-click the "Grave Shovel" to spawn a grave; then go to "a decomposing villager" and right-click "Corpse Rope" on it; lead them to the spawned grave. Mobs will attack as you lead the villagers away. Make sure you take off any form of levitation for this part._
=====================
Kill the fire spirits and use their essences near the piles of rotting villagers so that the corpses ignite 0/5 (Ethernere Tainted West Karana)
_Task Window Says:_ There are so many piles of rotting corpses in the village that the stench has become overwhelming. Brother Estle wants you to burn the piles. To kindle a proper flame, you will need to kill fire spirits, take their Fire Spirit Essences, and use them on the piles of rotting villagers.
_If You Get This Step: Kill mephits called "a fire spirit", located northeast of the floating village. Loot the essences from their corpses, and use them on the groups of rotting villagers around the area._

---

**STEP #5**
Speak with a spirit crier 0/1 (Ethernere Tainted West Karana)
_Task Window Says:_ A mysterious spirit has appeared, calling out to all the villagers. Speak with the spirit crier and see what it wants.
_If You Get This Step: The spirit caller can be found roaming beneath the floating house._
a spirit crier shouts 'Members of the living, take heed! Listen to my words! The Horde is coming! The Brother shall lead them here! You will join our ranks!'
a spirit crier shouts 'Your place is among the dead, and the Horde will be your masters!'
a spirit crier shouts 'The Brother has spoken: The Karanas are damned! Lay down your lives to us and prepare for your inevitable servitude!'
_Hail it:_
You say, 'Hail, a spirit crier'
a spirit crier says 'You are wise to stand before me, yes. You are wise to yield. Soon we come. Soon the living will become the dead!'

**STEP #6**
Speak with Brother Estle 0/1 (Ethernere Tainted West Karana)
_Task Window Says:_ Talk to Brother Estle about what the spirit said and what is in store for the village.
_If You Get This Step: Hail update._
Brother Estle says 'So the spirit speaks of the glowing creatures that have harried this village since the disaster? They must be planning an assault, but I cannot say what the spirit meant by 'Brother.' I could not think that Chintle would be in collusion with them...' Brother Estle cups his hands around his mouth and yells, 'Everyone, retreat to the floating house! The Horde is coming, and we have no chance on the ground!' Brother Estle watches as people run about, panicked. He sees a group dashing into the underground tunnels. 'No! I have seen creatures of the Horde emerge from there! Surely they will be lost if they enter the realm below! Go after them. Hopefully nothing ill befalls them, but if the worst happens, pay back the Horde in kind!'

Search for the villagers that ran into the Ethernere tunnels, and kill any resistance you meet 0/10 (Ethernere Tainted West Karana)

**STEP #9**
Kill the eradicators when Estle weakens them 0/10 (Ethernere Tainted West Karana)
_Task Window Says:_ Kill the eradicators as they climb the ramp to the floating house. They come from the tunnels in the village and the eastern outskirts. Brother Estle will weaken them when they are close to him.
_If You Get This Step: Kill updates: gehein eradicators, skirth eradicators, tirun eradicators. These are staggered spawns that make their way towards Brother Estle's location. Once in range of him, he weakens them to 30% health. They hit for a max ~12,000 and summon when damaged, so don't take them too lightly. Note you can kill them without bringing them to Brother Estle, but will just have to do so from 100% health._
=====================
Trap the soul slurpers with Estle's trap and then kill them before they can reach the villagers 0/7 (Ethernere Tainted West Karana)
**Task Window**: The soul slurpers are coming from the tunnels in the village or the eastern outskirts. They seek the villagers hiding in the floating house. Use the Magical Trap to freeze the soul slurpers in place for a short while, and then kill them.
"Sippin suggests below to put your group at the top of the transparent ramp. When we first did that, we quickly became overrun by the soul slurpers, and our tank died. After rez and rebuff, we ended up just treating this as a 'kill task'. There appeared to be no need to trap the soul slurpers, just set up somewhere safe, pull them to the group, and kill. Updates came after each kill with no use of the trap." - asru
_If You Get This Step: Mobs called "a soul slurper" spawn and start walking up the spiral ramp. Kill these mobs. Note that you can use the "Magical Trap" received in the previous step to spawn an aura that freezes the slurpers in place (up to 40 seconds)._
=====================
Kill Brutallion to prevent it from completely destroying the village 0/1 (Ethernere Tainted West Karana)
_Task Window Says:_ ?

a spiritual emissary says 'You have vanquished but a small number of the Horde. And where did their souls flee? Back to the land from whence they came, waiting to rise again and return. When the Brother comes, there will be no defeat. You will be the ones returning with us.'

Now that we are out of immediate danger, we must hasten to find Brother Chintle. He may know more about this than I would hope. You should [leave] and rest after such hard work. Return to me soon, and we will begin the search.'
You have successfully helped Brother Estle fend off the Horde attack and restore order to the village. Now he will need help searching for Brother Chintle.

**Call of the Forsaken Info & Guides: Overview \| Progression & Task List \| Raiding \| Visible Armor**
**About Heroic Adventures**
\- Heroic adventures in Call of the Forsaken scale in level: As low as **Level 75** and up to current maximum level.
\- Scale is based on the highest level player **present in the zone** and affects difficulty, loot quality, and mob levels.
\- There is some variation/randomization in the task steps you'll be assigned as you progress through the task.
\- Time of completion will vary by your group's makeup (anywhere from 30-90+ minutes).
\- Collection items appear as ground spawns midway through tasks (you'll see an emote indicating their appearance).
\- Named mobs (or their PHs) spawn midway through tasks (usually, but always, at the same time collection items appear).
\- These tasks have individual **3-hour lockout timers** (timer gained upon completion).
**Experience in Heroic Adventures**
\- Mobs in these instances grant some experience, but the bulk of experience is **earned upon task completion**.
\- As of a patch in November 2014, less difficult tasks were tuned to reward less experience and more difficult ones were tuned to reward more.
\- For an added experience bonus, check out **Clayton Teek's "Daily Tasks"** [quest=7988].

---

_This is a heroic adventure (1-6 players) that begins with Brother Estle ( **click for map image**) in the Ethernere Tainted West Karana. He is located in the northwestern part of the zone, just southwest of the floating village (/location 265, -280, -91)._
_Pre-Requisites for Requesting:_
\- Level 75
\- Completed "Life After Death" (heroic adventure)

\- Say "help" to request the task and "go" to zone in.

**TASK IN DETAIL**
TRIGGERING THE TASK
You say, 'Hail, Brother Estle'
Brother Estle shakes his head as he surveys the devastation. 'When I first came to minister to the people of West Karana, I never thought this would be their fate. I imagined I would carry the message of the Prime Healer to all the people and bring peace to their souls. I imagined these lands would prosper and that my brothers and I would establish a new temple that would be the spiritual foundation of the Karanas. But when the light of [reality] shines, imagination often withers like shadows in the noonday sun .'
You say, 'What reality?'
Brother Estle says 'Who can say what shadow plays of imagination will foretell the future? The first act in my mind's theatre never showed to me the betrayal by my brother, Trintle. His spirit having been fouled by the cult of Bertoxxulous in the sewers below Qeynos, he attacked me one night on the road. Were it not for the help of a passing adventurer much like you, my body would have been as broken as these lands are now. Instead, Trintle went to his grave, and Chintle and I thought long about whether to abandon our mission and return to the temple. We decided the souls of the people were more important than our anguish at the loss of our brother. We thought our [vision] was still possible.'
You say, 'What vision?'
Brother Estle says 'Now I can see how naive it was. The play has become a tragedy.' Brother Estle sighs. 'The old visions are unwelcome. I can think of nothing lofty. What is left to me now is worry and duty. As a healer, I must attend to the injured. If I lose their bodies, I will also lose their souls. If only [Brother Chintle] were here to aid me in such an impossible task.'
You say, 'Brother Chintle?'
Brother Estle says 'He was always even more naive than I. He could never accept that Brother Trintle's evil was not some sort of possession or mind control, just as he could not accept that such an enormous calamity could fall upon West Karana apart from the wrath of some evil spirit or god. Shortly after the tragedy occurred, Chintle began speaking of visions -- visions of Brother Trintle and the truth behind what happened here. He ran off in the night, and I have not seen him since. I fear for his safety and sanity, but now my [task] has doubled in difficulty without him. I cannot search for him when there is so much suffering with which I must contend.'
You say, 'Task?'
Brother Estle says 'You have given much aid to this village thus far. I thank you for that. Now that the area has stabilized, we must look for Brother Chintle in earnest. Will you [help]?'
You say, 'I'll help'
Brother Estle says 'Thank you, \_\_\_\_\_. [Go] and prepare. Speak to me when you are ready.'
You have been assigned the task 'Remember the Dead'.

---

In this brief respite between attacks from spirits and the Harrowing Horde, Brother Estle wants you to find Brother Chintle and bring him back. This missing brother must know something about the recent attacks and plans of the Horde.
You say, 'Let's go'
Brother Estle says 'We shall yet rise above this tragedy, \_\_\_\_\_.'
LOADING, PLEASE WAIT...

Once the wall is killed, head to the end of the bandit camp where you'll find "a frightened bandit". Hail it to update the step._
