**Call of the Forsaken Info & Guides: Overview \| Progression & Task List \| Raiding \| Visible Armor**
**About Heroic Adventures**
\- Heroic adventures in Call of the Forsaken scale in level: As low as **Level 75** and up to current maximum level.
\- Scale is based on the highest level player **present in the zone** and affects difficulty, loot quality, and mob levels.
\- There is some variation/randomization in the task steps you'll be assigned as you progress through the task.
\- Time of completion will vary by your group's makeup (anywhere from 30-90+ minutes).
\- Collection items appear as ground spawns midway through tasks (you'll see an emote indicating their appearance).
\- Named mobs (or their PHs) spawn midway through tasks (usually, but always, at the same time collection items appear).
\- These tasks have individual **3-hour lockout timers** (timer gained upon completion).
**Experience in Heroic Adventures**
\- Mobs in these instances grant some experience, but the bulk of experience is **earned upon task completion**.
\- As of a patch in November 2014, less difficult tasks were tuned to reward less experience and more difficult ones were tuned to reward more.
\- For an added experience bonus, check out **Guard Hamarn's "Daily Tasks"** [quest=7988].

---

_This is a heroic adventure (1-6 players) that begins with Battlebreaker Kraklest ( **click for map image**) in the Bixie Warfront (found at the bottom of the ramp, at the edge of the water, /location -915, -1885, -165)._
_Pre-Requisites for Requesting:_
\- Level 75
\- No tasks

---

**TASK SUMMARY**
\- Say "embark on an adventure" to request the task and "ready" to zone in.
**Step #1:**
Get a bixie disguise from Iniltrator Nallai 0/1
\-\-\- Dialogue update ("bixie disguise").
**Step #2:**
\- Convince General Sagrinta to give you her battle plans 0/1
\-\-\- Kill 5x "a cliknar scout" and turn in their heads.
\- Steal the bixie battle plans inside the hive 0/1
\-\-\- Click on the explosive barrel in the room, then click on the scroll box on the center table.
\- Kill skittering cliknar to recover the stolen bixie battle plans 0/1
\-\-\- Kill the mobs under the hive until you loot the item.
**Step #3:**
\- Get in the northwest bixie trenches to avoid the cliknar bombers 0/1
\-\-\- Get in one of the trenches to avoid the zone AEs.
\- Get in the northwest bixie trenches to avoid the cliknar swarm 0/1
\-\-\- Get in one of the trenches to avoid the swarming mobs.
\- Get in the southwest bixie trenches to avoid the explosive seeds 0/1
\-\-\- Get in one of the trenches to avoid the explosions.
**Step #4:**
\- Loot cliknar gas pouches 0/5
\-\-\- Kill "a sprinting gasser" until you've looted 5x "Cliknar Gas Pouch".
\- Kill cliknar gassers in the trenches 0/10
\-\-\- Kill update.
\- Knock out young bixie soldiers 0/6
\-\-\- DPS young soldiers to under 40% health (note: spawns an add at 40%), then right-click the bludgeon.
\- Heal and defend injured bixie soldiers 0/5
\-\-\- Find a honeycomb in the middle area of the trenches in the western part of the zone, right-click it on injured bixies, then kill the spawned cliknar.
**Step #5:**
\- Report to Scout Jugzz in the southeast cliknar trenches 0/1
\-\-\- Speak with Scout Jugzz (located in a trench in southwestern part of the cliknar's side of the zone).
\- Report to Medic Grulina in the southeast cliknar trenches 0/1
\-\-\- Speak with Medic Grulina in the western part of the cliknar trenches.
**\- At this point in the task, collectibles spawn in the zone (ground spawns) and a named (or its PH) has a chance to spawn.**
**Alternate Step #5:**
\- Report to Infiltrator Ynoxi in the southeast cliknar trenches. 0/1
\- Bixie assault teams are trying to contain the cliknar army across the river, but have run into trouble. Fight or sneak your way across the river, and find Infiltrator Ynoxi in the southeast cliknar trenches to offer your aid. 0/1
Ynoxi is in the southeast trenches, west side, in the second cubby up from the bottom.
**Step #6:**
\- Recover bixie wings from the cliknar soldiers in the southeast trenches 0/30
\-\-\- Kill and loot cliknar mobs in the eastern half of the zone until you've gathered 30 of their wings.
\- Defend incapacitated bixie soldiers in the southeast trenches 0/5
\-\-\- Speak with "an incapacitated soldier" to spawn 2x "a hungry rager"; kill the adds; repeat x5.
\- Save disarmed bixie soldiers in the southeast cliknar trenches 0/3
\-\-\- Kill cliknar mobs around disarmed bixies, then hail the bixies.
**Optional for Step# 6:**
\- Kill cliknar soldiers in the southeast cliknar trenches
**Step #7:**
\- Stop or aid the bixie assault in between the eastern cliknar trenches 0/1
\-\-\- Aid or kill "an injured cliknar" (either way, kill off a wave of mobs: Either the Lieutenant + 4x soldiers, or 6x "a nest defender")
\- Find and protect scared bixies from cliknar aggression 0/7
\-\-\- In the northern part of the eastern half of the zone are scared soldiers; speak with them to either update the step or spawn a hostile mob (kill the mob if one spawns).
\- Help General Sagrinta slaughter 100 cliknar 0/100
\-\-\- Speak with the General, and then follow her and kill 100 mobs alongside her.
**Step #8:**
\- Report to Infiltrator Nallai on the northern river camp 0/1
\-\-\- Dialogue update.
\- As soon as the objective for Step #8 is done, the task completes.

You say, 'Battlefield?'
Battlebreaker Kraklest says 'The Stone Hive managed to start a [war] while we searched for the cause of their madness.'
You say, 'What about the war?'
Battlebreaker Kraklest says 'The [cliknar] came from all over, digging through the walls, floors, and ceilings of the entire complex. At the time, the Stone Hive welcomed the war and established a massive battlefield just south of here.'
You say, 'Cliknar?'
Battlebreaker Kraklest says 'You've probably seen them before, but not like this. They're just as mad as the Stone Hive. The numbers of soldiers on both sides is near endless. They're throwing them into a massive [meat grinder].'
You say, 'Meat grinder?'
Battlebreaker Kraklest says 'They're lack of respect for life, and for the honor of fighting, is disgusting. We need to [stop] the war before it decimates both sides.'
You say, 'How can we stop it?'
Battlebreaker Kraklest says 'Prisoners of war, a struggling front line, and an isolated general: there are several situations that could use your help. Are you ready to [embark on an adventure]?'
You say, 'Embark on an adventure?'
Battlebreaker Kraklest says 'Good. Let me know when you're [ready] to depart and I'll ensure you make it to the battlefield safely.'

The bixies do not trust outsiders.

_If You Get This Step: Speak with Nallai at the zone-in._
You say, 'Hail, Infiltrator Nallai'
Infiltrator Nallai smiles and speaks quietly, 'It's good to see you again, \_\_\_\_\_. You're my most-trusted agent after our mission to break Jacyll out of his cell. I'm going to need your [help] here again.'
You say, 'Help?'
Infiltrator Nallai says 'As you know, the powerful war-drum that corrupted the Stone Hive affects more than just bixies. At the height of its strength, the war-drum attracted a nearby [cliknar] colony, who came in search of it.'
You say, 'What cliknar?'
Infiltrator Nallai says 'You've probably encountered them before. Insectoid, antennae, and like to travel in huge packs. If not, well, you'll see them soon. The cliknar invaded the hive and, of course, the bixies rushed off to start a large-scale [war] with them.'
You say, 'War?'
Infiltrator Nallai says 'We can't blame it all on the bixies. The cliknar were driven into just as blind of a bloodlust as the bixies were. But they've been killing each other at an alarming rate. The bixies are losing the war, but you're going to [hold off] the cliknar.'
You say, 'Hold off?'
Infiltrator Nallai says 'The cliknar have pushed the battle line dangerously close to the main hive. If they make their way into the chamber with the main hive, countless [civilians] will be slaughtered. We can't let that happen.'
You say, 'Civilians?'
Infiltrator Nallai says 'Yep, and you'll be posing as one, coming to aid the bixie army. It's the only way they'll trust you. Let me know when you're ready for a [bixie disguise]. I borrowed some [magic thingamajiggies] for this mission.'
You say, 'What magic thingamajiggies?'
Infiltrator Nallai laughs and shows you a collection of small stones in her hand, 'I don't pretend to understand magic, \_\_\_\_\_. But Queen Shulriya Fiume personally assured me these magical disguises will fool anyone.

STEP #5
========================================================================
Report to Scout Jugzz in the southeast cliknar trenches 0/1 (Bixie Warfront)
_Task Window Says:_ The bixie army has walked into a trap! While they pushed their way into the enemy's trenches across the river, the cliknar army snuck behind them and cut off their retreat route across the bridge. Find a way around the cliknar-barricaded bridge, and find Scout Jugzz near the front of the southeast cliknar trenches.
_If You Get This Step: Find Scout Jugzz in the southeastern cliknar trenches. He is a bixie in a dead-end down in the trench in the southwestern part of the cliknar's half of the zone._
You say, 'Hail, Scout Jugzz'
Scout Jugzz jumps as you speak. He clearly didn't notice you. He says quietly, 'Honey help us! We're in a heap of [trouble] over here!'
You say, 'Trouble?'
Scout Jugzz says 'You saw how dense the cliknar population was near the bridge, right? There's at least 700 of them, and they're as tall as mountains! They must be readying a full-scale assault on our hive! You saw the defenses there. We wouldn't stand a chance! We need to [strike] them hard now, in their own trenches to distract them!'
You say, 'Let's strike...'
Scout Jugzz tries to hide himself behind you and pushes you into the trenches. Here are my orders from the general. They're yours now! For the hive!
=====================
Report to Medic Grulina in the southeast cliknar trenches 0/1 (Bixie Warfront)
_Task Window Says:_ The bixie army is taking heavy losses across the river, but have secured the bridge. Run across the bridge, and find Medic Grulina in the southeast cliknar trenches near it.
_If You Get This Step: Find Medic Grulina._
You say, 'Hail, Medic Grulina'
Medic Grulina wipes blood from her hands and says slowly, 'Another one run into the slaughter, eh? I hope you fair [better] than the last pack.'
You say, 'Better?'
Medic Grulina kicks the skull of the skeleton at her feet across the trench and mutters, 'It's blood for blood, and we're losing. I suppose you'll want [orders]?
You say, 'Orders?'
Medic Grulina wipes her surgery knife on her skirt and says quietly, 'Here they are. Many good bixies died here today. Don't add to that count, okay?'
========================================================================
STEP #6
========================================================================
Recover bixie wings from the cliknar soldiers in the southeast trenches 0/30 (Bixie Warfront)
_Task Window Says:_ Cliknar soldiers have begun collecting bixie wings as trophies. The most veteran warriors have hundreds of them. Kill the cliknar soldiers in the southeast cliknar trenches and take back their gruesome trophies.
_If You Get This Step: Kill cliknar mobs in the eastern half of the zone until you've collected 30 of their wings. These drop from about every mob in the area, four at a time._
=====================
Defend incapacitated bixie soldiers in the southeast trenches 0/5 (Bixie Warfront)
_Task Window Says:_ During the last retreat some bixie soldiers were left behind because they were unable to move due to injuries. Find the incapacitated bixie soldiers in the southeast cliknar trenches and speak with them. They will shoot up a flare to attract a medic to carry them out. Defend them until the medic arrives.
_If You Get This Step: Throughout the cliknar trenches are bixie mobs feigned death, called "an incapacitated soldier". Speak with them to spawn 2x "a hungry rager" (hit for a max ~9,000; stunnable; mezzable). Kill the adds to progress the step._
You say, 'Hail, an incapacitated soldier'
an incapacitated soldier struggles to breathe. It's wounded very bad. It looks at the [flare] on its belt.
You say, 'flare'
an incapacitated soldier nods and mutters, '[Light it] and the medic will come for me. It'll bring cliknar too.'
You say, 'Light it'
an incapacitated soldier tenses as the flare shoots up into the air.
_2x "a hungry rager" spawn and rush in. A combat medic also appears (non-aggro)._
a combat medic says 'Don't ask questions, just drink the potion.'
a combat medic says 'Have another sip from the healing potion, soldier.'
a combat medic says 'You've done your duty for today, soldier. I'll get you patched up before tomorrow's push.'
=====================
Save disarmed bixie soldiers in the southeast cliknar trenches 0/3 (Bixie Warfront)
_Task Window Says:_ The bixie army was forced to retreat from the nearby trenches hastily. Sneak through the southeast cliknar trenches and kill cliknar near disarmed bixie soldiers. Then speak with the disarmed soldiers to let them know it's safe to retreat.
_If You Get This Step: In the area you will find disarmed bixie soldiers with cliknar mobs around them. Clear the cliknar around from the soldier, and then hail the soldier._
Optionally on this step you can do the below.
Kill cliknar soldiers in the southeast cliknar trenches
The cliknar army has recently retaken the nearby trenches. Kill cliknar soldiers in the southeast cliknar trenches to afford the bixie army some breathing room.
========================================================================
STEP #7
========================================================================
Stop or aid the bixie assault in between the eastern cliknar trenches 0/1 (Bixie Warfront)
_Task Window Says:_ A pack of rabid bixie warriors are charging into the cliknar base, targeting the weak and injured. Find the injured cliknar worker between the two east trenches, and speak with it. When the bixie warriors arrive to kill the injured cliknar, you must choose to either help the bixies kill the cliknar, or defend it from the bloodthirsty bixies.
_If You Get This Step: Find "an injured cliknar" in about the middle of the cliknar's half of the zone (/location -2300, -2375, -132). Say "honeycombs" to spawn a Lieutenant, then either say "protect" (to attack and kill the Lieutenant and his four bloodthirsty soldiers -- all splittable, mezzable, stunnable) or "kill it" to aggro the injured cliknar (in which case, you'll have 6x "a nest defender" to kill -- all splittable, mezzable, stunnable)._
You say, 'Hail, an injured cliknar'
an injured cliknar is trying to limp away on its torn-up leg. It says desperately, 'Please, bixie. Have mercy! I'm a simple laborer, and have been [crippled] by an explosion.'
You say, 'How were you crippled?'
an injured cliknar looks exhausted and says pitifully, 'I was delivering medical supplies to our injured, when one of the [honeycombs] exploded right next to us. I'm the only survivor.
You say, 'Honeycombs?'
an injured cliknar looks up at you and begs, 'Please, convince them I'm not a threat. I just want to go home.'
Lieutenant Relikk says 'That was our honeycomb, and it's more than you deserved, cliknar scum!'
You say, 'Hail, Lieutenant Relikk'
Lieutenant Relikk says 'Good work tracking this cliknar, soldier. [Help] us kill it and join our march into their base.'
You say, 'Help'
Lieutenant Relikk says 'You didn't believe its sob story, did you? I watched this cliknar kill three of our troops with its bare hands!'
an injured cliknar says 'That's not true! I've never killed anyone.'
Lieutenant Relikk says 'Well, they all look the same to me. Will you help us [kill it], or are you a traitor who would actually [protect] a cliknar that's murdered your brothers-in-arms?'
You say, 'kill it'
Lieutenant Relikk says 'That's the spirit!'
an injured cliknar says 'I was wrong to appeal to a bixies' sympathy. You clearly have none.'
an injured cliknar says 'We were created to fight, you and I.'
an injured cliknar has been slain by \_\_\_\_\_!
an injured cliknar's corpse says 'I thought... we could make peace... for once.'
Lieutenant Relikk says 'There's more cliknar ahead. Let's charge!'
a bloodthirsty soldier is killed by a brutal cliknar attack.
a nest defender bares its teeth as it readies its first attack.
=====================
Find and protect scared bixies from cliknar aggression 0/7 (Bixie Warfront)
_Task Window Says:_ ?
_If You Get This Step: In the northern part of the eastern half of the zone are scared bixies. Speak with them to either update the step or spawn a hostile mob. If a mob spawns, you need to kill it to update the step. Note you may get a named drone, "Cruncher"._
=====================
Help General Sagrinta slaughter 100 cliknar 0/100 (Bixie Warfront)
Task Window Says:
"The bixie general, Sagrinta, has stormed across the bridge and is ready to stomp the clicknar army into oblivion. Speak with General Sagrinta, located between the east clicknar trenches and follow her into battle. If she falls down during combat, you'll need to defend her while she recovers her strength"
When you hail her at the end of the bridge:
You say, 'Hail General Sagrinta'
General Sagrinta says, 'Enough bloodshed! Are you with me, soldier? This ends right [now].'
You say, 'now'
General Sagrinta says, 'Then gather your courage. we will slaughter them in the trenches!'
General Sagrinta says, 'Stay close and follow my lead. Into the trenches!'
General Sagrinta cackles as she zooms into battle.
She moves into the trenches, and after a short while a bunch of clicknar attack her, you can help but she kills them so fast its almost pointless. After 20 are dead, she drops to the ground:
Zonewide message: Defend General Sagrinta while she recovers her strength!
3 clicknar spawn and run to her, they don't actually attack her, so if you're invis you can take this time to recover, med whatever. They are not runspeed immune, they do summon, and they do not appear to reset, so its hard to split them. After killing the 3 clicknar:
General Sagrinta says, 'Don't worry, that almost never happens to me. Lets get back to the fighting!'
This happens 3 more times, for me at 47 kills, 70 kills and 97 kills, not sure if its always that or not. Once you kill the last 3:
General Sagrinta says, 'It was an honor fighting alongside you, soldier. report to your superiors with my full recommendation for a promotion.'
========================================================================
STEP #8
========================================================================
Report to Infiltrator Nallai on the northern river camp 0/1 (Bixie Warfront)
_Task Window Says:_ The cliknar are in full retreat! You've bought the bixie army time to recover and hopefully rebuild their defenses before the next cliknar attack. Report to Infiltrator Nallai, who's moved to the northern ramp along the river that segments the battlefield.
_If You Get This Step: This is a dialogue update._
You say, 'Hail, Infiltrator Nallai'
Infiltrator Nallai says 'Well done, \_\_\_\_\_! The bixies have secured their side of the battlefield, and the hive should be [safe] for awhile longer.'
You say, 'Safe?'
Infiltrator Nallai says 'That was our goal, remember?' She gives you a concerned look, 'You didn't get caught up in the bloodlust did you? Don't forget that we're trying to [stop] this war.'
You say, 'Stop the war?'
Infiltrator Nallai pulls out your reward. 'Well, let's not worry about that now. Here's your reward for this mission. I hope to see you again soon. I doubt the cliknar are gone for good.'
You've successfully bolstered the defense of the exposed bixie civilian areas, pushed back the cliknar offensive, and brought some level of peace to the bixie soldiers. The hive will survive another day, and hopefully long enough for them to recover their senses and stop this crazy war.
Infiltrator Nallai pats you on the back and says cheerfully, 'Well done, \_\_\_\_\_! The bixies should survive one or two more days out here, at least, thanks to your work. Are you ready to [leave]?
You say, 'Let's leave'
Infiltrator Nallai nods and begins to lead you back to camp.
LOADING, PLEASE WAIT...
You have entered Bixie Warfront.

**Call of the Forsaken Info & Guides: Overview \| Progression & Task List \| Raiding \| Visible Armor**
**About Heroic Adventures**
\- Heroic adventures in Call of the Forsaken scale in level: As low as **Level 75** and up to current maximum level.
\- Scale is based on the highest level player **present in the zone** and affects difficulty, loot quality, and mob levels.
\- There is some variation/randomization in the task steps you'll be assigned as you progress through the task.
\- Time of completion will vary by your group's makeup (anywhere from 30-90+ minutes).
\- Collection items appear as ground spawns midway through tasks (you'll see an emote indicating their appearance).
\- Named mobs (or their PHs) spawn midway through tasks (usually, but always, at the same time collection items appear).
\- These tasks have individual **3-hour lockout timers** (timer gained upon completion).
**Experience in Heroic Adventures**
\- Mobs in these instances grant some experience, but the bulk of experience is **earned upon task completion**.
\- As of a patch in November 2014, less difficult tasks were tuned to reward less experience and more difficult ones were tuned to reward more.
\- For an added experience bonus, check out **Guard Hamarn's "Daily Tasks"** [quest=7988].

---

_This is a heroic adventure (1-6 players) that begins with Battlebreaker Kraklest ( **click for map image**) in the Bixie Warfront (found at the bottom of the ramp, at the edge of the water, /location -915, -1885, -165)._
_Pre-Requisites for Requesting:_
\- Level 75
\- Completed "Endless War" (heroic adventure)

---

**TASK SUMMARY**
\- Say "embark on an adventure" to request the task and "ready" to zone in.
**Step #1:**
Speak with Iniltrator Nallai near the bridge 0/1
\-\-\- Dialogue update.
**Step #2:**
\- Get captured by cliknar kidnappers on the bridge 0/1
\-\-\- Location update on the bridge.
**Step #3:**
\- Revive and speak with Engineer Friliim in the prison 0/1
\-\-\- Right-click the item received and speak with Friliim.
\- Convince Engineer Friliim to attempt an escape 0/1
\-\-\- Dialogue update.
**Step #4:**
\- Escape your curiously unguarded prison pit 0/1
\-\-\- Head out of the pit and be ready for a possible mob ambush (two mobs, if any).
\- Find a rock to bash the cliknars guarding your prison pit with 0/2
\-\-\- Pick up the rock from the ground, target the distracted guards, and right-click the rock.
**Step #5:**
\- Kill prison wardens 0/4
\-\-\- Kill the prison wardens roaming about the area (each spawns three low-difficulty adds every 20% health).
\- Free bixie prisoners 0/30
\-\-\- Kill 10x "a cliknar slaver" to free prisoners (three prisoners each); prisoners are aggro-linked with cliknar until the cliknar dies.
\- Kill cliknar prison guards 0/12
\-\-\- Kill 12x "a pit guard".
**\- At this point in the task, collectibles spawn in the zone (ground spawns) and a named (or its PH) has a chance to spawn.**
**Step #6:**
\- Find Friliium's toolkit in one of the cliknar chests and give it to him 0/1
\-\-\- Lead Engineer Friliium to equipment crates in the prison area (he'll keep warping to players in your group, similar to a mercenary).
\- Recover Friliium's toolkit from the kleptomanic cliknar and give it to him 0/1
\-\-\- Kill "a kleptomanic cliknar" (wanders the eastern edge of the zone), loot the toolkit, and hand it in to Friliim.
\- Build some tools for Friliim at the nearby forge and give them to him 0/1
\-\-\- Kill Kreppo the Curious in the far southeastern part of the zone, loot it, then right-click the item at the large forge.
**Step #7:**
\- Free 6 captured mole-mounts 0/6
\-\-\- Kill hungry cliknar around the captured moles, then hail the moles.
\- Kill rageblind bixies 0/10
\-\-\- Kill update.
\- Taste-test roasted moles 0/10
\-\-\- Around the area are several roasts over fires with cliknar mobs around them; kill the cliknar, then click the roast.
**Step #8:**
\- Find Sticky Feet in the southern trenches and free it from a trap 0/1
\-\-\- Sticky Feet, found in the southwestern part of the cliknar trenches, is trapped; click on the trap to free it.
\- Find Friliim's sister in the southeast cliknar trenches 0/1
\-\-\- Kill 2x "a grave digger" next to Skyrider Elena (found in the southwestern part of the cliknar trenches).
\- Help Friliim heal poisoned bixies in the southeast cliknar trenches 0/3
\-\-\- In the trenches, hail "a poisoned soldier" to spawn a cliknar; loot antidote from the cliknar; turn item in to Friliim.
**Step #9:**
\- Take explosive seeds from cliknar technicians and activate them near the bridge 0/3
\-\-\- Kill blast technicians on the bridge, loot explosive seeds, and right-click them at the eastern end of the bridge.
\- Kill the stalwart cliknar guards that are fighting prisoners on the bridge 0/4
\-\-\- Kill update.
\- Keep Hepons the Merciless busy while bixies escape
\-\-\- Aggro Hepons until 15 bixie prisoners get across the bridge
**Step #10:**
\- Talk with Engineer Friliim 0/1
\-\-\- Dialogue update.
**Step #11:**
\- Report to Infiltrator Nallai 0/1
\-\-\- Dialogue update.
\- As soon as the objective for Step #11 is done, the task completes.

_If You Get This Step: Speak with Nallai at the zone-in._

Infiltrator Nallai smiles and says proudly, 'We're making real progress here, \_\_\_\_\_! The Stone Hive generals have agreed to accept the Wet River Hive's aid directly. No more disguises and [working from the shadows]!
You say, 'Working from the shadows?'
Infiltrator Nallai says 'Well, actually, we'll be working from the shadows this time too. But this'll be the last time! Maybe. Probably not. Hey, there's [nothing wrong] with shadows!'

You say, 'How to we get him back?'
Infiltrator Nallai chuckles and says, 'You're not going to like this, \_\_\_\_\_. I need you to get [captured] by the cliknar.
You say, 'Captured...?'
Infiltrator Nallai says 'It's the easiest way to find out where Engineer Friliim was taken, and get yourself there! Once you find him, [convince] him to trust you.'
You say, 'How do we convince him?'
Infiltrator Nallai says 'It shouldn't be hard. He's in a cliknar prison! Break him out and [escort] him back here to me. I'll deliver him to the bixie generals and ensure we have a permanent ally here.'
You say, 'Escort him?'
Infiltrator Nallai says 'Do whatever it takes to get him back here safely, \_\_\_\_\_. You can try to rescue any others you find, but every one else is expendable.'

Get captured by cliknar kidnappers on the bridge 0/1 (Bixie Warfront)

Run onto the bridge and let the cliknar kidnappers capture you. They'll take you to the prison where you can meet up with Friliim and help orchestrate an escape.

_If You Get This Step: This is a dialogue update._

Engineer Friliim smiles as he rolls up his sleeves. Then let's get out of here, \_\_\_\_\_.

Escape your curiously unguarded prison pit 0/1 (Bixie Warfront)
_Task Window Says:_ You can't see anyone guarding your prison pit. Cautiously try to walk out, and hope no one spots you. If they do, kill them.
_If You Get This Step: Walk out of the prison. Sometimes, nothing spawns. Sometimes, you get ambushed by 2x "a prison ambusher" (hit for a max ~11,000; stunnable; mezzable; fully slowable). Sometimes, you get ambushed by 2x "a rampaging bixie" (hit for a max ~11,000; stunnable; mezzable; fully slowable; go non-aggro at 40% health). If mobs spawn, you have to deal with them for the update._
Frenzied bixie warriors run forward and start to attack you without realizing you're prisoners.
a rampaging bixie recovers from its bloodlust and realizes you're on their side, 'Sorry, Thunder Gun! I don't know what came over me. I'll go take out some cliknar to help you get out of here alive!'
Engineer Friliim shakes his head and says, 'These meatheads are more pain than they're worth!'
=====================
Find a rock to bash the cliknars guarding your prison pit with 0/1 (Bixie Warfront)
_Task Window Says:_ Your prison pit is guarded by two cliknar guards. Find a rock in the pit and use it to bash the guards' heads in. That should buy you time to escape.
_If You Get This Step: Pick up a "Surprisingly Aerodynamic Rock" from the ground, target the distracted guards, and right-click the rock._

_If You Get This Step: Pull and kill the cliknar prison wardens in the area. Each of these is a standard trash mob, except they spawn adds at 80%, 60%, 40%, and 20% health (3x "a corpse hider" - adds hit for a max ~2,100; have low hitpoints; and are mezzable and stunnable)._

a cliknar slaver has been slain by \_\_\_\_\_!

_Task Window Says:_ The cliknar army has baited the southeast cliknar trenches with moles roasting over barbecue pits, some of which have been poisoned. Find roasting moles in the trenches, kill any cliknar around them, and then taste-test the meal to see if it's safe for prisoners to eat.
_If You Get This Step: Around the area are several roasts over fires with cliknar mobs around them. Kill the cliknar next to the roast, then click the roast. Mobs called "a wimpy cliknar" are just that (relatively easy mobs). Mobs called "a poisoner" are regular trash mobs (hit for a max ~13,000)._
_Some of the meat will be safe; some of it will be poisoned. There is no way to tell which is which before clicking it, and if it's poisoned, you will be death touched. We're kidding about that, but you do receive a minor 4-tick DOT for 5,000/tick._

**Call of the Forsaken Info & Guides: Overview \| Progression & Task List \| Raiding \| Visible Armor**
**About Heroic Adventures**
\- Heroic adventures in Call of the Forsaken scale in level: As low as **Level 75** and up to current maximum level.
\- Scale is based on the highest level player **present in the zone** and affects difficulty, loot quality, and mob levels.
\- There is some variation/randomization in the task steps you'll be assigned as you progress through the task.
\- Time of completion will vary by your group's makeup (anywhere from 30-90+ minutes).
\- Collection items appear as ground spawns midway through tasks (you'll see an emote indicating their appearance).
\- Named mobs (or their PHs) spawn midway through tasks (usually, but always, at the same time collection items appear).
\- These tasks have individual **3-hour lockout timers** (timer gained upon completion).
**Experience in Heroic Adventures**
\- Mobs in these instances grant some experience, but the bulk of experience is **earned upon task completion**.
\- As of a patch in November 2014, less difficult tasks were tuned to reward less experience and more difficult ones were tuned to reward more.
\- For an added experience bonus, check out **Guard Hamarn's "Daily Tasks"** [quest=7988].

---

_This is a heroic adventure (1-6 players) that begins with Battlebreaker Kraklest ( **click for map image**) in the Bixie Warfront (found at the bottom of the ramp, at the edge of the water, /location -915, -1885, -165)._
_Pre-Requisites for Requesting:_
\- Level 75
\- Completed "Endless War" (heroic adventure)
\- Completed "The Thunder Gun Escape" (heroic adventure)

---

**TASK SUMMARY**
\- Say "embark on an adventure" to request the task and "ready" to zone in.
**Step #1:**
\- Keep General Sagrinta alive 0/1
\-\-\- This updates automatically at the end of the task.
**Step #2:**
\- Speak with General Sagrinta 0/1
\-\-\- Dialogue update.
**Step #3:**
\- Feed the Honey-Soaked Ham to Sticky Feet on the bridge 0/1
\-\-\- Give Sticky Feet the ham to trigger the waves of mobs.
\- Talk to Engineer Friliim on the bridge 0/1
\-\-\- Dialogue update to trigger the first wave of mobs.
\- Plant Boom Blooms on or near the bridge 0/10
\-\-\- Right-click 10 of these on and around the bridge to spawn the first wave of mobs..
**Step #4:**
\- Kill bixie traitors 0/5
\-\-\- Kill the traitorous bixies that attack (spawned about 20 seconds apart).
\- Kill cliknar commandos 0/5
\-\-\- Kill the commandos that attack (spawned from the east about 20 seconds apart).
\- Kill cliknar vanguards 0/5
\-\-\- Kill the vanguards that attack (spawned from either side; if left alive too long, these spawn two additional adds).
\- Kill slow moles 0/5
\-\-\- Kill the slow charges that attack (aggro one at a time, one every 20 seconds or so).
**Step #5:**
\- Kill cliknar assassins 0/6
\-\-\- Kill the focused assassins that attack the General (spawned about 20 seconds apart).
\- Kill cliknar enslavers 0/6
\-\-\- Kill the cliknar enslavers that attack (spawns in sets of two, about 45 seconds apart).
\- Kill wild stingers 0/6
\-\-\- Kill the wild stingers that attack (spawn in sets of two, about 30 seconds apart - from either side of the bridge).
\- Kill bloody recruits 0/6
\-\-\- Kill the bloody recruits that attack.
**Step #6:**
\- General Sagrinta is bleeding out! Recover Bixie Bandages and heal her 0/5
\-\-\- Find dying medics throughout the trenches in the western half of the zone, kill the cliknar with them, loot Bandages, and deliver to the General.
\- General Sagrinta is dehydrated! Find Soggy Kelp and give them to her 0/5
\-\-\- Kill sporalis in the river, loot 5x "Soggy Kelp", and turn in the items to the General.
\- General Sagrinta is demoralized! Rescue kidnapped bixie children to reassure her 0/5
\-\-\- Kill cliknar mobs around kidnapped bixie children (in the northeast and cliknar trenches).
**Step #7:**
\- Kill the swarming stingers 0/20
\-\-\- Kill all of the swarming stingers (spawn in two sets of 10, about 30 seconds part).
\- Kill General Rikkik 0/1
\-\-\- Slightly harder than the average cliknar in the zone; mem blurs self; procs a stun on random targets.
\- Kill Trook, the Hive Breaker 0/1
\-\-\- Slightly harder than the average mob in the zone; procs a damage nuke on its target; has a minor emote-based AE.
**\- At this point in the task, collectibles spawn in the zone (ground spawns) and a named (or its PH) has a chance to spawn.**
**Step #8:**
\- Kill cliknar gassers 0/2
\-\-\- Kill any two of the cliknar gassers that swarm the bridge.
\- Kill body snatchers 0/2 (Bixie Warfront)
\-\-\- Kill two of the body snatchers that appear on either side of the bridge.
\- Kill a possessed bixie 0/1 (Bixie Warfront)
\-\-\- Several "a possessed \_\_\_\_\_" mobs swarm the bridge; kill one of them.
**Step #9:**
\- Flee to the tunnel path in the northwest 0/1
\-\-\- Location update (tunnel in the northwestern corner of the battlefield).

\-\-\- Location update (tunnel that extends north from the river).
\- Flee to the hive in the west 0/1
\-\-\- Location update -2430, -1180, -50.

**TASK IN DETAIL**
========================================================================
TRIGGERING THE TASK
========================================================================
You say, 'Embark on an adventure?'
Battlebreaker Kraklest says 'Good. Let me know when you're [ready] to depart and I'll ensure you make it to the battlefield safely.'
You have been assigned the task 'General Sagrinta's Last Stand'.
A swarming cliknar army has cut off General Sagrinta from the rest of the bixie military. Stranded on the central bridge with a small consort of troops, she'll need a miracle to make it out alive.
You say, 'Ready'
Battlebreaker Kraklest says 'Be swift and strong, \_\_\_\_\_. But most of all, be brave. It's a dark world you're stepping into.'
LOADING, PLEASE WAIT...
You have entered Bixie Warfront.
Battlebreaker Kraklest says 'Thanks for coming with me, \_\_\_\_\_. As you can see, the situation is dire. We couldn't possibly [leave] them stranded here.'
========================================================================
STEP #1
========================================================================
Keep General Sagrinta alive 0/1 (Bixie Warfront)
_Task Window Says:_ Your primary objective is to keep General Sagrinta alive, at all costs.
_If You Get This Step: This step updates automatically at the end of the task. Note that the General has a signiciant amount of hitpoints and apparently cannot die (seems to lock health at 1%)._
========================================================================
STEP #2
========================================================================
Speak with General Sagrinta 0/1 (Bixie Warfront)
_Task Window Says:_ You're holed up with General Sagrinta on the bridge over the river in the battlefield, and the cliknar are getting ready to assault your position. Speak with General Sagrinta on the bridge to find out how you can help prepare defenses.
_If You Get This Step: Run through General Sagrinta's dialogue._
You say, 'Hail, General Sagrinta'
General Sagrinta interrupts her conversation with Battlebreaker Kraklest to address you. 'I'm glad you're here, \_\_\_\_\_. I owe you and Kraklest a big debt for sneaking through the river passage to [help] us.'
You say, 'Help?'
General Sagrinta smiles and says, 'It's happened so fast, I've barely had time to process it myself. The cliknar [offensive] caught us all off-guard.'
You say, 'Offensive?'
General Sagrinta says 'The cliknar army launched a [raid] in the middle of last night, and took most of the Stone Hive's territory on this battlefield before we were even aware of it.'
You say, 'Raid?'
General Sagrinta says 'Soldiers, drones, traitors. They threw everything at us. We were lucky to [escape] with what we have here.'
You say, 'Escape?'
General Sagrinta glares menacingly at the cliknar gathering in the nearby trenches and says, 'Make no mistake, \_\_\_\_\_. We will not surrender. We will [hold this ground].'
You say, 'Hold this ground?'
General Sagrinta says 'The bulk of the bixie army was able to retreat back to the main hive while we held our ground and drew the cliknars' attention. But now we're cut off. We can't expect reinforcements. We only have [each other].'
You say, 'Each other?'
General Sagrinta slams you on the back and smirks, 'We've fought through impossible odds before, \_\_\_\_\_. I have no doubt we'll do it again.
_If the next step involves Sticky Feet, you see:_
General Sagrinta says 'And, for what it's worth, Sticky Feet, the finest battlemount in the Skyrider Legion, flew down to aid us! Why don't you feed him this Honey-Soaked Ham to make sure he's ready for battle?'
Sticky Feet looks hungrily at the Honey-Soaked Ham in your bag.
_If the next step involves Engineer Friliim, you see:_
General Sagrinta says 'We're lucky that Thunder Gun stayed with us during the chaos last night. I heard you fought alongside him in the prison break, so I'm sure you know what he's capable of.'
General Sagrinta blushes slightly and glances toward Engineer Friliim and whispers, 'Plus, he is totally... Never mind! Carry on!'
General Sagrinta says 'Tell Thunder Gun I'm glad he's here.'
_If the next step involves explosives, you see:_
General Sagrinta says 'Besides, we've got explosive flowers this time! Plant some of these Boom Blooms around the area. They'll explode when cliknar step on them, so spread them out.'
========================================================================
STEP #3
========================================================================
Feed the Honey-Soaked Ham to Sticky Feet on the bridge 0/1 (Bixie Warfront)
_Task Window Says:_ Sticky Feet, the butterfly battlemount that you rescued while breaking Engineer Friliim out of prison, has flown to the bridge to aid General Sagrinta. Feed it the Honey-Soaked Ham to get it ready for combat, and receive the Swarm Whistle. The Swarm Whistle can be used one time only to summon a large pack of butterfly battlemounts that will defend the camp for a short while.
_If You Get This Step: When ready for a fight, hand in the ham._
_You receive "Elena's Skyrider Legion Horn" which allows a one-time click to summon help during the event._
=====================
Talk to Engineer Friliim on the bridge 0/1 (Bixie Warfront)
_Task Window Says:_ Engineer Friliim, the bixie hero you recently helped rescue from prison, has vowed to defend General Sagrinta to the death. Go talk to Engineer Friliim on the bridge and get his fan whistle. The Thunder Gun Fan Whistle can be used one time by each person in your group to summon fanatic Thunder Gun fans to aid you for a short while.
_If You Get This Step: This is a dialogue update. Don't talk to him until you're ready for a fight._
You say, 'Hail, Engineer Friliim'
Engineer Friliim smiles at you and says, 'Boy, am I glad to see you, \_\_\_\_\_! There's no one I want by my side more when facing down [death].'
You say, 'Death?'
Engineer Friliim says 'Forgive my morbidness, friend. I've just never seen such daunting odds. Not even during the prison break. We're [vastly outnumbered].'
You say, 'Vastly outnumbered?'
Engineer Friliim smirks and shows you a whistle, 'But you know that [The Thunder Gun] always has a trick up his sleeve.'
You say, 'The Thunder Gun?'
Engineer Friliim looks a little embarrassed and says, 'Having your own fan club has its perks. There are a few [super fans] that'd follow me anywhere. Even way out here.'
You say, 'Super fans?'
Engineer Friliim says 'I can't thank you enough for coming out here to help us. You're risking your life for ours.'
Engineer Friliim hands you the whistle and laughs, 'Don't worry. I know I'll still owe you drinks after we make it out of here. But, this is my thanks for now. Just blow on the whistle when you're in trouble, and some bixies will arrive to help.
=====================
Plant Boom Blooms on or near the bridge 0/10 (Bixie Warfront)
_Task Window Says:_ General Sagrinta has given you Boom Blooms to defend the area. Boom Blooms will detonate when an enemy troop steps on it, damaging and knocking them backwards. You can only place 10, so choose their positions carefully. A quick scan of the surrounding area makes you believe that enemy troops will charge from both sides of the bridge, east and west.
_If You Get This Step: Right-click the item on and around the bridge. Don't do this until you're ready for a fight._
========================================================================
STEP #4
========================================================================
Kill bixie traitors 0/5 (Bixie Warfront)
_Task Window Says:_ Bixie traitors see an opportunity to sabotage the general once and for all. They launch an assault from the sky around the bridge. Kill them.
_If You Get This Step: Hostile bixies can spawn from either side of the bridge (separated in spawns by about 20 seconds) and attack friendly NPCs._
=====================
Kill cliknar commandos 0/5 (Bixie Warfront)
_Task Window Says:_ Cliknar commandos launch an assault from the east. Kill them before they hurt the general.
_If You Get This Step: Cliknar commandos spawn from the east side of the bridge (separated in spawns by about 20 seconds) and attack friendly NPCs._
=====================
Kill cliknar vanguards 0/5 (Bixie Warfront)
_Task Window Says:_ Cliknar vanguards launch an assault from both sides of the bridge! Kill them quickly, before they can summon reinforcements.
_If You Get This Step: Mobs called "a cliknar vanguard" spawn one at a time, separated by about 20 seconds from either side of the bridge. If left alive too long, 2x "a summoned guardian" spawn and attack as well. Vanguards hit for a max ~12,000 while guardians hit for a max ~4,500. All mobs are mezzable and stunnable._
=====================
Kill slow moles 0/5 (Bixie Warfront)
_Task Window Says:_ A herd of stampeding moles, released from their cages, run at the bridge from the west! Avoid their crushing herd and kill the slow chargers.
_If You Get This Step: Stampeding moles spawn and rush across the bridge. Soon after, mobs called "a slow charger" spawn and attack (separated by about 20 seconds). They "charge" random targets. Kill them to update the task._
========================================================================
STEP #5
========================================================================
Kill cliknar assassins 0/6 (Bixie Warfront)
_Task Window Says:_ Cliknar assassins launch an assault from both sides of the bridge! They will sprint directly to General Sagrinta. Control them and kill them to protect the general.
_If You Get This Step: Mobs called "a focused assassin" spawn one at a time (separated by about 20 seconds), and can appear at either side of the bridge. They go straight for the General, and can backstab for 40,000 dmg (mezzable; stunnable; hit for a max ~13,000)._
=====================
Kill cliknar enslavers 0/6 (Bixie Warfront)
_Task Window Says:_ Cliknar enslavers launch an assault from the east! Kill them without mercy, because these cruel manipulators will charm your companions to fight with them.
_If You Get This Step: Cliknar enslavers spawn and attack from the east in groups of two (separated in spawns by about 45 seconds). They cast a charm called "Enslave" that inflicts friendly NPCs (mezzable; stunnable; hit for a max ~13,000)._
=====================
Kill wild stingers 0/6 (Bixie Warfront)
_Task Window Says:_ Wild stingers swoop through the cavern and assault your position from the sky around the bridge. Kill them.
_If You Get This Step: Mobs called "a wild stinger" spawn two at a time, one from each side of the bridge. These hit for a max ~11,000 and backstab for ~39,000. They are mezzable and stunnable._
=====================
Kill bloody recruits 0/6 (Bixie Warfront)
_Task Window Says:_ ?
_If You Get This Step: Bloody recruits spawn and attack._
========================================================================
STEP #6
========================================================================
General Sagrinta is bleeding out! Recover Bixie Bandages and heal her 0/5 (Bixie Warfront)
_Task Window Says:_ General Sagrinta was wounded and is losing blood rapidly. Find bixie medics in the northwest and southwest bixie trenches and kill the cliknar attacking them to get Bixie Bandages. Once you've collected enough bandages, return to the bridge and give the Bixie Bandages to General Sagrinta to close her wounds.
_If You Get This Step: Head into the trenches in the western half of the zone, seek out "a dying medic", and kill the cliknar torturer next to it. The cliknar drops "Bixie Bandages". Do this five times, then deliver them to the General._
=====================
General Sagrinta is dehydrated! Find Soggy Kelp and give them to her 0/5 (Bixie Warfront)
_Task Window Says:_ General Sagrinta has collapsed from dehydration. Find kelp draggers in the river beneath the bridge to get Soggy Kelp. Once you've collected enough, return to the bridge and give the Soggy Kelp to General Sagrinta to restore her strength.
_If You Get This Step: Kill mobs called "a kelp dragger" in the river until you've looted 5x "Soggy Kelp", and then deliver them to the General._
=====================
General Sagrinta is demoralized! Rescue kidnapped bixie children to reassure her 0/5 (Bixie Warfront)
_Task Window Says:_ General Sagrinta is demoralized and believes hope for the bixies' future is lost. Find kidnapped bixie children in the northeast and southeast cliknar trenches and kill the cliknar guarding them to rescue them. Once you've freed them, lead them back to General Sagrinta on the bridge to show her there's still hope.
_If You Get This Step: In the northeast and southeast trenches, kill cliknar around kidnapped bixie children._
========================================================================
STEP #7
========================================================================
Kill the swarming stingers 0/20 (Bixie Warfront)
_Task Window Says:_ A swarm of angry stingers has erupted from the battlefield. Kill all the swarming stingers on the bridge before they do the cliknar's work for them!
_If You Get This Step: Kill 20x "a swarming stinger" (hit for a max ~2,000 each and spawn in sets of 10, about 30 seconds apart)._
=====================
Kill General Rikkik 0/1 (Bixie Warfront)
_Task Window Says:_ The cliknar general has rushed onto the battlefield from the west to end this himself. Kill General Rikkik on the bridge before he wipes out your small camp.
_If You Get This Step: General Rikkik hits for a max ~15,000; has 2-3 times the hitpoints of an average mob here; mem blurs himself; and procs a stun spell on random targets._
=====================
Kill Trook, the Hive Breaker 0/1 (Bixie Warfront)
_Task Window Says:_ ?
_If You Get This Step: "Trook, the Hive Breaker" spawns from the west and attacks. It hits for a max ~13,000; has an emote-based AE for 9,750 (fully mitigated), as well as a damage proc. It also randomly leaps onto its opponents._
========================================================================
STEP #8
========================================================================
Kill cliknar gassers 0/2 (Bixie Warfront)
_Task Window Says:_ Cliknar gassers rush toward the bridge from the east, attempting to kill everyone with poisonous gas. Kill cliknar gassers on the bridge as quickly as you can.
_If You Get This Step: The bridge gets swarmed by mobs called "a volatile gasser" which cover the bridge in a damage aura (about 3,250/tick fully mitigated with spell shielding). Kill two of them to update the task, and then flee the bridge. Note that these mobs have odd aggro in that they may not aggro you in favor of simply gassing the bridge. They can and do, however, attack back if provoked, and assist each other once one is attacked._
=====================
Kill body snatchers 0/2 (Bixie Warfront)
_Task Window Says:_ ?
_If You Get This Step: Kill two of the body snatchers that appear at either end of the bridge._
=====================
Kill a possessed bixie 0/1 (Bixie Warfront)
_Task Window Says:_ ?
_If You Get This Step: Several "a possessed \_\_\_\_\_" mobs swarm the bridge; kill one of them._
========================================================================
STEP #9
========================================================================
Flee to the tunnel path in the northwest 0/1 (Bixie Warfront)
_Task Window Says:_ The bridge is lost! General Sagrinta has called for a full retreat through the tunnel path in the northwest. Run through the west battlefield and meet up with Boogzt Umporz in the tunnel.
_If You Get This Step: Head northwest into the tunnel (the same tunnel that was the zone-in point for the first heroic adventure in this task arc)._
Battlebreaker Kraklest says 'General, we're losing troops too quickly. We'll be completely defenseless soon!'
General Sagrinta says 'We're surrounded and have nowhere to run. We must hold this position!'
Boogzt Umporz shouts, 'I help all bixies! I can smash wall to main hive for escape route!'
Battlebreaker Kraklest says 'If he opens up a path to the main hive, we need to stop the cliknar from rushing through it.'
General Sagrinta says 'You're right. There are thousands of civilians in there.'
General Sagrinta shouts, 'All able bixies and allies, to me! The main hive needs our aid!'
Battlebreaker Kraklest says 'We will not let them hurt civilians, general. You have my word.'
=====================
Flee to the underwater passage in the north 0/1 (Bixie Warfront)
_Task Window Says:_ The bridge is lost! General Sagrinta has called for a full retreat to the northern water passage. Jump in the river and swim north until you find Infiltrator Nallai. Keep swimming until you're safe.
_If You Get This Step: Along the northern boundary of the river is an underwater passageway (the entrance is near the surface of the water). Enter the tunnel and swim to the end to update the task._
=====================
Flee to the hive in the west 0/1 (Bixie Warfront)
_Task Window Says:_ ?
_If You Get This Step: Location update (somewhere?)._
========================================================================
STEP #10
========================================================================
Speak with General Sagrinta 0/1 (Bixie Warfront)
_Task Window Says:_ General Sagrinta spent the last of her energy to conjure a magical purge of the battlefield, wiping out the cliknar threat. Speak with the dying General Sagrinta to receive her last wishes before she passes.
_If You Get This Step: Dialogue update._
You say, 'Hail, General Sagrinta'
General Sagrinta looks back towards the battlefield and says with concern, 'There are too many of them, \_\_\_\_\_. We can't just [run away].
You say, 'Run away?'
General Sagrinta says 'Someone needs to stop them. I have the power, but it will be taxing.'
General Sagrinta pauses before saying, '[Swear] to me that you'll defend my people, \_\_\_\_\_. Hold the Wet River Hive to their [promise] to aid us.'
You say, 'I swear...'
General Sagrinta says 'Thank you, \_\_\_\_\_.'
General Sagrinta begins to shake with energy and shouts, 'Everyone back away from me!'
General Sagrinta begins casting a powerful spell.
General Sagrinta chants rhythmically as magical power ripples off of her body.
General Sagrinta roars as a final swell of power bursts out of her body toward the battlefield.
You hear the terrified cries of cliknar echo in the distance.
General Sagrinta collapses to the ground, her body still pulsating with power.
You fought alongside General Sagrinta and the bixie army in a hopeless situation. Casualties were high, but the Stone Hive military will survive to defend against the cliknar onslaught for at least one more day.

_This group mission begins with Guard Hambert ( **click for map image**) in the Ethernere Tainted West Karana (found in the same guard camp as Commander Galenth, due north of the dragon's pit)._
_Pre-Requisites for Requesting:_
\- Level 95
\- Completed "Overcoming the Cataclysm"
\- Completed "Not of this World"
\- Completed "Answering the Call"
\- Completed "The Doomscale Horde" (group mission)
You say, 'Hail, Guard Hambert'
Guard Hambert says 'Shhh... The commander will hear you! I know you fought along side Galenth against the Doomscale, but I worry he has become obsessed with this veiled dragon near the corruption. If Galenth knew what I had [done], I'd be dead by now.'
You say, 'What have you done?'
Guard Hambert says 'I couldn't help it. I had to get a look at this dragon for myself, and once I did, I felt compelled to speak to it. Please, you musn't tell the commander, I have a family to feed back in Qeynos, and he'd have my head if he knew I [spoke] to the Veiled Lady.'
You say, 'You spoke to it?'
Guard Hambert says 'I know I shouldn't have, but I didn't want to just follow orders and kill such a magnificent creature! What she told me goes against everything Galenth has said. He seems unwilling to listen to any discussion or planning that doesn't involve her death. I am just one man, but you, you have an army behind you! Perhaps Galenth would [listen] to you. I fear the end of Norrath as we know it if he cannot be convinced.'
You say, 'Listen to me?'
Guard Hambert says 'Would you be willing to [try] to talk sense into him? It could be the only way to stem the tide of [blood] being shed here. I have seen enough death and destruction for a lifetime in these short months.'
You say, 'We'll try'
Guard Hambert says 'Perhaps there is yet hope for us all! The commander is leaving soon to lead the attack upon the dragon. If you were to [hurry], I think you could get there first, and hopefully talk some sense into him.'
You have been assigned the task 'The Defense of Lendiniara'.
With the Doomscale Cult no longer barring the way, Commander Galenth now seeks to destroy Lady Lendiniara. New information suggests she is not the evil he thinks her to be, and in fact her death could bring about more corruption and destruction on Norrath.
You say, 'Let's hurry...'
LOADING, PLEASE WAIT...
You have entered Ethernere Tainted West Karana
_NOTE: This task is locked from the start._

---

**Task Steps**
Speak to Commander Galenth 0/1 (Ethernere Tainted West Karana)
_Task Window Says:_ Speak to Commander Galenth and try to talk to him out of assaulting Lady Lendiniara.
Kill Guard Vraymon 0/1 (Ethernere Tainted West Karana)
Kill Guard Teclisin 0/1 (Ethernere Tainted West Karana)
Kill Lieutenant Rolth 0/1 (Ethernere Tainted West Karana)
_Task Window Says:_ Defeat the Qeynos guards sent in to attack Lady Lendiniara.
Kill Commander Galenth 0/1 (Ethernere Tainted West Karana)
Open the Armory Cache left by the Qeynos Guards 0/1 (Ethernere Tainted West Karana)

---

**In Detail**
_Here, you have to kill several waves of Qeynos guards, each including a named guard, ending with the death of Commander Galenth. Lady Lendiniara (their kill target) cannot die, or the task fails._
_All hostile mobs in this task prioritize Lady Lendiniara as their target, so if left idle (or memblurred), they will attack her._
_Say "die" to Commander Galenth, then either "test" (to trigger the named guards one-by-one) or "quick death" (to trigger them all at once):_
You say, 'Hail, Commander Galenth'
Commander Galenth says 'Stand aside, welp! I have a job to do and nobody, not even you will stand in my way. I suppose I should thank you for helping dispatch the Doomscale cult, but if you stand against me, you'll [die] like all the rest. My blade has tasted Dragon blood before. Your only hope for survival would be to tuck your tail, and [leave] now.'
You say, 'Die? Us?'
Commander Galenth says 'The only question is how. I would like to [test] the capabilities of my subordinates one at a time, but I would grant you a [quick death] if you prefer. The choice is yours, but the end will be the same, regardless.'
You say, 'Let's test them...'
Commander Galenth shouts 'Very well then! Guard Vraymon, end these traitor's lives, and be quick about it!'
Guard Vraymon shouts 'Your faith in me will be rewarded with dragon blood, Commander!'
_Guard Vraymon is solo and hits for a max ~18,000. He casts two spells:_
Cursed Ether: PB AE 200', (0)
1: Decrease Spell Haste by 25%
2: Limit: Minimum Mana Cost (10)
3: Decrease Attack Speed by 25%
4: Decrease Movement by 25%
5: Decrease Hitpoints by 9800 per tick
6: Increase Curse Counter by 24
Etherblade: PB AE 50', Magic (0)
1: Decrease Hitpoints by 41550
Guard Vraymon has been slain by \_\_\_\_\_!
Commander Galenth shouts 'A shame really. Vraymon was a dedicated soldier, and an exemplary guardian. Sad that his combat prowess did not match his loyalty. Teclisin, FRONT AND CENTER! May Qeynos' light guide your blade!'
Guard Teclisin shouts 'To arms! We dine on dragon flesh, tonight!'
_Guard Teclisin is also solo and hits for a max ~18,000. He traps random targets in a burning prison (30 seconds; can be DPS'd to free the target sooner):_
Burning Prison: Single Target, Unresistable (0)
1: Decrease Spell Haste by 10000%
2: Limit: Minimum Mana Cost (1)
3: Decrease Attack Speed by 99%
4: Decrease Movement by 10000%
5: Negate CHA Effect
Guard Teclisin has been slain by \_\_\_\_\_!
Commander Galenth shouts 'The light of Teclisin has gone dark. Perhaps I should take care of this myself?'
Lieutenant Rolth shouts 'Stand fast, sir! My squad and I can handle this!'
_Lieutenant Rolth comes with 4x "a Qeynos guard". The guards don't cast spells, but the Lieutenant casts "Etherburn":_
Etherburn: Single Target, Unresistable (0)
1: Decrease Hitpoints by 11220 to 14685 (random)
Lieutenant Rolth has been slain by \_\_\_\_\_!
Commander Galenth shouts 'It seems it falls to me. As great men have said, if you want something done right, do it yourself. Prepare yourselves, for death is upon you!'
_Commander Galenth is last, and also comes with 4x "a Qeynos guard". The Commander hits for a max ~27,000; occasionally memblurs himself; and casts "Cursed Ether" (as above) and "Whirling Blade":_
Cursed Ether: PB AE 200', (0)
1: Decrease Spell Haste by 40%
2: Limit: Minimum Mana Cost (10)
3: Decrease Attack Speed by 40%
4: Decrease Movement by 50%
5: Decrease Hitpoints by 9800 per tick
6: Increase Curse Counter by 65
Whirling Blade: Single Target, (-950)
1: Decrease Hitpoints by 44275
_Kill Commander Galenth (and any remaining adds) to complete the event._
Commander Galenth has been slain by \_\_\_\_\_!
Lady Lendiniara shouts 'Poor soul. He did not fully comprehend the forces at work here, and was too blinded by his conviction to listen to reason. Hopefully not all will be as single-minded. You have fought bravely. Take what you wish from the fallen, but know, this is far from over!'

**Call of the Forsaken Info & Guides: Overview \| Progression & Task List \| Raiding \| Visible Armor**

---

_This raid expedition begins with Adana Baxter ( **click for map image**) in the Dead Hills. She's found outside Skulk's tent (western side) at about -1310, -525, 3._
_Pre-Requisites for Requesting:_
\- Level 95
\- Completed "For the Sake of the Forsaken"
\- Completed "The Doomscale Horde" (raid event)
\- Completed "Xulous Prime" (raid event)
_Pre-Requisites for Participating:_
\- Completed "The Doomscale Horde" (raid event)
\- Completed "Xulous Prime" (raid event)
You say, 'Hail, Adana Baxter'
Adana Baxter says 'Hey, can't you see I'm busy? Wait a minute. Aren't you \_\_\_\_\_? Oh, I've heard of you. Listen, I could use a [hand]. I'm working for the Qeynos government.'
You say, 'A hand?'
Adana Baxter says 'Right. You see this Ethernere business? Well, it looks like it's affecting the Tower of Rot. Yes, that Tower. And it appears that the long lost should-be-dead Kyle Bayle is finally emerging from that decaying, festering eyesore. Things are moving quickly here, and if we don't stop him, who knows what would happen next? I was just trying to figure out a way to do it, and then a hero walks right up to me. Convenient, that. So, what do you think? You'll help stop that old Bayle, right? Did you want to bring a small [group] to take care of him quickly or a larger [raid] force to meet those monsters head on?'
You say, 'I have a raid'
Adana Baxter says 'A little risky, but you're still alive so far so I guess you know what you're doing. Are you [ready]?'
You say, 'Ready!'
Adana Baxter says 'This way.'
LOADING, PLEASE WAIT...
You have entered The Dead Hills.

---

**Summary**
\- Clear undead mobs from the area (a handful of "a royal \_\_\_\_\_" in the area - relatively simple mobs).
\- Say "work" to Kyle Bayle to trigger the event, making the Captain + 3 Sergeants auto-aggro.
\- Kill Sergeant Grady, Sergeant Brenn, Sergeant Larenzo, and Captain Kanteer to complete the event.
\-\-\- Sergeant Grady casts an AE root + melee slow.
\-\-\- Sergeant Brenn procs a nuke for 75,000 dmg on his target.
\-\-\- Sergeant Larenzo mitigates incoming melee damage by 50% and is extremely spell-resistant.
\-\-\- Captain Kanteer spawns 3 adds every 10% health and every 2 minutes; casts Brenn's nuke; and uses Larenzo's buff.

---

**Triggering the Event**
_On the other side of the tower, you find Kyle Bayle, his Captain, and three Sergeants. Say "work" to Kyle Bayle to make them all aggro._
You say, 'Hail, Kyle Bayle'
Kyle Bayle speaks with determination, 'Look around you, \_\_\_\_\_. The [Tower] lives and breathes, built with the will of my army and the people of Qeynos.'
You say, 'What about this tower?'
Kyle Bayle says 'We labor and toil, our work never finished. Yet here you stand before me, wasting precious time with your idle chatter.' If you aren't here to [work], then we'll use your bones the build the tower!'
You say, 'Your work will stop!'
Kyle Bayle says 'The delusional ghost of Kyle Bayle calls out to his sergeants, 'Larenzo! Grady! Brenn! We have a visitor who has offered up their blood and bones to help build our tower!''
_At this time, the Captain and all three Sergeants attack. Kyle Bayle remains non-aggro._

---

**Event Mechanics**
_\- Event mobs can be pulled anywhere in the event area; they have no leash range._
_\- There are no balance requirements._
_\- The Sergeants can be killed in any order._
_\- The Captain cannot be killed until all three Sergeants have been killed._
_\- None of the mobs summon until damaged below 98%._
_\- The Sergeants and Captain are immune to all forms of crowd control (but are kiteable)._
_\- The royal guard adds are immune to stuns and roots. Soldiers are mezzable; others are not._
_\- All of the royal guard adds are charmable._

---

**The Three Sergeants**
_Sergeant Grady hits for a max ~65,000 and casts "Arrows of Rot" every 20 seconds:_
Arrows of Rot: Area PC 100', Disease (-900)
1: Decrease Hitpoints by 9000 per tick
2: Root
3: Inhibit Melee Attacks by 15%
_Sergeant Brenn hits for a max ~65,000 and casts "Guardsman's Fury" every 60 seconds:_
Guardsman's Fury: Single Target, Corruption (-200)
1: Decrease Hitpoints by 75000
2: Stun (0.01 sec)
_Sergeant Larenzo hits for a max ~65,000 and buffs himself with "Protection of Bayle" every 20 seconds (buff lasts 36 seconds, so will remain on him indefinitely):_
Protection of Bayle: Self 0', Unresistable (0)
1: Mitigate Melee Damage by 50%
2: Increase All Resists by 1750
3: Increase Corruption Resist by 1750
4: Increase Corruption counter by 99
5: Increase AC by 60

---

**Captain Kanteer**
_Captain Kanteer hits for a max ~95,000 and spawns a wave of 3x "a royal \_\_\_\_\_" undead adds every 2 minutes and every 10% health. Adds hit for a max ~15,500._
_Every 60 seconds, he buffs himself with "Protection of Bayle":_
Protection of Bayle: Self 0', Unresistable (0)
1: Mitigate Melee Damage by 50%
2: Increase All Resists by 1750
3: Increase Corruption Resist by 1750
4: Increase Corruption counter by 99
5: Increase AC by 60
_He casts "Hidden Misericorde" on random targets (3-tick debuff):_
Hidden Misericorde: Single Target, (-200)
1: Decrease HP when cast by 28000
2: Decrease Hitpoints by 2500 per tick
3: Inhibit Melee Attacks by (90%)
4: Decrease Movement by 5%
5: Decrease STR by 500
6: Decrease DEX by 500
7: Decrease AGI by 500
8: Decrease STA by 500
9: Decrease INT by 500
10: Decrease WIS by 500
_Every 20 seconds, he casts "Guardsman's Fury" on his target:_
Guardsman's Fury: Single Target, Corruption (-200)
1: Decrease Hitpoints by 75000
2: Stun (0.01 sec)
_If he is below 75% health and any of the Sergeants are still alive, he casts "Motivate the Troops":_
Motivate the Troops: Self 0', Unresistable (0)
1: Increase Hitpoints by 1000000
_If any Sergeants are still alive, Captain Kanteer remains health-locked at 50% health. He is otherwise killable._

---

**Failure & Reset**
_If the Captain or any of the Sergeants are left idle for a short period of time, the event does a full reset:_
Kyle Bayle's troops have had time to regroup. You have failed.

---

**Event Completion**
_Kill the three Sergeants and Captain Kanteer to complete the event. Note that the royal undead adds do NOT despawn upon the event's completion._
Captain Kanteer has been slain by \_\_\_\_\_!
If you stand against me, you stand against Qeynos. Progress cannot be stopped, my friend. This Tower will be completed, and neither you nor the others will stop us.
_Kyle Bayle disappears into the tower, and a chest appears at Adana Baxter's location near the zone-in._

---

**Loot**
_NOTE: In addition to chest loot, everyone in the zone receives 20x "Medal of Heroism" (used as an alternative way obtain raid drops, from Requisitioner Sheon ( **map image**), the Tier 1 raid vendor in the northwestern Qeynos camp of the Ethernere Tainted West Karana)._
_Chest loot consists of 2 armor pieces (first list) + 2-3 items (second list) + 1-2 weapons (third list) + 4 "Captured Essence of Ethernere" (spell rune):_
Armguards of Suppressed Ether
Band of Unquenched Bloodlust
Brilliant Ring of Empty Oaths
Buckler of the Roaring Lion
Dirt Stained Band of Twisted Roots
Emblem of Lost Vows
Emerald Loop of Salvation
Fallen Corporal's Engagement Band
Gaudy Ruby Encrusted Band
Loop of Nature's Call
Sycophant's Carved Loop
Tarnished Captain's Wedding Ring
Lance of the Icestorm
Lieutenant's Ornamental Blade
Wand of Wanton Disease
- Medal of Heroism [item=113132]

---

**Call of the Forsaken Info & Guides: Overview \| Progression & Task List \| Raiding \| Visible Armor**

---

_This group mission begins with Noxhil V`Sek ( **click for map image**) in Neriak Fourth Gate._
_Pre-Requisites for Requesting:_
\- Level 95
\- Completed "The Missing Scroll"
\- Completed "Fourth Gate Investigation"
\- Completed "Cautious Recruitment"
\- Completed "Houses of Thex" (group mission)
\- Must be illusioned as a Dark Elf
**Steps & Summary**
\- Say "band" to request the task and "meet" to zone in.
Gain entry to the library 0/1 (Neriak Fourth Gate)
Obtain the Scroll of G'Han 0/1 (Neriak Fourth Gate)
Open the chest 0/1 (Neriak Fourth Gate)
\- Note: The NPCs Kavi and Xavier have beneficial auras that mitigate spell and melee damage, so it's in your interest to keep them alive (they can be buffed and healed). (There is an achievement for allowing them to die, however.)
FIRST PHASE:
\- Inside the instance, say "ready" to Prince Tarant Thex to begin the event.
\- After a short delay, two mobs spawn ("a spiteful lich" and "a spiteful shade").
\-\-\- "a spiteful lich" has a damage aura around it and is snareable/mezzable.
\-\-\- "a spiteful shade" casts a 10k spell damage shield when low in health (is mezzable).
\- There a minimum number of mobs that must be killed to progress the task, and until you meet that requirement, you'll always have at least two mobs up at a time.
\- Mobs called "a spiteful specter" spawn randomly upon lich and shade deaths. These also must be killed.
\- Specters will focus their DPS on the friendly NPCs (Kavi and Xavier).
\- Once you've completed this step, you'll have a short break.
SECOND PHASE:
\- Meet Prince Tarant Thex in the upper floor of the library.
\- A script unfolds, and then he goes aggro along with two Dreadguard adds.
\- The Prince summons two Dreadguard adds every 20% health.
\- All adds must die before the event can be completed.
\- Dreadguard swordsmen are charmable.
\- Dreadguard conjurors cast AE damage spells.
\- Kill all adds and bring the Prince to 3% health for the win.

---

**Requesting the Task**
You say, 'Hail, Noxhil V`Sek'
Noxhil V`Sek says 'The prince has informed me that the time is nigh for infiltrating the library. I will leave it up to you whether to proceed with a small [band] of friends or a large [force]. Otherwise, you may recruit a [group] for infiltrating the library or let me know if you are [interested] in keeping the scroll out of the hands of the King and Queen.'
You say, 'I have a small band'
Noxhil V`Sek says 'Let us hope you are up to the task. I will arrange for you to [meet] with Prince Tarant when you are ready.'
You have been assigned the task 'Hate Rising'.
The prince has agreed to help you gain entry to the library and help you acquire the Scroll of G'Han. He does not wish either the king or queen to have it as it would upset the balance of power in Neriak.
You say, 'We'll meet him there'
Noxhil V`Sek says 'Success unto you, \_\_\_\_\_.'
LOADING, PLEASE WAIT...
You have entered Neriak - Fourth Gate.

---

**PHASE ONE: Triggering the Event**
_Speak with Prince Tarant Thex to begin the event ("ready"). He and his two cohorts will move to the library door, then two adds will spawn (1x "a spiteful lich" + 1x "a spiteful shade")._
You say, 'Hail, Prince Tarant Thex'
Prince Tarant Thex says, in Dark Elvish, 'I have managed to obtain the key to the library, though its caretakers will likely discover it missing soon. I know not what we will find inside so be prepared for anything. Let me know when you are [ready] to enter. You may also [leave] if you are too cowardly to continue.'
You say, 'Ready!'
Prince Tarant Thex says, in Dark Elvish, 'Very well. Let us proceed.'
_The three NPCs path up to the library entrance, at which point you see:_
Prince Tarant Thex leaps backwards as he shouts 'Get back, there's a spell on it!'

---

**PHASE ONE: The Spiteful Undead**
_"a spiteful lich" is snareable and mezzable. It has an aura around it called "Lich Touch":_
Lich Touch: Single Target, Cold (-650)
1: Decrease Hitpoints by 6500
_It also casts "Icy Rain":_
Icy Rain: Targeted AE 30', Cold (-999)
1: Decrease Hitpoints by 15000
_"a spiteful shade" is mezzable. It procs "Shade Touch":_
Shade Touch: Single Target, Cold (-999)
1: Decrease Hitpoints by 1500
_When low in health, they cast "Shield of Spite" on themselves:_
Shield of Spite: Self 0', Unresistable (-650)
1: Spell Damage Shield (10158)
_Occasionally, as you kill the above mobs, you'll get "a spiteful specter" spawn. These mobs focus their DPS on Kavi and Xavier, so if you're keeping them alive, be sure to pay attention to their health while a specter is up. The specters don't cast any AEs._
_Kill a certain amount of "spiteful" mobs to progress the task._
Prince Tarant Thex mutters to himself 'Nothing is ever easy when mother is involved.' He surveys the scene around him and says 'We must must move quickly now. I can give you a minute to rest but no more.'

---

**PHASE TWO: Encounter in the Library**
_Next, find Prince Tarant Thex upstairs. A script will unfold:_
Prince Tarant Thex shouts, in Dark Elvish, 'This is it!'
Prince Tarant Thex says, in Dark Elvish, 'And now that I have it, you are no longer necessary.'
Prince Tarant Thex shouts, in Dark Elvish, 'Dreadguards!'
_Two Dreadguards enter the area (not aggro yet)._
Prince Tarant Thex smiles as he says 'You see, I had no intention of allowing you to possess the scroll as I have already promised it to my love. Once I have silenced you, I will deliver it to her.'
Prince Tarant Thex shouts, in Dark Elvish, 'Dreadguards, forward!'
_All three mobs are now aggro._
_"Dreadguard conjuror" casts two AEs:_
Molten Beam: Area Hatelist 30', Fire (-999)
1: Decrease Hitpoints by 15000
Molten Rain: Targeted AE 30', Fire (-999)
1: Decrease Hitpoints by 15000
_"Dreadguard swordsman" casts no spells._
_The Prince summons two additional Dreadguard adds (one of each type) every 20% health. All adds have to be killed before Prince Tarant Thex can be defeated._
_Once all adds are dead and the Prince reaches 3% health, you've won the event._

---

**Event Completion**
_The Prince falls to the ground, seemingly unconscious, and Lanys T`Vyl appears..._
Lanys T`Vyl steps out from the shadows.
Lanys T`Vyl says, in Dark Elvish, 'It looks like I arrived just in time to save you.'
Lanys T`Vyl quickly searches the prince.
Lanys T`Vyl says in a sultry, sarcastic tone, 'I'll be taking that, my prince.'
Lanys T`Vyl looks up from the wounded prince toward your party, 'As for the rest of you, I shall leave you with these paltry rewards. After all, you've earned them. Despite the fact that you were driven here by the ramblings of a madman, your assistance in obtaining the scroll has proven invaluable.'
Lanys T`Vyl speaks with uncharacteristic concern, 'You're like mad dogs, powerful but aimless. So eager to serve at the hand of any master that offers you your next meal. You do realize that if that madman gets his hands on this scroll it will mean the end of Norrath, don't you?'
Lanys T`Vyl says, in Dark Elvish, 'I don't often waste my sincerities on your lot, but I offer you this truth, it is imperative that this treasure not fall into the hands of the forsaken. I suggest you reflect upon your arrangement with Zebuxoruk and really question where your loyalties lie.'
_Lanys T`Vyl gates out of the library while the Prince remains unconscious..._
_Rewards:_
225 platinum
Experience
_Possible Chest Loot (1 item + 0-1 armor piece + 0-1 spell per task):_
Cerulean Sash of the Spire
Elaborate Vine Pendant
Enigmatic Teir`Dal Eye

Rough Leather Girding
Royal Bixie Scepter
Boots of Latent Ether
Bracer of Latent Ether
Tunic of Latent Ether

Spell: Force of Abrogation Rk. II
Spell: Insidious Deflection Rk. II
Spell: Pillar of Ro Rk. II
Tome: Focused Clamor of Claws Rk. II
Tome: Focused Storm of Blades Rk. II
Tome: Reflex Rk. II
