**Call of the Forsaken Info & Guides: Overview \| Progression & Task List \| Raiding \| Visible Armor**

---

_This is an overview of pre-launch events leading up to the releases of the Tower of Rot (release: May 21, 2014) and Argin-Hiz (release: July 16, 2014)._

---

**Currently Live**
July 4, 2014 to September 6, 2014
Solo Task: Source of the Ash Wind [quest=7604] (Lanys T`Vyl in Ethernere Tainted West Karana - across from Lady Lendiniara)
Solo Task: Sympathy for the Forlorn [quest=7599] (Ambassador of Solusek Ro -- South Ro - east of the wizard spires - map)
Solo Task: No Sympathy for the Forlorn [quest=7600] (a frightened inhabitant -- South Ro - east of the wizard spires - map)
Solo Task: Dousing the Flames [quest=7601] (Guard Sloop -- South Ro - east of the wizard spires - map)
Solo Task: Fire Prevention [quest=7602] (Guard Jepper -- South Ro - east of the wizard spires - map)
Solo Task: Sifting Through the Ashes [quest=7603] (a Doomscale scout -- South Ro - east of the wizard spires - map)
July 7, 2014 to September 6, 2014
Solo Task: Embers of the Giants [quest=7607] (a Doomscale scout -- South Ro - east of the wizard spires - map)
Solo Task: Still the Ash Hurricane [quest=7608] (Guard Jepper -- South Ro - east of the wizard spires - map)
Solo Task: Topple the Ash Giants [quest=7606] (Guard Mulk -- North Ro - due south of the Freeport gate - map)

---

**Finished Events**
May 14, 2014 to July 7, 2014
Solo Task: Ether Too Near [quest=7525] (Captain Deidre in North Qeynos - next to the POK portal stone).
Solo Task: Heads for the Hills [quest=7527] (Captain Deidre).
Shared Task: From Whence They Came [quest=7526] (Gahlith Wrannstad in North Qeynos - next to the POK portal stone).
May 17, 2014 to July 7, 2014
Shared Task: Beyond the Barrier [quest=7529] (Erron Ailspoc in the Dead Hills - map)

---

**Tower of Rot Pre-Launch Achievement**
Completion of all four pre-launch tasks gives you credit for the " Defender of Qeynos [quest=7528]" achievement (access to title - suffix: "Defender of Qeynos")

---

**Argin-Hiz Pre-Launch Achievement**
Completion of all nine pre-launch tasks gives you credit for the " Flamewarder [quest=7604]" achievement (access to title - suffix: " , Flame Warder")

---

**What's Going On: Tower of Rot**
The lead-up to the Tower of Rot's opening began with Captain Russell Cooper [npc=44047]'s heroic adventure chain.
After having cleared a path [quest=7274] to the excavation site [quest=7275] in the Dead Hills [zone=942], you got a look at the Descending Tower [quest=7276]...
In time, you would encounter a rather angry Undead Lord by the name of Kyle Bayle [npc=45701], the apparent long-dead leader of the original ill-fated Qeynos expedition into the Dead Hills many years prior. While you managed to defeat Kyle Bayle's Captain and Sergeants, the Undead Lord managed to retreat back into his tower [quest=7413].
Meanwhile, the guards of Qeynos have been trying to remedy the disaster site that is West Karana. This situation has gone unresolved, however, and creatures of the Ethernere (having initially appeared due to the time ripples caused by Lady Lendiniara [npc=44340]'s appearance) have managed to breach the Qeynos Hills [zone=17] and the city of Qeynos [zone=4], itself.
Captain Deidre Harath [npc=46981] has arrived to deal with the problem in Qeynos, and Gahlith Wrannstad [npc=46980] \- one of the Qeynos caster guildmasters - has been tasked with finding a way to rid Qeynos of the Ethernere creatures for good.
The citizens of Qeynos aren't the only ones having a problem with the Ethernere invasion. These creatures have also begun to invade the site of the Tower of Rot, itself, and are being fought off by the tower's undead forces ( Erron Ailspoc [npc=47029] has been sent to recover some samples from these beings).
What links the Tower of Rot with those time ripples is not yet known for sure, but rumors suggest something inside the tower will lead to an answer.

---

**What's Going On: Argin-Hiz**
_From a June 2014 patch message:_
Warm winds begin to blow in Ethernere-Tainted West Karana [zone=939] and the South Desert of Ro [zone=43]. Winds like these must portend the arrival of something or someone. Stay alert for when the time comes!
_From a news announcement on July 2, 2014:_
"The ash pours into our lands, threatening what meager homes and crops we have! Won't you [destroy] these wandering ash monsters?"
A hot ash wind is blowing from the portals that have appeared in Ethernere-Tainted West Karana and South Ro. Creatures of ash have ventured out of the portals and are roaming menacingly throughout South Ro and North Ro. Guards from Freeport have come here to bring order to this region and are beseeching powerful adventurers to travel here to assist with quelling the blaze.
Freeport guards aren't the only group thatâ€™s investigating this disturbance. Solusek Ro has sensed a form of his own power flowing from these portals, and has sent an ambassador here on his behalf to learn more.
The Argin-Hiz launch event has been designed for groups of level 100 characters. The first phase of the Argin-Hiz launch event begins on Friday, July 4th, 2014. The second phase will begin on Monday, July 7th, 2014. This event content will be available in game until Saturday, September 6th, 2014.
"You still do not trust me. I understand. Be wary of all things, but do not be foolish! We both can see that [doom] approaches." - Lanys T`vyl
Lanys T`vyl is waiting for you in the Ethernere-Tainted West Karana. She would like for you to further investigate this situation. The last artifact needed to send Lady Lendiniara back to her home may be located beyond the ash portals that have appeared in these areas, and the only way to know for sure is to gather other powerful allies to your side to investigate for yourself.

**Call of the Forsaken Info & Guides: Overview \| Progression & Task List \| Raiding \| Visible Armor**

---

_This solo task is the first in a series of six. It begins with Dedire Harath ( **click for map image**) on the zone-in level of the Tower of Rot._
_Pre-Requisites:_
\- Level 95
\- No tasks
_Say "help" to get the task._

---

**Summary & Requesting the Task**
\- Speak with Brendaleen, Quincy, and Kentash.
\- Hail Deidre again.
\- Get explore updates on the floors above and below the zone-in level.
\- Kill "a tower scion" mobs (bottom two floors) until you get an update.
\- Hail Deidre to complete.
You say, 'Hail, Deidre Harath'
Deidre Harath says '\_\_\_\_\_? Is that you. Thank the gods. We might actually have a chance, now. I'll catch you up to date when you are ready. You are here to [help], aren't you?'
You say, 'I will help, of course'
Deidre Harath says 'Well, where to begin. Alright, there is a lot to cover. Take a moment to look around, and then come back to me when you are ready for the full story.'
You have been assigned the task 'The Scions of the Tower'.
The Tower of Rot is under the control of Kyle Bayle, but a group called the Scions of the Tower are seeking something inside of it. Deidre Harath and the other people ripped from around Qeynos by the portals there are now trapped inside the tower. Find out more by speaking to the others and exploring the tower.

---

!

---

03. Find out about the current situation from Deidre Harath 0/1 (Tower of Rot)



You say, 'Hail, Deidre Harath'




Deidre Harath says 'I'll be frank, and quick. We're currently in the [Tower of Rot]. Most of us were transported here by these strange portals near Qeynos. Apparently, whatever is causing the portals also woke up [Kyle Bayle] and some of his soldiers. Of greater immediate danger though, a man named [Narius] has organized a group he calls the [Scions of the Tower]. I don't know what he's planning, but it can't be good. It's my [job] to stop him. First, talk with some of the others. I have to speak with the other Qeynos guards.'




You say, 'How did you get this job?'




Deidre Harath gives a small, proud smile. 'Qeynos guard veteran, serving over twenty-five years. The people here are good people, and tough, even if we haven't defeated gods or discovered new continents or whatever other things you adventureous types do. We just usually deal with oversized rats and criminals, not undead monsters that used to be part of our city's history.'




You say, 'What is the Tower of Rot?'




Deidre Harath says 'A long time ago, Qeynos attempted to build a structure here. While it apparently seemed like a good idea at the time, eventually people started dying and disappearing. All contact was lost, and Kyle Bayle himself went to the Tower. He never returned. That's all I know as history was never my speciality. If you want to know more, speak to Kentash Andebar, a scholar also stuck here.'




You say, 'Who is Kyle Bayle?'




Deidre Harath says 'A son in the Bayle line. He's no longer who he once was though, so I don't think it's in our job description to stop you from attacking him. Quite the opposite, really, given how many citizens are trapped here.'




You say, 'Who is Narius?'




Deidre Harath says 'He started this mess. I don't know what his issues are, but he wants something in this tower, and he's started amassing followers to do it. Don't ask me why anyone would want to come here, but it can't be good. Especially if you bring along criminals and crazies.'




You say, 'Tell me about Adana'




Deidre Harath says 'Adana Baxter. Assuming that's even her real name. She's an excellent liar, and an even better killer. The guard knows about her, but lots of people just assume she's a ghost. Even if we caught her, I doubt we'd have enough proof to make anyone believe she's done what we know she's done.'




You say, 'What about Fenn?'




Deidre Harath says 'Fenn Tarrens is a brigand with an excellent sense of command and a command of her blade that outmatches me and just about everyone on the guard. Thankfully, she's not too bright. Of course that means she was easy pickings for [Narius].'




You say, 'What are the Scions of the Tower?'




Deidre Harath says 'The Scions of the Tower are a new, cultish organization. I don't know much about [Narius], but I'm familiar with [Adana] and [Fenn.]'




_Idle chatter:_



Deidre Harath says 'Everyone stay alert. Keep the non-combatants away from the stairs.'




Deidre Harath glares at her surroundings.




Deidre Harath mutters to herself about the undead.




Deidre Harath calls out to one of the Qeynos guards and orders a patrol of the perimeter.




Deidre Harath says 'Getting out of here alive is not going to be easy.'







---


04. Speak to Brendaleen Thundershield to get information about the Tower 0/1 (Tower of Rot)



You say, 'Hail, Brendaleen Thundershield'




Brendaleen Thundershield says '\_\_\_\_\_? [Deidre] sent you? She told you about the [Scions of the Tower], right? A man named [Narius] commands them, and they're looking for something in here. Given this is the [Tower of Rot], that something can't be good. The walking dead are mostly on the lower floors. The top has more of the portals. [Kyle Bayle] is on the top floor. The bottom floors are flooded and blocked. The Scions are everywhere. On the plus side, there is still some treasure.'




You say, 'Tell me about Deidre'




Brendaleen Thundershield says 'She's the Qeynos guard in command around here. Smart and tough enough, but she's in over her head. I bet she's thrilled you are here so she doesn't have to do this herself.'




You say, 'What about the Scions of the Tower?'




Brendaleen Thundershield says 'They're weird. Not in a funny, friendly way either. Take them out if they see you. They'll stop anyone who tries to get in Narius' way.'




You say, 'What do you know about Narius?'




Brendaleen Thundershield says 'I don't know a lot. I know he's after something in this tower, and then he'll probably murder us all. It's how people like him operate, right? Let's get out of here before that.'




You say, 'What about the Tower of Rot?'




Brendaleen Thundershield looks around. 'This place. Not the nicest ancient ruin I've plundered. If you want the boring history details, talk to that scholar, Kentash. If you want to help me look for treasure and find a way out for us, well, then we can talk.' She smirks.




You say, 'Where is Kyle Bayle?'




Brendaleen Thundershield says 'He's locked up on the top floor. For now. But given the fact he got attacked right outside his tower, I'm pretty sure he's planning something, not cowering in defeat. Undead monsters don't fear much, you probably know by now.'




_Idle chatter:_



Brendaleen Thundershield appears to be going over a rough map for the Tower she's sketched out.




Brendaleen Thundershield starts digging through her sack of treasures and trinkets.







---


05. Meet with Quincy Hollows to find out about the group's resources 0/1 (Tower of Rot)



You say, 'Hail, Quincy Hollows'




Quincy Hollows says 'Oh? I've heard of you. Sorry, I'm rather distracted with our deadly crisis and all. This is seriously cutting into my profits, but let it not be said that Quincy Hollows is not a team player. Besides, I don't want to die, and quite enjoy life. Anyway, we have enough resources for now. They won't last forever, though. I hope you will save us. I'm sure there will be [rewards] for your efforts.'




You say, 'What rewards?'




Quincy Hollows says 'Don't look at me. I sell normal things, not anything like that garish equipment you have on that probably can fry me with magic if I were so much as to brush up against it. I don't know what to believe about what I've heard about you, but I don't really care if you save us for selfish reasons or for love and peace. Just save us, please!'




_Idle chatter:_



Quincy Hollows says 'I want to go home.'




Quincy Hollows tugs at his beard, frowning, appearing to be deep in thought.




Quincy Hollows rolls his eyes as one of his assistants drops something. 'No, really, break more of our very limited resources while we are trapped in this terrible Tower. That'll go over very well with Guard Harath.'







---


06. Talk to Kentash Andebar to find out about the Tower's history 0/1 (Tower of Rot)



You say, 'Hail, Kentash Andebar'




Kentash Andebar says 'Greetings. I am Kentash Andebar, [historian] and an avid student of the [Tower of Rot]. It's so fascinating a tale. I recogonize we are in a bit of trouble, but to see Kyle Bayle so self-aware while most of his people toil mindlessly in the crumbling depths of the Tower below -- it is incredible! I know these Scions, as they call themselves, are the first priority, but if you are ever interested in learning more about the Tower, please see me. There is always knowledge to be gained from the past.'




You say, 'A historian?'




Kentash Andebar says 'Indeed. I've had a particular interest in the history of [Kyle Bayle] and the [Tower of Rot] for years. When I saw the Tower through the haze of one of those [portals], I couldn't stop myself as I walked through. Deidre calls me insane, and I realize my life is in peril, but what an opportunity for firsthand research!'




You say, 'What do you know about the Tower of Rot?'




Kentash Andebar says 'You'd like an overview? Certainly. Long ago, Antonius III sent many on an expedition to explore these lands. They attempted to extend the reach of Qeynos and [construct] a structure here that would act as a beacon of civility in these mostly unfriendly lands, beginning the work of establishing trade routes. However, contact was lost with this group, and much of the exact details for this remain shrouded in the past. The tower, however, stood above everything and was viewable in the distance. Antonius sent his son, [Kyle Bayle], to head up construction and to immediately send word upon arrival explaining why communication from the tower had ceased. None were ever heard of again, and this rotting tower has sat in silence for lifetimes, with explorers and adventurers that lived to see it reporting no sign of life.'




You say, 'What about Kyle Bayle?'




Kentash Andebar says 'Proud son of Antonius III, the kind to inspire others by all accounts, and master of the claymore sword. It's unfortunate he is trying to kill us all. The things he could tell us!'




You say, 'What about the portals?'




Kentash Andebar says 'I'm familiar with Ethernere and the odd effects in West Karana. Distortions of time itself? Fascinating. This is only a working theory, but I surmise that whatever force controls the rot of this Tower is affected by Ethernere, and some aspect or artifact here must be the catalyst that caused these portals to exist in the first place. I told Deidre, but she told me to shut up because I was making her head hurt. Brendaleen just kept asking about treasure.'




You say, 'How did they construct this place?'




Kentash Andebar says 'About that: From what Brendaleen and a few others have told me, the workers seem to never stop. It's strange. Could the rotting effects of the tower have an effect on the undead repeating the hopeless task of endlessly building the tower? Are they just mimicking what they did in life, or does this tower compel one to counter the effects it produces? And, most importantly, what produces these effects?'




_Idle chatter:_



Kentash Andebar says 'It is sad to see the brave citizens of Qeynos who ventured out here to build a tower reduced to the status of mindless undead.'




Kentash Andebar says 'I will certainly write a text on this whole adventure after it is done. If I survive, of course.'







---


07. Return to Deidre Harath to discuss options 0/1 (Tower of Rot)



You say, 'Hail, Deidre Harath'




Deidre Harath says 'The fact you are back here and haven't already left tells me you are ready to help. Good. Frankly I'm not really concerned what your intentions are, and whatever blasted artifacts you find in this Tower are between you and Brendaleen to squabble over. My only interest is stopping Narius, and with luck, dealing with Kyle Bayle before he decides to return home and attack Qeynos with an army of the dead. First, we need information. Explore the floors above and below, and find any clues you can as to what Narius might be planning. I have others looking, and everyone else is on defense. But in all honesty, our best bet is with you. Good luck.'







---


08. Defeat Scions of the Tower cultists to gain information 0/1 (Tower of Rot)

09. Explore the chaotic floors above filled with brigands and gnolls 0/1 (Tower of Rot)

10. Explore the well-furnished floors further above filled with the dead 0/1 (Tower of Rot)

11. Explore the rotted floors below filled with undead workers 0/1 (Tower of Rot)

12. Explore the flooded floors further below filled with strange creatures 0/1 (Tower of Rot)



_For the explore steps, just run around on the noted levels until you get updates._



_For the scion step, kill mobs called "a tower scion", located throughout the bottom two floors (the second and third floors down from the zone-in level). These mobs hit for a max ~17,500 and summon when damaged. This is an uncommon kill update, so it may take several kills before you get it._



a tower scion grins, thirsty for blood!




a tower scion has been slain by \_\_\_\_\_!


a tower scion's corpse falls over dead.




a tower scion had nothing of interest on them. You may need to find a different Scion in order to find some useful information.




a tower scion had a document on them. You take it for Deidre and the others.







---


13. Report your findings to Deidre Harath 0/1 (Tower of Rot)



You say, 'Hail, Deidre Harath'




Deidre Harath says 'Good work. I think we can't even start dealing with the Scions until we deal with the undead. They've attacked us while you were gone, and I have a feeling that won't be the last incident.'




You've explored the Tower of Rot and started gathering information about the Scions of the Tower.

_Rewards:_
150 platinum
Experience (about 1% in Level 100)

---

_This solo task is the second in a series of six. It begins with Dedire Harath ( **click for map image**) on the zone-in level of the Tower of Rot._
_Pre-Requisites:_
\- Completed "The Scions of the Tower"
_Say "undead" to request the task._

---

**Summary & Requesting the Task**
\- Hail Kentash.
\- Kill 5 rockbreakers and/or excavators (one floor below zone-in level).
\- Kill Sergeant Whisken and Foreman Lavers (same floor as rockbreakers).
\- Pick up 3x "Scion Missives" ground spawns (pre-lootable) and turn in to Deidre Harath.
\- Hail Deidre Harath.
You say, 'Hail, Deidre Harath'
Deidre Harath says 'I take it you have a pretty good understanding of what we are up against now? Good, because we are going to need all the help we can get. First things first: Before we can even start on Narius' goons, we need to take care of the [undead].'
You say, 'What about the undead?'
Deidre Harath says 'Find out more from Kentash Andebar before doing anything else. Despite not being a combatant, next to you, he's one of the most valuable people here.'
You have been assigned the task 'The Tower's Victims'.
Deidre Harath has charged you with halting the flow of the undead. Legions of lost occupy large sections of the Tower of Rot.
Kentash Andebar has more information about the undead occupants of the tower. Speak to him.

---

!

1\. Discuss the Tower of Rot with Kentash Andebar 0/1 (Tower of Rot)
You say, 'Hail, Kentash Andebar'
Kentash Andebar says 'This tower was to be a point of pride of Qeynos. Expeditions were sent. However, workers and soldiers sent never returned, and communication was quickly lost. Kyle Bayle himself was the last party sent, a last attempt. He never returned. After that, the tower project was abandoned, and it aquired its unflattering names, Tower of Rot chief among them. The undead here still work on the tower, constantly rebuilding that which cannot be constructed. It appears that some of them awoke, so to speak, when these Ethernere portals started appearing. You must defeat as many undead as you can, but most importantly, defeat their leaders.'

---

2\. Eliminate undead workers and soldiers 0/5 (Tower of Rot)
_Kill rockbreakers and excavators on the floor below the zone-in level._
3\. Take out Sergeant Whisken 0/1 (Tower of Rot)
Sergeant Whisken readies their weapon. 'You have caused enough trouble. There will be no quarter.'
_Sergeant Whisken is a paladin (standard fight for the zone, except more hp than typical trash mobs)._
Sergeant Whisken has been slain by \_\_\_\_\_!
Sergeant Whisken's corpse collapses in pieces.
4\. Take out Foreman Lavers 0/1 (Tower of Rot)
Foreman Lavers locks baleful, undead eyes on their prey. 'You interrupt our work.'
_Foreman Lavers is pure melee (also a standard fight with no tricks)._
Foreman Lavers has been slain by \_\_\_\_\_!
Foreman Lavers's corpse falls apart, the creature's last sound a cry of relief.
5\. Search for clues to the Scions of the Towers plans and give them to Harath 0/3 (Tower of Rot)
_Pick up "Scion Missives" ground spawns (found in various locations below and above the zone-in floor)._
You offered 3 Scion Missives to Deidre Harath.
Your task 'The Tower's Victims' has been updated.
Your task 'The Tower's Victims' has been updated.
Your task 'The Tower's Victims' has been updated.
Deidre Harath says 'Ah, you found something? Excellent. We'll need all the information we can get.'
You complete the trade with Deidre Harath.

You say, 'Hail, Deidre Harath'
Your task 'The Tower's Victims' has been updated.

You've destroyed the long dead leaders and discovered more information.
You receive 225 platinum .
You gain experience!

**Call of the Forsaken Info & Guides: Overview \| Progression & Task List \| Raiding \| Visible Armor**

_This solo task is the fourth in a series of six. It begins with Dedire Harath ( **click for map image**) on the zone-in level of the Tower of Rot._
_Pre-Requisites:_
\- Completed "The Scions of the Tower"
\- Completed "The Tower's Victims"
\- Completed "Fenn's Job"
_Say "ask" to request the task._

---

**Summary & Requesting the Task**
\- Hail Brendaleen.
\- Location update just inside the first basement floor (one floor below zone-in).
\- Kill 5x "a rockbreaker" and/or "an excavator".
\- Two floors below the zone-in level, DPS "a tower scion" mobs to under 10% health and hail them (you'll need to finish them off after they re-aggro).
\- In the southwestern corner of the same floor, say "hidden booty" to spawn Jrint the Scout. Just as you enter the southwest room he will spawn on the right just inside the entrance.
\- Hail Jrint the Scout to receive "Tower Scout's Grappling Hook".
\- At three separate locations on this floor:
\-\-\- Use the grappling hook to fling yourself up to a ledge, and wait.
\-\-\- A short time after doing this, 1x "a Scion bonemage" and 1x "a Scion deathcrafter" spawn on the ground below you.
\-\-\- You'll have a short time to engage them before they run off (mobs are NOT social with each other, so you'll need to manually aggro both mobs).
\-\-\- You can respawn these mobs if you miss one or both of them.
\- Location update in the far western room of the bottom floor to spawn non-KOS Narius.
\- Say "gifts" to spawn 2x "a deadskin bodyguard" (aggro-linked).
\- Kill the bodyguards.
\- Hail Deidre to complete.
You say, 'Hail, Deidre Harath'
Deidre Harath frowns. 'I must know more, \_\_\_\_\_. The Scions scour the floors for supplies and harry us at all corners, but what is their [aim]?
You say, 'What do you know of their aim?'
Deidre Harath says 'The Scions gather in the floors below, and the sounds of their picks echo up the stairways. They delve deeper. They must be searching for [something] in the depths.'
You say, 'Something in the depths?'
Deidre Harath says 'I almost do not wish to know what might lurk at the heart of this dreadful tower, but if Narius seeks it, no good can be found. If we have any hope at stopping him, we must know his [plans].'
You say, 'What plans?'
Deidre Harath says 'I tasked Brendaleen Thundershield with discovering Narius' intentions, but she's late with her report. Will you [ask] her why I'm still trying to sketch an attack route in the dark? When I ask for a candle, bring me a torch!'
You say, 'I'll ask her'
Deidre Harath smiles. 'That's what I like to hear. You're one of the more pleasant surprises I've met with in this tower.
You have been assigned the task 'Narius' Plan'.
Deidre Harath has asked you to find out what Narius is planning.

---

**Map**
_Note: Jrint the Scout's spawn location was moved in a June 2014 patch. He is now found in the southwestern room of Floor B2 (you may need to refresh this page to see the updated map)._
!

---

Speak with Brendaleen Thundershield 0/1 (Tower of Rot)
_Task Window Says:_ Brendaleen Thundershield has been tasked with discovering Narius' plans, but thus far she been unsuccessful. Find out why.
You say, 'Hail, Brendaleen Thundershield'
Brendaleen Thundershield says 'Damn it, \_\_\_\_\_! I'm a treasure hunter, not a spy! You ask me to snatch the chalice from the skeletal hand of a long-dead king, and I'm your woman! But if you want secrets, that's Jrint's business. I sent him to the lower levels days ago -- or what I think was days ago; I can't really tell time in this place. Perhaps the undead miners below ripped him apart before he could move deeper and spy on the Scions. If you want answers for Deidre, find Jrint. He was wearing a black cloak the last time I saw him. Oh, and he answers to the password 'hidden booty.''

---

Find the first floor of the basement and begin your search for the spy 0/1 (Tower of Rot)
_Task Window Says:_ Brendaleen sent Jrint to the lower levels. Descend to the first floor of the basement and see what you can find.
_This updates on Floor B1 (one floor below the zone-in level) at about -10, -60, -196 (just outside the ramps)._

---

Kill the undead miners to find any clues about the spy 0/5 (Tower of Rot)
_Task Window Says:_ Jrint might have been stymied by the undead miners. Kill them and see if you can find any clues of Jrint's whereabouts.
_Kill undead mobs in this same area until you see an emote about footsteps leading to the floor below. Upon the 5th kill, you see:_
You hear a scuffling of feet heading down the stairs to the lower level. Did you just miss the spy? Perhaps there are Scions below who know of Jrint's fate.

---

Beat down the scions in the second floor of the basement and interrogate them 0/3 (Tower of Rot)
_Task Window Says:_ You heard someone trying to stealthily make their way to the second level of the basement. Perhaps the Scions there have heard strange noises and can point to Jrint's location. Soften up the Scions and then interrogate them.
_On Floor B2 (two floors below the zone-in level), DPS "scion" mobs to 10% health, and then hail them. The scion will give you a directional emote indicating where to find an NPC named Jrint. (The scion then goes non-aggro and cannot be hailed again.)_
a tower scion says 'Don't hurt me! I... I heard some shuffling and rustling in a corner around here... it must have been to the northwest, about 250 paces away...'

---

Say the password in the spy's hiding spot in the second floor of the basement 0/1 (Tower of Rot)
_Task Window Says:_ You think you have a good idea of Jrint's location on the second level of the basement. Go there and speak the password Brendaleen gave you.
_Note: Jrint the Scout's spawn location was moved in a June 2014 patch. He is now found in the southwestern room of Floor B2 (you may need to refresh this page to see the updated map above)._
_Jrint the Scout spawns in the southwestern room on the floor. Say "hidden booty" at this location to make him appear (you have to be at this step for this to work)._
You say, 'Hidden booty'
Jrint the Scout appears out of the shadows, muttering as loudly as he dares. 'Quiet! Are you trying to blow my cover?! And I told Brendaleen not to call me that! She's already spread that name among the ladies in the camp, and apparently, she's bragging to every adventurer from here to Qeynos. I wish to be known for my stealthiness, not my backside! So, what is it you want?
You say, 'Hidden booty!'
Jrint the Scout says 'Shut up!'

---

Speak with Jrint the Scout 0/1 (Tower of Rot)
_Task Window Says:_ Jrint has come out of hiding. Speak with him.
You say, 'Hail, Jrint the Scout'
Jrint the Scout says 'Yes, let's talk business. I've been trying to listen to the Scion bonemages and deathcrafters as they discuss Narius' plans, but they have ways of seeing through my stealthy tricks. I haven't been able to hear their conversations. I was thinking I might use this grappling hook to watch them from the ledges above... but now that you're here, you might put it to better use. See if you can hide on the ledges and hear what the Scions say before beating them senseless and looking for clues on their corpses. You seem to have Brendaleen's confidence, so I trust you. Besides, I think my backside looks better living than undead.'
_You receive "Tower Scout's Grappling Hook"._

---

Get onto the ledges overlooking the second floor basement and wait for Scions 0/1 (Tower of Rot)
Listen to the bonemages and deathcrafters, kill them, and look for clues 0/1 (Tower of Rot)
Listen to the bonemages and deathcrafters, kill them, and look for clues 0/1 (Tower of Rot)
Listen to the bonemages and deathcrafters, kill them, and look for clues 0/1 (Tower of Rot)
Listen to the bonemages and deathcrafters, kill them, and look for clues 0/1 (Tower of Rot)
Listen to the bonemages and deathcrafters, kill them, and look for clues 0/1 (Tower of Rot)
Listen to the bonemages and deathcrafters, kill them, and look for clues 0/1 (Tower of Rot)
_Task Window Says:_ Jrint wants you to spy on the bonemages and deathcrafters. Use the Tower Scout's Grappling Hook near certain ledges on the second basement level to find a hiding spot. Listen for the conversations, and then kill the bonemages and deathcrafters. Take any clues from their bodies you can find.
_This series of steps updated on the platforms that overlook Floor B2. You'll need to use the Tower Scout's Grappling Hook at three locations:_
\\* 35, 120, -401
\\* 20, -105, -402
\\* -85, -40, -400
_At each of these locations, right-click the Tower Scout's Grappling Hook to fling yourself up to a ledge. Once on a ledge, wait there until two mobs spawn below: "a scion bonemage" and "a scion deathcrafter". You'll see some emotes from them, and then they'll walk away and despawn. You need to aggro them before they despawn, kill them, and loot their corpses (repeat x3 for six loots total in three different locations)._
_Note that the mobs /con KOS, but don't attack until provoked, and they are NOT social with each other. Bonemages are necromancers and deathcrafters are enchanters (will cast, among other things, a disease-counter AE slow)._
_These are the conversations you'll overhear:_
a Scion bonemage says 'The preparations have been made.'
a Scion deathcrafter says 'The altars have been raised.'
a Scion bonemage says 'I will ready the bodies.'
a Scion bonemage says 'The others have almost breached the wall.'
a Scion deathcrafter says 'Narius will want to be the first inside.'
a Scion bonemage says 'He will control the power of rot!'
a Scion bonemage says 'We will close the breach after Narius?'
a Scion deathcrafter says 'None shall pass once he is inside.'
a Scion bonemage says 'Then the spells are almost prepared?'
a Scion deathcrafter says 'Yes, we cannot be stopped now. Let us hurry. Narius waits below.'
_The items you'll loot:_
\\* First Sealing Spell Fragment
\\* Encoded Scion Plans - First Scrap
\\* Second Sealing Spell Fragment
\\* Encoded Scion Plans - Second Scrap
\\* Third Sealing Spell Fragment
\\* Encoded Scion Plans - Third Scrap

---

Find Narius on the third floor of the basement and confront him 0/1 (Tower of Rot)
Deal with the consequences of confronting Narius 0/2 (Tower of Rot)
_Task Window Says:_ Narius is waiting on the third floor of the basement for his followers to breach a hidden room. Confront him and deal with the consequences.
_Head to the very basement to about -45, 120, -496 (far western room). This spawns Narius. Speak with him and be prepared to two aggro-linked hostile mobs._
You say, 'Hail, Narius'
Narius says 'My child, have you come to repent of your errors? There are many fools in this tower who believe it is inevitable to prostrate ourselves before the power of death. It creeps like a tide up the shore. Kyle Bayle drowned beneath it, as did my family, my friend... and so shall Deidre. Will you do the same? Will you not take my [hand] and be saved from the rising waters?'
You say, 'Your hand?'
Narius holds out a withered hand for but a moment. He clenches and withdraws it. 'Then there is no salvation for you. When I reach the source of rot, there will come a day of reckoning. The honorable dead shall live; the dishonorable living shall die. I once studied the healing arts and wished to grant the touch of life to all. But now I know very few deserve such [gifts].
You say, 'What gifts?'
Narius says 'You are not worthy of the gift of life. You are lower than my thralls -- creatures fit only to live as my servants. To you I grant only death.'
_2x "a deadskin bodyguard" spawn in front of him and aggro. These are enchanter mobs; hit for a max ~17,500; are immune to root/snare and root; and proc "Rotting Fist". Kill them to update these steps._
Rotting Fist: Single Target, Disease (-975)
1: Decrease HP when cast by 30769
2: Decrease Hitpoints by 7692 per tick
3: Increase Disease Counter by 30
a deadskin bodyguard has been slain by \_\_\_\_\_!

---

Speak with Deidre Harath 0/1 (Tower of Rot)
_Task Window Says:_ Tell Deidre what you discovered.
You say, 'Hail, Deidre Harath'
Deidre Harath says '\_\_\_\_\_, you certainly proved yourself more competent than Brendaleen! So, Narius seeks the source of rot? If what you say is true and he plans to seal himself inside the bowels of the tower, I don't see how we can stop him! We must move as swiftly as possible before he breaches the wall and constructs his defenses!'
You have discovered that Narius is trying to enter the lowest chamber of the tower's basement and take control of what he calls the 'source of rot.'

**Call of the Forsaken Info & Guides: Overview \| Progression & Task List \| Raiding \| Visible Armor**

---

_This solo task is the fifth in a series of six. It begins with Deidre Harath ( **click for map image**) on the zone-in level of the Tower of Rot._
_Pre-Requisites:_
\- Completed "The Scions of the Tower"
\- Completed "The Tower's Victims"
\- Completed "Fenn's Job"
\- Completed "Narius' Plan"
_Say "prevent" to request the task._
You say, 'Hail, Deidre Harath'
Deidre Harath smashes a fist into her hand. '\_\_\_\_\_, this is not going as well as I had hoped! You have done well, though; don't mistake my anger. It's Adana with whom I am angry. While you attempted to descry Narius' plan, Adana swept through here with a band of Scions and stirred up [mayhem].
You say, 'Mayhem?'
Deidre Harath says 'Three of our own were killed, and six wounded. Adana also destroyed a fair portion of Quincy's supplies, including a week's worth of rations. Until we can [secure] our position, stopping Narius will be impossible.'
You say, 'How can we secure it?'
Deidre Harath says 'Quincy is taking stock of what supplies he has left. He also lived through the brunt of the attack. As we put the camp in order, I need you to seek Adana and [prevent] her from returning to finish what she started.'
You say, 'I'll prevent her from it'
Deidre Harath says 'Once again, \_\_\_\_\_, we would be lost without you. Speak with Quincy Hollows first. He might have an idea where Adana was headed.'
You have been assigned the task 'The Adana Problem'.
Adana Baxter and a Scion raiding party have attacked Deidre's position and destroyed some of Quincy's supplies. Now you must retaliate and ensure this doesn't happen again.

---

**Summary**
\- Hail Quincy to receive "Quincy's Bloodstone Detector".
\- Right-click the "Quincy's Bloodstone Detector" to receive directional emotes to an update location.
\-\-\- Tells you above or below; direction (north, west, etc.); and how many paces away.
_Note: If you have multiple people in your group on the same ambush step, ambush mobs will continue spawning at their timed intervals until you've gotten all kill credits. If, on the first ambush step, for example, you're DPS'ing the second thrall spawn and the 60-second timer passes before it dies, a third thrall will spawn. (You may be able to avoid multiple spawns by moving away from the trigger point, killing the spawned mobs, and then returning to the trigger point to spawn more.)_
\- Ambush area #1: One floor above the zone-in (bandits/grave robbers level):
\- Step over the update location (may be fixed or random - not confirmed) to trigger ambush spawns.
\-\-\- 3x "a bubonian thrall" auto-aggro spawns (staggered, separated by about 60 seconds).
\- Ambush area #2: Two floors above the zone-in (gnolls level):
\- Step over the update location (may be fixed or random - not confirmed) to trigger ambush spawns.
\-\-\- 4x "a Scion harrier" auto-aggro spawns (staggered in waves of two, separated by 105 seconds).
\- Ambush area #3: Four floors above the zone-in (undead level):
\- Step over the update location (may be fixed or random - not confirmed) to trigger ambush spawns.
\-\-\- 4x "a Scion thrasher" auto-aggro spawns (staggered in waves of two, separated by 150 seconds).
\-\-\- Once thrashers are dead, you have another set to deal with: 4x "a Scion deathmask" (has same spawning mechanism).
\- Hail "an injured Scion".
\- Move down one floor and spawn Lieutenant Caridi in the western room of the floor.
\- Say "Adana" to him to make him aggro; then kill him.
\- Head to the northern room of this same floor to spawn Adana Baxter.
\- Say "ploy" to make her aggro; DPS her to low health for the update.
\- Hail Quincy, then deliver 3x "Pile of Rot" to him.
\- Hail Deidre to complete.

---

**Map**
!

---

02. Speak with Quincy Hollows 0/1 (Tower of Rot)



_Task Window Says:_ Speak with Quincy Hollows and find out what happened when the Scions attacked.




You say, 'Hail, Quincy Hollows'




Quincy Hollows says 'Oh, \_\_\_\_\_, it was just terrible... terrible! That cruel woman and her Scions crept out of the shadows while we were asleep and started slaughtering us and destroying my wares. If my guards hadn't been on duty we'd all be dead! As we began to rally, Adana and her band fled up the stairwell. It looked like one of her companions was pricked by our swords, since she left drops of blood behind. And that will be their [undoing]!'




You say, 'Their undoing?'




Quincy Hollows says 'I found this stone in one of my crates. I don't remember where I picked it up... probably from one of my bulk deals buying pirate plunder sight unseen. It vibrates in the presence of blood and should help you follow the trail of that injured Scion. Go get them for us, \_\_\_\_\_! I always settle my debts, so you'll be the agent of my payback!'




_You receive "Quincy's Bloodstone Detector". This item, when right-clicked, tells you where to go to get the next seven updates (will tell you to go above, below, or somewhere on your level; a direction; and how far away a location update is)._






---


03. Follow the Scion raiding party's blood trail 0/1 (Tower of Rot)

04. Deal with any Scions or thralls you find 0/3 (Tower of Rot)



_Task Window Says:_ Use Quincy's Bloodstone Detector to follow the trail of Scion blood and find out where the raiding party went. Deal with any threats you discover.




_When you right-click the Detector, you'll see an emote like this (right-clicking has a cast time and breaks invis):_



You detect a concentration of blood above your location, to the east, at approximately 123 paces away.




_Head to the direction indicated (click effect has unlimited charges). Upon entering the update area for this step, you'll see an emote:_



You feel the presence of Scion raiders.




_Soon after this emote (30 seconds or so), 1x "a bubonian thrall" spawns and attacks (auto-aggro). 60 seconds later, another one spawns. The thralls hit for a max ~13,000._



_Note: Update locations may also be visually indicated by a trail of blood drops on the ground. It's not known for sure whether the ambush update locations are fixed (the same locations every time), or if they're randomized._



_Warning: If you have multiple people in your group on the same ambush step, ambush mobs will continue spawning at their timed intervals until you've gotten all kill credits. If, on the first ambush step, for example, you're DPS'ing the second thrall spawn and the 60-second timer passes before it dies, a third thrall will spawn. (You may be able to avoid multiple spawns by moving away from the trigger point, killing the spawned mobs, and then returning to the trigger point to spawn more.)_






---


05. The trail leads higher. Follow the Scions. 0/1 (Tower of Rot)

06. Deal with any Scions you find 0/4 (Tower of Rot)



_Task Window Says:_ Continue using Quincy's Bloodstone Detector to follow the Scion trail. Deal with any threats you discover.




_Same as above, except you get 2x "a Scion harrier" at a time (waves separated by 105 seconds), for four total. These hit for a max ~14,000._






---


07. Discover the end of the blood trail 0/1 (Tower of Rot)

08. Prepare for any treachery 0/4 (Tower of Rot)

09. Deal the final blows to the Scion raiders 0/4 (Tower of Rot)



_Task Window Says:_ Quincy's Bloodstone Detector indicates the blood trail climbs still higher into the tower. Be prepared to face any Scion threats that you find.




_Same as above two encounters, except you get 2x "a Scion thrasher", followed by 2x "a Scion thrasher" 2.5 minutes later. Once the thrashers are dead, another set of mobs will spawn: 2x "a Scion deathmask" followed by 2x "a Scion deathmask" (waves separated by 2.5 minutes). Each of these mobs hit for a max ~14,000._



_Warning: Keep in mind that if you have multiple people in group at this step, waves of mobs will continue spawning every 2.5 minutes until everyone has the task updated. (You may be able to avoid multiple spawns by moving away from the trigger point, killing the spawned mobs, and then returning to the trigger point to spawn more.)_






---


10. Examine the scene 0/1 (Tower of Rot)



_Hail "an injured Scion"._



You say, 'Hail, an injured Scion'




an injured Scion says 'Welcome to your death, fools! The poison now flows within you! Best start eating any piles of rot you can find, or face a swift death. If Lieutenant Caridi hadn't gone to the floor below, he'd laugh at how well Adana's plan has worked. ' The injured Scion coughs into her hand and slumps to the floor. 'If only I could be there... To laugh when you breathed your last...'




The injured Scion's blade has punctured your side and injected poison into your veins. You feel weakness spreading through your body.




_You become poisoned with "Mild Bone Rot" (limits hitpoints to 80%), which you'll have to deal with for now._






---


11. Find Lieutenant Caridi 0/1 (Tower of Rot)

12. Confront Lieutenant Caridi 0/1 (Tower of Rot)



_Task Window Says:_ You have been poisoned by the injured Scion. You must consume Piles of Rot from the ground in order to prevent the poison from killing you. Before the Scion died, she mentioned that Lieutenant Caridi had gone to the floor below. Seek him out and confront him.




_Optional: You can temporarily cure the poison buff on you by using one of the green ground spawns ("Pile of Rot") scattered around the floor. This stops the effects for 35-40 seconds._



_Head to /loc 15, 120, 302 to spawn Lieutenant Caridi and say "Adana" to make him aggro:_



You sense Lieutenant Caridi is nearby.




You say, 'Hail, Lieutenant Caridi'




Lieutenant Caridi laughs and shoulders his club. 'Walked right into it, didn't ya? Feel it hot in yer belly, like a slug of drink? That's the rot, and yer done for! There'll be nothin' left for [Adana] to kill. She'll be so sad!




You say, 'Adana?'




Lieutenant Caridi says 'Oh, she had business elsewhere on this floor, but ya got nothin' to worry about. Yer never gonna see her again anyway. She said if I see you I'm to bust yer head open, so that's what I'm gonna do!'




_After a short delay, he goes aggro. He hits for a max ~17,500; has ~8.5 million hitpoints; and casts "Club to the Head" on random targets (nuke for 40,000 dmg + spinstun)._



Lieutenant Caridi has been slain by \_\_\_\_\_!







---


13. Find Adana Baxter 0/1 (Tower of Rot)

14. Confront Adana Baxter 0/1 (Tower of Rot)



_Task Window Says:_ The poison within you is intensifying. You must confront Adana Baxter on the floor where you fought Caridi if you wish to learn the truth.




_While on this portion of the task, you are inflicted with "Moderate Bone Rot" (limits hitpoints to 70%). Again, this can be temporarily (optionally) cured by using the "Pile of Rot" items (green ground spawns) scattered throughout the floor._



_Head to /loc 90, 60, 306 to spawn Adana Baxter. Now, you get inflicted with "Severe Bone Rot" (reduction in hitpoints by 65%):_



You hear Adana laughing nearby.




As you approach Adana, the rot intensifies and seeps into your bones.


You feel yourself rotting from the inside out.




_Hail her, and follow her dialogue, saying "ploy" to her to make her aggro:_



You say, 'Hail, Adana Baxter'




Adana Baxter smiles at you. 'Why, is that my old friend, \_\_\_\_\_? So wonderful to see you! And are you well? Oh, my, you don't look so well! Are you ill? Have you eaten something foul? Or did one of my little drones sting you? You really are too [trusting], always believing whatever apparent truth is thrust in front of you!




You say, 'Trusting?'




Adana Baxter says 'There are more important things in this world than friendship, loyalty, or honor -- things like the power to control life and death. I once thought you would be the one to kill Kyle Bayle and make my path clear, but obviously Narius is more capable than you are. You can't even recognize an obvious [ploy]!'




You say, 'What ploy?'




Adana Baxter says 'Quincy's guards didn't cut my little Scion drone. My blades are sharper than that merchant's will ever be. The only surprise is that you've lived this long, despite ambushes and a gut full of rot. By now your bones should be turning to gruel, and you are of no more use than a pot of spoiled porridge. I think it's time we ended our working relationship.'




_Ater a short delay, she goes aggro. She has ~8.5 million hitpoints; and casts "Break Brittle Bones" on random targets (nuke for 46,000 dmg). DPS her to 10% for the update._



Adana Baxter steps back, laughing. 'Quite tenacious! But don't start thinking I'll regret choosing Narius over you. A tenacious fool is still a fool, and an honorable corpse is still dead. I don't have time to trifle further with the doomed; Narius is about to enter the source of rot, so I must be off. Enjoy your last few minutes before the rot consumes you!' Adana Baxter disappears into the shadows.







---


15. Speak with Quincy to find a cure 0/1 (Tower of Rot)



_Task Window Says:_ Tell Quincy Hollows what has happened. Perhaps he can make a cure for you using several Piles of Rot.




_Remove any "Pile of Rot" items from your inventory, and hail Quincy Hollows. You'll need to bring him 3x "Pile of Rot" (if you had some on you, they don't count) and hail him again._



You say, 'Hail, Quincy Hollows'




Quincy Hollows says '\_\_\_\_\_, you look awful! Do you have three handfuls of those rot piles you found? I think I have an elixir that might bind with the rot and be able to purge it once and for all from your body.'




_With 3x "Pile of Rot" in your inventory, hail him again:_



Quincy Hollows takes three piles of rot from you and drops them into an elixir. As the concoction bubbles and smokes, you drink it down and feel strength creeping back into your body. 'You're looking better already! I think the worst of the malady has passed, but I would take it easy. Oh, I hope those horrible raiders don't return with poisoned blades for the lot of us! How will I ever have enough elixirs?







---


16. Speak with Deidre Harath 0/1 (Tower of Rot)



_Task Window Says:_ Quincy has cured you. Tell Deidre Harath all that has passed.




You say, 'Hail, Deidre Harath'




Deidre Harath says 'So Adana still lives... At least you put an end to her fellow raiders. You look a bit worse off for your efforts, but I still must ask you for yet another sacrifice. Narius is entering the source of rot and must be stopped. With our camp secured, now is the time.'




You have killed Adana Baxter's raiding party, although Adana herself escaped. Now she and Narius are about to enter the source of rot.

---

_Rewards:_
300 platinum
Experience (about 3% in Level 100)

**Call of the Forsaken Info & Guides: Overview \| Progression & Task List \| Raiding \| Visible Armor**

---

_This solo task is the final task in a series of six. It begins with Dedire Harath ( **click for map image**) on the zone-in level of the Tower of Rot._
_Pre-Requisites:_
\- Completed "The Scions of the Tower"
\- Completed "The Tower's Victims"
\- Completed "Fenn's Job"
\- Completed "Narius' Plan"
\- Completed "The Adana Problem"

---

**Summary & Requesting the Task**
_Say "open it" to request the task._
\- Hail Brendaleen.
\- Hail "a captured apprentice" (two floors below zone-in).
\- Location update in the southern room of the basement to spawn Ritualist Farji.
\- Kill Ritualist Farji, and loot the "Raw Mana Flask" from his corpse (drops one per kill).
\- Hail Brendaleen.
\- Hail Guard Werben (top floor).
\- Kill "a cowering apprentice" until you've looted 8x "Magelock Fragment".
\- Hail Quincy Hollows.
\- Use the jewelry table next to him to craft a "Magelock Stone" (combine the flask + 8 fragments).
\- DPS ghost mobs to under 20% health and click the stone on them (repeat x10 to get "Empowered Magelock Stone").
\- Head to the bottom floor and use the Magelock Stone on an altar to make it damageable, then kill it.
\-\-\- 1-2x "a defender of rot" (200k hp; AEs a 27k nuke) every 20 seconds while the altar is engaged.
\-\-\- Do this for each of the three altars in the basement.
\- Hail Deidre to complete.
You say, 'Hail, Deidre Harath'
Deidre Harath says 'Glad you are still with us. I've been convinced so far that nothing can kill you, \_\_\_\_\_, but that seemed a little too close. In good news, Brendaleen confirmed that Narius has locked himself in the lowest chambers. We think Fenn and Adana are both there. This is our chance to launch an attack, but Narius or his people have sealed the door with a magical lock. Getting past it is beyond my knowledge. Can you [open it]?'
You say, 'Of course, I'll open it'
Deidre Harath says 'Brendaleen might just have an answer for us. One of her scouts caught a Scion apprentice and has learned more about the seal Narius has placed on the source of rot. You should assist Brendaleen in breaking the seal.'
You have been assigned the task 'The Source of Rot'.
Narius has sealed himself within the source of rot, and Deidre needs you to find a way inside.

---

**Map**
!

---

03. Speak with Brendaleen Thundershield 0/1 (Tower of Rot)



_Task Window Says:_ One of Brendaleen's scouts has caught a Scion apprentice that has knowledge of the magic used to seal the source of rot. Speak with Brendaleen.




You say, 'Hail, Brendaleen Thundershield'




Brendaleen Thundershield says 'You know, I think I'm getting to like this spy business, \_\_\_\_\_! It's like treasure hunting for secrets. And when I think about it, with the right buyer, [secrets] fetch more than gold...'




You say, 'Secrets?'




Brendaleen Thundershield says 'Yes, I was getting to that! My lovely little spy, Jrint, turned up some interesting treasure. He followed one of the Scions' ritualist apprentices, and my guard scooped her up when she fell behind the procession of Scions that recently ascended the stairwell. The guard is holding her on the second floor of the basement.'







---


04. Speak with the apprentice that was captured on the second floor of the basement 0/1 (Tower of Rot)



_Task Window Says:_ To learn more about the sealing ritual, speak with the ritualist apprentice that was captured and is being held on the second floor of the basement.




_Find "a captured apprentice" at /loc -5, 65, -397, and hail her._



You say, 'Hail, a captured apprentice'




a captured apprentice says 'You're not going to hurt me, right? I told you all I know! Ritualist Farji sealed the entrance to the source with the altars of rot and the Magelock Stone. There's no getting in there as long as those altars remain, and you can't destroy them without using the fully powered Magelock Stone -- and it shattered after Farji used it, and he would never let you have the raw mana to power it -- and you're not going to kill me, right?'




a guard says 'I've heard enough of her whining! She's told us all that's useful!'




a captured apprentice died.







---


05. Find the entrance to source of rot on the lowest level of the basement 0/1 (Tower of Rot)

06. Confront Ritualist Farji and take the Raw Mana Flask from him 0/1 (Tower of Rot)



_Task Window Says:_ You must find Ritualist Farji near the entrance to source of rot, on the lowest level of the basement. Confront him and take the Raw Mana Flask from him by any means.




_Head to the basement level, /loc -155, -20, -496, to spawn Ritualist Farji:_



You sense the presence of Ritualist Farji.




_Follow his dialogue, saying "Magelock Stone" to him to make him aggro:_



You say, 'Hail, Ritualist Farji'




Ritualist Farji says 'Oh, ho! You think to follow the master? What courage you have! It's so amusing when braggarts think their courage more powerful than my [rituals]!'




You say, 'Rituals?'




Ritualist Farji says 'The door is sealed, the key shattered. My apprentices have seen to that. By the looks of your coarse hands and atavistic face, you wouldn't be able to mend a torn sleeve let alone the [Magelock Stone]!'




You say, 'What is the Magelock Stone?'




Ritualist Farji says 'Its description would be too much for your simple mind to comprehend. I will not explain its power to bind or release defenses. The only explanations fit for creatures such as you are spoken in the language of brutal violence!'




_After a short delay, he goes aggro. He hits for a max ~23,000 (no flurry; no rampage) and has an emote-based AE "Mana Pulse" (50' range nuke for 45k + 10k mana drain -- 15-to-20 second delay between emote and cast):_



Ritualist Farji prepares to expel a mana pulse.


Ritualist Farji expels a mana pulse.




_Kill him and loot "Raw Mana Flask" from his corpse._






---


07. Speak with Brendaleen Thundershield 0/1 (Tower of Rot)



_Task Window Says:_ Now that you have learned more about the seal and the Magelock Stone, speak with Brendaleen and see what she thinks.




You say, 'Hail, Brendaleen Thundershield'




Brendaleen Thundershield says 'A Magelock Stone and a mana flask? Tresures? Now you're speaking my language! When you're done with these, don't forget your friend Brendaleen! Now, if you're after apprentices, I think those Scions that ascended the stairwell earlier had a green look about them. I sent Guard Werben to follow them after we snatched the straggler. Get up there and talk to Werben; he should know by now what the Scions are planning.'







---


08. Speak with Guard Werben 0/1 (Tower of Rot)



_Task Window Says:_ Guard Werben has been following the apprentices that might be carrying fragments of the Magelock Stone. Speak to Werben on the highest floor of the tower.




_Guard Werben is found on the top floor at /loc -55, -30, 503._



You say, 'Hail, Guard Werben'




Guard Werben says 'Quiet! You don't want to spook the apprentices! They're scared enough as it is. They're trembling at the sight of all these malevolent spirits here, and I don't blame them! From what I've heard, they were sent with some stone fragments to try to perform some rituals to clear the way to Kyle Bayle. But they've been too afraid to do their duty. Cowards!'







---


09. Kill the cowering apprentices and recover the Magelock Fragments 0/8 (Tower of Rot)



_Task Window Says:_ The apprentices are trying to gain access to Kyle Bayle's sanctum. Kill the cowering apprentices and retrieve the Magelock Fragments from them.




_Kill "a cowering apprentice" mobs on the top floor of the tower until you've looted 8x "Magelock Fragment". As far as DPS goes, these mobs are average difficulty (mezzable; hit for a max ~17,500). It's been reported they may be highly resistant to magic-based spells._






---




Speak with Quincy Hollows 0/1 (Tower of Rot)



_Task Window Says:_ Quincy Hollows might know how to repair the Magelock Stone. Speak with him.




You say, 'Hail, Quincy Hollows'




Quincy Hollows looks over the fragments of stone and the mana flask. 'I'm not an expert on magical stones, but I'd say you would need a jeweler's skill to repair such an intricate object. Perhaps combining the mana flask with the fragments would help fuse the stone together?







---


10. Repair the Magelock Stone using a jewelry crafting table 0/1 (Tower of Rot)



_Task Window Says:_ Quincy thinks you can repair the Magelock Stone using a jewel crafting table, the Magelock Fragments, and the Raw Mana Flask.




_There is a jewelry crafting table near Quincy Hollows (/loc -135, 50, 4). In it, combine 8x "Magelock Fragment" + 1x "Raw Mana Flask" to produce 1x "Magelock Stone"._






---




Speak with Quincy Hollows 0/1 (Tower of Rot)



You say, 'Hail, Quincy Hollows'




Quincy Hollows holds the repaired Magelock Stone and frowns. 'It still looks... dead. I think it needs some sort of power. I've traded a few trinkets like this before, and the sellers -- always frightening, threatening-looking folks -- told me such objects could be powered by souls and spirits. There are plenty of ghosts at the top of the tower, right? Perhaps they could bring life back to the stone!







---


11. Weaken the spirits haunting the tower and capture them with the Magelock Stone 0/10 (Tower of Rot)



_Task Window Says:_ You need to charge the Magelock Stone in order to use it. Quincy thinks you should weaken the ghosts in the tower and use the stone to absorb their spirits.




_DPS ghost mobs to low health (under 20%) and right-click the Magelock Stone (this is an instant kill of the mob). Repeat until this step updates and you receive "Empowered Magelock Stone". Note that if you're doing this as a group, a single click will update your entire group._



a pine protector's spirit is captured in the Magelock Stone.


a pine protector's energy is absorbed into the Magelock.


a pine protector's corpse is silent and still.


a pine protector died.




_Upon the last click, you'll see:_



You have successfully powered the Magelock Stone!




_You receive "Empowered Magelock Stone" on your cursor._






---


12. Destroy the first altar of rot on the lowest level of the basement 0/1 (Tower of Rot)

13. Destroy the second altar of rot on the lowest level of the basement 0/1 (Tower of Rot)

14. Destroy the third altar of rot on the lowest level of the basement 0/1 (Tower of Rot)

third altar of rot's defenses are weakened by the Magelock.


Third altar of rot's defenses are destroyed.




third altar of rot regains its defenses.

Skeleslash: PB AE 25', Disease (-975)


1: Decrease Hitpoints by 27692

15. Speak with Deidre Harath 0/1 (Tower of Rot)



You say, 'Hail, Deidre Harath'

**Call of the Forsaken Info & Guides: Overview \| Progression & Task List \| Raiding \| Visible Armor**

---

This group mission begins with Deidre Harath [npc=47047] ( **click for map image**) on the zone-in level of the Tower of Rot.
Pre-Requisites:
\- Completed The Scions of the Tower [quest=7531]
\- Completed The Tower's Victims [quest=7532]
\- Completed Fenn's Job [quest=7533]
\- Completed Narius' Plan [quest=7534]
\- Completed The Adana Problem [quest=7535]
\- Completed The Source of Rot [quest=7536]
**Summary & Requesting the Task**
_Say "final attack" to request the task and "start" to zone in._
\- Hail Captain Deidre Harath at the zone-in.
\- Make your way down the ramp, killing the two tower scions on the way down.
\- DPS Fenn Tarrens to 25% health, then click on the blockade.
\-\-\- Casts "Dagger Barrage" (60' range AE for 17K dmg).
\-\-\- Casts "Tendon Slice" (negates dodge + avoidance).
\- Make your way to the bottom of the ramp, killing tower scions along the way.
**\- Task LOCKS upon location update at Adana.**
\- DPS Adana Baxter to 25% health.
\-\-\- Casts "Splash of Acid" (single-target 6-second blind, cureable by sitting).
\-\-\- Casts "Mana Scorch" (single-target dispell + mana burn up to 15K hp dmg).
\-\-\- The scions behind her cast nukes on random targets (3k per nuke).
\-\-\- These three scions become aggroable after Adana flees.
\- Make your way towards the heart, killing tower scions along the way.
\- Say "interruptions" to make Narius aggro.
\- DPS Narius to 50% health.
\-\-\- Casts "Life Siphon" (dispellable lifetap effect).
\-\-\- Casts "Wither" (increase incoming damage by 25% + DOT for 6,000/tick for 18 seconds).
\-\-\- Adana and Fenn (non-aggroable at this time) cast their "Mana Scorch" and "Dagger Barrage" spells.
\- Once Narius reaches 50%, he goes non-aggro; Adana and Fenn attack.
\- Kill Adana and Fenn, after which Narius re-aggros.
\- Kill Narius.
\- Hail Captain Deidre Harath, who runs down to the entrance of the heart area after Narius' death.
\- Open the chest to complete.
You say, 'Hail, Deidre Harath'
Deidre Harath says 'The door's open, and the way is clear. I've sent the guards I can spare down to keep an eye on the door. Can you lead the [final attack]? If there is anyone you can gather up, it would be a huge help, I'm sure.'
You say, 'I'll lead the final attack'
Deidre Harath says 'I wish I could go with you on your way down, but we'd leave ourselves too vulnerable. I'll send what aid I can, but this is going to mostly on your shoulders. You've got our lives in our hands. Best of luck. Let me know when you want to [start].'
You have been assigned the task 'A Rotten Heart'.
Captain Harath has asked you to investigate what the Scions of the Tower are doing in the basement of the Tower of Rot. Rumors say that there is some sort of monstrous heart down there and the cultists have been talking about it. She insists that the Scions need to be cleared out of the tower and that you should not spare them a moment's pity. She needs to know what they are up to down there.
You say, 'Let's start'
Deidre Harath says 'Good luck!'
LOADING, PLEASE WAIT...
You have entered Tower of Rot: A Rotten Heart.

---

**NAMED MOBS**
_"Mad Martyr" has a chance to spawn next to the entrance to the heart platform (you can't miss her). She casts "Bludgeoning" (69K frontal AE) + "Telekaria" (targeted 40' range AE for 93K dmg + 3-second stun) + "Fire Beam" (123K frontal AE)._
_Known loot from this named:_
Martyr's Loop of Dedication
Sanguine Bloodband
Strength of the Adherent
Shroud of the Forgotten _(bonus)_

---

Speak with Deidre Harath 0/1 (Tower of Rot)
You say, 'Hail, Captain Deidre Harath'
Captain Deidre Harath says 'Good, you're here. I'm not entirely sure what's going on here, but as you can see, it's been bloody. I need you to find out what's going on and see if you can find this massive heart that the rumors speak of. Clearly some of those Scions have been here too. That proves that they are involved in this, if not behind this whole mess. I expect you to deal with them harshly when you encounter them. If you need to [leave], I can assist you.'

---

Clear the area of opposition 0/19 (Tower of Rot)
_This step updates as you progress through the task. Just kill "a scion of the tower" as you come across them while moving through the zone._
Locate the rumored massive heart 0/1 (Tower of Rot)
_This location updates at Fenn Tarrens, a short distance down the ramp from the zone-in. Adana Baxter appears there as well, but leaves Fenn's side:_
Fenn Tarrens says 'Well, it looks like the screams we heard weren't just those bootlickers seeing shadows. This doesn't look like a real problem, but you might want to go warn the boss anyhow.'
Adana Baxter says 'Sure. But make sure to kill a few before you die. We don't have the time to deal with this right now.'
Fenn Tarrens says 'Get out of here Adana before I kill you too.'
Adana Baxter smiles. 'As you say.'
Fenn Tarrens says 'You over there, come along then. I have to kill you quick so I don't miss the fun downstairs.'

---

Defeat Fenn Tarrens 0/1 (Tower of Rot)
_Fenn Tarrens casts "Dagger Barrage" (60' range AE for 17K dmg) and "Tendon Slice" (negates dodge and avoidance)._
_DPS Fenn Tarrens to 25% health to update this step:_
Fenn Tarrens says 'What? The life shield! Well, I guess the boss doesn't want me to finish this, lucky for you. Good thing this shield makes me almost completely invulnerable. That will make my exit much more expedient.'

---

Push past the blockade 0/1 (Tower of Rot)
_Just click the table at the blockade._
\_\_\_\_\_ pushes aside the table, opening a passage through the blockade.
Fenn Tarrens leaps from the bridge, looking over her shoulder. 'See ya later, fools!'

---

Explore the rumored massive heart 0/1 (Tower of Rot)
Defeat Adana Baxter 0/1 (Tower of Rot)
_Find Adana Baxter at /loc 240, -140, -849 (bottom of the twisting ramp)._
**TASK LOCKS upon the location update.**
_Adana Baxter appears alongside three cultist mobs (non-aggroable; will proc 3K nukes on random targets while Adana is engaged):_
Adana Baxter says 'You're not getting past me. The boss has his ritual to perform and I'm not about to let you interfere. I have too much riding on this. It's cost me too much.'
_She casts "Splash of Acid" (single-target blind, stun, nuke for 6 seconds) and "Mana Scorch" (single-target mana burn, up to 15K HP dmg). DPS her to 25% health to update this step. Note that the scions with her become aggroable after she flees._
Adana Baxter says 'Well, I guess I've been called back to work. Get 'em, lackeys!' A magical shield envelopes Adana as similar shields fall off the scions.'

---

Explore the rumored massive heart 0/1 (Tower of Rot)
_This updates at /loc 0, -45, -841 (just enter the platform area that houses the giant heart)._
Confront Narius 0/1 (Tower of Rot)
_Follow his dialogue to update the step ("interruptions" makes him go aggro)._
You say, 'Hail, Narius Grandon'
Narius Grandon says 'Hello. So you are the intruders that my friends mentioned. I can only assume that you have been fighting with them because you don't [understand] what is going on here.'
You say, 'No, I don't understand...'
Narius Grandon says 'Of course, of course. You see, I've come here to heal this place. I've studied the arts of healing and of necromancy and, not meaning to brag, I have mastered them both. I am the only one that can repair the damage that has been done here. I can heal this heart, which will surely weaken the undead and their terrible [master].'
You say, 'Who is their master?'
Narius Grandon says 'His name is Kyle Bayle. All these undead are under his control. They pose no real danger to me, but clearly they are dangerous to others. It's impossible to know what his plans are, but he can't be trusted with such power. This tower needs to be healed and [control] of it wrested from Bayle and his undead.'
You say, 'Who would control it?'
Narius Grandon says 'I am probably the only one capable of understanding and harnessing the power that resides here. The Scions of the Tower have studied this place and battled to gain control of this room. We have sacrificed much to be here, and we have earned the [right] to take that power!'
You say, 'You don't have that right!'
Narius Grandon says 'You doubt me? Are you one of those that mock the Scions? Are you one of those that doubt that we will find the source of eternal life? Look here, upon this great heart. It exists in a state of eternal decay. I can reverse that state. I can make it a source of eternal life! And once I have obtained that power there will be no more doubters. You and your kind will have to understand the truth of the Scions, the truth of the Tower! Now leave this place immediately! I have no time for foolish [interruptions]!'
You say, 'Interruptions!'
Narius Grandon says 'Fine, I shall encourage you to leave.'

---

Eliminate Narius 0/1 (Tower of Rot)
Eliminate Adana 0/1 (Tower of Rot)
Eliminate Fenn 0/1 (Tower of Rot)
_Narius casts "Life Siphon" (dispellable lifetap effect) and "Wither" (increase incoming damage by 25% + DOT for 6,000/tick for 18 seconds). Fenn Tarrens and Adana Baxter (non-aggroable at this time) uses their "Dagger Barrage" and "Mana Scorch" abilities, respectively._
_When Narius reaches 50% health, he goes non-aggro:_
Narius Grandon shouts 'Enough! Fenn! Adana! Get over here and kill these heretics!'
_Adana and Fenn attack. Adana is leashed to her spot. Each of them starts at 40% health, so it won't take long to deal with them. Once they're dead, Narius re-aggro's, and can be killed._
Narius Grandon has been slain by \_\_\_\_\_!
_The remaining Scions flee:_
a scion of the tower shouts 'Let's get out of here while the life shield lasts!'

---

Report the fate of the Scions of the Tower to Captain Harath 0/1 (Tower of Rot)
_After Narius is killed, Captain Deidre Harath will run down from the zone-in, so it may take a bit before you see her. She stops at the entrance to the heart platform._
You say, 'Hail, Captain Deidre Harath'
Captain Deidre Harath says 'Thank you for your efforts. All of Norrath should be grateful for what you have done. It is time for us to [leave] this place, I think.'
Open the chest for your reward 0/1 (Tower of Rot)
Captain Harath was grateful for your assistance. She thinks you might have prevented a great tragedy.
